# LCA website redesign: implementation brief

**For:** whoever builds the redesign, working in `github.com/Kobi-Lipari/lca-website`
**Owner and reviewer:** K
**Version:** 1.4, Thursday, October 22, 2026. Adds K's decisions on canvas pages 7 to 16, on who may create a tournament (in three phases, ending with members' own unlisted events), on the scanner and on a club's contact, and what WS01 handed on to later workstreams, from DESIGN_REPLAN_phase2.md sections 4, 7 and 8 and `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`.
**Design source:** the canvas "LCA Look & Feel Options". Every page from 1 to 16 now carries decided or picked boards. Pages 1 to 5 carry the decided boards (copies in `docs/redesign/decided-boards`). Page 6 (Registration) is decided as the Reg-C household checkout with the family model; its decided board is drawn when WS06 starts. Pages 7 and 10 to 12 carry the boards decided on Fri, Oct 9, 2026, and pages 8, 9 and 13 to 16 the boards K picked on Thu, Oct 15 and Mon, Oct 19, 2026 (copies of pages 7 to 16 in `docs/redesign/canvas/boards`, all but `Member-D`, which is opened on the canvas). Where K asked for a redraw that is not on the canvas yet, the board it grows from, read with K's change as section 1.2 writes it, is the reference. Board names in this brief, such as `Event-Final-1` or `Clubs-H`, refer to artboards on that canvas. Open the named board before building a screen.

This brief turns the redesign options K picked from into buildable work. It sets out:
- the look,
- the pick for every section of the site,
- 15 workstreams, each with data, API, frontend and acceptance criteria,
- six phases, then a walkthrough of everything built,
- the decisions still open, with a recommendation for each.

**Changes in 1.2** (the reasoning for each is in `docs/redesign/DESIGN_REPLAN_phase0.md`, sections 1 to 5 and 8):
- **Looks (1.1, WS01):** Heritage Club becomes a full scope for LCA's lasting record (About, governance, Champions) with its own fonts; Bright Scholastic's fonts and rollout move to WS11; the live tag's dot is static.
- **Logo (D1 amended):** the rook mark is the header's brand mark at every width; the official logo keeps the footer, the homepage identity band and the other roomy placements.
- **Header (1.2, WS02):** H1 as one 64px row with no condensing, H4's search as a Search button and panel, H5's event strip for involved people only. Renew shows only once a membership has expired. When the row is tight, Donate moves into the About menu first, then the brand name hides.
- **Phones (1.2, WS02):** M2's bottom tab bar with M1's sheet behind Menu, and M4's banner and a docked event bar; Donate is two taps on phones.
- **Footer (1.2, WS02):** F1's four columns, rebuilt from `nav.ts`, with the Appearance control in the base line.
- **Search (WS02, WS03):** one combobox in the header panel, on the homepage and at `/search`; no Players group, no person names.
- **Home (1.2, WS03):** a ten-rung hero ladder with a 30-day window (eleven rungs from 1.3, with the Featured rung); new block order (hero, identity and search band, News, doors, upcoming, results and champions, membership); in the quiet and clubs rungs the hero carries the doors.
- **Platform (2.2, 0.2):** a per-user event-mode endpoint, `functions/utils/events.ts` moved to WS03, the Phase 0 flag list and the deviations to record.
- **WS12 AC6** follows the new Donate placement.

**Changes in 1.3** (the reasoning for each is in `docs/redesign/DESIGN_REPLAN_phase1.md`, sections 1 to 12):
- **Tournaments list (1.2, WS04):** one list with List, Calendar, Map and Table views from the decided page 4 boards; one filter-and-sort line (When, Type, Region, Sort) with an info note; a preview pane that replaces the rail; reminders by bell (week before, day before, early price, opens, and "If this listing changes" on partner pages); a public entrants endpoint; partner pages with human-error rules.
- **Tournament page (1.2, WS05):** one unified page for every LCA-run and club-run event (the `layout` column is not added); `EventSubNav` replaces `ChampionshipTabs`; entry limits overall or by section; fee tiers only when set; one or two schedules with a merge round; side events; Venue & travel as one block; round alerts by email for registered viewers; festivals as an admin-created page under WS05 with a Featured rung on the home hero (WS03, `homeFeatured`).
- **Registration and family (WS06):** the one-player-one-profile family model (`member_guardians`, logins from 13 with recorded consent, no `dependents` table); the Reg-C household checkout with the "Who's playing?" picker; one receipt system for every payment with `stripe_events` idempotency.
- **One entry price (WS05, WS06, WS08, WS12):** no member price or discount anywhere; `member_discount` is retired; an LCA membership is required to enter LCA events, with the per-tournament `requires_lca_membership` switch and a planned site-wide override.
- **Live mode (WS07):** round alerts are email only, to registered players who have not switched off, routed through `recipientsFor`.
- **Director tools (WS08):** setup steps for the tagline and championship title, the entry-limit mode and caps, the fee ladder and membership switch, Schedules, Side events and Venue & travel; the `listing_report` queue source; `/admin/festivals`; long forms scroll in their own container; check-in search by name, US Chess ID or parent's name.
- **Champions (WS10):** `tournaments.championship_title` links the event page to the honour roll.
- **Membership and My LCA (WS12, WS13):** Family coverage chosen by ticks, no child emails, one card and QR per player profile, the TD membership desk; the family section's cards, guardians panel and claimed teen's dashboard; `uscf_expires_on` replaced by the existing `members.uscf_expiration`.
- **Decisions:** D11 to D16 added; D15 supersedes the abbreviation mechanism in D3. The replan's open questions are carried at the end of section 5 with their defaults as working assumptions.

**Changes in 1.4** (the reasoning for each is in `docs/redesign/DESIGN_REPLAN_phase2.md`: section 4 for pages 7 and 10 to 12 and the brief-wide changes, section 7 for pages 8, 9 and 13 to 16 and K's decisions of Thu, Oct 15 to Tue, Oct 20, 2026, and section 8 for the walkthrough K asked for on Wed, Oct 21, 2026; where sections 4 and 7 differ, section 7 wins, so the club rep stays):
- **Working rules (0.1, 0.2):**
  - Rule 3 reads as D15 settled it.
  - Rule 8 and the migrations line say how previews stay off production since WS01: a preview has a database of its own, only the migrate and seed scripts write to it from a terminal, and production migrates only when a PR merges to `main`.
  - No logo, photo or other picture is written from a preview until the club logos bucket has a preview bucket of its own.
  - Every visible change still waits for its switch; only what a screen reader hears ships with every switch off (Q34).
  - A server change that a switch governs follows a constant set in the same change as the switch.
  - The switch table gains the 1.4 rows and names the three older switches of today's Tournaments page. The setting for member-created events is an admin setting, not a switch.
  - The id rule adds the club ids and the other text ids.
  - A part of a "Depends on" line that waits for a later workstream or phase no longer holds up the start.
  - What WS01 left for every workstream: contracts, the accessibility matrix, the bundle check, stored times, and no `fetch` in pages.
  - The "Deviations from v1.3" list, workstream by workstream.
- **The picks (1.1, 1.2, Appendix B):**
  - One row for each part of each canvas page from 1 to 16, naming its decided or picked boards.
  - The Heritage look covers the tabbed About page.
- **Design system (WS01):** built and merged on Thu, Oct 22, 2026. Its section stands as built, and what it handed on is in 0.2 and in each workstream.
- **Navigation (WS02):**
  - The About menu's rows open the new About tabs.
  - The My LCA menu follows page 14's hub once My LCA has its rail.
  - One label for each destination.
  - One listing rule that every public list of events uses, so a member-created event is never listed.
  - WS01's follow-ups on focus rings, the browser bar's colour, the loading line and the event strip's inputs.
- **Homepage (WS03):**
  - The compact Facebook rail reads the mirror (WS10).
  - Member-created events never reach the home page (D26).
  - The this-week strip waits for P2.14.
  - The slideshow is deleted once the new look is on.
- **Tournaments list (WS04):**
  - Season at a glance on the Upcoming tab (assumed, P2.22).
  - Member-created events stay off every list, calendar feed, map, sitemap and structured data (D26).
  - The week-before reminder keeps today's rule for the reminders people already have.
- **Event page and registration (WS05, WS06):**
  - A member-created event's page, checkout and emails say "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." (D27). Its pages tell search engines not to list them (noindex), and its emails go out with the organizer as the reply-to.
  - Who may charge an entry fee above $0 (D24).
  - Today's event page keeps reading today's answer until the new page is on.

- **Live mode (WS07):**

  - Report your result, as a steer the TD confirms (D6 amended).
  - The hall TV can show every section on one screen, suggested at 30 players or fewer.

  - Round emails go only when the director's publish asks for them, and emails to followers wait for a switch of their own until the pilot is reviewed.

- **Director tools (WS08):**

- **Clubs (WS09):**

  - No calendar, and no computed meeting dates on public pages.

- **News and champions (WS10):**

- **Architecture (2.1 to 2.4):**

It is deliberately ambitious. Every workstream is meant to be built, phase by phase.

## 0. Read this first (working rules for whoever builds it)

### 0.1 K's standing rules (non-negotiable)
1. **No AI references anywhere.** That covers commits, PR titles and descriptions, code comments, docs and changelogs:
   - no `Co-Authored-By` lines naming an AI
   - no session links
   - no "Generated with" lines

   Write commits and PRs as plain human-authored text.
2. **Read the real file before you change it,** and match the repo's existing conventions (helpers, naming, response shapes, test harness). This brief names real paths as they were on Monday, October 5, 2026, or, where it says so, as WS01 left them on Thursday, October 22, 2026. If the code has moved, follow the code and note the difference in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`. Items marked `verify:` were not confirmed and must be checked first.
3. **Collect only what's needed.**
   - Confirm eligibility with a checkbox ("In 8th grade or below?"). Never ask for a child's grade or birthdate.
   - LCA doesn't get involved in families' finances: no fee waivers and no lunch-status or need-based questions (decision D5).
   - Results, standings, winners and recaps show players' full names, kids included, because they congratulate performance (decision D3). Pre-event entrants lists show under-18s (a player with an active guardian link, or an entry with a ticked eligibility box) as first name and last initial; TDs and admins see full names (decision D15).
4. **Keep the hard-coded "300+ members" stat. Keep every Facebook link.** The Facebook feed is pulled server-side.
5. **Partner events register on the organizer's site; LCA-run events register on louisianachess.org.** Partner events are Gulf South clearinghouse listings.
6. **Accessibility target is WCAG 2.2 AA.**
   - Status is always shown as words plus colour, never colour alone.
   - Brand gold `#c8a94a` is never text on a light background (2.28:1). Use dark gold `#866a1e` (5.13:1 on white).
   - Gold text on navy `#1a2744` is fine (6.51:1).
7. **Plain language.**
   - Write "US Chess" and "US Chess ID", not USCF, in the UI.
   - Every date carries its weekday: "Sat, Oct 24".
   - Write times as "7:00 PM".
   - Write half points as ½, never 0.5 or 3.5.
   - Chess shorthand gets a plain companion the first time: "G/90+30 · 90 min each + 30 sec per move".
8. **Previews never touch the production database.** Since WS01, `wrangler.toml` binds `DB` to its own database, `lca-db-preview`, under `[env.preview]` for every branch except `main`, so a preview reads and writes that database and never `lca-db`. Nothing run for a preview may reach `lca-db`, the live site, a Pages production address, or a single deployment's own address (`<8 hex characters>.lca-website.pages.dev`), which can run on production data. From a terminal, only `npm run db:migrate:preview` and `npm run db:seed:preview` write to the preview database, both through `scripts/db/preview-guard.ts`. Two things are still shared with production: the `CLUB_LOGOS` bucket, so a logo or photo uploaded on a preview shows on the live site, and the sign-in project, so a sign-in on a preview is a real account. Until `CLUB_LOGOS` has a preview bucket of its own (WS09 binds it, or WS04 first if it stores its link-preview pictures there), no logo, photo or other picture is written to it from a preview.

### 0.2 How to work
- **One workstream per branch.** Name branches like `redesign/ws01-design-system` and open one PR per workstream; split large ones into numbered sub-PRs. Don't start a workstream until everything in its "Depends on" line is merged, apart from a part that waits for a later workstream or phase, as that line or section 4 says.
- **Ship behind flags.** Every user-visible change goes behind a flag in `src/lib/features.ts` (the `FEATURES` object, defaulting to `false`). K flips flags after checking the preview. Each flag gets the existing style: a short plain-English comment saying what it turns on. The flags are listed in the table below.
- **Every visible change waits for its switch** (restated in 1.4). Words on a page wait for that page's switch, and a fix that shows only in the new look waits for `newLook`. A change in what the server answers or sends that a visitor would see (a message, a Stripe line name, an email) waits for the server constant set with its switch (below). The one kind of change that ships with every switch off is state a screen reader hears and nobody sees (`aria-pressed`, `aria-current`, `aria-expanded`), as WS01 step 32 added it, recorded in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md` when its PR lands. Anything else that would ship with every switch off needs K's yes before its PR merges (Q34).
- **Server constants.** Functions and jobs cannot read `src/lib/features.ts`, so a server change that a switch must govern (one that widens or narrows who may do something, changes what today's pages read or show, changes the words or the emails a visitor gets, or moves a notice from email into Admin) follows a constant set in the same change as its switch, with a unit test that the two agree: `CLUB_EMAIL_HIDDEN` (`clubEmailHidden`), `ROSTER_SHORT_NAMES` (`rosterShortNames`), `CREATION_ROLES_ON` (`standingDirectors`), `EVENT_PAGE_ON` (`newEventPage`), `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` (`householdCheckout`), `TD_CONSOLE_ON` (`tdConsole`), `FOLLOWER_ALERTS_ON` (`followerAlerts`), `CLUB_PAGES_ON` (`newClubs`), `NEWS_MIRROR_ON` (`newNews`) and `ADMIN_HOME_ON` (`adminHome`). A change that only takes out a field no page reads, such as the officers' emails and the internal fields the public-answer trim removes (section 4), needs neither.
- **Keep `REDESIGN_STATUS.md` at the repo root (new),** in the same spirit as the scanner's STATUS.md protocol. For each workstream record:
  - status, branch and PR
  - decisions taken, deviations from this brief and follow-ups
  - any `verify:` item resolved

  Update it in every PR.
- **Gate before every commit:** `npm run lint`, `npm run build`, `npm run typecheck:functions`, `npm run test:all`. Run `npm run scanner:check` too when you touch `scanner/` or `functions/utils/scan/`. The route-audit test that checks every `fetch('/api/…')` in `src/lib/api.ts` against a handler file must stay green.
- **Migrations** continue after the highest-numbered file on the branch the PR lands on. The brief's check of Monday, October 5 ended at `migrations/0051_club_map_location.sql`; `0052_sections_schedules.sql` and `0053_sections_schedules_backfill.sql` are on `main` since WS01: they add `tournament_sections` (with `cap` and the fee tiers `fee_early`, `fee_regular` and `fee_late`), `tournament_schedules` (`label`, `time_control`, `is_primary`, `merge_round`), `tournament_schedule_rounds`, and `registrations.section_id` and `schedule_id`, with triggers that keep the tables filled from the `sections` and `round_schedule` JSON. This brief names migrations `00xx_<name>.sql`; the replan's `0052_player_profiles.sql` takes the next free number instead. Number them in the order they land.
  - Apply locally with `npm run db:migrate:local`. Merging to `main` applies new migrations to the production database (the GitHub Actions job in `migrate-db.yml`), so a migration reaches production only through a merged PR: never run one against `lca-db` by hand (`npm run db:migrate:remote` is not used). The only other remote database is the preview's: before reviewing a branch's preview, K applies its migrations there with `npm run db:migrate:preview`, since nothing migrates `lca-db-preview` automatically (ordering rule 2 in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`). A read of production that this brief asks for ("verify on production") is K's: one `SELECT` per command, never a write. Since WS01, `main` holds migrations up to `0054_requires_lca_membership.sql`, and `npm run db:generate` writes the next one from the Drizzle schema in `functions/db/schema.ts`.
  - When a table must be rebuilt in SQLite, use the existing table name rather than a `_new` rename while child rows exist. This is a known D1 gotcha in this repo.
  - `tournaments.id`, `registrations.id` and `members.id` are TEXT (migrations 0001 and 0046): every `tournament_id`, `parent_event_id`, `registration_id`, `cloned_from_id` or member id sketched as INTEGER in this brief, and any other column that references one of those three tables, is TEXT when built.
  - Added in 1.4: `clubs.id` is TEXT as well (migration 0001), so every `club_id` is TEXT too, and so is any column that references another existing TEXT id (`tournament_games`, `governance_documents`, `lca_posts` and `club_news` among them).
- **Tests.** Unit tests go in `test/unit`. Integration tests go in `test/integration` and use the `invoke()` harness in `harness.ts` and the seed factories in `factories.ts`. Every new endpoint gets an integration test, including a role-safety case. `role-safety.test.ts` and `club-permissions.test.ts` are the models to follow.
- **Write files directly.** Write TSX that holds special characters in an editor, not through shell heredocs.
- **What WS01 left for every workstream** (added in 1.4; the detail is under Follow-ups in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`):
  - **Contracts.** A PR that changes an endpoint adds its request and response contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test in `test/integration/contracts/`, moves the handler to `parseBody` where it reads a body, and takes the endpoint out of `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts`, lowering `PENDING_COUNT` (110 endpoint methods at the end of WS01). The list only shrinks.
  - **The accessibility matrix.** A PR that rebuilds a page adds it to `PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`, which reads each page in every look, and runs `npm run test:a11y` with `test/a11y/baseline.json` (the site with every switch off) unchanged. `REDESIGN_STATUS.md` lists the 26 routes still waiting and the workstream that brings each one in.
  - **The gate** also runs `npm run check:bundle`. The lint error count never rises, and every lint error in a file the PR touches is fixed.
  - **Stored times.** A `datetime('now')` column shown with `formatDate` or `formatTime` passes `{ stored: 'utc' }`; the default reads wall-clock text and would show those times 5 to 6 hours early.
  - **No `fetch` in pages.** A call goes in its area's `api.ts` (`src/areas/<area>/api.ts`), and a page a workstream rebuilds is split into area components as it is rebuilt. The large ones left are `TournamentDetailPage.tsx` (WS05, WS06), `TournamentManagePage.tsx` (WS08), `TournamentsPage.tsx` (WS04) and `ScannerPage.tsx` (WS15).

**Phase 0 and Phase 1 flags, the rows added in 1.4, and three older switches.** Version 1.2 added the Phase 0 rows, 1.3 the Phase 1 rows, and 1.4 the rows from `membershipRequirementSwitch` to `scanner2`. The phase 1 replan names only `homeFeatured` of the Phase 1 rows, and the phase 2 replan names `resultReports`, `seasonSchedule`, `newScholastic`, `newNews` and `newAbout` of the 1.4 rows. This brief names the rest, so every PR uses the same names. Add these to `src/lib/features.ts`, all default `false`, each with a plain comment. The Phase 0 and Phase 1 rows and `membershipRequirementSwitch` are in the file since WS01, and the last three rows were there before the redesign; of the other 1.4 rows, the public-answer trim adds `clubEmailHidden` and `rosterShortNames` in Phase 0, and each workstream adds its own. `member_events_enabled` is the one row that is not a switch in that file: it is an admin site setting, as its row says. The last three rows are switches on today's Tournaments page, which go when WS04 removes that page.

| Flag | Turns on | Can go on in |
|---|---|---|
| `newLook` | WS01 base tokens, dark mode, focus ring, formats | Phase 0 |
| `themeHeritage` | About, Board, Bylaws, Minutes, Annual meeting and Champions in the Heritage Club look | Phase 0 |
| `themeScholastic` | Scholastic pages in the Bright Scholastic look | WS11 |
| `newNav` | the six-section header, My LCA menu, footer, tablet hybrid, and on phones the app bar, tab bar and sheet (one flag, so no combination leaves a phone without navigation); with it on, the build also gives the page's viewport tag `viewport-fit=cover`, since only the new bars pad for a phone's notch | Phase 0 |
| `siteSearch` | the header Search button, ⌘K panel and `/search` | Phase 0 |
| `homeSearch` | the search box in the homepage band | Phase 0 |
| `eventStrip` | the personal event strip on desktop and tablet in the week, day before, check-in, event day, final and after states (`final` = `status = 'completed'` or a non-null result in every section's last scheduled round) and, from check-in on day 1, the phone event bar and banner | Phase 0, after a preview check with a real registration |

**Deviations from v1.1 to record in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`** when the matching PR lands:
- WS01: Heritage loads Libre Caslon Display and Source Serif 4 instead of reusing Instrument Serif. Baloo 2 and Nunito move to WS11. The event-mode phase machine lives in `domain/events/eventMode.ts`, not `functions/utils/eventMode.ts` (recorded in step 5). Step 7 records three page routes outside `functions/api`, not two (`functions/news/[slug].ts` as well), and that the client API types move to the contracts in step 13 (recorded in step 7). Step 8 replaces the planned schedules JSON column with the existing `round_schedule` column (kept, with `sections`), and adds archived section rows, the revive-on-return rule and a sync trigger that outlives checkpoint B (recorded in step 8). Step 9 (recorded in step 9) adds decision 7 (a section with entries that are not withdrawn, or with games, cannot be removed), the Drizzle conversion rule (a query converts only when a step changes its SQL or bound values), and 400/409 refusals for repeated section names and a save that raced another. Step 10 (recorded in step 10) lets `priceEntry` take sections as `loadSections` returns them, answers malformed registration bodies with `{ error, fields }`, refuses an unknown payment status with 400 before the role check, prices a text section fee at the event fee, rewords two "USCF ID" messages and shows the weekday in the membership warning, and writes a section move and its repriced payment in one batch. Step 12 (recorded in step 12) answers `round_schedule` as a list and the club page's sections as objects, adds `id`, `cap` and `fees` to every section answer, validates the registration settings PATCH with `{ error, fields }` on a malformed body, and says "US Chess" and shows the weekday in the rating report, all without a flag. Step 13 (recorded in step 13) types the site's tournament answers from the contract type `SavedSection` (not `tournamentSectionSchema`), prices the tournament page and the family panel through a new `priceShownSection` helper instead of `priceEntry` directly, reads the price clock once when those pages open, adds `ApiSectionDraft` for the editing screens, removes `entryPrice` and `sectionBaseFee`, adds `npm run check:bundle`, and leaves a rename box to the setup redesign. Step 14 (recorded in step 14) answers `schedules[]` in every tournament answer and keeps `round_schedule` as the primary schedule's rounds, writes the JSON mirror from normalised rows, and adds 400/409 refusals (both body forms sent, removing a schedule that has entries, a round count below a merge round) without a flag. Step 15 (recorded in step 15) removes member pricing without a flag (the setup form's member-discount input, the tournament page's "LCA members save" line and the home page's "discounted tournament entry"), adds migration 0054 with a hand-written backfill that sets existing club-run events to off, lets only an LCA admin or the organizing club's rep set `requires_lca_membership` (an assigned director gets 403), and puts the setup page's switch behind `membershipRequirementSwitch`, off until WS06 enforces the rule at checkout. Step 16 (recorded in step 16) groups the server helpers into `functions/utils/events`, `registration`, `membership`, `live` and `admin` with a scripted move, leaves the four `domain/` re-exports and `family.ts` at the root, so there is no `households/` folder, and adds `scripts/move-files.ts`, `scripts/verify-move.ts` and ten tests; the helper paths named elsewhere in this spec and in the phase plans date from before the move. Step 17 (recorded in step 17) moves nine site library files into `src/areas/events`, `clubs` and `admin` with the same scripted move, adds an `index.ts` to each of eight area folders (governance as the eighth), and keeps the platform files and the one-line `domain/` re-exports (`pricing`, `sectionRules`, `lcaTime`, `regions`, `family`) in `src/lib`; the `src/lib` paths this spec gives for `clearinghouse.ts` and `roles.ts` date from before the move. Step 18 (recorded in step 18) splits `src/lib/api.ts` into `src/lib/http.ts` and one `api.ts` in each of the eight area folders with a scripted split, keeps `src/lib/api.ts` as a barrel so no importer changes, puts news posts with `events`, support tickets, the contact form, campaigns and announcements with `admin`, the US Chess rating lookup with `membership` and the scanner's call with `live`, leaves each area's `index.ts` without the area's `api.ts`, and also points `client-sections-audit.test.ts` at the events module; a reference in this spec to a call in `src/lib/api.ts` now means the area module that `scripts/moves/api-split.json` names. Step 19 (recorded in step 19) moves the domain components from `src/components` into `src/areas/<area>/components` (`events`, `live`, `households`, `admin`, `governance`, `clubs`, `membership` and `registration`) with the same scripted move and `scripts/moves/client-components.json`, keeps the pieces every area uses in `src/components`, leaves the area `index.ts` files without component re-exports, and adds three lint rules to `eslint.config.js` (an area file importing a page is an error; a deep import of another area, past its `index.ts` or `api.ts`, and a page calling `fetch` are warnings until the imports and calls listed in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md` are fixed); the component paths this spec gives under `src/components/tournaments`, `family`, `admin`, `governance`, `maps` and `uscf` date from before the move. Step 20 (recorded in step 20) adds the browser test layer: Playwright and axe, pinned, with `npm run test:a11y` outside the gate, which builds the site twice with `vite build --mode a11y` before Playwright starts (a `globalSetup` cannot, because Playwright starts its web servers first) and serves both builds on seeded local data with fake secrets; `VITE_FLAGS` counts only in that mode and the `/__fixtures/:name` route exists only in that build; `scripts/seed/fixtures.sql` is the synthetic seed that `db:seed:preview` loads through the guard; the family registration and round publishing specs are `test.fixme` and run only through a dispatch-only preview run that refuses the live site and any single deployment's address; and `eslint.config.js`, `src/vite-env.d.ts` and `test/unit/app-routes.test.tsx` change outside the planned files. Step 21 (recorded in step 21) keeps the Badge's full-strength `ring-ring` on `focus`, which the old `--focus` cannot draw; takes the Looks-3 "Opens soon" text (`#705718`) and the scholastic coral (`#b23e1a`) and sky (`#2563c9`) from the tiles; keeps navy as the base ink where the Looks-3 tile names `#0b1220`; adds `--field` and the live, scholastic and heritage extras as tokens; turns the focus ring gold inside a navy band or an element marked `data-ground="dark"`, and the shadcn glow off under it; keeps `src/lib/features.ts` in a chunk of its own and has `vite.config.ts` read `newLook` from that file's text; adds `test/unit/helpers/cssTokens.ts` and six test files beyond the planned three (`token-utilities`, `tokens-scopes`, `geist-mono-glyphs`, `appearance-control`, `appearance-control-off` and `redesign-status-step21`); and notes on `themeHeritage` and `themeScholastic` in `src/lib/features.ts` that those looks need `newLook` on. Step 22 (recorded in step 22) declares the Barlow Condensed and Barlow faces in `src/styles/themes/live.css` without binding them, which WS07 does; leaves JetBrains Mono and Big Shoulders Display out, because Geist Mono stays the only mono face; gives `ThemeScope` a `layout` prop and an `as` prop; reports a bad nesting in every mode except the production build; has `routeScope` return only base or heritage; makes the AC8 switch in the test rather than with a control on the fixture page; and adds four unit test files and a second browser spec beyond the planned three. Step 23 (recorded in step 23), a step this brief does not name, moves the pages' navy text, white grounds and navy bands to the tokens with `scripts/codemods/semantic-colours.ts` and a reviewed map, because dark mode follows the device as soon as `newLook` is on; beyond the five rewrites it was given, it maps navy text on an opaque gold fill to `text-brand-navy` and keeps every navy tint in the brand navy at its opacity, adds `--color-brand-navy` to the brand `@theme` block and `.bg-brand-navy` to the gold focus ring rule, with a second rule that gives a light menu inside a navy band the look's own ring back, gives the three navy Buttons of the support pages `dark:text-white`, leaves the grey classes and the dark-mode look of the navy tints to step 24, keeps five white mats and the translucent white veils as map entries with reasons, marks the homepage hero, the red and blue announcement tones and the two club heroes drawn with an inline navy with `data-ground="dark"`, runs the dark check as two specs, adds eight unit test files and a helper beyond the planned identity test, and adds three modules to fix the lint errors in the files it rewrites. Step 24 (recorded in step 24), a step this brief does not name either, moves the pages' status colours (emerald, green, red, amber, yellow, blue and purple), their greys and slates, the navy lines, ring and faint tints step 23 left and their hex colour classes to the tokens with the same script and a second reviewed map, `scripts/codemods/semantic-colours.status.map.json`; it adds a token for each Tailwind shade a page used, at that shade on the plain `:root` and at its tone's words or tint under the new look, rather than mapping the greys to `--ink-muted` and `--hairline`, which would change today's look; it keeps the light tones on navy, StatusBadge's tones (step 26), the print page's row rules and the external-event dots with their reasons, and leaves every gold hex text class (34 uses in 20 files) to step 25; it adds `test/unit/raw-colours.test.ts`, a ratchet over every Tailwind palette colour, `lca-navy` class, `bg-white` in any form and hex colour class in TSX, whose allowlist counts every use that no keep entry names in its one string; it gives the club map's loading note two tokens that stay as the light look draws them, because the map's paper stays light in every look; it adds a block for browsers that cannot mix colours, puts the dark look, the live look and heritage in dark mode inside `@media screen` so a printed page takes the light look, extends the script's map to hex classes and keep entries for a whole file, and moves three values out of `GradeConfirm.tsx` to `src/areas/events/gradeConfirm.ts` to fix its lint errors. Step 25 (recorded in step 25) moves every gold text class off the light grounds, not only `text-lca-gold` and the two strings it names: `scripts/codemods/gold-ink.ts` reads the ground of each `text-lca-gold` and rewrites the 82 on a light ground to `text-gold-ink`, keeping brand gold in a dark container only where it reads 4.5:1 there (a ground marked `data-ground="dark"` counts only with a navy of its own, so the home page hero's darkened photo is a person's decision), and a third map, `scripts/codemods/semantic-colours.gold.map.json`, moves the 34 gold hex text classes step 24 left it to three new tokens, `--gold-ink-badge`, `--gold-ink-badge-club` and `--gold-on-dark-badge`, rather than to `--gold-ink` and `--gold-on-dark`, which are brand gold on the plain `:root`; it gives the News page's pin its word under `newLook` and adds no name to the reminder bell, which is decoration beside its words; it draws a club's colour as words through a new `clubTextColor`, since the database gives every club brand gold; it moves `GOLD_BUTTON` to `text-brand-navy`; and its guard, `test/unit/goldText.test.ts`, reads `#705718` and `#866a1e` as hex colours in the code, with WS02's three places in its fixture matching nothing yet, and holds the new badge and pill tokens, brand gold as an icon's paint, and the `on` prop of the filter dropdowns and StatusBadge to their grounds as well. ---

## 1. The picture

louisianachess.org becomes the place Louisiana chess runs on. A visitor finds a game in seconds, whether a tournament this weekend or a club that meets near them. A parent registers a whole family in one checkout. Players follow a round live from their phones. Results build up into history. Volunteers keep it all current with tools that do the remembering for them.

### 1.1 The look (canvas page 1)
| Scope | Look | How it's applied |
|---|---|---|
| Site-wide base | **Modern Tech-Minimal** (`Looks-3-Minimal-Tile`, `Looks-3-Minimal-Page`) | Geist and Geist Mono, with one Instrument Serif accent. Navy ink and white/near-white grounds. Gold is a fill or accent only, plus `#866a1e` for gold-toned text on light grounds. Chess character comes from coordinates (the a to h and 8 to 1 grid motif), notation and real data. It's the closest to today's site, since Geist is already loaded, and the most data-friendly. |
| Event-day surfaces | **Broadcast Night** (`Looks-7-*`) | Scoped dark theme via `data-theme="live"` on pairings, standings, My board, hall TV and the event page while a round is live, plus a "Night" toggle, the homepage hero's live mode (colour tokens only, no live fonts on `/`), and the header event strip's colour tokens while a round is live (Geist stays; Barlow loads only on the live routes). Never the site default. The live tag's dot is static; the word "Live" carries the meaning. |
| Scholastic pages | **Bright Scholastic** (`Looks-6-*`) | Scoped via `data-theme="scholastic"` on `/scholastic/*` only, never on registration, checkout, shared event pages, pairings or standings (those show a grade-band chip with its words, "K-5"). Navy carries all text and buttons. Colour only ever means a grade-band section, always beside the band's words; status tags use the base status tokens. WS01 ships the token block and an axe fixture; the Baloo 2 and Nunito chunk and the rollout ship in WS11 behind `themeScholastic`, after K previews it. |
| Heritage pages | **Heritage Club** (`Looks-1-*`) as a scope | **Rule: Minimal is for doing, Heritage is for the lasting record, Broadcast is for what is live now.** Full scope (a `ThemeScope` box inside the site's `<main>`, heritage fonts load) on `/about` and its tabs (`/about/board`, `/about/bylaws`, `/about/minutes`, `/about/champions`), `/meeting`, and, until `newAbout` and `newNews` redirect them, `/governance/board`, `/governance/bylaws`, `/governance/minutes` and `/champions` (the editors and forms inside them reset to base through `ThemeScope scope="base"`, the one permitted nesting; the history timeline is not built, assumed, P2.40); later on the membership card, certificates and the final-report cover; and as a component scope for the `HonorBoard` above final standings of events with `is_state_championship = 1` and status completed. Accent only (base scope, Geist, no heritage fonts) on the homepage identity band, Champions band and Results-mode winners block, using `ThickThinRule` and `Seal`. Heritage changes headings, body type, rules, seal, grounds and gold-ink only; it never restyles `Button`, `Input`, chips, nav, `StatusBadge`, the focus ring, control radius, or date and score formats. The header and footer are always base. Everything else, including `/membership`, `/give` (today's `/donate`), every form, the print kit and all email, stays Minimal. |
| Logo and mark | The board-voted full-colour LCA logo, plus the one-colour rook-shaped Louisiana mark (today's favicon) | **Decided (D1, amended in 1.2).** The rook mark is the header's brand mark at every width, desktop, tablet and phone, and also goes on the favicon, small badges, QR centres and social avatars. The official logo keeps the footer brand block, homepage identity band, About and history pages, emails, print kit, certificates and the membership card. Never recolour the logo; set it on white or cream. |

`Looks-Compare` shows all seven looks side by side. Editorial Sports, Swiss Grid and Louisiana Rooted are not recommended as the base:
- **Editorial Sports** needs a writer after every event.
- **Swiss Grid** is cold and effectively a rebrand.
- **Louisiana Rooted** needs parish data that doesn't exist yet.

Louisiana Rooted's region and parish chips are worth revisiting once region data exists (D10).

### 1.2 The pick for each section (canvas pages 1 to 16)
One row for each part of the site, with its canvas page; every page from 1 to 16 names its decided or picked boards. Where K asked for a redraw that is not on the canvas yet, the row names the board with K's change, and that board read with the change is the reference until the redraw lands.

| Section | Build from | Borrow |
|---|---|---|
| Looks (page 1) | `Looks-Applied`, where each look applies (decided; it replaces `Looks-2`, `Looks-4` and `Looks-5`), with Modern Minimal (`Looks-3-Minimal-Tile`, `Looks-3-Minimal-Page`) as the base | Broadcast Night (`Looks-7-*`), Bright Scholastic (`Looks-6-*`) and Heritage Club (`Looks-1-*`) as scopes, as 1.1 sets out |
| Site structure (page 2) | `Nav-IA-Final`: structure 1, task and topic: Tournaments · Clubs · Scholastic · News · Membership · About | Scanner moves under Tournaments ("Scoresheet scanner") and into the footer |
| Header (page 2) | `Nav-Header-Final` (bar, menus, signed in) and `Nav-Header-Strip` (the event strip's states, the public view, dark mode), from H1 Refined: one 64px navy row led by the rook mark and the two-line name (D1), with no condensing on scroll. When the row is tight, Donate moves into the About menu first, then the name hides | H4's search as a field-shaped "Search" button (the word visible at every desktop width, ⌘K / Ctrl K from 1280px) opening the search panel; the full box lives on the homepage. H5's event strip, personalised to involved people (registered players, guardians, the event's TDs, LCA admins) with H2's "same line, other moments" lifecycle; light gold tint before and after the event, the live tokens while a round is live. No public bar; the public gets a worded "Live" tag on Tournaments. "LCA on Facebook ↗" in the About menu's footer row. The About menu's Board & regions, Bylaws & rules, Minutes and State champions rows point at the `/about` tabs once `newAbout` is on (State champions once `newNews` is on), and the My LCA menu follows page 14's hub (`Account-Final-Hub`) once `myLcaRail` gives My LCA its rail. |

M4's pill as a 56px event bar docked above the tab bar inside one `BottomDock`, and M4's banner as an in-flow `role="status"` card. 768 to 1023px keeps the hybrid header (with a Donate text link) and no tab bar. |

Keeps K's split: `/admin` for LCA admins, with officers keeping today's view-only Admin (`Account-P`), `/workspace` for TDs, regional representatives and auditors, and the club rep's page at `/my/club/:slug` (WS09) |
| Scanner (page 16) | One pick per part: getting in `Scan-E` with `Scan-D`'s states, signed in first; the reading job `Scan-G`, with no live feed of the read; review `Scan-H`; fixing a misread `Scan-K`; save, share and export `Scan-L`, saved privately at once as `Scan-M` does, with no Public option; My games `Scan-O`; into the tournament `Scan-Q` and `Scan-Q-2`, with scanning optional and several ways to verify a game; the printed notation kit `Scan-S` | Piece images, not letters, on `Scan-H` and `Scan-S`, from an open-licensed set (cburnett, as lichess draws it), never chess.com's own art; games outside tournaments shown well on My games; active LCA members only (D28). Redraws pending for `Scan-E`, `Scan-H`, `Scan-L`, `Scan-O`, `Scan-Q` and `Scan-S` |

|---|---|---|

## 2. Architecture and conventions

### 2.1 Repo map (verified Monday, October 5, 2026, with what WS01 changed)
**Since WS01** (merged Thu, Oct 22, 2026), the code is grouped by area, and the paths in the lists below are as they were on Mon, Oct 5, 2026; `scripts/moves/*.json` maps every moved file to where it went:
- shared rules and formats live in `domain/` (`clubs`, `contracts`, `events`, `format`, `households`, `membership`, `registration`), imported as `@domain/...`;
- the site's code lives in `src/areas/<area>/` (`admin`, `clubs`, `events`, `governance`, `households`, `live`, `membership`, `registration`), each with its `api.ts`, `index.ts` and `components/`, and `src/lib/api.ts` is kept as a barrel over the area modules;
- the server helpers are grouped in `functions/utils/<area>/` (`admin`, `events`, `live`, `membership`, `registration`), the Drizzle schema is `functions/db/schema.ts`, and `npm run db:generate` writes new migrations;
- `main` holds migrations up to `0054_requires_lca_membership.sql`, and previews are bound to `lca-db-preview` (0.1 rule 8);
- the browser tests run with Playwright and axe (`npm run test:a11y`), and the gate adds `npm run check:bundle` (0.2).

**Frontend**
- React 19, TypeScript, Vite, Tailwind v4 and shadcn/ui.
- Tailwind v4 is configured through `@theme` / `@theme inline` blocks in `src/index.css`, which also holds the shadcn tokens and a `.dark` block that uses stock greys today.
- Pages are `src/pages/*Page.tsx`; route definitions live in `src/App.tsx`.
- Library code: API wrappers in `src/lib/api.ts`, flags in `src/lib/features.ts`, time helpers in `src/lib/lcaTime.ts`, plus `brand.ts`, `pricing.ts`, `family.ts`, `roles.ts`, `regions.ts`, `clubColors.ts`, `clearinghouse.ts`, `champions.ts` and `tournamentStatus.ts`.
- Components: `src/components/` contains `PageHero`, `StatusBadge`, `FacebookFeed`, `DonateButton`, `AnnouncementBanner` and `ImpersonationBanner`, plus the `layout/` (`Navbar`, `Footer`), `tournaments/`, `admin/`, `family/`, `uscf/`, `maps/`, `governance/` and `ui/` folders.

**Functions**
- Cloudflare Pages Functions with file routing under `functions/api/**`.
- Auth helpers in `functions/utils/auth.ts`: `requireUser`, `requireAuthedMember`, `optionalAuthedMember`, `requireRole`, `requireAdmin`, `requireAdminView`, `requireClubRep`, `requireClubView`, `requireTournamentManager`, `requireTournamentView`, `requireGovernanceEditor`, `requireMailer`, `requireMemberDirectory`, `requireSeatAccess` and `isObserver`.
- Response helpers in `functions/utils/response.ts`: `jsonResponse`, `errorResponse`, `parseJsonBody`, `handleOptions` and `isResponse`.
- Domain helpers in `functions/utils/`: `pricing`, `sectionRules`, `family`, `prizes`, `swiss/` (the pairing engine), `ratingSnapshots`, `membershipExpiry`, `membershipActivation`, `registrationEmails`, `registrationOpenNotify`, `email` + `emailLayout` (Resend), `stripe`, `uscf`, `linkPreview`, `audit`, `tickets`, `campaigns`, `regions`, `time`, `permissions`, `tournament-manage` and `scan/`.

**Data**
- D1 binding `DB` (`lca-db`), with migrations `0001`…`0051` at the check of Mon, Oct 5, 2026; `0052_sections_schedules.sql` and `0053_sections_schedules_backfill.sql` (`tournament_sections` with `cap` and the fee tiers, `tournament_schedules` with `label`, `time_control`, `is_primary` and `merge_round`, `tournament_schedule_rounds`, and `registrations.section_id` and `schedule_id`) were committed on `redesign/ws01-design-system` on Thu, Oct 8, 2026, and are on `main` with `0054_requires_lca_membership.sql` since WS01.
- Main tables: `tournaments`, `registrations`, `tournament_games`, `tournament_directors`, `tournament_reminders`, `tournament_attendee_reminders`, `members`, `payments`, `clubs`, `club_news`, `club_officers`, `clearinghouse`, `lca_posts`, `governance_documents`, `board_members`, `board_seat_assignments`, `seat_regions`, `state_champions`, `support_tickets`, `support_messages`, `scan_usage`, `uscf_rating_history`, `email_campaigns`, `email_campaign_recipients`, `admin_audit_log`, `impersonation_log`, `site_announcement(s)`, `facebook_feed_cache` and `contact_messages`.
- Storage is the R2 binding `CLUB_LOGOS`.

**Workers**
- `workers/clearinghouse-sync` syncs the Gulf South feed.
- `workers/daily-emails` runs two crons, a daily run at noon UTC (7:00 AM Central in summer, 6:00 AM in winter) and a five-minute run that sweeps half-sent campaigns, with a per-phase isolation pattern and marks a send done only once it succeeds.

**Integrations**
- Supabase Auth: password sign-in, with the Bearer JWT verified server-side.
- Stripe: Checkout plus `functions/api/stripe/webhook.ts`. Endpoints include `registrations/[id]/pay.ts`, `registrations/batch.ts`, `membership/checkout.ts`, `membership/confirm.ts` and `donations/checkout.ts`. Every online payment goes into LCA's own Stripe account (`functions/utils/stripe.ts`); nothing uses Stripe Connect yet (see the note on fees under 2.2).
- Resend, Google Maps JS (`LCAMap`) and the US Chess lookup (`uscf/lookup.ts`, `uscf/search.ts`).

**Tests:** unit tests and integration tests (`foundation`, `family`, `pairing-flow`, `registration-rules`, `role-safety`, `club-permissions`, `coverage`, `prizes`, `rating-upload`, `public-feed`, `lifecycle`, …).

The daily run keeps today's phases and takes the other daily jobs: WS04's reminders, WS09's 14-day clock and road miles, WS12's renewal reminders and WS15's retention | WS06 (its first phases, Phase 1) | WS06, WS07, WS08, WS09, WS10, WS15 |
| Automation runs: `automation_runs` and `recordRun` (`functions/utils/admin/automations.ts`), one row per scheduled run with its outcome and each job's expected interval | WS08 (Phase 2, the console's migration) | the clearinghouse sync and every phase of both runs of `workers/daily-emails` (the scheduled runs, above); read by WS08's admin home and Needs attention |
| WS01's components, ready for every workstream: `StatusBadge` and `StatusDot` (status in words beside colour), `FilterChip`, `DateBlock`, `ScrollPanel`, `ResultCell` with `ColorMark` and `ResultLegend`, the table primitives with a sticky first column, `ThemeScope` and `HeritageLayout` (`src/layouts`), `Seal`, `ThickThinRule`, `LcaMark`, `LcaLogo`, `ResponsiveImage`, `AppearanceControl`, the gold `Button`, and `Crosstable` and `StandingsTable` (`src/areas/live/components`) | WS01 (built) | WS02 to WS15 |

**Fees and connected accounts.** Today every online payment on the site goes into LCA's own Stripe account, and nothing uses Stripe Connect. For now only LCA admins, club reps, the president and standing directors may set an entry fee above $0 (D24). Until a connected account exists for a standing director, that director's fees are paid into LCA's account and LCA passes them on, and the director is told so before setting a fee. A connected account is an account for one organizer inside LCA's own Stripe account, so the organizer's money is kept apart from LCA's and reaches them; it is the only way anyone else charges more than $0. Who pays Stripe's fee on a connected account, and who handles refunds and disputes, is Q3; until K answers, none is built and no payment is routed to one.

### 2.4 Performance and quality budgets (site-wide)
- **Mobile pages:** LCP ≤ 2.5 s, CLS < 0.1, INP ≤ 200 ms (lab, mid-tier mobile on 4G).
- **Images:** the hero image on mobile is ≤ 200 KB, and every image has width and height set.
- **Accessibility:** zero serious or critical axe violations on the key routes in every theme scope.
- **Live endpoint:** pages that poll it must make no request while the tab is hidden.
- **Caching:** public GET endpoints set `Cache-Control`.
- **Edge caching:** use the Cache API for aggregates (`/api/home`, `/api/events`, `/api/clubs`), short-lived on live data.

## 3. Workstreams

Every workstream uses the same order: Why · Canvas boards · User-facing scope · Data model · API · Frontend · Jobs & integrations · Acceptance criteria · Tests · Depends on · Risks & open decisions.

---

## WS01 · Design system, themes & accessibility foundation
**Why:** Today's site has five problems in this area.
- The homepage `HeroSlideshow` auto-advances every 5 s with no pause control and ignores reduced-motion settings. That fails WCAG 2.2.2, which is Level A.
- The focus ring measures 1.54:1.
- Gold is used as text on white at 2.28:1, in about 135 uses of `text-lca-gold`.
- Status is shown by coloured dots alone, and there is faint white text on navy.
- The five hero JPEGs weigh about 2.1 MB, roughly a whole median mobile page. `.dark` uses stock shadcn greys. No mono font is set, so `Crosstable` falls back to the OS default and prints "3.5" instead of "3½".

**Canvas boards:**
- Base: `Looks-3-Minimal-Tile`, `Looks-3-Minimal-Page`.
- Live: `Looks-7-Broadcast-Tile`, `Looks-7-Broadcast-Page`.
- Scholastic: `Looks-6-Scholastic-Tile`, `Looks-6-Scholastic-Page`.
- Heritage scope: `Looks-1-Heritage-Tile`.
- `Looks-Compare`, `Start-Audit` (the full fix list) and `Start-Principles` (the review checklist).

**User-facing scope**
- One token system. The **base** look comes from Looks-3: navy `#1a2744` ink, white and `#f7f8fa` surfaces, borders `#e5e7ed`, muted text `#5b6478`, ok `#047857`, bad `#c0362c`, gold `#c8a94a` as a fill, gold-ink `#866a1e`.
- A real **dark** mode from the Looks-3 dark palette: `#0b1220` ground, `#111a2c` surface, `#1a2744` raised, ink `#e8ecf4`, muted `#9aa6bf`, gold `#c8a94a`. It is toggleable and respects `prefers-color-scheme`.
- **`[data-theme="live"]`** (Looks-7): `#070b14` ground, `#0e1526` panel, `#1a2744` raised, ink `#f3f5f9`, muted `#8e9ab2`, gold `#c8a94a`, gold-hi `#e5c768`, live `#ff4757` (dark text only, since white on it is only 3.34:1), win `#34d399`, loss `#ff6b6b`, draw `#a7b0c2`.
- **`[data-theme="scholastic"]`** (Looks-6): `#fffaf1` ground, navy ink, and section hues with their tiles:
  - coral `#c2471f` / `#ffe4da`
  - mint `#1f7a55` / `#dcf5e9`
  - sky `#2f6fde` / `#e3eeff`
  - grape `#6a3fc1` / `#eee6ff`
  - gold tile `#fdf1cc`
- **Heritage tokens** (`[data-theme="heritage"]`): paper `#f7f3e8`, card `#fffdf8`, inset `#ece4d0`, ink `#1a2744`, muted `#5e5a52`, hairline `#d8cdb4` (decorative only), field border `#857d6b`, gold-ink `#705718` (`--gold-ink` resolves to this inside the scope and to `#866a1e` in base), baize `#2d5a45`, claret `#8a2b38`. Brand gold is rules, seals and text on navy only (2.05:1 on paper). The 2px radius applies to cards, tables and hero mats; controls keep the base radius. Under `.dark`, heritage keeps type, rules and seal on the Minimal dark grounds. Scopes nest one level only, base-inside-heritage being the one permitted nesting (`ThemeScope scope="base"` around editors and forms); heritage never renders inside live.
- Take the exact values, contrast pairs and component shapes from the tile boards. Where a tile and this list disagree, the tile wins.
- **Typography**:
  - Geist for UI.
  - Geist Mono for chess data in every scope, with tabular figures and ligatures off. It is the only mono face on the site.
  - Instrument Serif as a single display accent in the base look. It never appears inside a heritage scope.
  - Live scope adds Barlow Condensed and Barlow; JetBrains Mono only if the Looks-7 tile requires it.
  - Heritage scope adds Libre Caslon Display 400 (h1 and h2 at 32px and up, and display numerals; never ratings, scores or tables) and Source Serif 4 variable (body at 18px / 1.6 in the readers with a 70ch measure, true small-caps labels with `font-variant-caps: all-small-caps` on labels of 15px or more, lining and tabular figures). The italic face is declared but fetched only when used. Budget ≤ 110 KB woff2, latin subset. A Georgia fallback with `size-adjust` and `ascent-override` keeps CLS under 0.1. If the Fontsource subset lacks `smcp` / `c2sc`, use uppercase at 0.85em with .06 to .08em tracking, never synthesised small caps.
  - Scholastic scope adds Baloo 2 and Nunito in WS11, not here.
  - Scope fonts load only inside their scope, through a lazily imported CSS module. All scope token blocks live in `src/index.css`, so a scope switch never flashes base colours.
- **Chess data**:
  - `formatScore` returns "3½".
  - Ratings and scores use tabular figures.
  - New `ResultCell` shows letter, tint and colour marker: hollow ○ for White, filled ● for Black, e.g. "W ○ 17". Never colour alone.
- **Components**:
  - `StatusBadge` always renders a text label. Its tones map to status tokens: open, soon, full/waitlist, closed, live, partner, LCA.
  - Buttons: gold fill with navy text; never white on gold.
  - New `FilterChip` (removable, with count), `DateBlock` (weekday + date), `LcaMark` (one-colour rook-Louisiana SVG, for the header at every width and other tight spaces) and `LcaLogo` (the board-voted full-colour logo, for roomy placements). Decision D1 lists where each one goes.
  - Table primitives with a sticky first column and real `<th scope>`.
  - `PageHero` without any slideshow.
- **Focus ring:** 2 px navy plus a 2 px offset on light grounds and gold on dark or live grounds. It must reach ≥ 3:1 in every scope.
- **Motion:**
  - Delete the auto-advance.
  - Add a global `prefers-reduced-motion` rule that zeroes transitions and animations.
  - Any ticker or rotating element must have a visible pause control.
  - The live tag's dot is static. Nothing pulses.
- **Gold text clean-up:** codemod `text-lca-gold` on light grounds to `text-gold-ink`. Review icons that carry meaning, such as the NewsPage "Pinned" pin and the reminder bell.
- **Image pipeline:**
  - Generate AVIF and WebP at 640/1024/1600 widths for `src/assets/LCA_Slide_*.jpg` and `hero.png`.
  - Serve them through `<picture>` with `sizes`, explicit `width` and `height`, `fetchpriority="high"` on the LCP image and `loading="lazy"` below the fold.
- **Safe previews:** add `[env.preview]` with its own D1 database (`lca-db-preview`) in `wrangler.toml`, plus scripts `db:migrate:preview` and seed. Document it in `README.md`.

**Data model:** none, apart from the preview D1 database.

**API:** none.

**Frontend**
- Change `src/index.css` (tokens, scopes, `.dark`, reduced motion, focus):
  - `src/components/StatusBadge.tsx`
  - `src/components/PageHero.tsx`
  - `src/components/ui/button.tsx`, `src/components/ui/table.tsx`, `src/components/ui/badge.tsx`
  - `src/components/tournaments/Crosstable.tsx` and `StandingsTable.tsx` (mono, ½, colour markers)
  - `src/pages/HomePage.tsx` (remove `HeroSlideshow`)
  - `src/lib/brand.ts`
  - every `text-lca-gold` call site
- New:
  - `src/lib/format.ts`
  - `src/components/ui/filter-chip.tsx`
  - `src/components/DateBlock.tsx`
  - `src/components/LcaMark.tsx` and `src/components/LcaLogo.tsx` (wraps the existing logo file; verify which of `src/assets/lca-logo.webp` and `public/lca-logo.jpg` is current)
  - `src/components/ThemeScope.tsx` (sets `data-theme` and lazily loads scope fonts)
  - `src/components/ResultCell.tsx`
  - `scripts/images.mjs` (sharp-based generator, or `vite-imagetools`)
  - `test/a11y/*.spec.ts` (Playwright + `@axe-core/playwright`)
- New for the heritage scope: `src/styles/themes/heritage.css`, `live.css` (font imports only), `src/layouts/HeritageLayout.tsx` (lazy layout route wrapping `AboutPage`, `BoardPage`, `BylawsPage`, `MinutesPage`, `AnnualMeetingPage` and `ChampionsPage`, with `GovLayout` kept inside), `src/components/ThickThinRule.tsx`, `src/components/Seal.tsx` (SVG built from `LcaMark`), a `.small-caps` utility. `ThemeScope.tsx` accepts `heritage` and `base` (a `data-theme=""` reset, used by `GovernanceDocuments` around its form and `RichTextEditor`).
- The gold-text guard becomes scope-aware: `#c8a94a` is never text on `#f7f3e8` or any light ground, including the phone tab bar; `#705718` is allowed inside heritage and as the About menu's "You are here" label; `#866a1e` is allowed as text at 13px or more on `#fbfaf6` and as the tab bar's current-tab bar. The allowlist is a test fixture.

**Jobs & integrations:** Fontsource packages: `@fontsource-variable/geist-mono`, `@fontsource/instrument-serif`, `@fontsource/barlow-condensed`, `@fontsource/barlow`, `@fontsource/libre-caslon-display`, `@fontsource-variable/source-serif-4`. Verify each package name on npm, and verify the latin subset keeps `smcp` / `c2sc`. `@fontsource-variable/baloo-2` and `@fontsource-variable/nunito` move to WS11. Also Playwright with axe.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. axe reports zero serious or critical violations on `/`, `/tournaments`, a tournament detail page, `/clubs`, `/membership`, `/scholastic`, `/about`, `/governance/minutes` and `/champions` in the light, dark, live and heritage scopes, plus the scholastic token fixture page.
2. A unit test fails if `text-lca-gold` or a raw `#c8a94a` text colour appears in TSX outside an allowlist of navy/dark contexts.
3. The focus ring reaches ≥ 3:1 against its ground in every scope.
4. Nothing on the site auto-advances. With `prefers-reduced-motion: reduce`, no animation runs.
5. Every status is readable without colour: each `StatusBadge` renders its label.
6. Standings and crosstables show "3½", never "3.5", with figures in tabular alignment.
7. The home LCP image is ≤ 200 KB at a 390 px viewport, and Lighthouse mobile performance is ≥ 90 on `/` (lab).
8. Switching `data-theme` on a container swaps tokens with no layout shift.
9. `wrangler pages deploy` on a branch uses `lca-db-preview`, never `lca-db`.
10. The official logo appears in every roomy placement listed under D1 and the rook mark in every placement D1 gives it, including the header at every width. The logo is never recoloured.
11. The heritage font chunk never appears in the `/` network log. CLS on `/about` and `/governance/bylaws` stays under 0.1 across the font swap.
12. A unit test over the route-to-scope map fails if any workspace or admin route resolves to heritage, and a render test mounts `RichTextEditor` and the `GovernanceDocuments` form inside `HeritageLayout` and asserts that the closest `data-theme` above each is `""` (their `ThemeScope scope="base"` boundary), never `heritage`. No Instrument Serif renders inside `[data-theme="heritage"]`, and Caslon is bound only to the `--font-display` token used by h1 and h2 inside the scope.
13. Inside the heritage scope, `Button`, `Input`, `StatusBadge` and the focus ring render pixel-identical to base (visual test).
14. The live tag's dot does not animate.

**Tests:**
- Unit: `format.ts` (scores, dates with weekday across DST, time controls).
- `StatusBadge` label rendering.
- Unit `test/unit/themeScopes.test.ts`: the route-to-scope map (no workspace or admin route is heritage); `--gold-ink` resolves to `#705718` inside heritage and `#866a1e` in base.
- Unit `test/unit/governanceEditors.test.tsx`: `RichTextEditor` and the `GovernanceDocuments` form rendered inside `HeritageLayout` have no heritage ancestor inside their base boundary.
- Unit `test/unit/goldText.test.ts`: the scope-aware gold guard with its allowlist fixture (`#705718` in heritage and the "You are here" label, `#866a1e` at 13px+ on `#fbfaf6` and the tab bar's current-tab bar; `#c8a94a` never text on a light ground).
- a11y `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` (Playwright + axe): AC1's pages in light, dark, live and heritage plus the scholastic fixture; CLS under 0.1 on `/about` and `/governance/bylaws` across the font swap; the heritage chunk absent from the `/` network log; the live tag's dot static; `Button`, `Input`, `StatusBadge` and the focus ring pixel-identical inside heritage (visual test).
- Playwright and axe suite (new `npm run test:a11y`).

**Depends on:** nothing. This goes first.
**Risks & open decisions:** Font weight could grow, so keep scope fonts lazy. The codemod needs a human pass on meaningful icons.

---

## WS02 · Information architecture & navigation
**Why:**
- On desktop, a hamburger menu was used 27% of the time against about 50% for visible or combo navigation, was at least 39% slower, and cut discoverability by more than 20% (NN/g).
- Audience-based navigation fails because people choose by task (NN/g, GOV.UK).
- Today, Scanner occupies a top slot while Membership hides under "More", and there is no visible Donate entry point anywhere.
- A volunteer with several roles needs each role's work one click away, without a longer menu for the 300+ members who hold none. K's page 14 frame (Mon, Oct 19, 2026) puts those rooms in a rail inside My LCA (WS13), so the header's My LCA ▾ menu stays short for everyone.

**Canvas boards** (decided; copies in `docs/redesign/decided-boards`, and page 14's in `docs/redesign/canvas/boards`):
- `Nav-IA-Final`: the six sections, every page under each with its route and the workstream that brings it, the utilities by role, and section F's one label per destination.
- `Nav-Header-Final`: the bar at 1280 and 1024px, the About menu open on Minutes, the signed-in menu and Renew in the Join slot.
- `Nav-Header-Strip`: the event strip in every state and for every role, the public view during a live round, and dark mode.
- `Nav-Mobile-Final`: the app bar, the tab bar, the Menu sheet, the docked event bar and the banner on phones, and the tablet header.
- `Nav-Footer-Final`: F1's four columns, F3's trust line, the Appearance control and the fold under 768px.
- `Account-Final-Hub-2` frame E, with `Account-Final-Hub` (page 14): the short My LCA ▾ menu drawn for three people, and the rail it leads to.
- Where a board and this text differ, this text wins. `Nav-Header-Final` draws a bar that condenses to 56px after 80px of scroll, with 8px section padding up to 1439px; K's 1.2 decisions keep one 64px row that never condenses, 12px section padding and the squeeze described below. `Nav-IA-Final` and `Nav-Header-Final` draw role rows (Workspace, Board inbox, My region's clubs) in My LCA ▾; page 14's frame moves them into the rail once WS13 builds it.

**User-facing scope**
- **Desktop (1024px and up):** one navy row, 64px, sticky, with no condensing on scroll (the `--header-h` scroll padding below keeps a focused control clear of it, WCAG 2.4.11). The brand link leads with the rook mark (`LcaMark`, D1; WS01 step 28 already draws it in today's header under `newLook`) beside the two-line name "Louisiana Chess / Association", with no cream plate; it is always `aria-label="Louisiana Chess Association, home"`, and its mark is `aria-hidden`. Then **Tournaments · Clubs · Scholastic · News · Membership · About ▾**. "About" opens on click, with a caret. Its rows: About LCA, Board & regions, Bylaws & rules, Minutes, Annual meeting, State champions, Contact (the current row: `#f3eedc` tint, title navy 600, "You are here" at 13px/600 in `#705718`); footer row "LCA on Facebook ↗". Board & regions, Bylaws & rules and Minutes open the About page's tabs (`/about/board`, `/about/bylaws`, `/about/minutes`) once `newAbout` is on (WS14), and State champions opens `/about/champions` once `newNews` is on (WS10); until then each opens today's address (`/governance/board`, `/governance/bylaws`, `/governance/minutes`, `/champions`). One label per destination from `nav.ts`, as `Nav-IA-Final` section F sets them: "Board & regions", "Bylaws & rules", "Minutes", "State champions", "Scholastic" and "Contact" read the same in the header, the sheet, the footer, the trust line and search. A field-shaped **Search** button sits left of the utilities (icon plus the visible word at 1024 to 1279px; 220px with a ⌘K / Ctrl K hint from 1280px; an `<a href="/search">` underneath). Top right, in this order on every page: **Donate · Log in** (or **My LCA ▾** when signed in) **· Join LCA** (gold). A member whose membership is active sees neither Join LCA nor Renew; once a membership has expired, **Renew** (gold fill, navy text) takes the Join slot. The "Renew membership" to-do inside My LCA may still appear within 30 days of expiry (see the to-do count below). The My LCA trigger carries the to-do count from `src/lib/todo.ts` (accessible name "My LCA, 2 things to do"). **When the row is tight** it gives way in a fixed order, measured by a `ResizeObserver` on the row rather than set by breakpoint (in practice from 1024 to 1279px, or wherever Renew and the Live tag both show): first Donate leaves the bar and becomes the About menu's last item, directly above the "LCA on Facebook ↗" footer row (the same `nav.ts` entry); then the two-line name hides, leaving the rook mark, while the link keeps its name. Section padding stays 12px, and the six sections, Search, Log in or My LCA ▾, and Join LCA or Renew never leave the bar. The current section is marked with `aria-current="page"` plus a visible underline. A `--header-h` variable from a `ResizeObserver` over every sticky element in the top stack (`AnnouncementBanner` when sticky, the header, the strip in round states) feeds `scroll-padding-top`; z-order top to bottom is the search panel and menus, the header, the strip, `AnnouncementBanner`. A "Skip to content" link is the first focusable element on every page. Log in links at every width pass `state={{ from }}` to `/login`, which is what `LoginPage.tsx` reads.
- **The My LCA ▾ menu** is short for everyone, as `Account-Final-Hub-2` frame E draws it: the to-do items first (a "To do · 2" group, only when there are any), then My LCA (`/dashboard`, "Your dashboard"), the active event line while event mode is on, Family (for a parent or guardian), Membership card (once WS12's card exists, behind `newMembership`), the Admin row the person holds ("Admin" for an LCA admin, with what needs attention in words once WS08's `GET /api/admin/summary` exists, Phase 4; "Admin (view only)" for anyone who has today's view-only Admin, the officers and the president, as `Account-P` draws), then Log out. Once `myLcaRail` gives My LCA its left rail (WS13), the rail carries every other room, one entry per section the person holds, in its fixed order (Today · You · Family · Events you direct · Your region · Your club · Board · Admin · Member lookup), and the menu holds no Workspace, Board inbox or My region row: the word Workspace is never a menu row, a title or a breadcrumb trail. Until then the menu keeps today's role rows as `Nav-IA-Final` draws them (Workspace, Board inbox and My region's clubs, by role, after Membership card), so no volunteer loses a way in. Every row is a `nav.ts` entry and shows only while its route is live, so the menu never holds a dead link. The same menu opens at every width; only its trigger moves.
- **Tablet (768 to 1023px):** the same header component: the rook mark and the two-line name, Tournaments and Clubs visible, Search, a Donate text link, Log in or My LCA ▾ (with the to-do count), and a bordered "Menu" button opening the mobile sheet as a 400px right drawer. No tab bar. The event strip renders as one 48px line: status tag · event name · the viewer's sentence (truncated, full text in `title` and in My LCA) · primary button · the 44×44 Hide button; secondary links and the "Players see:" echo move into My LCA ▾.
- **Phones (under 768px):** a 56px navy app bar (rook mark and the two-line name; a 44×44 "Search" button to `/search`; the current section name when the Menu tab is current) and a bottom tab bar in `<nav aria-label="Main">`, 56px plus `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)`: **Tournaments · Clubs · Log in / My LCA (to-do count) · Menu**. Labels come from `nav.ts` and always show; the current tab is bold navy with a 3px top bar in gold-ink `#866a1e` and `aria-current="page"`. Menu opens the full-height sheet: the six sections with the current one expanded as "You are here"; Scoresheet scanner under Tournaments, with "This weekend" (`/tournaments?when=weekend`, once `newTournaments` is on) and "Results" (once `newNews` brings WS10's `/results`) added only when their routes are live; Donate · Log in · Join LCA in the desktop order, with the same Join and Renew rule; the Admin row the person holds (and, until `myLcaRail` is on, today's role rows, as in the menu); Facebook · Contact · Website help; the theme switch, a shortcut to the footer's; and "Hide event bar until the next update" while event mode is on, writing the same `event:phase:round` key as the bar's Hide button. One `BottomDock` holds the page action bar, the event bar and the tabs, writes `--bottom-chrome-h` from a `ResizeObserver`, and drives `<main>` padding-bottom and `scroll-padding-bottom`. The dock hides while the on-screen keyboard is open, on routes that declare `hideTabBar` (registration, checkout, success, the Stripe hand-off, scanner capture, TV, print), and under 480px of height or at high zoom, where the app bar shows the bordered Menu button instead. The to-do count is computed on the client from `/api/me` (unpaid entries for self or children for events not yet ended; an `active` membership expiring within 30 days or an `expired` one, never `pending`; a US Chess membership ending before an entered event), fetched on load and focus, never polled; volunteer queues and event-day items never count. DOM order: app bar, "Skip to content" link, `<main>`, `BottomDock`, then the portalled sheet. With `newNav` on, the page's viewport tag gains `viewport-fit=cover`, which `vite.config.ts` writes into `index.html` at build time from `src/lib/features.ts`, as its `prepaintPlugin` reads `newLook` since WS01 step 21; with `newNav` off, today's pages, which pad for no notch, keep today's tag. There is no separate phone flag: `newNav` ships the app bar, tab bar and sheet with the header.
- **Event mode:** a personal strip for events run on this site (LCA-run and club-run events, and member-created events once WS08 adds them, since those keep every function but listing, D26), never for partner events. It shows to a signed-in registered player (including waitlisted), the guardian of a registered dependent, the event's TDs and `lca_admin`, from 6 days before day 1 until `eventEnd` + 48 hours, where `eventEnd = (end_date ?? date) 23:59` Central. On a member-created event an `lca_admin` who does not direct it gets no line, since admins follow those events from Admin (WS08; assumed, Q18). The phases come from `domain/events/eventMode.ts` (WS01 step 5), whose doc comment is the rule wherever it and this paragraph differ: `week`, `dayBefore` (the calendar day before day 1, and that night until 6:00 AM), `checkin` (6:00 AM until the day's first scheduled round time, or all day while that time is not set), `eventDay` (Phase 0 only: from the first round time until 6:00 AM the next morning, "Today · event · Round 2 at 2:30 PM"), `roundPosted` / `roundInProgress` / `between` (WS07), `final` (`status = 'completed'`, or a non-null result in every section's last scheduled round) and `after` (until `eventEnd` + 48 hours: a final event enters it at 11:59 PM on its last day, one that is not final at 6:00 AM the next morning; the TD's rating-report line until completed; the admin "not marked completed" nudge is WS07). A day of the event with no round is never `checkin` (WS01's decision "Event-mode phase gaps"). Light gold tint `#fbf7e9` before the event, during check-in and after the final (scrolls away); while a round is posted, in progress or between rounds, the live colour tokens with a 2px gold top rule (sticky, Geist type), drawn through `ThemeScope`'s colours-only mode (below), so no live font ever loads for the strip. One line per person: status tag in words (`StatusBadge`'s status path) · event name · the viewer's own sentence · primary button · secondary links · a 44×44 "Hide until the next update" button. Absolute times, no countdown. TD and admin lines end with "Players see: <the public line>" and may carry one fix-it ("Round times missing: players see 'time to be announced'" → Add round times). Precedence TD > admin > player > guardian; exactly one strip, and with two events the one whose next moment is sooner; the rest in My LCA ▾. Hidden state is keyed `event:phase:round` in `localStorage` inside try/catch, with one label ("Hide until the next update") and one key for the strip, the phone bar and the sheet item, and the event stays listed in My LCA ▾. On a failed poll the strip keeps its last payload and "Updated" time, retries with backoff and hides only after three consecutive failures in a round phase; it never shows an error. A persistent visually hidden `role="status"` node announces state changes only. Buttons: "TD console" on the strip and bar for TDs and admins alike, "Open TD console" on banners, "Open check-in" only when the console deep-links to its check-in step; no "R3"-style shorthand anywhere. On phones the same data renders, from check-in on day 1, as a 56px two-line event bar docked above the tab bar (hidden on that event's own pairings and standings pages) and an in-flow `role="status"` banner inserted only on page load or at the top of the page; otherwise the bar shows a "New" tag and the dock's status node speaks (it stays silent when a banner is inserted). "Something wrong? Tell the TD" opens the support form prefilled with the event and the person's name and email, tagged with the tournament id; no personal email is shown. The public sees no strip: while `/api/live/now` names at least one listed LCA event, the Tournaments nav item carries a worded "Live" tag (`liveMarker`), named by the count ("Tournaments, 1 event live now", "Tournaments, 2 events live now"), and (WS07, `newLive`) the event's own pairings and standings pages show a "Playing today?" line beside WS07's Find your board box, in WS07's words, on events run on this site. Neither ever appears for a partner event or on a checkout page. The round-alert bell is hidden until WS07's email alerts exist.
- **Search:** ⌘K / Ctrl+K and the header Search button open a search panel over tournaments (LCA and partner), clubs and regions, pages, news, results (finished LCA events) and champions (title and year only), with a role-gated "Your tools" group for signed-in volunteers and a "Look up a rating on US Chess ↗" row for name-like or ID-like queries. "Your tools" names the rooms the person holds by the rail's names (Events you direct, Your region, Your club, Board, Admin, Member lookup), never the word Workspace; until `myLcaRail` is on it holds today's tools by role. There is no Players group and no person name is ever matched (D9). Tournaments and results come only from listed events: a hidden draft is never returned, and neither is a member-created event (D26), because the search's event queries take `tournaments` through the one listing condition of the shared event query (section 2.2): `listedWhere` in `functions/utils/events/listing.ts`, which WS02 creates with today's rule (`is_visible = 1`), WS03 builds the shared query beside, and WS08 extends when it adds member-created events. The "/" shortcut is not bound (WCAG 2.1.4). On phones the button goes to `/search?q=`. The same combobox renders inline on the homepage (WS03, `homeSearch`). `/search?q=&type=` offers a chip per group in group order (Champions included; Your tools when signed in).
- **Footer (F1):** four columns, every link a `nav.ts` entry by id: Play (Tournaments, Scholastic, State champions, Scoresheet scanner; Calendar, at `/tournaments?view=calendar`, joins when `newTournaments` is on, and Results when `newNews` brings WS10's `/results`) · Community (Clubs, Club map (`/clubs#map`), List or update your club (the contact form with the club subject preselected, and WS09's Suggest a club once `newClubs` is on), News, LCA on Facebook ↗) · About LCA (About LCA, Board & regions, Bylaws & rules, Minutes, Annual meeting; the three governance links follow the About menu's rows to the tabs) · Contact (LouisianaChess@gmail.com as a mailto link, Contact, Membership help, Website help, Donate). The brand block keeps the official logo through `LcaLogo` (D1; WS01 step 28 draws it with no plate; the cream plate `Nav-Footer-Final` draws is K's call on the preview, and the default is no plate, as WS01 drew it, assumed, Q32), from a file sized for its 56px place rather than the 400px source (about 15 KB a page, found by WS01's step 29 lab check), with Join LCA and Donate. The base line holds the F3 trust line ("US Chess state affiliate · Louisiana's chess community since 1915 · Bylaws & rules · Minutes · Site last updated <build date with its weekday>") and the Appearance control (`AppearanceControl`: "Appearance: System · Light · Dark", a three-button radio group stored in `localStorage` inside try/catch, default System, the one theme switch that exists at every width). No Privacy or Accessibility links until those static pages exist. Under 768px each column heading becomes `<h2><button aria-expanded aria-controls>` toggling its list; all collapsed by default except Contact; the brand block and base line are always visible; no animation under reduced motion.
- **Switches** (0.2's table, each off by default): `newNav` turns on the header, the menus, the footer and the phone bars together; `siteSearch` the Search button, the ⌘K panel and `/search`; `eventStrip` the personal event strip with its phone bar and banner, and `eventStripLive` and `liveMarker` the round states and the Live tag once WS07 lands; WS03's band takes the inline box behind `homeSearch`. Rows that wait for another workstream follow its switch, as the Depends on line says, and the short My LCA menu follows `myLcaRail` (WS13).
- **Routes:** WS02 adds `/search` and changes no existing address. The About page's tabs and the redirects to them (`/governance/*`, `/champions` and `/state-champions`) come with `newAbout` (WS14) and `newNews` (WS10); until each is on, the menu, the sheet, the footer and search open today's addresses, and `/governance/rules` still redirects to `/governance/bylaws`. `/results` is WS10's. Verify the route list in `src/App.tsx`.
- **Focus, gold and the browser bar:**
