  - Under `newLook`, which draws the ring, the header, the app bar, the round-state strip and the footer set the ring's colour themselves: each navy element gives `--focus` the gold-on-dark token, and each light surface they open (the About and My LCA menus, the Menu sheet and drawer, the search panel) gives it back the look's own ring. None of them relies on the navy-band rule in `src/index.css` (`.bg-lca-navy :focus-visible` and its `.bg-popover` exception), which stays for the page heroes that still use it until their workstreams rebuild them (WS01 step 21's follow-up).
  - On an opaque gold ground (the gold tone of `AnnouncementBanner`, and the Exit button of the impersonation banner) the ring under `newLook` is navy in the light look and in dark mode, never gold on gold (WS01 step 23's follow-up; navy on brand gold reads 6.51:1).
  - The gold WS02 draws on a light ground is exactly the three uses `WS02_GOLD` names in `test/unit/fixtures/goldAllowlist.ts`, each in its own context and file: the About menu's "You are here" (`#705718` at 13px/600 on `#f3eedc`, `AboutMenu.tsx`), the sheet's "You are here" (`#866a1e` at 13px on `#fbfaf6`, `MobileMenu.tsx`) and the current tab's top bar (`#866a1e`, `BottomTabs.tsx`). A use drawn with a token instead takes its entry out.
  - Under `newLook`, once a visitor picks Light or Dark in the Appearance control, the page's `theme-color` is the one `index.html` gives that look (`#1a2744` for Light, `#0b1220` for Dark) whatever the device setting; with System the two media metas follow the device, as today (WS01 step 21's follow-up).
- **Pages loaded on demand:** with `newNav` on, the loading line `src/App.tsx` shows while a page's code arrives holds the height of the screen under the header, so the footer no longer jumps when the page arrives (a first visit measured a layout shift between 0.23 and 0.74 at WS01 step 31, against the 0.1 budget of section 2.4).
- **Under `.dark`** (every value is a WS01 token; nothing is invented in WS02):

| Surface | Light | Dark |
|---|---|---|
| Header, app bar | `#1a2744` | `#1a2744` |
| Tab bar, event bar | white, 1px `#d9d8d1` top border, labels navy, current-tab bar `#866a1e` | `#111a2c`, `rgba(255,255,255,.08)` top border, labels `#9aa6bf`, current `#e8ecf4` with a 3px `#c8a94a` bar |
| Event strip, pre and post states | `#fbf7e9`, navy text, 1px `#866a1e` rule | `#1a2744`, `#e8ecf4` text, 1px `#c8a94a` rule |
| Event strip, round states | live tokens (`#0e1526` / `#f3f5f9`) | unchanged |
| Banner card | white card, border token | `#111a2c`, border token |
| About and My LCA menus, Menu sheet, search panel | `--popover` white, current row `#f3eedc` | `--popover #1a2744`, `#e8ecf4` text, current and active rows `rgba(255,255,255,.1)` plus a 2px `#c8a94a` ring on the active search row |
| Focus ring | navy on the light menus, sheet and panel; gold-on-dark on the header, app bar, round-state strip and footer; navy on the banners' gold | gold-on-dark on every dark ground, menus included; navy on the banners' gold |

**Data model:** none. The listing condition is a query rule in code, not data.

**API:** Every endpoint WS02 adds or changes answers to a zod contract in `domain/contracts` (WS01 step 7), registered in `domain/contracts/registry.ts` and checked by a contract test in `test/integration/contracts/`; the server and the tests import the schemas, and the site imports only their types. The shapes below are those contracts' types. `GET /api/me` takes its contract in this workstream, so its line (`'GET me'`) leaves `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` and `PENDING_COUNT` drops by one (`POST` and `PATCH` keep theirs until a workstream changes them); the two new endpoints ship with theirs, since `test/unit/contract-coverage.test.ts` fails a new endpoint without one.
- `GET /api/search?q=` (new, public): `q` of 2 to 64 characters, normalised (lowercase, NFD with diacritics stripped, "&" → "and", "saint" → "st", whitespace collapsed); bounded `LIKE` queries over `tournaments` (listed, through `listedWhere`; upcoming for the Tournaments group, completed within 3 years for the Results group, never both), `clearinghouse` (upcoming, Louisiana first, then Gulf South with the state), `clubs` (with regions from `domain/clubs/regions.ts`), `lca_posts` (published, 24 months; once WS10's columns exist, never a hidden post, and a mirrored Facebook post links to Facebook unless it was promoted, as WS10 sets out) and `state_champions` (title and year, displayed as title and year). Static pages are matched on the client. At most 20 results, 5 per group, edge cache 60 s under the key `search:v1:${normalised}`. The response is identical signed in and out and contains no member, registration, email or player-name fields.

```ts
interface SearchResponse {
  q: string
  groups: Array<{
    type: 'tournaments' | 'clubs' | 'news' | 'results' | 'champions'
    label: string
    items: Array<{
      id: string; title: string; meta?: string; href: string
      tag?: { label: string; tone: 'open' | 'soon' | 'closed' | 'partner' | 'lca' | 'final' }
      external?: boolean
    }>
  }>
}
```

- `GET /api/me/event-mode` (new; `requireAuthedMember`; `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; ETag with 304). Computed from `registrations`, `members.guardian_id` (then `member_guardians` once WS06 lands), `tournament_directors`, `tournaments` and, after WS07, `round_publications` and `live_state`. The phase is `eventPhase(input, now)` from `domain/events/eventMode.ts`, with `input = { date, endDate, status, firstRoundTimes, lastRoundResultsComplete }`: the endpoint passes `firstRoundTimesFromSchedule` the primary schedule's rounds as `functions/utils/events/schedulesRepo.ts` reads them from `tournament_schedule_rounds` (`loadSchedulesFor`, then `roundScheduleResponse`), with `date` and `end_date`, and never reads the `round_schedule` column, which `test/unit/schedules-audit.test.ts` lets nothing outside that repository read and WS05 drops; it works out `lastRoundResultsComplete` itself (a non-null result in every section's last scheduled round). It builds `key` with `phaseKey(id, phase, round)`, passing the first round of the next playing day for `week` and `dayBefore` (1 before day 1) and the first round of that day for `checkin` and `eventDay`, so a strip hidden before day 1 comes back before round 2. When WS02 or WS07 changes these inputs, only `domain/events/eventMode.ts` and its tests change, and `eventPhase` keeps its one definition there (`test/unit/domain-shims.test.ts`). The client calls the endpoint only when `/api/me` shows an involvement inside a window (`eventModeWindow` from the same module), polls every `pollSeconds` while visible, never while hidden. Or 204.

```ts
type EventPhase =   // EVENT_PHASES in domain/events/eventMode.ts
  | 'week' | 'dayBefore' | 'checkin' | 'eventDay'
  | 'roundPosted' | 'roundInProgress' | 'between' | 'final' | 'after'

interface EventModePlayer {
  memberId: string; firstName: string; section: string; paid: boolean
  waitlistPosition?: number
  bye?: { round: number; points: '½' | '1' | '0' }
  checkedInAt?: string
  board?: number; color?: 'white' | 'black'; opponent?: string; opponentRating?: number
  result?: 'won' | 'lost' | 'draw'; score?: string   // "2½", a string so ½ survives
  // WS07 adds myReport: the viewer's own report for the current game, with the opponent's if any (see WS07)
}

interface EventModeTd {
  registered: number; unpaid: number; waitlisted: number; checkedIn: number
  resultsIn?: number; resultsTotal?: number; missingBoards?: number[]
  fixits: Array<{ code: 'round_times_missing'; text: string; href: string }>
}

interface EventModeItem {
  tournamentId: string; name: string; href: string
  viewer: 'player' | 'guardian' | 'td' | 'admin'   // never 'admin' on a member-created event the admin does not direct
  phase: EventPhase; round?: number; roundsTotal: number
  nextRoundStart?: string   // ISO-8601 with the America/Chicago offset
  updatedAt: string         // same format
  players: EventModePlayer[]   // own entry, or one row per child the viewer guards (members.guardian_id in Phase 0, member_guardians after WS06)
  td?: EventModeTd
  playersSee: string
  key: string               // phaseKey(tournamentId, phase, round): `${tournamentId}:${phase}:${round ?? 0}`
}

interface EventModeResponse { items: EventModeItem[]; pollSeconds: 20 | 300 }
```

  `pollSeconds` is 20 in `checkin`, `eventDay`, `roundPosted`, `roundInProgress` and `between` (so it stays 20 until 6:00 AM on an event night, while `eventDay` lasts), and 300 otherwise. Items are sorted so the event whose next moment is sooner comes first; the client renders only the first. After WS07: `checkin` ends at the round's `round_publications` row; `roundPosted`, `roundInProgress` and `between` come from `round_publications` and `live_state`; `eventDay` is never produced.
- `GET /api/live/now` (new, built in WS07; its contract is this one; public, edge-cached 30 s): `{ events: Array<{ tournamentId: string; name: string; href: string; round: number; roundsTotal: number; publishedAt: string }> }`, one entry for each event live now, the one whose round started first at the top, or 204 when none is. An event is live while `live_state = 'live'` and its next scheduled round (read from its schedule rows through `schedulesRepo`, never the `round_schedule` column) starts today (America/Chicago); off from the last published round's results-complete time until the next round's day. It names listed LCA events only: never a hidden draft, a partner listing or a member-created event. It drives the `LiveTag` (named by the count), the Popular list's "Live now" rows, the homepage live hero (WS03) and the Live tag on WS04's rows and previews.
- `GET /api/me` (change): the registrations query also selects `t.end_date, t.registration_url, t.is_visible, t.registration_status`, and `directedTournaments` also selects `end_date, registration_url`. The new fields are documented in the response contract so the client-side window gate and `todo.ts` can place multi-day events and exclude partner rows.

**Frontend**
- Change: `src/components/layout/Navbar.tsx`, `src/components/layout/Footer.tsx`, `src/App.tsx` (the `/search` route and the loading line's height), `vite.config.ts` (`viewport-fit=cover` in `index.html`'s viewport tag when `newNav` is on), `src/hooks/useColorScheme.ts` (the `theme-color` from the Appearance choice; verify where the choice is applied, the hook or the module it wraps), `src/index.css` (the header's and footer's ring tokens and the banners' gold-ground ring), `src/components/AnnouncementBanner.tsx` and `src/components/ImpersonationBanner.tsx` (their ring on gold), `src/components/ThemeScope.tsx` (a colours-only mode, for example `fonts={false}`, that applies a look's tokens without loading its font file, for the strip's round states and WS03's live hero), `functions/api/me.ts` (the four registration fields and two directed-event fields above), `test/unit/domain-shims.test.ts` (`eventPhase` in its one-definition list).
- New: `src/lib/nav.ts` (sections, the About rows with their switch-dependent addresses, the My LCA rows by role, sheet, tabs with icons, footer columns by id, `hideTabBar` routes, pages and synonyms for search, each entry gated on the switch or route that makes it live), `src/lib/eventModeCopy.ts` (one copy builder; it reads `status` and `lastRoundResultsComplete` to choose between the final-standings line and a results-still-coming-in line, WS01 step 5), `src/lib/todo.ts`, `src/hooks/useEventMode.ts`, `src/components/layout/SearchTrigger.tsx`, `AboutMenu.tsx`, `MyLcaMenu.tsx`, `EventStrip.tsx`, `LiveTag.tsx`, `MobileAppBar.tsx`, `BottomTabs.tsx`, `BottomDock.tsx`, `MobileMenu.tsx`, `EventDock.tsx`, `EventBanner.tsx`, `src/components/search/SearchCombobox.tsx`, `SearchPanel.tsx`, `SearchResults.tsx`, `src/pages/SearchPage.tsx`, `functions/api/search.ts`, `functions/api/me/event-mode.ts`, `functions/utils/events/listing.ts` (`listedWhere`; WS03 adds the shared event query beside it), and the contracts in `domain/contracts` (for example `me.ts` and `search.ts`, registered in `registry.ts`). There is no `functions/utils/eventMode.ts`: the endpoint imports `eventPhase`, `phaseKey` and `firstRoundTimesFromSchedule` from `domain/events/eventMode.ts`, and the site imports the same module as `@domain/events/eventMode`.
- WS01's parts in use: `LcaMark` (the brand link), `LcaLogo` (the footer), `StatusBadge`'s status path (the strip's tag, search tags, the Live tag with its static dot), `AppearanceControl` (the footer's base line), the gold `Button` (Join LCA and Renew), `ScrollPanel` (the Menu sheet and the search panel scroll inside themselves, D16), `ThemeScope` (the colours-only live mode) and `formatDate` and `formatTime` (every date with its weekday, "7:00 PM").
- `AnnouncementBanner` keeps its place above the nav, and the event strip stacks below the header; `--header-h` measures all three when sticky.
- WS02's first change to `src/index.css` also adds the four `@source not` lines WS01 step 32 measured (`../docs`, `../test`, `../scripts` and `../*.md`; 8,099 bytes off the site's stylesheet), after checking again that no page builds a class name only those files spell (WS01 step 23's follow-up, "Classes only docs and tests name reach the CSS").

**Jobs & integrations:** none.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. At 1024, 1280 and 1440 px the rook mark, all six sections, the Search button (icon plus the word "Search" at 1024 px) and the utilities are visible with nothing overlapping and nothing wrapping: Donate · Log in · Join LCA signed out, Donate · My LCA ▾ for an active member, and Donate · My LCA ▾ · Renew once a membership has expired. Where the row is tight the squeeze runs in order (Donate into the About menu first, then the brand name hides), section padding stays 12px, and no section, Search, Log in or My LCA ▾, Join LCA or Renew ever leaves the bar. The test includes the widest case, Renew plus the Live tag at 1024 px.
2. The About menu works with mouse and keyboard (Enter, Space, Esc, arrow keys), sets `aria-expanded` and closes on an outside click.
3. The mobile sheet opened from the Menu tab traps focus, returns focus to the Menu tab on close, and closes on Esc.
4. The event strip renders only for a signed-in registered player, guardian, TD or `lca_admin` of an event run on this site inside its window; never for partner events, never for the public, never on checkout pages; and on a member-created event never for an `lca_admin` who does not direct it (checked once WS08 adds that state). Hiding it keys on `event:phase:round`, and a new round, or the next playing day of a multi-day event, brings it back. The public Live tag renders only while `/api/live/now` names at least one listed LCA event, and its name gives their count. Role-safety tests cover a guardian (own dependents only), a TD of another event, a plain member (204) and partner events (never returned).
5. Search returns grouped results in under 300 ms (p95, warm) for queries of 2 or more characters, can be driven entirely from the keyboard in the panel, the homepage box and `/search`, never returns a person name, and never returns an event that is not listed (a hidden draft today, a member-created event once WS08 adds them).
6. Donate is one click on tablet, and on desktop wherever the row has room for it; when the desktop row is tight it is the About menu's last item, above "LCA on Facebook ↗", one click after opening About. On phones it is the first of the three buttons after the section list in the Menu sheet (two taps). WS12 AC6 is amended to match.
7. The footer shows the contact email and a build-time "Site last updated" date with its weekday.
8. All nav labels come from `src/lib/nav.ts`; a test asserts the header and sheet render the same sections and every footer link resolves to a `nav.ts` entry (see AC11).
9. No fixed element overlaps `<main>` content at 320, 360, 390, 768 and 1023px with the tab bar, the event bar and the Register bar in every combination; a focused control is never hidden under the header or the dock.
10. Posting a new round while a test page is scrolled mid-page causes no layout shift; the event bar gains a "New" tag and the status region announces once.
11. Every label rendered by the header, sheet, tabs and footer comes from `nav.ts`; the header and sheet render the same six sections in the same order; the first two tabs reuse the Tournaments and Clubs entries unchanged; the footer's links resolve to `nav.ts` entries by id; one label per destination holds across all of them ("Board & regions", "Bylaws & rules", "Minutes", "State champions", "Scholastic", "Contact"); and the first tab reads "Tournaments".
12. The strip, the event bar and the banner show no countdown, no auto-updating visible text, no pulsing element, and no "R3" or "Bd 6" shorthand; a page whose strip or bar is in a round state requests no live font file.
13. Tab from page load reaches "Skip to content" first; the tab bar is reachable after `<main>`; every footer link is reachable by keyboard at 390px with the folded columns.
14. axe reports zero serious or critical violations in light and dark on `/`, `/tournaments` and `/search` with the event strip and the tab bar mounted, and `/search` joins the AC1 matrix (`PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`).
15. The brand link is named "Louisiana Chess Association, home" at every width, including when the two-line name is hidden.
16. Log in from the tab, the sheet and the desktop header returns the person to the page they were on.
17. My LCA ▾ shows, in this order, only these rows: To do (only with a to-do), My LCA, the event line (only in event mode), Family (only for a parent or guardian), Membership card (only once its route is live), today's role rows (only while `myLcaRail` is off), the Admin row the person holds, Log out. With `myLcaRail` on, no combination of roles (a club rep's included) shows a Workspace, Board inbox or My region row, and the phone sheet holds the same Admin row and no other role row.
18. The About menu's Board & regions, Bylaws & rules and Minutes rows, and the footer's and the trust line's links to them, open `/about/board`, `/about/bylaws` and `/about/minutes` with `newAbout` on and today's `/governance` addresses with it off; State champions opens `/about/champions` with `newNews` on and `/champions` with it off.
19. Under `newLook`, the focus ring of every control in the header, the app bar, the round-state strip, the footer, the About and My LCA menus, the Menu sheet and drawer, the search panel and both banners reaches 3:1 against its own ground in the light look and in dark mode: navy on a light menu inside the navy header, and navy on a gold ground, never gold on gold.
20. Under `newLook`, after a visitor picks Light or Dark, the page's `theme-color` is that look's (`#1a2744` for Light, `#0b1220` for Dark) whatever the device setting; with System it follows the device.
21. The gold WS02 draws on a light ground is exactly the three uses `WS02_GOLD` names, each in its file and on its ground, and `test/unit/goldText.test.ts` finds each one.
22. `GET /api/me`, `GET /api/search` and `GET /api/me/event-mode` each answer to their contract, checked by a contract test; the line `'GET me'` is no longer in `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` and `PENDING_COUNT` is one lower; `eventPhase` is defined once, in `domain/events/eventMode.ts`.
23. With `newNav` on, a first visit to a page loaded on demand (`/about` and `/membership`) has a layout shift under 0.1 at 390 and 1280px.

**Tests:**
- Unit `test/unit/eventMode.test.ts` and `test/unit/eventMode-edges.test.ts` stand (WS01 step 5: every phase at its boundaries, midnight, both clock changes, multi-day events, days with no round, `end_date` NULL, `final`, `after`, the key format).
- Unit `test/unit/eventModeInputs.test.ts` (new): `lastRoundResultsComplete` from fixture games (every section's last scheduled round, a section with no games, a forfeit); the round passed to `phaseKey` for each phase of a Fri to Sun event, so a strip hidden on the day before day 1 comes back before round 2; `pollSeconds` for every phase, 20 until 6:00 AM on an event night; the sooner-first sort with two events.
- Unit `test/unit/eventModeCopy.test.ts`: every role × phase line in `DESIGN_REPLAN_phase0.md` section 3 verbatim, ½, weekday dates, "7:00 PM", the "Players see:" echo equal to the player line, the button labels, the final-standings and results-still-coming-in lines, no shorthand.
- Unit `test/unit/todo.test.ts`: one case per `membership_status`, unpaid entries for ended and upcoming events, children, US Chess expiry before an entered event, the "9+" cap.
- Unit `test/unit/nav.test.ts`: one label per destination; header and sheet sections in order; tabs reuse entries; footer links resolve by id; `hideTabBar` routes; the My LCA rows for a plain member, a parent, a TD, a club rep, a regional representative, an officer, an LCA admin and a person with all of these, with `myLcaRail` off and on; the About rows' and footer links' addresses with `newAbout` and `newNews` off and on; no row whose route is not live.
- Unit `test/unit/search.test.ts`: normalisation and the cache key; the synonym table; the name-like and ID-like query rule.
- Unit `test/unit/listing-rule.test.ts` (new): the search's queries of `tournaments` use `listedWhere` and name no `is_visible` of their own; WS03 and WS04 add their public queries to the test's list (the shared query, `/api/home`, `GET /api/clearinghouse`, `GET /api/events`, the list feed, the saved-search email, every branch of `GET /api/tournaments` and WS09's sitemap).
- Unit `test/unit/domain-shims.test.ts`: `eventPhase` in `DEFINITIONS`, at home in `domain/events/eventMode.ts`.
- Unit `test/unit/goldText.test.ts`: the three `WS02_GOLD` entries now match their files and grounds.
- Unit `test/unit/prepaint-gate.test.ts` (extended): the built `index.html` carries `viewport-fit=cover` with `newNav` on and today's viewport tag with it off.
- Unit `test/unit/theme-color.test.ts` (new): the meta for System, Light and Dark on a light and a dark device, with storage blocked.
- Unit `test/unit/theme-scope.test.tsx` (extended): the colours-only mode writes the look's `data-theme` and imports no font file.
- Unit `test/unit/contract-coverage.test.ts` and `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts`: `GET me` leaves the list and the count drops by one; the new endpoints are in the registry.
- Integration `test/integration/event-mode.test.ts`: role safety (a guardian sees only own dependents; a TD of another event sees nothing; a plain member, `lca_observer`, `lca_officer` and `lca_auditor` get 204; partner events never returned; TD counts never reach a player; once WS08 adds them, an `lca_admin` gets no line for a member-created event they do not direct, while its players, guardians and directors do); ETag 304; items sorted sooner-first.
- Integration `test/integration/search.test.ts`: grouping and caps, no event in two groups, no hidden draft, no member, registration, email or player-name field, champions rows with no name, identical output signed in and out.
- Integration `test/integration/me.test.ts`: the new registration and directed-event fields.
- Contract tests `test/integration/contracts/me.test.ts`, `test/integration/contracts/search.test.ts` and `test/integration/contracts/event-mode.test.ts`.
- a11y `test/a11y/nav.spec.ts`: AC1 at three widths, including each squeeze step and Renew plus the Live tag at 1024 px; the Join and Renew slot signed out, for an active member, within 30 days of expiry (no Renew in the bar) and once expired; Donate as the About menu's last item when squeezed; About menu by keyboard; sheet focus trap; skip link first; brand link name with the name hidden and shown; the Live tag's name with one and with two events live; the My LCA menu for each role with `myLcaRail` off and on; the focus ring measured on every surface of AC19 in light and dark; `theme-color` after a pick; the layout shift of a first visit to `/about` and `/membership`; axe light and dark on `/`, `/tournaments` and `/search` with the strip and tab bar mounted; footer links by keyboard at 390px and the fold's `aria-expanded`.
- a11y `test/a11y/dock.spec.ts`: no fixed element overlaps `<main>` at 320, 360, 390, 768 and 1023px with every bar combination; no layout shift when a round is posted mid-scroll, "New" tag and a single announcement; no live font file requested with the bar in a round state; the dock hides with the keyboard open and on `hideTabBar` routes; the landscape reflow fallback.
- a11y `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`: `/search` joins `PAGES`; the a11y-on build turns `newNav` and `siteSearch` on (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`), and `test/a11y/baseline.json` stays unchanged.

**Depends on:** WS01, merged to `main` on Thu, Oct 22, 2026. `eventStrip` (week, day before, check-in, event day, final and after states) can ship in Phase 0 on today's data; `final` is `status = 'completed'` or a non-null result in every section's last scheduled round. `eventStripLive` and `liveMarker` stay off until WS07 provides `round_publications`, `live_state` and `/api/live/now`. Rows that wait for another workstream appear when its switch or route does: the About tabs (`newAbout`, WS14; `newNews`, WS10), Membership card (`newMembership`, WS12), the Admin row's words (`GET /api/admin/summary`, WS08, Phase 4), Calendar and This weekend (`newTournaments`, WS04), Results (`newNews`, WS10), and the short menu itself once the rail exists (`myLcaRail`, WS13). The member-created cases are checked once WS08 adds those events (Phase 4, section 4).
**Risks & open decisions:** moving Scanner out of the top bar should be confirmed with usage data if available; the Nav-IA board recommends a quick first-click test with parents. The search index must stay small. The header menu and the My LCA rail must agree as roles come and go: both read the same grants (what `useAccountLinks()` in today's `Navbar.tsx` reads from `useAuth()`, with `src/areas/admin/roles.ts`: the role, the seats from `getMySeats`, the clubs a person manages and the events a person directs), so a role never shows in one and not the other. Whether the strip shows on a member-created event, and for whom, is Q18 in section 5, and the footer logo's plate is Q32.

---

## WS03 · Homepage
**Why:** a homepage must:
- say who you are,
- give the top three or four tasks a clear start,
- show real, dated content,
- keep motion to a minimum (NN/g).

Carousels draw about 1% of clicks, mostly on slide 1, and the current slideshow fails WCAG 2.2.2 (Level A). The site already has the live data to be useful every day: events, results and the clubs where people play.

**Canvas boards** (decided; copies in `docs/redesign/decided-boards`):
- `Home-Final`: the whole page in the Register rung at 1440px, every block below the hero, and the footer.
- `Home-Hero-Modes-1`: the hero in the Live, Event day, Register last call and Results rungs.
- `Home-Hero-Modes-2`: the hero in the Announced, Week, This week in Louisiana, Quiet and Clubs rungs.
- `Home-Final-Phones`: the page at 390px, with the doors band as an accordion and the live, results, this-weekend, quiet and clubs heroes on a phone.
- `Event-Festival-Final-2`: the hero in the Featured rung (WS05's festivals).
- The boards were drawn before some of K's later decisions, and this text wins where they differ: there is no member price, so no block says "LCA members save" or promises an entry discount (D11); the this-week strip and the Week rung wait for P2.14 (item 2); and the third door's landing line names My LCA, never Workspace (page 14's frame).

**User-facing scope** (in page order, K's order of Thu, Oct 8, 2026, the same on desktop and phones)
1. **Hero ladder**, picked by data on the server in this order, first match wins (full table in `DESIGN_REPLAN_phase0.md` section 4, with the quiet and clubs rungs as amended in its section 8): **Live** (WS07, `homeLive`; the live colour tokens on the hero only, through `ThemeScope`'s colours-only mode from WS02, so no live font loads on `/`; round row in words, never "R3") · **Event day** (today is between an LCA event's `date` and `eventEnd`) · **Featured** (WS05 festivals, `homeFeatured`: an admin pins one festival from `/admin/festivals`, only while its page is live and until its end date, cleared by Take offline; the festival's name, dates with weekdays, city and tagline, one primary "Choose your event" and a plain "See the schedule" link; when the live or event-day event belongs to the featured festival, the festival hero stays and carries the live line; the doors band keeps its lower position) · **Register, last call** (an `isOpen` or `isClosedOnline` LCA event starts within 14 days or early entry ends within 7) · **Results** (`homeResults`: an LCA event with `eventEnd` within 7 days, `status = 'completed'` and a non-null result in every section's last scheduled round, else fall through; the Heritage accent, `ThickThinRule` and `Seal` in Geist; winners by full name with ½, as plain text (D9); WS10's `event_results` once it exists) · **Register** (within 30 days or early entry within 14; the Register card `Home-Final` draws, with the primary by state: Register when `isOpen`, Join the waitlist when `isFull`, Event details when `isClosedOnline`, and a "You're entered" variant) · **Announced** (`isAnnounced`, within 60 days; "Email me when registration opens" signed in, Event details and a calendar file that collects nothing signed out) · **Week** (`homeWeek`; skipped while P2.14 waits, as `Home-Hero-Modes-2` draws, item 2) · **This week in Louisiana** (LCA and partner rows within 14 days, Louisiana only; partner rows only while the sync is fresh, LCA rows always) · **Quiet** (the hero itself carries Home-E's three doors on the usual navy hero ground with the a to h and 8 to 1 grid motif, headed "Find your next game in Louisiana", with the Next LCA event line beneath and the region select, which opens `/clubs` filtered to the region picked; the soonest Louisiana events are in the upcoming six further down; primary: Find a club near you) · **Clubs** (the floor when nothing matches or `/api/home` fails: the same doors hero, with the seven region chips from `domain/clubs/regions.ts` in place of the select; primary: Find a club). In these two rungs the doors band lower on the page is omitted, so the doors never appear twice; every other rung keeps the doors band in its lower position. The doors' links are plain links, never a second primary. Exactly one primary action per mode, a gold `Button`. Every non-LCA mode ends with a "Next LCA event" line when one is announced within 180 days ("Registration open ›", "Registration opens Fri, Jan 15 ›" or "Details ›"). Partner rows carry "Partner event · Registers on the organizer's site ↗", never Register, never a count, never the LCA hall photo; a single `EventAction` component enforces this, and any `tournaments` row with a `registration_url` counts as organizer-registered; the "Also this weekend" partner line links to the organizer's site until WS04 ships the internal partner page. Every rung, the Next LCA event line, "Also this weekend" and the upcoming six take events from the shared event query (section 2.2) through its listing condition (`listedWhere`, WS02), so a hidden draft never reaches the home page, and neither does a member-created event (D26; its part is built with WS08's member-created events in Phase 4, section 4). Personal lines are added on the client: "You're entered" and "You finished 4th" from `/api/me`, boards and colours from `useEventMode()`. While `/api/home` loads the hero reserves 360px at 390px and 440px at 1280px with a neutral skeleton; if it fails, the hero renders the Clubs floor from `domain/clubs/regions.ts` with no error chrome. Below the hero in every mode is the **identity and search band**: the official logo through `LcaLogo` (D1; WS01 step 28 left this placement to WS03), "Louisiana's chess community since 1915 · 300+ members · 25+ clubs · 7 regions" (each stat a link), a `ThickThinRule`, and the Home-B search box (WS02's `SearchCombobox` inline, behind `homeSearch`, with a visible label; the quick-pick chips stay hidden until WS04 and WS09 ship the filters they point at).
2. **This-week strip** (Home-C), waiting for P2.14. K kept this strip on Thu, Oct 8, 2026 for when the club information is richer: Mon to Sun day chips with meeting counts, today highlighted, and the weekend's tournaments, directly under the identity and search band. It needs each club's meeting days as data. The decided clubs pages, `Clubs-H` and `Club-G` (picked on Thu, Oct 15, 2026), keep each club's meetings in its own words, with no day filter and nothing computed weekly, and `Home-Final` itself notes that free-text schedules are never parsed into a Tonight list, since a wrong time sends a parent to a locked door. So neither the strip nor the Week rung is built, and `homeWeek` stays off, until K answers P2.14 again; the phase 2 replan's day link (`/clubs?day=`) is not built, since `Clubs-H-2` has no day filter.
3. **News:** three latest items, each dated with its weekday (`formatDate`), plus the compact `FacebookFeed`. Once WS10's Facebook mirror exists (`newNews`), the compact rail reads the mirrored `lca_posts` rows, and the three items leave out hidden posts and link a mirrored post to a page only once it is promoted, as WS10 sets out. Every Facebook link stays (0.1 rule 4).
4. **Doors band** (Home-E), headed "Where do you want to start?": three doors, "I want to play", "My child plays" and "I run a club or tournament". Each has three links into shared pages, from `nav.ts`, some pre-filtered such as `/tournaments?type=scholastic`, whose filters apply once `newTournaments` is on (until then the link opens the page unfiltered). A grey line under each link names where it lands; the third door's "Run a tournament" lands on My LCA (`/dashboard`) and its grey line says My LCA. It is a band, not navigation, and sits below the fold. On phones it is an accordion: one door open at a time, each opened by a real button with `aria-expanded`, opening in place without animation (`Home-Final-Phones`). In the quiet and clubs rungs the hero carries the three doors instead (item 1) and this band is omitted.
5. **Upcoming tournaments:** the next six listed events in Louisiana, the hero's own events left out, as dated rows: a `DateBlock` (one tile with both dates for an event of more than one day, below), LCA / Partner tags, status in words through `StatusBadge`'s status path, and counts on LCA rows only. Partner rows say "Registers on the organizer's site ↗" and drop out when the sync is more than 3 days old. Links to `/tournaments`.
6. **Results & champions** (Home-D): the "Current champions" band ships in Phase 0 from `state_champions` (`homeChampions`) with the Heritage accent (`ThickThinRule` and `Seal` in Geist, base scope, no heritage fonts on `/`), hidden when the newest title is more than 13 months old. Names are plain text; once WS10's archive exists, each title's year links to its results (assumed, P2.37). The recap card waits for WS10 (`homeRecap`) and never shows a member-created event, which has no recap (assumed, Q13).
7. **Membership band:** Adult $15 · Scholastic $5 · Family $25 · Senior $10, then **Join LCA**. Its words promise no entry discount (D11).

- **Dates:** `DateBlock` (WS01) takes one date today. WS03 gives it an end date, drawn as one tile with both weekdays and both days, as the boards draw a weekend event, with the words of both dates from `formatDate` (WS01 step 26's follow-up). The hero cards and the upcoming rows use it, and so do WS04's rows. Every date on the page, partner rows included, comes from `formatDate` with its weekday.
- **Deleted once `newLook` is on: `HeroSlideshow`.** WS01's brief removed the slideshow, which fails WCAG 2.2.2, and WS01 step 29 left the removal to WS03. Since WS01, `newLook` already draws the still hero in the slideshow's place (`src/pages/HomePage.tsx` line 229), so the deletion waits until K has turned `newLook` on (Phase 0's "Done when", section 4): from then the still hero is what every visitor sees, and deleting `HeroSlideshow` and the slideshow's half of today's hero (the track of slides and its controls) changes nothing a visitor sees. If the rest of WS03 is ready first, it merges without the deletion, which follows in a small PR of its own. After the deletion, with `newHome` off, the page keeps today's blocks under the still picture of slide 1, with today's words and buttons, in every look. With it go:
  - the `HomePage.tsx` entry in the allowlist of `test/unit/no-rotation.test.ts` (its two timer calls);
  - the slide controls' four entries in `test/unit/fixtures/raw-colours-allowlist.json` (`bg-white/20`, `bg-white/40`, `hover:bg-white/30` and `hover:bg-white/60`, six uses), and the Join LCA button's `bg-white/10` and `hover:bg-white/20` if the still hero drops them;
  - the dots' six `target-size` entries in `test/a11y/known-violations.json`, with the pin in `test/unit/browser-test-wiring.test.ts`;
  - the flag-off half of `test/unit/hero-no-autoplay.test.tsx`, `test/unit/fixtures/home-hero-today.ts`, and the two flag-off timer tests in `test/unit/hero-images-edges.test.tsx`;
  - the `a11y-off` tests of the slideshow in `test/a11y/home.spec.ts`, and the `a11y-off` run of the failing-lists test in `test/a11y/home-edges.spec.ts`;
  - the imports of the photos of slides 2 to 5, which take 1,601,855 bytes out of every build (slide 1's JPEG stays as the fallback `ResponsiveImage` names);
  - the page's `exhaustive-deps` lint warning, which sits in `HeroSlideshow`.
  `test/a11y/baseline.json` is recorded again on purpose for `/`, and the pins of its sha256 and of its sample of `/` in `test/unit/hero-images-edges.test.tsx` move with it. Before turning `newLook` on, K looks at the still hero on a preview, with the step 29 judgement calls (`REDESIGN_STATUS.md`, "K reviews the step 29 judgement calls (step 29)").
- **First paint.** WS01's step 29 lab check found the hero's picture missing from the first HTML (the page asks for it only once its main script has run), a layout shift of up to 0.36 as the lower lists and the featured line arrived, and the font swap under the hero's heading. So: in a rung that shows the hall photo, the first HTML of `/` names the hero picture, through a `<link rel="preload" as="image">` with its `imagesrcset` and `imagesizes` that a page function for `/` writes into the head from the same cached data as `/api/home`, as the event, club and news pages write their link previews today; every block below the hero holds its height while its data loads; and the hero's heading keeps its box across the font swap. Today's still hero shows the same picture, so the preload serves it too. verify: a page function cannot read `src/lib/features.ts`, which is built for the site, so the function writes only the preload, which changes nothing a visitor sees.
- **Today's page:** its own call, `fetch('/api/clearinghouse?upcoming=true')` (`src/pages/HomePage.tsx` line 216 at the end of WS01), becomes a call in `src/areas/events/api.ts`, and the page leaves `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY` in `test/unit/area-boundaries.test.ts` and its line under Follow-ups in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`; the new page reads `/api/home` through `getHome()` in the same module. Today's page keeps its own `StatusDot` (line 179) and the clearinghouse module's own date formatter, which writes no weekday, until today's page is removed after `newHome` is turned on; the new page uses neither, but `StatusBadge`'s status path and `formatDate` (WS01 steps 19, 26 and 29's follow-ups).

Removed with `newHome` on: the three equal columns.

**Data model:** none of its own. It consumes WS04, WS05, WS07 and WS10, and, if K keeps the strip (P2.14), the club meeting days it would need.

**API:** `GET /api/home` (new, public). Its contract is `HomeResponse`, a schema in `domain/contracts` registered in `registry.ts` and checked by a contract test (WS01 step 7); the types below are that schema's:

```ts
interface HeroEvent {
  id: string; name: string; href: string; city: string
  date: string; endDate?: string; dates: string   // both dates with their weekdays, from formatDate
  organizer?: string; format?: string; sections?: string[]
  tag: 'lca' | 'partner'; external?: boolean         // partner rows: organizer's site, new tab
}
interface LiveHero { mode: 'live'; event: HeroEvent; round: number; roundsTotal: number; publishedAt: string; nextRoundStart?: string
  rounds: Array<{ round: number; state: 'final' | 'live' | 'scheduled'; startsAt?: string }> }
interface EventDayHero { mode: 'eventDay'; event: HeroEvent; nextRound?: { round: number; startsAt: string } }
interface RegisterHero { mode: 'register' | 'lastCall'; event: HeroEvent
  registration: 'open' | 'full' | 'closedOnline'
  priceFrom?: number; registered?: number; maxPlayers?: number   // priceFrom is the lowest entry price across sections (D11)
  earlyDeadline?: string; checkIn?: string; round1?: string; startsIn?: string; alsoThisWeekend?: HeroEvent }
interface ResultsHero { mode: 'results'; event: HeroEvent; isStateChampionship: boolean
  winners: Array<{ section: string; names: string[]; score: string }>; moreSections: number
  facebookHref: string; recapHref?: string; photosHref?: string }
interface AnnouncedHero { mode: 'announced'; event: HeroEvent; registrationOpensAt?: string; alsoThisWeekend?: HeroEvent }
interface WeekHero { mode: 'week'; range: string; meetings: number; clubs: number   // never sent while P2.14 waits
  tonight: Array<{ time: string; club: string; place: string; href: string }>; weekend: HeroEvent[] }
interface NearbyHero { mode: 'nearby'; heading: 'This weekend in Louisiana' | 'Coming up in Louisiana'; events: HeroEvent[] }
interface QuietHero { mode: 'quiet'; regions: Array<{ slug: string; name: string }> }   // doors are static; events stay in upcoming
interface ClubsHero { mode: 'clubs'; regions: Array<{ slug: string; name: string }> }
interface FeaturedFestival { id: number; slug: string; name: string; href: string; city?: string; dates: string; tagline?: string; heroPhotoUrl?: string; events: HeroEvent[] }
interface FeaturedHero { mode: 'featured'; festival: FeaturedFestival
  live?: { event: HeroEvent; round: number; roundsTotal: number; publishedAt: string } }   // when the live or event-day event belongs to the festival
interface NextLca { event: HeroEvent; status: 'open' | 'opensAt' | 'details'; opensAt?: string }

interface HomeResponse {
  hero: (LiveHero | EventDayHero | FeaturedHero | RegisterHero | ResultsHero | AnnouncedHero | WeekHero | NearbyHero | QuietHero | ClubsHero)
    & { reason: string; nextLca?: NextLca }
  week: WeekHero | null                       // null while P2.14 waits
  upcoming: HeroEvent[]                       // Louisiana only in Phase 0, hero events excluded, listed events only
  recap: { title: string; href: string; date: string } | null   // WS10
  champions: Array<{ year: number; title: string; name: string }> | null
  news: Array<{ id: string; title: string; date: string; href: string }>
  featured_festival: FeaturedFestival | null  // WS05 festivals, homeFeatured; null until an admin pins one
}
```

No field in `HomeResponse` is personal. Mode selection is `selectHeroMode(now, data)` in `functions/utils/events/homeHero.ts` (1.3 named `functions/utils/homeHero.ts`; WS01 step 16 keeps server helpers in area folders), in America/Chicago time, with the window constants `LAST_CALL_DAYS = 14`, `LAST_CALL_EARLY_DAYS = 7`, `REGISTER_DAYS = 30`, `REGISTER_EARLY_DAYS = 14`, `RESULTS_DAYS = 7`, `ANNOUNCED_DAYS = 60`, `NEARBY_DAYS = 14`, `NEXT_LCA_DAYS = 180`, `SYNC_STALE_DAYS = 3`. Events come from the shared event query in `functions/utils/events/listing.ts` (1.3 named `functions/utils/events.ts`: the LCA and clearinghouse union moved out of `functions/api/clearinghouse.ts`, which then calls it, beside WS02's `listedWhere`). Registration state comes from `isOpen`, `isFull`, `isClosedOnline` and `isAnnounced` in the same module (the phase 0 replan's question 4, in its section 2), one unit test each, and `eventEnd(t) = (t.end_date ?? t.date) 23:59` Central is `eventEndMs` from `domain/events/eventMode.ts`, never a second copy. Partner rows are dropped when the newest `synced_at` is more than 3 days old or the start date has passed, and an admin alert is raised; LCA rows never depend on the sync. Edge cache 60 s, dropping to 15 s in the live and event-day modes. The Featured rung reads `festivals` where `featured = 1`, `status = 'live'` and today is on or before `end_date`; it sits after Live and Event day, except that when the live or event-day event belongs to the featured festival the festival hero stays and carries the live line (assumed, S9.3). Every block degrades to `null` when its source isn't built yet.

**Frontend**
- Rewrite: `src/pages/HomePage.tsx` (with `newHome` on, the new page; with it off, today's page under the still hero).
- New: in `src/components/home/` (the home page's own parts, which no area owns), `HomeHero.tsx` (one subcomponent per mode, each with a skeleton at the reserved height), `HeroEventCard.tsx` (lca, partner, compact), `NextLcaCard.tsx`, `EventAction.tsx`, `IdentitySearchBand.tsx`, `UpcomingList.tsx` (`excludeIds`), `DoorsBand.tsx` (`variant="band"` lower on the page, an accordion on phones; `variant="hero"` on the navy hero ground in the quiet and clubs rungs), `RecapCard.tsx`, `ChampionsBand.tsx`, `MembershipBand.tsx`; `src/lib/addToCalendar.ts`; `functions/utils/events/homeHero.ts`; `functions/utils/events/listing.ts` (the shared query and the four registration-state rules, beside `listedWhere`); `functions/api/home.ts`; the page function for `/` that writes the hero picture's preload (verify the file name Pages gives the root route, for example `functions/index.ts`; it joins the page routes excluded in `test/unit/contract-coverage.test.ts`, with its HTML reason); and the `HomeResponse` contract in `domain/contracts`. `ThisWeekStrip.tsx` is not built while P2.14 waits. The hero photo is `src/assets/LCA_Slide_1.jpg` through `ResponsiveImage`, alt "Players at long tables in a tournament hall", unless K supplies another.
- Change: `src/components/DateBlock.tsx` (the end date), `src/areas/events/api.ts` (`getHome()` and today's page's call), `functions/api/clearinghouse.ts` (calls the shared query).
- WS01's parts in use: `ResponsiveImage` (the hall photo, with `priority` in its rungs), `LcaLogo` (the identity band), `DateBlock`, `StatusBadge`'s status path, `ThickThinRule` and `Seal` (the Heritage accent, in the base scope), the gold `Button` (the one primary), and `formatDate`, `formatTime` and `formatScore` (½); with WS02's `SearchCombobox` and `ThemeScope`'s colours-only mode, `FacebookFeed variant="compact"` and `RegistrationReminderButton` (`src/areas/registration/components`).

**Jobs & integrations:** none.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. Mode selection is unit-tested for every rung, at every window boundary, for ties (earliest event), at midnight and across DST, for `end_date` NULL, for a stale sync with an LCA row in range, for a closed-online and an announced LCA event inside 30 days, for a completed event with no results, for a last round finishing after midnight, and for the Featured rung (a pinned festival; the live or event-day event belonging to it; a draft or offline festival that is never featured). A mode missing its data falls to the next rung, no mode renders an empty frame, and the Week rung is never chosen while `homeWeek` is off.
2. Nothing auto-advances, and the hero has exactly one primary action.
3. Every date shows its weekday and every status is in words.
4. Partner events never show Register buttons or counts in any mode; a partner-only fixture renders no "Register" text and no count. Out-of-state events never take the hero.
5. On a 390 px phone, as on desktop, the order is hero, identity and search band, news, doors, upcoming, results and champions, membership (K's order of Thu, Oct 8, 2026); the this-week strip would sit directly under the identity and search band only if K keeps it (P2.14). In the quiet and clubs rungs the doors render inside the hero and the doors band is omitted; nothing else is hidden by hero mode.
6. LCP ≤ 2.5 s and CLS < 0.1 (lab, mobile), measured with `npm run perf:home` (WS01 step 29) on the build with `newHome` on and recorded with the LCP element; K's run on a preview is recorded beside it.
7. If WS07 or WS10 data is missing, or the strip is not built, those blocks are absent; no empty frames or placeholder text reach production. Partner rows are absent when the clearinghouse sync is more than 3 days old.
8. The live hero has a visible "Pause updates" control, polls every 20 s only while the tab is visible, and shows the time of its last update through `formatTime` ("Updated 7:42 PM").
9. Personal lines ("You're entered", "Board 6 · White", "You finished 4th") never appear in the cached `/api/home` response.
10. The heritage font chunk never loads on `/`.
11. The live font chunk never appears in the `/` network log, in any mode, including the live hero.
12. While `/api/home` loads, the hero holds its reserved height and CLS stays under 0.1; when the request fails, the Clubs floor renders with no error text, and the lower blocks keep their error states.
13. The live hero's round row and every strip, bar and banner line carry no "R3"-style shorthand; the round row reads in words with every label in the DOM.
14. The inline search box opens its list only on click or Tab focus, never on page load, and the open list never shifts the page.
15. The three doors render exactly once on the page in every rung: inside the hero in the quiet and clubs rungs (including the fetch-failure Clubs floor), and in the doors band in every other rung. The doors hero keeps exactly one primary action.
16. No hidden draft appears in any block of `/` or in `/api/home`, in any rung; once WS08 adds member-created events (Phase 4), none of those does either (D26).
17. The deletion merges only once `newLook` is on. After it, `HeroSlideshow` is gone: `src/pages/HomePage.tsx` sets no timer and has no entry in `test/unit/no-rotation.test.ts`, no build imports the photos of slides 2 to 5, `test/a11y/known-violations.json` holds no `target-size` entry, and with `newHome` off `/` shows today's blocks under the still picture of slide 1 in every look.
18. An event of more than one day shows one `DateBlock` tile with both dates and both weekdays, in the hero cards and the upcoming rows.
19. The identity band shows the official logo through `LcaLogo`, never recoloured (D1).
20. In a rung that shows the hall photo, the first HTML of `/` names the hero picture (Lighthouse's "Request is discoverable in initial document" passes), and every block below the hero holds its height while its data loads.
21. `src/pages/HomePage.tsx` calls no `fetch` (it is off `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY`), and lint reports no warning in it.
22. `GET /api/home` answers to its contract, checked by a contract test.

**Tests:**
- Unit `test/unit/homeHero.test.ts`: every rung and boundary from AC1, ties, the Next LCA line's three statuses, the "Also this weekend" line, the registration-state primary per rung, the Featured rung and its live-line case, and the Week rung never chosen with `homeWeek` off.
- Unit `test/unit/events.test.ts`: `isOpen`, `isFull`, `isClosedOnline`, `isAnnounced` and `eventEnd` (through `eventEndMs`), one case per clause.
- Unit `test/unit/date-block.test.tsx` and `test/unit/date-block-edges.test.tsx` (extended): the end date, both weekdays in the words, one `<time>` for each date, the same month and two months, the end date before the start or unreadable.
- Unit `test/unit/listing-rule.test.ts` (WS02's): gains the shared query, `/api/home` and `GET /api/clearinghouse`.
- Unit `test/unit/no-rotation.test.ts`, `test/unit/hero-no-autoplay.test.tsx`, `test/unit/hero-images-edges.test.tsx` and `test/unit/browser-test-wiring.test.ts`: the slideshow's entries, halves and pins removed or moved, as the scope's list says; `test/unit/area-boundaries.test.ts`: `HomePage.tsx` off `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY`.
- Integration `test/integration/home.test.ts`: a partner-only fixture renders no "Register" text and no count; no personal field; `null` blocks for unbuilt sources; cache headers per mode; out-of-state rows never in the hero; the Results rung with a completed, resultless event falls through; a hidden draft in no block (and a member-created event in none, once WS08 adds them).
- Contract test `test/integration/contracts/home.test.ts`.
- Unit `test/unit/identity-band.test.tsx` (new): the official logo through `LcaLogo` in the identity band, never recoloured (criterion 19), with the stats line and its links.
- a11y and visual `test/a11y/home.spec.ts` and `test/a11y/home-edges.spec.ts` (WS01 step 29's, extended): a snapshot of each mode at 390 and 1280px; the page order at 390 and 1280px; the doors rendered once in every mode, inside the hero for quiet and clubs, and the phone accordion's `aria-expanded`; the heritage and live font chunks absent from the `/` network log; the skeleton height and CLS; the fetch-failure Clubs floor; Pause updates in the live hero; the inline search overlay's open and focus rules; the still hero with `newHome` off once the slideshow is deleted; the hero picture's preload in the first HTML of `/`.
- Lab: `npm run perf:home` with `newHome` on (AC6).

**Depends on:** WS01 and WS02 (`SearchCombobox`, `listedWhere` and `ThemeScope`'s colours-only mode). The strip and the Week rung wait for K's answer to P2.14 (`homeWeek` stays off). Live mode needs WS07 (`homeLive`). The results hero (`homeResults`) and the Champions band (`homeChampions`) ship in Phase 0 from existing standings and `state_champions`; only the recap card waits for WS10 (`homeRecap`). WS03 builds the shared event query that WS04 adopts. The Featured rung waits for WS05's festivals (`homeFeatured`). The member-created part of AC16 is checked with WS08's member-created events in Phase 4 (section 4). The slideshow's deletion waits until K has turned `newLook` on (Phase 0's "Done when", section 4).
**Risks & open decisions:** the hero is thin between LCA events; that is exactly why the ladder's lower rungs (this week in Louisiana, quiet with the doors, clubs) exist. Review the windows after one season using hero clicks per mode. The still hero is what every visitor sees once `newLook` is on, which is why the slideshow is deleted only after that, and K looks at it on a preview before turning `newLook` on. If K brings the strip back (P2.14), it needs club meeting days as data, which the decided club pages do not keep.

---

## WS04 · Event discovery & sharing
**Why:**
- For finding dates across month breaks, a dated list beat a month grid (Hund, Dowell & Mueller 2014), so the calendar should navigate the list, not replace it.
- Applied filters belong in removable chips with a result count, and filter state belongs in the URL (Baymard, NN/g).
- A count line such as Luma's "N going" answers "should I hurry?".
- NC gives every clearinghouse event an internal page.
- Chess67's pages preview as "Loading…" on Facebook.
- K asked for chips, counts and link previews.
- K's member-created events (Tue, Oct 20, 2026) are shared by link and never listed, so discovery keeps them out of every list, feed and index while their links still preview well.

**Canvas boards:**
- `Tourn-List-Final` is the default List view with the rail and the preview pane; `Tourn-List-States` holds the Results tab and the bell's signed-out, partner and saved states.
- `Tourn-Calendar-Final` is the calendar view (month beside the list).
- `Tourn-Map-Final` is the map view (map first, list drawer).
- `Tourn-Table-Final` is the table view (compact table, side panel).
- `Tourn-Phones-Final` covers every phone layout.
- `Event-Partner-Final` is the partner page (page 5 is decided; WS05 lists it too).
- Season at a glance is not drawn yet; it is drawn once K confirms it (assumed, P2.22). `Schol-Final`'s season inset shows the rows it shares with WS11.

**User-facing scope**
- **One list, four views.** `/tournaments?view=list|calendar|map|table`; `list` is the default and is omitted from the URL. The page band holds Upcoming · Results at the left, the view switcher in the centre and the tournament search box at the right, on every view. On the Results tab the centre holds the season switcher instead (2026 · 2025 · 2024 · Earlier); Calendar, Map and Table do not apply to past results, and the chrome is otherwise identical. Every view and both tabs list only listed events (below).
  - **List:** month-grouped rows, each with a `DateBlock` (one tile with both dates and both weekdays for an event of more than one day, as WS03 extends it), name, city and organizer, type, "23 of 60 registered" with a fill bar and the next price step on LCA rows, and status in words (`StatusBadge`'s status path); partner rows are lighter and say "Registers on the organizer's site ↗". On hover or focus a row shows the Remind me bell; a partner row's popover offers only the two date reminders. At 1280px and wider the right column is the rail (New to tournaments?, Latest results, For organizers) until a row is selected; the rail and the preview share one 400px column and are never shown together. The selected row takes a cream fill and the selected ring (below). On desktop each row carries a source tag; the tag and the register wording mark the source now that there is no Source filter.
  - **Calendar:** a small Monday-first month beside the week-grouped list; LCA days filled, partner days hollow, today ringed (below), multi-day events as one bar; tapping a day scrolls the list; the grid works with arrow keys and has a text alternative. The calendar has no season strip of its own; Season at a glance (below) sits above the filter line on every view of the Upcoming tab.
  - **Map:** `LCAMap` across the full width with filled pins for LCA events, hollow pins for partners, clusters with counts and region labels, and a list drawer below kept in sync with the pins; selecting a pin highlights the row and the other way round. "Update the list as I move the map" starts ticked; the map opens on the Gulf South when Region is at its default and zooms to Louisiana when a Louisiana region is picked (assumed, S3.3). Distance from a city or ZIP comes later.
  - **Table:** dense 13px rows with the columns Date, Event, City, Type, Source, Entries and Status, drawn with WS01's table primitives (`stickyFirst={2}` pins Date and Event, `TableRowHeader` names each row by its event, `label` and `bounded` give the box its name and its held header); sortable column headers; Date and Event frozen when the table scrolls sideways; Entries as "23 of 60" with a mini bar; Status in words; no bell column (Remind me lives in the panel); a picked row carries the selected ring (below) and opens the preview as a side panel with the List's button row at the bottom; below 1280px the panel becomes a 400px drawer over the right edge. There are no saved table views: the URL holds every view, and a weekly saved search covers the recurring case (assumed, S3.4).
  - **Results tab:** the State champions card at the top, then the season's results, finished listed events only (WS10 keeps member-created events out of every results list). The card's names are plain text, and once WS10's archive exists each title's year or event links to its archived results (assumed, P2.37). "Your first tournament, step by step" lives in the rail's New to tournaments card; the two links that sat under the page title are gone.
- **Season at a glance** (assumed, P2.22; not drawn yet, drawn once K confirms): on the Upcoming tab, one strip under the page band and above the filter-and-sort line, closed by default: "2026 to 2027 LCA season · N events · Show all", opening to dated rows with weekdays (date, name, city), each linking to its event page once the event exists; rows for events not yet created show the date, name and city, with no link. With no open season published, the strip says "The 2026 to 2027 LCA tournament schedule is being set." with one email field (`season_notify`, kind `open`; assumed, P2.44). On phones it sits under the two-row toolbar, closed. It reads WS11's `season_schedule` (kind `open`) through `GET /api/season?kind=open`, and its rows are drawn by `SeasonSchedule.tsx` (in `src/areas/events/components`), which WS11 builds and shares. It is built with WS11 in Phase 3, behind `seasonSchedule`; WS04's Phase 1 PRs do not wait for it, and criterion 15 is checked when WS11 lands.
- **One filter-and-sort line,** 44px, on every view: the live count ("16 tournaments"), then **When** (This weekend · Next 30 days · Next 3 months · Any time · Pick dates) · **Type** (Classical · Quick · Blitz · Scholastic · FIDE) · **Region** (Louisiana and nearby states by default; Louisiana's seven regions; Mississippi, Texas, Alabama), then the applied chips, Clear all, and Sort at the far right ("Sort: Soonest first", also Name A to Z, and Closest to me once a region or city is set). The applied chips are WS01's `FilterChip`s in `FilterBar`, which replaces today's page's own `Chip`; removing a chip moves focus to the next chip, or to the line when none is left (WS01 step 26's follow-up). Each dropdown's menu that scrolls keeps the classes the rule WS01 step 32 added to `src/index.css` finds (a `bg-popover` menu with `overflow-y-auto` and button options), which draws an option's focus ring just inside its edge so the menu never clips it, or extends that rule to its own classes. The Rated and Source chips are dropped. All state lives in the URL (the open preview too, as `event=`, `Tourn-List-Final` note 8), so back and forward restore it and the address bar is the link; there is no "Copy link to this view" control (Share stays per event, in the preview). Under the line sits one muted info note with the info icon saying what is showing and how each source registers: "Showing Thu, Oct 8 to Wed, Jan 6 · 3 register on LCA · 13 partner listings. LCA events register here. Partner events come from the Gulf South Tournament Clearinghouse and register on the organizer's site, opens in a new tab." The `.ics` "Subscribe to this list" link sits at the end of the note (assumed, S3.5). On phones the chips become a Filters button with the applied count beside the live count; it opens a bottom sheet (When, Type, Region with counts, Clear all, "Show 16 events"), which scrolls inside itself (D16), with the info note folded into it.
- **Preview pane** (at 1280px and wider on List and Calendar; inside the drawer on Map; the side panel on Table): the facts, sections and fees, See who's registered (public: under-18s as first name and last initial, adults in full, grouped by section with counts, D15; TDs and admins in full), then Register (gold, with the current price) · Remind me · Add to calendar · Share · Full event page. Partner events show "Registers on the organizer's site ↗" in navy, never Register, never a count and no sections table. An LCA event not yet open leads with "Remind me when it opens", then Add to calendar, Share and Full event page, and gains Register when registration opens (assumed, S3.6). The preview replaces the rail; Close brings the rail back.
- **Phones:** one toolbar on every view, two rows: Upcoming · Results on the first row; on the second a **Display** dropdown at the left (a select-style button with a sheet of four options and a tick on the current one, not a segmented control), the live count beside it, and a **Filters** button with the applied count ("Filters · 2") at the right. Search stays in the app bar; there is no chip row. List B rows with a sticky month band and the live count carry no LCA or Partner tag text and no bell (the navy date block and the status words mark LCA events; partner rows read "Registers on the organizer's site ↗"). One preview sheet for all four views: "‹ Back to list" (or map, table, calendar) as a 44px pill at the top left, Close at the top right, the same facts, sections and fees as desktop, See who's registered with minors abbreviated, and the full button row pinned at the bottom; the bell lives only in the sheet's Remind me. Map A: the map on top, a list sheet below that drags up, the pin preview rising over the lower half with "‹ Back to map" first under the thumb. Table A: Date and Event frozen, swipe for the rest, no bell column and no tag text; partner rows read "Organizer's site ↗"; the phone table takes the desktop notes (no copy link, the info note folded into the Filters sheet, minors abbreviated, the preview's buttons). Calendar A (the month grid over the selected day's agenda) by default (assumed, S3.1).
- **Partner pages** at `/tournaments/p/:slug` (`Event-Partner-Final`), with rules for every way a hand-kept spreadsheet can be wrong or late:
  - a fixed facts card using the feed's fields (organizer, city and state, venue, rating system, eligibility, contact) that never hides a row: a blank row reads "Not in the listing yet"
  - buttons: "Registers on the organizer's site ↗" (navy), Add to calendar (disabled with "Date to be confirmed" when the date is unconfirmed) and Remind me (the two date reminders plus "If this listing changes", kind `listing_changed`, sent when the sync's diff touches the date, venue, link or cancellation)
  - the source line "Listed from the Gulf South Tournament Clearinghouse · updated <weekday date>"
  - a map; a missing venue uses the city for the map and the drive times ("from the city centre"); a missing city hides both
  - "Also near <city>"
  - a TBA, month-only, unparseable or impossible date reads "Date to be confirmed by the organizer"; a missing link makes "Contact the organizer" the main action; a bare link gets https:// and is checked before the button shows; a stale sync and a listing that has left the feed are stated in words and the page is kept; a changed date adds a history row with weekdays (`clearinghouse_history`); a probable duplicate is merged under one page with an admin-only tag
  - "Report a problem with this listing", which creates a support ticket tagged `listing_report` with the clearinghouse id (in `support_tickets.kind` and `about_id`, which WS08's `00xx_ticket_kinds.sql` adds first in Phase 1, section 4); the admin queue's `listing_report` source (WS08) lists those tickets
- **Listed events only.** `/tournaments` (every view, both tabs), `GET /api/events`, the list's calendar feed and the weekly saved-search email take events from the shared event query (section 2.2, built in WS03), and every branch of `GET /api/tournaments` from its own query, all through the one listing condition, `listedWhere` (WS02), so a hidden draft never shows: the signed-out branch answers listed events only; a signed-in member's branch adds the member's own events (those they created, direct or entered, and a club rep's own club's drafts, as today); and the branch for LCA admins and the view-only roles keeps every event, as today, until WS08 keeps member-created events to LCA admins (assumed, Q35). A member-created event (D26) is never listed: not on any view or tab, in any feed or email, in any sitemap, or in structured data. It is shared by link and keeps every other function, so its own page's Add to calendar and its link preview still work (below). That part is built with WS08's member-created events in Phase 4 (section 4): WS08 extends `listedWhere`, and the tests here gain a member-created fixture. WS04 writes no sitemap: WS09's `functions/sitemap.xml.ts` lists the listed events through the same condition (WS09).
- **Sharing:** server-rendered OG and Twitter meta (title, a date line with weekdays from `formatDate`, city, entries, image) on `/tournaments/:id`, `/tournaments/p/:slug`, `/clubs/:id` and `/news/:slug`, plus a generated preview image. A member-created event is shared by link, so its page keeps a full preview, whose description ends with its page's own line, "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." (D27; assumed, Q12). Every page of an event that is not listed (`/tournaments/:id` and the pages under it) gets `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` and an `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` header in the head the server writes, and no Event JSON-LD; this is where WS05's member-created event page gets its noindex (D26). A hidden draft keeps the generic preview, as today.
- **Calendar feeds:** a "Subscribe to this list" link (`webcal://`) for the current filters at the end of the info note on every view (assumed, S3.5), holding listed events only, and "Add to calendar" for each event, in every preview and on any event page a visitor can open, a member-created event's included.
- **JSON-LD:** `Event` markup on both detail page types (eventStatus, location, organizer, offers for LCA events), for listed events only; never on a member-created event's page or a hidden draft's.
- **Reminders** (`tournament_reminders`, extended): the bell on a row, and Remind me in every preview, open a popover (a sheet on phones) with "One week before" and "One day before" ticked by default, plus "Before the early price ends" and "When registration opens" where they apply; partner events offer only the two date reminders and, on the partner page, "If this listing changes". Signed out, the bell still appears on hover and opens a small "Log in to set reminders" card, so the feature is discoverable and nothing is saved without an account. Saved reminders show a filled bell with "Reminders on · 2" and a saved toast. Reminders go by email to the account's address, which can be changed in My LCA, where reminders can also be changed or stopped. Keep today's "Notify me when registration opens" (`functions/api/tournaments/[id]/remind.ts`) as the "When registration opens" option; its POST keeps writing today's pair, an `opens` row and a `week_before` row marked `unregistered_only`, so a subscription made with it gets what it gets today. Saved searches stay: "Email me when new events match these filters" (weekly; listed events only).
- **Live:** with `liveMarker` on (WS07), a listed LCA event with a round being played shows "Live" in words (`StatusBadge` status `live`, its dot static) on its row and in its preview, from `/api/live/now`; never a partner listing or a member-created event.
- **Selected and pressed marks.** The selected row in the List and Table views and today's date in the calendar are marked with a ring in the gold-ink token on a light ground (brand gold only on a dark one), since brand gold on a light ground reads 2.28:1, under the 3:1 a mark that carries meaning needs (today's calendar rings today in brand gold, `src/areas/events/components/MiniCalendar.tsx` line 79 at the end of WS01, which WS01 step 32 left to WS04); the selected row also says so with `aria-current`. Every toggle that shows which of a set is picked (Upcoming · Results, the view switcher, Display's options, the calendar's days, the filter options) takes `FilterChip`'s picked look (the soon tint, a gold-ink line and a check mark) or another mark that reads 3:1 in dark mode, where today's navy fill on the dark card reads 1.17:1, and keeps the `aria-pressed` or `aria-current` WS01 step 32 gave it.
- **Links in words:** a link inside a run of words on the page (the info note's, the rail cards', the Results tab card's) is underlined, never set apart by colour alone (WCAG 1.4.1). WS01 step 32 underlined today's State champions link under `newLook`; with `newTournaments` off, today's page keeps it.
- This replaces the `FEATURES.tournamentQuickFilters` and `FEATURES.externalTags` stubs with the When, Type and Region filter line and the per-row source tag on desktop; there is no Source or Rated chip any more. The new page draws no By organizer column, so `clubTournaments` stays with today's page; the three switches go when today's page is removed after `newTournaments` is turned on.
- **Today's page,** kept while `newTournaments` is off: its two calls, `fetch('/api/clearinghouse?upcoming=true')` and `fetch('/api/clearinghouse?upcoming=false')` (`src/pages/TournamentsPage.tsx` lines 402 and 413 at the end of WS01), become calls in `src/areas/events/api.ts`, and the page leaves `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY` in `test/unit/area-boundaries.test.ts` and its line under Follow-ups in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`. The page file (869 lines) is thinned: it reads the URL and draws today's page, moved whole into a component of its own, or the new views, so no view's markup stays in `src/pages/TournamentsPage.tsx` (WS01's follow-up "Pages to thin in WS05, WS06 and WS08 (step 32)", which names this page for WS04).

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_event_discovery.sql: numbered in landing order after 0054, written with `npm run db:generate` from functions/db/schema.ts
-- (the backfill and the table rebuild by hand, with --custom), and held to the schema by test/unit/schema-drift.test.ts
ALTER TABLE clearinghouse ADD COLUMN slug TEXT;          -- none today (migration 0014); backfill, then unique index
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clearinghouse_slug ON clearinghouse(slug);
ALTER TABLE clearinghouse ADD COLUMN last_seen_at TEXT;
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN slug TEXT;             -- none today (migrations 0001 to 0054)
ALTER TABLE tournament_reminders ADD COLUMN kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'opens'
  CHECK (kind IN ('opens','early_price','week_before','day_before','listing_changed'));
ALTER TABLE tournament_reminders ADD COLUMN sent_at TEXT;   -- one per row, in place of today's two flags and the notified_at column
ALTER TABLE tournament_reminders ADD COLUMN unregistered_only INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;   -- 1: today's week-before rule, sent only while the member has not registered
-- verify: today's table (migrations 0005, 0022 and 0046) has UNIQUE (member_id, tournament_id), the flags sent_registration_open and sent_week_before,
-- and 0022's registration_opened_notified_at.
-- Today's one row carries two sends: the registration-open email (sent_registration_open, registration_opened_notified_at) and the
-- week-before email to subscribers not yet registered (sent_week_before; workers/daily-emails phase 3). The migration writes two rows per
-- existing row, kind 'opens' and kind 'week_before', carrying each flag as that row's sent_at (the notified_at timestamp where there is one,
-- else the migration's own time for a set flag, else NULL), and marks each week_before row it writes unregistered_only = 1, so a member
-- who asked only to hear when registration opens, and has registered since, still gets no week-before email, as today. Today's id is reminder-<member>-<tournament> (functions/api/tournaments/[id]/remind.ts),
-- so the second row needs the kind in its id; the DEFAULT only keeps that insert working and is not the backfill. The unique constraint
-- becomes (member_id, tournament_id, kind), which in SQLite means a rebuild under the existing table name (0.2). tournament_id is TEXT and
-- references tournaments(id), so partner reminders need either a nullable clearinghouse_id column (TEXT) or a source column: verify which, and
-- keep one row per (member, event, kind).
CREATE TABLE clearinghouse_history (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  clearinghouse_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clearinghouse(id),   -- TEXT, as migration 0014 made clearinghouse.id
  changed_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),            -- shown with { stored: 'utc' } (0.2)
  field TEXT NOT NULL, "from" TEXT, "to" TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE saved_searches (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  query_json TEXT NOT NULL,          -- the URL filter state
  cadence TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'weekly' CHECK (cadence IN ('weekly')),
  last_sent_at TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
```

**API**
- `GET /api/events` (new, public): accepts the `from`, `to`, `when`, `type`, `state` and `region` filters and `sort=soonest|name|distance`; `rated` and `source` are not public filters (the merge keeps `source` in every row), and `view` and `event` are client state that is never sent. It merges the two sources into one shape, `{id, source:'lca'|'partner', slug, name, start_date, end_date, city, state, venue, type, rating_system, eligibility, organizer, status, registered_count?, capacity?, price_now?, price_next?, next_price_change?, external_url?}`; `price_now` is the lowest `fee_now` across sections (WS05's "From $X") and `price_next` the fee after `next_price_change`, so a List row can show the next price step. Counts and prices appear only for `lca` rows. Its `tournaments` rows come through `listedWhere`, so it never returns a hidden draft or a member-created event. Edge cache 60 s.
- `GET /api/tournaments` (exists, `functions/api/tournaments.ts`; today's Workspace and Admin pages read it): each branch takes its listed events through `listedWhere`, as above, and it answers no `keep_apart`, `created_by` or `report_settings` to a caller who does not manage the event, as the public-answer trim left it (section 4); its contract leaves them out, with a role-safety contract case.
- `GET /api/events/partner/:slug` (new, public), including the listing's `clearinghouse_history` rows.
- `GET /api/events/:id/entrants` (new, public): per-section entrant names for an LCA event with counts per section; under-18s as first name and last initial, adults by full name, where under 18 means a player with an active guardian link (`members.guardian_id` until WS06 lands, then `member_guardians`) or an entry with a ticked eligibility box (until WS06 adds `eligibility_json`, a non-null `registrations.grade`, which holds the range a ticked box confirmed, migration 0043; after it, `eligibility_json`, which WS06 backfills from that column). Names are written by `publicName(…, 'entrants')` from `domain/households/publicName.ts` (D15, WS01 step 4); whichever of WS04 and WS06 wires it first adds `v`, `2nd` and `3rd` to its suffix list, with a test (WS01 step 4's follow-up). `full=1` returns full names only for `requireTournamentView`, sent `Cache-Control: private, no-store`, as is any answer for an event the public cannot open; only the public answer for an event anyone may open is cached. It answers for any event a visitor can open by its link, a member-created one included, never for a hidden draft to the public, and returns an empty list for partner events.
- On selection the preview loads `GET /api/tournaments/:id` (the existing endpoint; WS05 adds per-section `fee_now`, the one entry price for the date under D11) for the sections and fees, with Register showing the lowest `fee_now` across sections ("From $X", WS05), and `GET /api/events/:id/entrants` for See who's registered. The `/api/events` row alone carries no sections and no fee.
- `GET`, `POST` and `DELETE /api/me/reminders` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): list, save and remove a member's reminders per event; the popover saves all ticked kinds in one call; partner events accept only the two date kinds and `listing_changed`.
- `GET /api/calendar.ics` with the same filters, listed events only, plus `GET /api/tournaments/:id/calendar.ics` (new) for any event a visitor can open.
- `GET`, `POST` and `DELETE /api/me/saved-searches` (new; `requireAuthedMember`).
- `GET /og/tournament/:id.png` and `/og/club/:id.png` (new): generated images.
