- **Contracts** (WS01 step 7): each new JSON endpoint ships with its contract in `domain/contracts`, registered in `registry.ts`, and a contract test in `test/integration/contracts/`. `GET`, `POST` and `DELETE tournaments/[id]/remind` take theirs when the reminder kinds change them, leaving `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts`, with `PENDING_COUNT` lowered to match; `GET clearinghouse` keeps its contract. The two calendar feeds (`text/calendar`) and the preview images (`image/png`) answer with no JSON: they join the excluded list of `test/unit/contract-coverage.test.ts`, each with its reason, and that test's rule that only HTML page routes are excluded widens to name these two media types (the choice WS01 step 7 left to the step that next adds such an endpoint).

**Share previews (how)**
- The event, club and news pages already write their link previews into the head of the site's HTML, one function per route: `functions/tournaments/[id].ts`, `functions/clubs/[id].ts` and `functions/news/[slug].ts`, through `withLinkPreview` in `functions/utils/events/linkPreview.ts` (1.3 named `functions/utils/linkPreview.ts`, which WS01 step 16 moved; it builds the preview metadata, so WS04 extends it rather than adding a second one).
- WS04 extends them: the date line with weekdays from `formatDate`, the entries, the generated image, the Event JSON-LD for a listed event, and, for an event that is not listed, noindex (the meta tag and the `X-Robots-Tag` header) with no JSON-LD. It adds `functions/tournaments/p/[slug].ts` for partner pages and a function for the paths under `/tournaments/:id` (today's matches `/tournaments/:id` only), so the noindex reaches every page of the event; each joins the page routes excluded in `test/unit/contract-coverage.test.ts`, with its HTML reason. The head is the same for people and for link crawlers.
- Generate images with a Workers-compatible renderer. If bundle limits bite, render at publish time and store the PNG in the `CLUB_LOGOS` bucket under `og/`; the PR that does so first binds `CLUB_LOGOS` to `lca-club-logos-preview` under `[env.preview]` (WS09's binding, brought forward), so a preview never writes a picture into the live bucket (0.1 rule 8).
- `/clubs/:id`'s head is WS09's to change when `Club-G` gives each club page its address by name.

**Frontend**
- Change:
  - `src/pages/TournamentsPage.tsx` (thinned: the URL state and the choice between today's page and the new views)
  - `src/areas/events/components/MiniCalendar.tsx` (replaced by `CalendarNavigator.tsx`, or refactored) and `src/areas/events/components/AgendaList.tsx` (it becomes the List view, renamed `ListView.tsx`); WS01 step 19 moved both from `src/components/tournaments/`, where 1.3 placed them
  - `src/areas/events/clearinghouse.ts`, `src/areas/events/api.ts` (today's two calls, then `getEvents`, `getPartnerEvent`, `getEntrants`, the reminders and the saved searches)
  - `functions/tournaments/[id].ts`, `functions/clubs/[id].ts`, `functions/news/[slug].ts` and `functions/utils/events/linkPreview.ts` (the heads above)
- New, in `src/areas/events/components/` (WS01 step 19 puts an area's components there; 1.3 named `src/components/events/`):
  - `EventRow.tsx` (no tag text or bell under 768px), `FilterBar.tsx` (the one line: count, dropdowns, `FilterChip`s, Clear all, Sort), `FilterSheet.tsx` (the phone sheet), `InfoNote.tsx`, `ViewSwitcher.tsx` (the centre segmented control on desktop, handing off to `DisplayMenu.tsx` under 768px), `DisplayMenu.tsx`, `Rail.tsx` (New to tournaments?, Latest results, For organizers), `CalendarNavigator.tsx`, `EventsMap.tsx` (on `LCAMap`, reached through the clubs area's `index.ts` or moved to `src/components` as a part two areas share, never by a deep import, WS01 step 19), `EventsTable.tsx` (on the table primitives), `PreviewPane.tsx` (renders as the pane, the drawer, the side panel or the phone sheet from one component, fetches `GET /api/tournaments/:id` and `GET /api/events/:id/entrants` on selection, and owns the button row so the partner and not-open variants cannot show Register), `RemindMePopover.tsx` (popover on desktop, sheet on phones), `EntrantsList.tsx`, `SeasonAtAGlance.tsx` (with WS11's shared `SeasonSchedule.tsx`), and the component today's page moves into
  - `src/pages/PartnerEventPage.tsx`
  - `src/areas/events/eventFilters.ts` (URL ↔ state, including `sort`, `view` and `event`; 1.3 named `src/lib/eventFilters.ts`, and WS01 step 17's rule places it here)
  - `functions/tournaments/p/[slug].ts`, the function for the paths under `/tournaments/:id`, the endpoints above, and their contracts in `domain/contracts`
- WS01's parts in use: `FilterChip`, `DateBlock` (with WS03's end date), `StatusBadge`'s status path (open, soon, full, waitlist, closed, live, partner and lca, each in words), the table primitives, `ScrollPanel` (the phone sheets and the preview's long sections, D16), the gold `Button` (Register, the only gold button on a row), `LcaMark` (in the LCA tag), and `formatDate`, `formatTime`, `formatTimeControl` and `describeTimeControl` (the plain companion of "G/90+30").

**Jobs & integrations**
- `workers/clearinghouse-sync` keeps `slug` and `last_seen_at` up to date, flags rows that vanish from the feed, writes a `clearinghouse_history` row for every changed field, and queues the `listing_changed` reminders when the diff touches the date, venue, link or cancellation.
- `workers/daily-emails` reworks its existing week-before phase to the `week_before` kind (a row marked `unregistered_only`, which the migration writes for every existing subscription and today's Remind me still writes, keeps today's rule and is skipped once its member has registered; a row saved from the new bell sends to everyone who ticked it) and adds the day-before reminder, the early-price reminder the day before `next_price_change` and the weekly saved-search email (Monday run, listed events only); the existing opens-reminder paths (phase 2 and `functions/utils/registration/registrationOpenNotify.ts`, where WS01 step 16 moved it) handle `opens`, reading `sent_at` in place of the two flags. Recipients come from `recipientsFor` (WS06) once it exists. From Phase 4 every reminder about a member-created event goes through WS08's `markMemberEventEmail` (D27; section 4).
- Google Geocoding (cached) is optional, for map pins on partner events that lack coordinates.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. `/tournaments` opens in List view, grouped by month, with weekday dates; the page band shows Upcoming · Results, the view switcher and the search box in that order; every filter, sort, view and selection change updates the URL and back/forward restores it. There is no Copy link control.
2. The filter-and-sort line reads count, When, Type, Region, applied chips, Clear all, Sort, in that order, on all four views, at 1024px and 1440px; "Clear all" restores the full list; the info note under it names the date range and the count by source.
3. Calendar view:
   - tapping a day scrolls the list
   - multi-day events render as one bar
   - the grid works with arrow keys and has a text alternative
4. Map pins and list rows stay in sync with filters, and selecting either one highlights the other.
5. No partner event shows an LCA Register button or counts. Every clearinghouse row has an internal page, and its exit link opens the organizer's site (`rel="noopener"`).
6. The Facebook Sharing Debugger shows the title, the weekday date line, the city and an image for a tournament URL, with no "Loading…".
7. The `.ics` subscription imports into Google Calendar and Apple Calendar and picks up new events on refresh.
8. JSON-LD passes Google's Rich Results Test for both detail types.
9. Selecting a row at 1280px replaces the rail with the preview pane and Close restores the rail; the pane's buttons read Register · Remind me · Add to calendar · Share · Full event page for an open LCA event, lead with "Remind me when it opens" for one not yet open, and show "Registers on the organizer's site ↗" with no count for a partner event.
10. The bell appears on hover and focus of a row (and never on phones), opens the Remind me popover with one week and one day before ticked, asks for a log in when signed out, offers only the two date reminders on a partner row (the partner page's Remind me adds "If this listing changes"), and shows "Reminders on · N" once saved. Saved reminders appear in My LCA.
11. The public entrants list shows under-18s as first name and last initial and adults in full, grouped by section with counts; a TD or admin sees full names; a partner event has no entrants list.
12. Under 768px the toolbar shows Upcoming · Results, then Display, the live count and Filters · N; Display lists the four views with the current one ticked; Filters opens the sheet with counts and "Show N events"; rows show no tag text and no bell; the preview sheet opens from a row, a pin, a table row and a calendar day with "‹ Back to <view>" at the top left and the button row pinned at the bottom.
13. Table view has no bell column; Date and Event stay frozen when the table scrolls sideways; a picked row carries the selected ring and opens the side panel; below 1280px the panel is a drawer that Esc or Close shuts.
14. A partner page never hides a facts row, reads "Date to be confirmed by the organizer" for an unconfirmed date, shows "Contact the organizer" as the main action when the feed has no link, and keeps the page when the sync is stale or the listing has left the feed.
15. With a published open season, the Tournaments page shows Season at a glance with dated rows and weekdays, each linking to its event page where one exists; with none published, it shows the placeholder and one email field; the Scholastic page's line links to it (checked when WS11 lands, behind `seasonSchedule`).
16. No hidden draft appears on `/tournaments` (any view or tab), in `GET /api/events`, in `/api/calendar.ics`, in the weekly saved-search email or in any Event JSON-LD, and every public query of `tournaments` WS04 adds or changes uses `listedWhere` (`test/unit/listing-rule.test.ts`), every branch of `GET /api/tournaments` included; `GET /api/tournaments` answers none of `keep_apart`, `created_by` and `report_settings` to a caller who does not manage the event; once WS08 adds member-created events (Phase 4), none of those appears there either, nor in WS09's sitemap (D26).
17. Every page of an event that is not listed carries `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` in the head the server writes and an `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` header, and no Event JSON-LD; a member-created event's link preview shows its title and its dates with weekdays and ends with "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event."; a hidden draft keeps the generic preview.
18. The applied chips are `FilterChip`s; removing one moves focus to the next chip, or to the line when none is left; every scrolling menu on the page shows its options' whole focus ring.
19. In the light look and in dark mode, the selected row (List and Table), today's date in the calendar and every toggle that shows which of a set is picked mark it at 3:1 or more against their ground, never in brand gold on a light ground, and say it to a screen reader (`aria-current`, `aria-pressed` or `aria-selected`).
20. Every link inside a run of words on the page is underlined.
21. `src/pages/TournamentsPage.tsx` calls no `fetch` (it is off `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY`) and holds no view's markup; every new JSON endpoint answers to its contract, checked by a contract test; `tournaments/[id]/remind` is off `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` once its three methods have contracts.
22. `/tournaments/p/:slug` joins the AC1 matrix (`PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`), and the matrix reads `/tournaments` in each of its four views.
23. An event of more than one day shows one `DateBlock` tile with both dates and both weekdays in the List, the calendar's list, the map's drawer and the phone rows.
24. With `liveMarker` on, a listed LCA event with a round being played shows "Live" in words on its row and in its preview, and no partner event ever does.

**Tests:**
- Integration `test/integration/events-list.test.ts`: `/api/events` (merge, filters, sort, no counts on partner rows, no hidden draft; a member-created event once WS08 adds them).
- Integration `test/integration/reminders.test.ts`: `/api/me/reminders` (save all kinds, partner events accept only the date kinds and `listing_changed`, signed out is 401).
- Integration `test/integration/reminders-migration.test.ts`: the `tournament_reminders` migration writes an `opens` and a `week_before` row per existing row, carries each sent flag into that row's `sent_at`, and marks each `week_before` row it writes `unregistered_only`; the week-before phase skips such a row once its member has registered, sends a row saved from the bell to a registered member too, and today's Remind me POST writes the marked pair.
- Integration `test/integration/entrants.test.ts`: `/api/events/:id/entrants` (minors abbreviated, an entry marked only by `registrations.grade` among them, `full=1` only for `requireTournamentView`, partner events empty, a hidden draft refused to the public).
- Integration `test/integration/calendar-feeds.test.ts`: `.ics` output for the list and for one event; the list feed holds listed events only.
- Integration `test/integration/link-previews.test.ts` (new; WS01 step 7's follow-up "Page route tests"): the head for a listed event (title, weekday date line, entries, image, Event JSON-LD), a hidden draft (the generic preview and noindex), an unknown id, a partner page, a club and a news post; once WS08 adds them, a member-created event (its preview with the "Not an LCA event" line, noindex on its page and the pages under it, no JSON-LD).
- Integration `test/integration/clearinghouse-sync.test.ts`: `clearinghouse-sync` writes `clearinghouse_history` and queues `listing_changed` only for the date, venue, link and cancellation fields.
- Integration `test/integration/public-feed.test.ts` (extended): `/api/clearinghouse` through the shared query, with no hidden draft; `GET /api/tournaments` signed out, signed in and as an admin, each branch's listed events through `listedWhere`, and none of the three trimmed fields to a caller who does not manage the event, with a role-safety case (criterion 16).
- Contract tests in `test/integration/contracts/` for `events`, `events/partner/[slug]`, `events/[id]/entrants`, `me/reminders`, `me/saved-searches` and `tournaments/[id]/remind`.
- Unit `test/unit/eventFilters.test.ts`: round-trip including `when`, `type`, `region`, `sort`, `view` and `event`.
- Unit `test/unit/public-name.test.ts` (extended): the entrants form, and `v`, `2nd` and `3rd` if WS04 wires `publicName` first.
- Unit `test/unit/listing-rule.test.ts` (WS02's): gains `GET /api/events`, the list feed, the saved-search email and every branch of `GET /api/tournaments`.
- Unit `test/unit/event-row.test.tsx` (new): an event of more than one day as one `DateBlock` tile with both dates and both weekdays (criterion 23), and, with `liveMarker` on, "Live" in words on a live LCA row and in its preview, never on a partner row (criterion 24).
- Unit `test/unit/area-boundaries.test.ts`: `TournamentsPage.tsx` off `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY`.
- Unit `test/unit/contract-coverage.test.ts`: the feeds and images excluded with their media types and reasons.
- a11y `test/a11y/tournaments.spec.ts` (new): the four views at 1280 and 390px; one tile for a multi-day event in the List, the calendar's list, the map's drawer and the phone rows (criterion 23); the calendar grid by arrow keys; focus after a chip is removed; every scrolling menu's option ring; the selected and pressed marks at 3:1 in light and dark; underlined links; the phone toolbar, Display menu, Filters sheet and preview sheet with "‹ Back to <view>"; the Table drawer closing on Esc; and, added by WS11 when it lands, Season at a glance with a published season and with none (criterion 15).
- a11y `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`: `/tournaments/p/:slug` and the four views of `/tournaments` join `PAGES`; the a11y-on build turns `newTournaments` on (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`), and `test/a11y/baseline.json` stays unchanged.
- By hand, recorded in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`: the Facebook Sharing Debugger on a tournament URL (criterion 6), the `.ics` subscription in Google Calendar and Apple Calendar, refreshed after a new event (criterion 7), and Google's Rich Results Test on both detail types (criterion 8).
- a11y `test/a11y/tokens.spec.ts` (extended): the new menus' option ring, light and dark.

**Depends on:** WS01, WS02 (`listedWhere`, and the nav entries for Calendar and This weekend) and WS03 (the shared event query and `DateBlock`'s end date); WS08's `00xx_ticket_kinds.sql` (the ticket kinds, first in Phase 1). WS05 adds per-section `fee_now` for the preview's sections and fees. WS06 widens the entrants rule to `member_guardians`. `liveMarker` waits for WS07's `/api/live/now`. Season at a glance waits for WS11 (`seasonSchedule`, Phase 3). The member-created part is built with WS08's member-created events in Phase 4 (section 4).
**Risks & open decisions:** OG image rendering may exceed Pages Functions size limits; the fallback is images pre-rendered into `CLUB_LOGOS` under `og/`, with the preview binding first (Share previews, above). Drive-time estimates need a routing API, so they're deferred. CSV export for directors moves to the TD tools (WS08, the entrants list), off the public table view (assumed, S3.2). A public query that skips `listedWhere` would show a member-created event to everyone; `test/unit/listing-rule.test.ts` guards each one.

---

## WS05 · Event page template & lifecycle
**Why:**
- On phones, the Register card currently renders after the entire player list.
- US Chess-style shorthand ("TLA") confuses newcomers.
- Information missing at the moment of decision causes abandonment (Baymard).
- The same page has to serve three moments: before an event, during it, and after.
- From the last phase of tournament creation (D20), a member's own event is shared by link with people who may never have heard of LCA, so its page has to say plainly who runs it and that LCA does not (D27).

**Canvas boards:**
- `Event-Final-1`: the unified page with two schedules that merge (signed out, travel details on, side events on, overall limit).
- `Event-Final-2`: the unified page with one schedule and limits by section (signed-in member, plus the club-run inset).
- `Event-Final-States`: six card variants, the family row, the button strips, the during and after tops, the registered sidebar.
- `Event-Final-Family`: the "Who's playing?" picker, the signed-out explainer and the phone picker (WS06).
- `Event-Final-Setup`: the TD setup panel with the new options (feeds WS08's setup checklist, `Work-L`).
- `Event-Partner-Final`: the partner page and its eight human-error states (WS04).
- `Event-Festival-Final-1` and `Event-Festival-Final-2`: the public festival page; the admin panel and the home hero in festival mode (the replan's sections 8.1 and 8.5 call them `Event-Festival-Final` frames A and B; the two names here are the ones in `docs/redesign/decided-boards`).
- `Event-Phones-Final`: the phone frames.
- No board draws a member-created event's page. It is the decided page with the changes under "A member-created event's page" below, and this text is the reference until a board exists.

**User-facing scope**
- **Switches** (0.2's table, each off by default): `newEventPage` turns on the unified page; `festivals` the festival page and `/admin/festivals`; the round alerts card also waits for `roundAlerts`, which WS05 adds to `src/lib/features.ts` (off, with its comment), since the card is the first thing behind it, and WS07's round emails go on with it. With `newEventPage` off, today's page stays as it is, and so does the answer it reads: `GET /api/tournaments/[id]` keeps today's `roster` and `myRegistration` until `EVENT_PAGE_ON` is set (API, below). The one change today's page may see first is the Phase 0 trim's: under-18s shortened in its entries list once `rosterShortNames` is on (assumed, Q38). The parts for member-created events are built with WS08's member-created events in Phase 4 (section 4) and show only on such an event, which exists only once an LCA admin has turned the site setting on (D25).
- **One page for every event run on this site:** LCA-run, club-run and, from Phase 4, member-created (`Event-Final-1`, `Event-Final-2`; the reasoning is in `DESIGN_REPLAN_phase1.md` sections 5 and 6). Its blocks and forms follow the event's setup, never a layout choice:
  - **Top band (from B):** the navy band with the title, dates with weekdays and city, and the key-facts card: days to Round 1 (a plain days figure that changes once a day; nothing ticks), the fill bar, "Register · from $X" and Remind me. For a club-run event the tag reads "Club event" and the organizer line names the club or organizer and its TD; a member-created event carries the line under "A member-created event's page" below in place of any tag.
  - **A status on the band** (open, soon, full, waitlist, closed, live) uses a navy form of `StatusBadge`'s status path, its words beside its colour. This workstream adds that form to `src/components/StatusBadge.tsx` and teaches the gold guard (`test/unit/goldText.test.ts`) to tell it from the light form at each place it is drawn; the tone path's light pills never sit on the band (WS01 follow-up "A status tag on a navy band").
  - **Words on a gold tint** over the navy band are white, as WS01 step 32 set "See pairings / results" under `newLook` (11.43:1); the navy words that read 1.29:1 in today's look do not come back in the rebuilt band.
  - **Sticky sub nav** (`EventSubNav`, from B, on every event): Overview · Sections & prizes · Schedule · Who's coming · Venue & travel · Good to know · Past champions ↗ · Pairings · Standings, addressed by hash (`#overview #sections #schedule #whos-coming #venue #good-to-know #pairings #standings`). Past champions is a link to the honour roll for the event's `championship_title` (WS10), never a block at the bottom, and is absent when the title is empty.
  - **Fact tiles in plain language:** "US Chess rated" · "5 rounds" · "G/90+30 · 90 min each + 30 sec per move" · "½-point byes available" · "Check-in closes 9:30 AM".
  - **Sections and schedule in the form the setup gives them:** with one schedule, A's section cards (rating band, current fee and the date of the next increase, the fullness bar, eligibility in plain words), then schedule and prizes side by side; with two schedules that merge, B's day-by-day schedule (rows tagged 3-day or 2-day, the merge row), then B's sections and prizes. Rounds carry weekday and time. Prizes read "based on N entries".
  - **Entry limits** are overall (the default) or by section. By section, the overall shown is the TD's number if entered, else the sum of the caps; a section with a blank cap is uncapped ("N entered") and counts toward no sum, so with any blank cap and no overall number the page shows the entered total with no cap; with no limit at all the page shows "38 registered" and no bar; a picked section shows its own count and cap with the overall in a smaller second line. Both limits are enforced at checkout (WS06), and the waitlist is per section when limits are by section.
  - **Side events** (child rows of kind `side` or `meeting`, a setup option on or off per event and editable after creation): listed with their own sections, fees, caps and counts and chosen from the same picker as the main sections. The State Championship's business meeting is a side event of kind `meeting` with an RSVP (assumed, S9.6; WS14 links `/meeting` to it).
  - **Who's coming** (from A): one table sorted by rating with the section chips (`FilterChip`) as filters and per-section counts, and no club column anywhere; under-18s show as first name and last initial publicly (D15), on every event, member-created ones included (D27); TDs and admins see full names.
  - **Venue & travel,** one block (`#venue`): the venue (name, address, map, parking, getting in, food), then "Getting here" when the setup's travel details are on (airport, drive times, between rounds), then an optional hotel at the bottom (host hotel, LCA rate, block deadline, booking link).
  - **"Good to know" strip:** rules, byes, refunds, the US Chess membership requirement, a Membership item ("LCA membership required to enter", or "No LCA membership needed" when the event has switched the requirement off; assumed, S11.1), what to bring.
  - Organizer and TD, share, and add to calendar.
- **Sidebar (from A):**
  - **Registration card** (sticky on desktop): the section picker (a segmented control up to four sections and a select above that; assumed, S9.2), the schedule picker where there are two schedules, the fee ladder with dates showing only the tiers the setup has (one line "Entry fee $50" when only Regular exists; no member price anywhere, D11), the count line by limit mode, the membership line by viewer state ("LCA membership required to enter · $15 a year, added at checkout if you don't have one"; an active member sees their expiry instead; the line is absent when the event does not require a membership), "You pay today", **Register** and **Register my family** (WS06), then Calendar · Share · Ask the TD. "From $X" on viewer-neutral surfaces (the top card signed out, the link preview, the phone bar signed out) is the lowest entry price across sections; a signed-in member's surfaces show the price for the picked section.
  - The card's US Chess line before entering shows the expiry with its weekday ("Your US Chess membership ends Tue, Sep 1, 2026"), as the warning after entering already does, and the card says "US Chess" and "US Chess ID" in every message, where today's page still uses the old abbreviation in its Register message and confirmation dialog (WS01 follow-ups from the checkpoint B review).
  - The card's price re-reads the clock every 30 seconds, as WS01 left it, and also when the tab becomes visible again and just before Register opens the flow, so a tier that changed while the page sat open shows before checkout (WS01 follow-up "Prices on a page left open").
  - **Register my family** shows to a signed-out viewer (it opens the explainer sheet "How family registration works", which advertises the Family membership) and to a signed-in member with an active Family membership. Signed-in Adult, Scholastic and Senior members and an expired Family membership do not see it, except a member who co-guards a child covered by another guardian's active Family plan (WS06's rule; assumed, S7.1b); a Family member with no children saved yet sees "Add your family first ›".
  - **Venue & travel summary card:** venue, city, Open in Maps, one parking line, the hotel line when set, and a link to the full block.
  - **Round alerts card** (`RoundAlertsCard`, behind `roundAlerts`): only for a registered viewer or the guardian of a registered player, by email only, with one switch per registered person, read and written through WS07's `GET` and `PUT /api/me/round-alerts`; no SMS and no phone-notification toggle (D8, WS07).
  - **Link preview card:** the event's own link (never a filtered view) with Copy link, Share to Facebook and QR code under it; on a member-created event the preview's description ends with the page's own line (WS04).
- **Phones:** every desktop change applies: the registration card inline under the title, chips for sections and schedules, the single-fee bar, no club lines, the registered sheet with the email switch only, Register my family on a phone. A **sticky bottom bar** ("From $40 · Register": the lowest entry price signed out, the picked section's price signed in) opens the registration sheet; once registered it changes to "Registered · Change section or byes". Padding keeps it from covering content, and the sheet scrolls inside itself (D16). Nothing on the page scrolls sideways at 390 or 320 px (today's page does on a phone, a WS01 finding left for this rebuild).
- **Lifecycle (`Event-Final-States`), computed automatically from dates and live state:**
  - *Before:* the registration focus.
  - *During:* round status, My board, Pairings and Standings in the sub nav (WS07).
  - *After:* final standings, prize winners, recap link, crosstable and photos (WS10).
  - A TD override (`display_state`) covers late sections and edge cases.
- **Layouts:** there is one. The `layout` column is not added; the multi-schedule form is derived from the count of non-archived `tournament_schedules` rows; festivals are their own table (below); `display_state` stays as the TD override.
- **A member-created event's page** (D26, D27; built in Phase 4 with WS08's member-created events):
  - Every block above works as on any event, and once its organizer publishes it the page answers to anyone with its link. It is never listed: WS04 keeps it out of every list, feed and index, and the head the server writes gives every page under `/tournaments/:id` noindex and no Event JSON-LD (WS04 builds that head; this page adds nothing that would index it).
  - Directly under the title: "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." (D27), where <name> is the organizer's full name, read live from their profile, never their email. The event's own content (the band, the key-facts card, the registration card, the phone register bar and its printed sheets) carries no LCA tag, official logo, rook mark or "LCA event" words, and the organizer line names the event's TD; the site's header and footer stay as D1 sets them, with the rook mark and the official logo (assumed, Q12). WS07's live pages and printed kit carry the same line (assumed, Q12).
  - The Good to know Membership item and the card's membership line follow the organizer's own choice (D26): "LCA membership required to enter", or "No LCA membership needed" (S11.1).
  - **Report this event** at the foot of the page (D26): a short form, one text box ("What should LCA's admins know?"), an optional email "in case there's a question" and the spam check (WS09's helper), which reaches the admin queue (WS08). WS07's live pages carry the same link.
  - While the site setting is off (D25), the event's entrants and its organizer, never the public, see one short note: "LCA has paused new member-run events. This event goes ahead as planned. Questions? Contact the LCA admins.", where Contact the LCA admins opens the support form with the event named (wording assumed, Q23).
  - An event an LCA admin has closed (WS08) answers only to its organizer, its entrants and LCA admins, with "This event was closed by LCA's admins." and the same way to reach them; anyone else gets the not-found page (assumed, Q20).
- **Festivals (new):** an admin-created page for big events (`Event-Festival-Final-1` and `-2`), LCA-run events only, never a member-created one: a hero photo, tagline and intro, the festival's events with their dates and Register links, a visiting note, blocks, partners and FAQ. Public page `/festivals/:slug`, live only. Admin panel `/admin/festivals/new` and `/:id` (admin only, under WS08's Content group) with Go live, Take offline and Feature on the home page (disabled while draft; one featured festival at a time; cleared by Take offline or the end date). Featuring pins the Featured rung in the home hero (WS03, `homeFeatured`). No bundle pricing: each event keeps its own fees (assumed, S9.5). Behind `festivals`.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_event_page.sql. Verify each column is not already present: 0052_sections_schedules.sql and 0053 added tournament_sections,
-- tournament_schedules, tournament_schedule_rounds and registrations.section_id and schedule_id, and 0054 added
-- tournaments.requires_lca_membership, all on main since WS01; this migration reuses them rather than duplicates them.
-- Write it with npm run db:generate, adding each table and column to functions/db/schema.ts; a column that carries a foreign key
-- goes in through --custom with its clause written by hand (WS01 follow-up "drizzle-kit and added foreign keys").
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN tagline TEXT;
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN championship_title TEXT;                 -- a slug matching state_champions.title (WS10)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN entry_limit_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'overall' CHECK (entry_limit_mode IN ('overall','by_section'));
-- the second schedule, its time control, rounds and merge_round live in tournament_schedules and tournament_schedule_rounds (0052)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN parent_event_id TEXT REFERENCES tournaments(id);   -- TEXT: tournaments.id is TEXT (migration 0001)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'main' CHECK (kind IN ('main','side','meeting'));
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN display_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto' CHECK (display_state IN ('auto','before','during','after'));
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN venue_notes TEXT;
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN good_to_know_json TEXT;
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN travel_json TEXT;                        -- "Getting here" and the hotel, inside Venue & travel
-- requires_lca_membership is on main since 0054 (default 1; club-run events backfilled to 0), so it is not added again (D12).
-- Per-section caps live in tournament_sections.cap (0052); the setup writes it to the table. max_players stays as the overall limit.

-- The JSON columns go in this migration, 00xx_event_page.sql, whatever an earlier migration adds to tournaments first (WS04's slug,
-- WS08's Phase 1 column) (WS01 follow-up "Retiring the JSON columns"):
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS tournaments_sections_sync_insert;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS tournaments_sections_sync_update;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS registrations_fill_section_insert;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS registrations_fill_section_update;
ALTER TABLE tournaments DROP COLUMN sections;
ALTER TABLE tournaments DROP COLUMN round_schedule;
ALTER TABLE tournaments DROP COLUMN member_discount;   -- unread since WS01 step 15 (D11); memberDiscount leaves the edit request and the contract row with it
-- Before the drop, verify each of these and fix any that fails in the same PR:
--   the reader and writer audits (test/unit/sections-reader-audit.test.ts, sections-writer-audit.test.ts, schedules-audit.test.ts,
--   client-sections-audit.test.ts, registration-readers.test.ts) find no reader or writer of either JSON column;
--   every answer builds sections and round_schedule from the tables (WS01 step 14 keeps round_schedule in answers as the
--   primary schedule's rounds);
--   every registration writer sets section_id (since WS01 steps 10 and 11) and schedule_id itself, since the 0053 trigger
--   fills schedule_id with the primary schedule today;
--   scripts/seed/fixtures.sql, which writes both JSON columns and lets the 0053 triggers build the rows, writes
--   tournament_sections, tournament_schedules and tournament_schedule_rounds itself, so npm run db:seed:preview and
--   test/unit/seed-fixtures.test.ts keep working.
-- If something still reads a JSON column that this PR cannot move, the drop moves to WS08's next migration that changes
-- tournaments (00xx_console.sql, Phase 2), and REDESIGN_STATUS.md says so.

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS site_settings (
  key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  value TEXT NOT NULL,
  updated_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id),
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);   -- verify: no migration creates it as of Thu, Oct 22, 2026; WS05 creates it
INSERT OR IGNORE INTO site_settings (key, value) VALUES ('membership_required_for_all', '0');   -- admin only, off (D12); changed in Admin › Site (WS08)
-- Not added: layout; public_minor_names (the entrants rule in WS04 and D15 replaces it). The member-created marker and the
-- columns for a closed event are WS08's (Phase 4).
```
Existing data to reuse: `time_control`, `registration_status`, `registration_closes_at`, `is_visible`, `end_date`, the sections with their caps, fee tiers (`fee_early`, `fee_regular`, `fee_late`) and prizes in `tournament_sections`, the schedules in `tournament_schedules` and `tournament_schedule_rounds`, and the pricing inputs read by `priceEntry` in `domain/registration/pricing.ts`, with `priceShownSection` for the pages (`functions/utils/pricing.ts` and `src/lib/pricing.ts` re-export them). Since WS01 steps 13 and 15 the helper takes the section, its tier dates and the date, nothing about the viewer, reads no `member_discount`, and `entryPrice` is gone (D11).

```sql
-- 00xx_festivals.sql
CREATE TABLE festivals (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, slug TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, name TEXT NOT NULL,
  start_date TEXT NOT NULL, end_date TEXT NOT NULL, city TEXT, venue TEXT, hero_photo_url TEXT,
  tagline TEXT, intro TEXT, visiting_note TEXT, blocks_json TEXT, visit_json TEXT, partners_json TEXT, faq_json TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'draft' CHECK (status IN ('draft','live','offline')),
  featured INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  created_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id), created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')), updated_at TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE festival_events (
  festival_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES festivals(id), tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  position INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (festival_id, tournament_id)
);
```

**API**
- Extend `GET /api/tournaments/[id]` (`functions/api/tournaments/[id].ts`) with these computed fields:
  - `state` (before/during/after)
  - `entry_limit_mode`, and per-section `entered` / `cap` / `fee_now` / `next_fee_change`
  - `schedules` and `merge_round` (from `tournament_schedules` and `tournament_schedule_rounds`), `side_events[]` (child rows with their own counts and caps), `travel`, `tagline`, `championship_title`, and `requires_lca_membership` as the event's requirement reads through `requiresLcaMembership` (`domain/membership/requirement.ts`) with `membership_required_for_all`
  - `myPeople[]`: everyone the viewer acts for, with each one's entry (WS06), beside today's `myRegistration` until `EVENT_PAGE_ON` is set
  - from Phase 4, for a member-created event: `organizer` (`{ name }`, never an email), `member_event`, `closed` and `creation_paused` (WS08)
  - Today's page reads the answer's `roster` (every entrant's full name) for its entries list, and `myRegistration` for Pay, Withdraw and byes. Both stay as today while `EVENT_PAGE_ON` (`functions/utils/events/eventPage.ts`) is unset, but for the Phase 0 trim's shortened names: once `ROSTER_SHORT_NAMES` is set, `roster` writes each under-18 entrant as first name and last initial for anyone who does not manage the event, while `computeStandings` keeps reading full names (assumed, Q38; section 4). The change that turns `newEventPage` on sets it, with a unit test that the two agree; from then the answer carries no `roster` and no `myRegistration`, and the new page reads its entrants from `GET /api/events/:id/entrants` (WS04), under the D15 rule. The answer's standings and prizes keep full names (D3) either way.
  - It answers `keep_apart`, `created_by` and `report_settings` only to the people who manage the event (WS01 follow-up "Public responses expose personal and internal fields"; the Phase 0 public-answer trim does this here and in `GET /api/tournaments`, section 4, and WS05 keeps it).
  - A closed member-created event answers 404 to anyone but its organizer, its entrants and LCA admins (WS08).
- Pricing: `priceEntry` and `priceShownSection` already take no viewer input (D11, done in WS01). How a section's own Early price applies is settled in WS08's Fees step, in `priceEntry` and `tierFees` alike, before any row has `fee_early`.
- Festivals: `GET /api/festivals/:slug` (public, live only); `GET`, `POST` and `PATCH /api/admin/festivals` and `/:id`, `POST /api/admin/festivals/:id/go-live`, `/take-offline` and `/feature` (`requireAdmin`, audited); `/api/home` reads the featured festival (WS03).
- Admin writes go through the existing `functions/api/admin/tournaments/[id].ts`, using the dirty-diff PATCH pattern (a save writes only the columns it changed), with `requireTournamentManager`; who may change which field is WS08's.
- "Report this event" posts to WS08's `POST /api/tournaments/:id/report`.
- Each endpoint this workstream adds or changes takes its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test, and leaves `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` (0.2).

**Frontend**
- Change: `src/pages/TournamentDetailPage.tsx` (1,121 lines at the end of WS01) becomes a thin page that composes area components and calls area `api.ts` modules; its registration parts go to WS06's `src/areas/registration`, and its old wording and bare dates go with the rewrite (D11's "LCA members save" line is already gone since WS01 step 15).
- Reuse: `PrizeWinners` and `PreviewBanner` (`src/areas/events/components`), `RegistrationReminderButton` (`src/areas/registration/components`), and from WS01 `StatusBadge` with its new navy form and `StatusDot`, `FilterChip`, `DateBlock` (with the end dates WS03 and WS04 add), `ScrollPanel` for the sheets, the table primitives with a sticky first column for Who's coming and the prize table, `ResponsiveImage` for the festival hero, `LcaMark` and `LcaLogo` (never on a member-created event), and `formatDate`, `formatTime`, `formatTimeControl` and `formatScore`.
- New, in `src/areas/events/components/`: `FactTiles.tsx`, `EventSubNav.tsx` (in place of the planned `ChampionshipTabs`), `RegistrationCard.tsx` (the section and schedule pickers, the fee block, the count line by limit mode, the membership line), `RegisterFamilyButton.tsx` (with the explainer sheet), `MobileRegisterBar.tsx`, `SectionsGrid.tsx` and `SectionsAndPrizes.tsx` (the two forms), `ScheduleList.tsx` (one or many schedules, the merge row), `SideEvents.tsx`, `PrizeTable.tsx`, `WhosComing.tsx` (the rating-sorted table with section chips, fed by the entrants endpoint; in place of the planned `EntriesList`), `VenueTravelBlock.tsx` with `TravelBlock.tsx` (the "Getting here" and hotel parts), `VenueTravelCard.tsx` (the sidebar summary), `GoodToKnow.tsx`, `RoundAlertsCard.tsx`, `LinkPreviewCard.tsx`, `LifecycleSwitch.tsx`, and for member-created events `OrganizerLine.tsx` (D27) and `ReportEventLink.tsx`; `src/pages/FestivalPage.tsx` and `src/areas/admin/components/FestivalsPanel.tsx`. `FestivalSchedule.tsx` and `ChampionshipTabs.tsx` are not built.
- `newEventPage` and `festivals` are in `src/lib/features.ts` since WS01; WS05 adds `roundAlerts` (off, with its comment) for the alerts card, and WS07's round emails share it. New on the server: `functions/utils/events/eventPage.ts` with `EVENT_PAGE_ON`, and `functions/utils/siteSettings.ts` (`readSiteSetting(db, key)`), which WS06's checkout and WS08's Admin › Site use.

**Jobs & integrations:** none new. The featured festival needs no job: `/api/home` features it only while today is on or before its `end_date` (WS03), and Feature on the home page clears any other festival's `featured`.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. At 390 px, the price and a Register control are visible without scrolling past the entries list, and the sticky bar never hides content.
2. Every time control shows its plain-language companion.
3. With limits by section, each capped section shows "N of cap" and the date of the next price change; with an overall limit the section cards show "N entered" and no bar; with no limit the page shows the entered count and no bar.
4. Who's coming shows under-18s (an active guardian link, or a marked entry) as first name and last initial and adults in full, from `GET /api/events/:id/entrants`, on every event, member-created ones included; TD, admin and US Chess report views keep full names; results, standings and winners keep full names (D3, D15).
5. The state switches automatically from before to during to after, and the TD override wins.
6. The sub nav is deep-linkable and keyboard accessible on every event; Pairings and Standings are `aria-disabled` before the event.
7. JSON-LD and OG meta (WS04) are present on the page of every listed event; the page of an event that is not listed carries noindex and no Event JSON-LD (WS04's head).
8. The card's fee block shows only the tiers the setup has; with Regular alone it shows one line. No surface shows a member price (D11).
9. Register my family renders only for a signed-out viewer or a viewer who meets WS06's Family rule (own active Family membership, or one held by another guardian that covers a child they guard; assumed, S7.1b); the explainer sheet opens signed out.
10. With two schedules the card and the family picker require a schedule choice and store it on the registration.
11. No club name renders in Who's coming.
12. Round alerts render only with `roundAlerts` on and only for a registered viewer or the guardian of one, with one email switch per registered person and no SMS or phone option.
13. The membership line reads "LCA membership required to enter · $15 a year, added at checkout if you don't have one" signed out or without an active membership, shows the expiry for an active member, and is absent when the event does not require a membership; Good to know's Membership item follows the same rule, and `membership_required_for_all` overrides the event's own switch.
14. A festival page renders only while `status = 'live'`; Feature on the home page is disabled while draft, pins one festival at a time, and is cleared by Take offline or the end date.
15. Nothing on the page scrolls sideways at 390 or 320 px, and `/tournaments/:id` with `newEventPage` on and `/festivals/:slug` with `festivals` on pass the AC1 matrix (`test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`) in every look at 1280 and 390 px, with `test/a11y/baseline.json` unchanged.
16. A status on the navy band is drawn in the status path's navy form with its words, the gold guard tells the navy form from the light one, and every word on a gold tint over the band reads at least 4.5:1.
17. The US Chess expiry before entering shows its weekday, and no message on the rebuilt page uses the old abbreviation for US Chess.
18. A member-created event's page shows "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." under the title, no LCA tag, official logo or rook mark in the event's own content (the band, the key-facts card, the registration card and the phone register bar) while the site's header and footer stay as D1 sets them, and Report this event at its foot; its card and Good to know follow the organizer's membership choice; while the site setting is off its entrants and organizer see the paused note and a visitor does not; once an admin closes it, a visitor gets the not-found page.
19. `GET /api/tournaments/[id]` returns no `keep_apart`, `created_by` or `report_settings` to a viewer who does not manage the event, and never an organizer's email.
20. After the migration no `sections`, `round_schedule` or `member_discount` column and none of the four 0053 triggers remain, every registration written afterwards carries its `section_id` and `schedule_id`, the reader and writer audits pass, and `npm run db:seed:preview`'s file still loads.
21. With `EVENT_PAGE_ON` unset, `GET /api/tournaments/[id]` answers today's `roster` (with under-18s shortened for anyone who does not manage the event once `ROSTER_SHORT_NAMES` is set, Q38) and `myRegistration`, so today's page lists its entries and offers Pay, Withdraw and byes as it does now; with it set the answer holds neither, `myPeople[]` holds everyone the viewer acts for, and the standings keep full names. A unit test holds `EVENT_PAGE_ON` equal to `newEventPage`.

**Tests:**
- Unit: `test/unit/event-state.test.ts` (new): the state computation and the TD override (criterion 5); `test/unit/fee-ladder.test.ts` (new): the fee ladder and next-change logic, with no viewer input (criteria 3 and 8); `test/unit/entry-limits.test.ts` (new): the TD's overall number, the sum of caps and blank caps (criterion 3); `test/unit/registration-card.test.tsx` (new): the fee block's tiers, the membership line and the US Chess line's weekday, with no old initials in any message (criteria 8, 13 and 17); `test/unit/goldText.test.ts` gains the navy status form; `test/unit/event-page-switch.test.ts` (new): `EVENT_PAGE_ON` equals `newEventPage`; `test/unit/features.test.ts`: `roundAlerts` starts off.
- Unit, the page's parts: `test/unit/fact-tiles.test.tsx` (new): every time control with its plain companion (criterion 2); `test/unit/sub-nav.test.tsx` (new): each hash address opens its block, the links work by keyboard, and Pairings and Standings are `aria-disabled` before the event (criterion 6); `test/unit/register-family-button.test.tsx` (new): Register my family for a signed-out viewer and under WS06's Family rule only, and the explainer sheet signed out (criterion 9); `test/unit/whos-coming.test.tsx` (new): under-18s shortened, adults in full and no club name anywhere in the table (criteria 4 and 11); `test/unit/round-alerts-card.test.tsx` (new): the card only with `roundAlerts` on and only for a registered viewer or the guardian of one, one email switch per registered person, and no SMS or phone option (criterion 12).
- Unit: `test/unit/migration-event-page.test.ts` (new): the columns added, the JSON columns, `member_discount` and the four triggers gone, the `site_settings` row present; `test/unit/seed-fixtures.test.ts` (extended): the seed file loads on the migrated tables.
- Integration `test/integration/tournament-detail.test.ts` (new): `/api/tournaments/[id]` with `EVENT_PAGE_ON` unset answers `roster` and `myRegistration` as today, and with it set answers neither and gives `myPeople[]`, with full names in its standings (criterion 21); none of the trimmed fields to a viewer who does not manage the event, with a role-safety case (criterion 19). The entrants endpoint rule and `full=1` gating are WS04's `entrants.test.ts`.
- Integration: `requires-membership.test.ts` (extend): `requires_lca_membership` on and off, and `membership_required_for_all` overriding it.
- Integration: `festivals.test.ts` (new): a draft page is 404 publicly; one featured at a time; Take offline clears featured.
- Integration: `registration-section-ids.test.ts` (extend): a registration written after the triggers are gone carries its section and schedule.
- Integration, Phase 4: `member-event-page.test.ts` (new): the organizer's name and no email in the answer, the paused flag for entrants only, the closed event's 404.
- Unit, Phase 4: `test/unit/member-event-page.test.tsx` (new): the "Organized by" line under the title, no LCA tag, official logo or rook mark in the band, the cards or the phone register bar, with the site's header and footer as D1 sets them, Report this event at the foot, and the card and Good to know following the organizer's membership choice (criterion 18).
- Browser: `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` gains the rebuilt page and the festival page, with `newEventPage` and `festivals` on in the a11y-on build (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`) and `test/a11y/baseline.json` unchanged; `test/a11y/event-page.spec.ts` (new) checks no sideways scroll at 390 and 320 px, the price and a Register control in view at 390 px without scrolling past the entries list, with the sticky bar covering no content (criterion 1), and the band's contrast in every look.
- Extend `lifecycle.test.ts`.

**Depends on:** WS01; WS04 (share meta, and the server-written head that gives noindex); WS06 (the sheet opened by Register and the family model behind Register my family: WS05's page lands first with today's registration in its card, and WS06 swaps in the sheet, section 4); WS07 (the during state, and the round alerts endpoints behind `roundAlerts`); WS08 in Phase 4 (the member-created marker, closing an event and the queue Report this event reaches), with WS09's spam check for Report this event. The festival page and `/admin/festivals` ship with WS05 and feed WS03's Featured rung (`homeFeatured`).
**Risks & open decisions:**
- TDs have to enter more data per event (caps, price dates, venue notes); WS08's setup checklist makes this a one-step-at-a-time job.
- Dropping the JSON columns cannot be undone by a later migration. The PR says so, and K migrates the preview database from the branch and checks the event pages before the merge migrates production (0.2).

---

## WS06 · Registration & households
**Why:** Baymard puts average checkout abandonment at about 70%:
- Unexpected extra costs cause 40% of it.
- Forced account creation causes 18% (24% in Baymard's 2022 survey).
- The average checkout has 11.3 fields where 8 are enough.

GOV.UK's pattern is to ask eligibility first, end with "check your answers", and confirm with "what happens next". One checkout for a whole family is LCA's edge over KingRegistration. A parent registering children without logins is a real need K has named. And once members run their own events (D26), the people who get those links must be told, before they pay or enter, that the event is not run or rated by LCA and where their details go (D27).

**Canvas boards:**
- `Reg-C`: the household checkout, the core model, decided with the family model below; its decided board is drawn when WS06 starts, so until then build from `Reg-C`, `Event-Final-Family` (the picker) and the picker content below.
- `Reg-A`: the in-card flow on desktop, the same engine's second presentation.
- `Reg-B`: one question per screen on phones, the third presentation.
- `Reg-D`: guest entry.
- `Event-Final-Family`: the "Who's playing?" picker, the signed-out explainer and the phone picker.
- `Account-I` and `Account-I-2`: page 14's Family section (picked Mon, Oct 19, 2026), for the family parts, in place of v1.3's `Account-B`; WS13 builds the section and renders this workstream's `GuardiansPanel`.
- No board draws a member-created event's checkout; it is `Reg-C` with the lines under "Member-created events" below.

**User-facing scope**
- **Switches** (0.2's table, each off by default): `householdCheckout` turns on the household checkout, the "Who's playing?" picker, Register my family and the family surfaces in My LCA (WS13); with it on, every Register and Register my family control, today's event page included, opens the household checkout. `membershipRequirementSwitch`, in `src/lib/features.ts` since WS01 step 15, shows the "LCA membership required" switch on a club-run event's Registration tab; it may go on only once this checkout enforces the requirement (acceptance criterion 15). A server change that alters what an existing endpoint accepts or answers, or the emails a payment sends, follows `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` in `functions/utils/registration/checkout.ts`, a constant set in the same change that turns `householdCheckout` on, with a unit test that the two agree (functions cannot read `src/lib/features.ts`; WS09's `CLUB_PAGES_ON` is the same pattern). The parts for member-created events are built in Phase 4 with WS08's member-created events (section 4).
- **Household model:** a household is a guardian plus the player profiles they guard. Each child is a `members` row (`child-<uuid>`, kept for life) linked through `member_guardians` (an owner plus any number of guardians, each with their own login). Links end with `ended_at` and `ended_reason` and are never deleted. Children need no email or login. A login can be attached later with `members.auth_user_id`, from age 13, with recorded guardian consent. A second guardian is in scope now, not later. Build on today's children (`functions/api/me/children.ts`, `children/[id].ts`, `functions/utils/family.ts`, `src/areas/households/components/FamilyRegistrationPanel.tsx`, `FamilyCard.tsx`); the model is set out under "Family accounts: one player, one profile" below.
- **One registration engine, three presentations:** the household checkout `/tournaments/:id/register` (Reg-C, the core), the in-card flow on the event page on desktop (Reg-A) and the full-height one-question sheet on phones (Reg-B). The guest path (Reg-D), the waitlist, holds and refunds stand.
- **"Register my family" visibility:** the button shows to signed-out visitors, where it opens "How family registration works" with a sample picker and a one-form path that creates the parent's login and first child, and to signed-in people with an active Family membership, their own or one held by another guardian that covers a child they guard (the co-guardian case; assumed, S7.1b). Everyone else sees "Register", whose "Who's playing?" step still lists saved children, so no parent is stranded.
- **Who's playing picker** (step 1; the picker's full content is set out below, after the family flows): tick players, pick an eligible section or side event per player (each option shows its fee and entered count only, never a member price), choose a schedule per player where there are two, check each name (the entrants-list form and the results form), and tick a required "These names are spelled the way they should appear on pairings and results". A running total updates live. A child already entered by another guardian is locked with "Already registered by Marc R. for K-8". Edit name locks once the child is checked in to a running event. More than 9 players gets a plain second-order message. One `registration_orders` row spans the main event and its side events.
- **Steps:**
  1. **Who's playing?** the picker above: me, saved children, add a family member inline (first name, last name, optional US Chess ID; no email), or someone else (guest); a section or side event per person, the eligibility checkbox before any scholastic section, a schedule per person where there are two.
     - **Section rules, per player:** only eligible sections are selectable, using `sectionRules.ts`, ratings and playing-up rules. A "Which section fits me?" helper is available.
     - **Eligibility per entry:** "In 8th grade or below?" is asked on each entry, never on the profile; the checkbox leaves the Add-a-child form. Where it is stored and where the check lives are under "The eligibility check" below.
  2. **Byes and US Chess IDs:**
     - **Byes:** checkboxes per round, up to one fewer than the number of rounds, respecting the half-point bye rules. Bye rounds are whole numbers (`byeRoundsSchema` in `domain/contracts/registration.ts` becomes `z.array(z.number().int())`), and the bye checks run before the capacity check (WS01 follow-up from step 10).
     - **US Chess:** inline lookup (`uscf/lookup`) autofills name, rating and expiry. If the membership expires before the event: "Your US Chess membership ends Mon, Nov 30, before this event. Renew →".
  3. **LCA membership:** for each player the step calls `needsMembershipAtCheckout(player, tournament, { forceAll })` from `domain/membership/requirement.ts` (built in WS01 step 15 for this checkout), with `forceAll` read from the `membership_required_for_all` row of `site_settings` (WS05 creates the table; WS08 builds its Admin control); it is skipped when no player needs one. For each such person the step offers the tier that applies (Adult, Scholastic for a K-12 player, Senior at 65 or older, or Family when it covers the household more cheaply), using the household optimizer to pick the cheapest valid combination (the pricing rule WS12's page shows; WS06 builds it beside `domain/membership/tiers.ts`, since WS12 comes later, section 4): Family covers the guardian and up to 3 children, and a 4th child is added with a Scholastic membership ($5) in the same payment. The membership is paid in the same payment as the entries (D12). Which tier fits is derived without asking for age or grade, since LCA stores no age, grade or birthdate: a player with an active guardian link (a child profile) gets Scholastic; an adult is asked whether they are 65 or older, as `/membership` asks; Family is offered when it covers the household more cheaply (the WS12 optimizer).
  4. **Check your answers:** one line per player at the price paid, one total. The price shown is the price paid, with no separate card-fee line (D2); the Reg-C board's "Card processing" line is dropped.
  5. **Pay:** Stripe Checkout in the same tab.
  6. **Confirmation:**
     - One confirmation card per player with its own Withdraw link.
     - What happens next: check-in time, add to calendar, a manage link, how to request a bye change.
     - One receipt to the payer and one confirmation email per player, through the receipt system below.
- **Every way in enforces the membership.** Once `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` is set, today's `functions/api/registrations.ts` and `functions/api/registrations/batch.ts` refuse an entry that `needsMembershipAtCheckout` says needs a membership, with "An LCA membership is needed to enter this event. Register from the event page, which adds it in the same payment.", until nothing calls them and they are retired; the walk-in desk sells the membership in person (WS08, WS12). Only then may `membershipRequirementSwitch` go on (WS01 follow-up "Enforcement at checkout").
- **Teen self-entry:** a child with her own login can choose "Ask my guardian to pay" at Check your answers, which creates an unpaid held entry that shows as rank 1 in each guardian's Today (WS13); a guardian pays in one tap. If a guardian has already entered her, her own attempt is blocked with "Already registered by Danielle R. for U1400".
- **Guest entry (Reg-D):**
  - No account needed, at most 8 fields, and a US Chess ID autofill.
  - The manage link is emailed with a signed token.
  - After payment: "Save these details, create an account", prefilled. Accepting it turns guest child rows into managed profiles under the new login, so results stay attached and no merge is needed. A US Chess ID typed in Add a child converts a guest row only when the guest entry's payer email is the signed-in parent's.
- **Entrants list:** first name and last initial while the player has an active guardian link or the entry is marked (its `eligibility_json` is set). Pairings, standings, results and winners show full names (D3, D15). WS04's entrants endpoint calls `publicName(player, 'entrants')` from `domain/households/publicName.ts` (built in WS01 step 4 and unused until now), and every results surface calls it with `'results'`; the public event endpoint answers no roster once `EVENT_PAGE_ON` is set (WS05), and its standings keep full names; its suffix list gains `v`, `2nd` and `3rd`, with a test, unless WS04 wired it first (WS01 follow-ups "Minor names on the entrants list" and "Name suffixes").
- **The eligibility check** (where it lives, a WS01 follow-up): today's `GradeConfirm` (`src/areas/events/components/GradeConfirm.tsx`, with `gradeBoxes`, `confirmedRange` and `NO_TICKS` in `src/areas/events/gradeConfirm.ts`) moves to the registration area, as `src/areas/registration/components/EligibilityCheck.tsx` and `src/areas/registration/eligibility.ts`, exported through `@/areas/registration`; the picker uses it, and the family panel's deep import into the events area ends (taken off `CROSS_AREA_TODAY` in `test/unit/area-boundaries.test.ts`). The server keeps checking with `eligibilityProblem` in `domain/events/sectionRules.ts`. The tick is stored per entry and never on the profile: the confirmed range stays in `registrations.grade` (migration 0043), which prizes read, and `registrations.eligibility_json` records the ticked boxes, backfilled from `grade` for existing entries, so the entrants rule reads one column.
- **Emails:** every sender uses `recipientsFor(memberId)` (`functions/utils/recipients.ts`), grouped per address. Receipts go to the payer only.
- **Receipts, one system for every payment** (D14): today no LCA receipt exists (the only post-payment email is "You're registered", memberships and donations send nothing, and Stripe's own receipt goes out separately). For every completed payment (registration orders, memberships, donations, walk-in card payments) the site sends **one receipt to the payer** ("Receipt: 2027 Paul Morphy Open, 3 players · $87.00": one line per player or item with its amount, the date with weekday, the last four digits of the card, the Stripe reference, a link to the order in My LCA, and the refund rule in one line) plus **one confirmation per player** ("You're registered: …", the existing template, grouped per recipient as today, without the Paid total, which the receipt carries). Stripe's email receipts are turned off in the Stripe dashboard so nobody gets two. The confirmation for a mixed order (free and paid entries) is sent once, when the order completes, not once for the free entries and again at the webhook. Receipts can be re-sent from My LCA ("Send me this receipt again") and from the admin's payment row. All of this follows `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON`: while it is unset, the webhook sends what it sends today ("You're registered" with its Paid total, and nothing for a membership or a donation) and Stripe's own receipts stay on; the change that sets it, with `householdCheckout`, starts LCA's receipt for every payment and takes the Paid total out of the confirmation, and K turns Stripe's email receipts off in the Stripe dashboard the day that change deploys, so nobody gets two receipts, or none.
- **Waitlist:**
  - When a section is full, join the waitlist; the waitlist is per section when the event's limits are by section (WS05). A section's cap (`tournament_sections.cap`, stored since WS01 step 8 and checked by nothing until now) sends that section's next entry to its waitlist, while the other sections keep taking entries and the overall limit still holds.
  - When a spot opens, the next person gets a claim link valid for 24 hours. If it expires, the offer moves to the next person automatically.
- **Holds:** starting checkout holds the seats for 15 minutes so they can't be oversold.
- **Schedules:** with two schedules each entry carries the schedule chosen for it. The order writes `registrations.schedule_id` itself, as it writes `section_id`, so nothing depends on the 0053 trigger that fills the primary schedule today (WS05's migration drops it; WS01 follow-up "Schedules in WS06 and WS08").
- **Duplicate guard:** the same US Chess ID or name in the same event and section, and an entry already made for the same `member_id` by another guardian.
- **Limits:** one order holds up to 9 players (`MAX_ENTRIES` in `functions/api/registrations/batch.ts`): "One order holds up to 9 players. Pay for these 9, then start a second order for the rest." A family list holds up to 8 profiles (`MAX_CHILDREN`, today in `functions/api/me/children.ts`): the add link is disabled with "Your family list is full (8)". The server refuses a 9th profile and a 10th player.
- **Scholastic emergency contact** (assumed, S7.9): asked once on the owner's account (`Account-I`; verify the board's field) in `members.scholastic_emergency_contact_json` (`00xx_player_profiles.sql`), reused on each child's scholastic entry (`registrations.emergency_contact_json`), shown only to that event's TD and never on public pages.
- **No financial questions (D5):** LCA doesn't run fee waivers or ask about need or lunch status. Drop the lunch-status discount shown on the Event-A and Schol-A option boards.
- **Refunds:** per player, even when several players were paid in one order.
- **Section changes:** moving an entry to another section charges or refunds the difference, `priceEntry` for the new section less `priceEntry` for the old one, each with the tiers that applied when the entry was made, in place of today's reset of the pending payment to the new section's regular fee (WS01 follow-up from step 10), once `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` is set; with it unset a section change prices as today.
- **Member-created events** (D26, D27; Phase 4, with WS08):
  - The checkout's heading carries "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." under the event's name.
  - Before Pay, or before Register when nothing is due, the form tells entrants in plain words: "This event is organized by <name>. It is not run or rated by LCA. If you register, each player's name and US Chess ID, and the email we use for them (a parent's or guardian's for a child), go to <name>." It is a notice with no box to tick (wording assumed, Q23).
  - Every email WS06 sends about the event (confirmations, waitlist offers, the manage link) goes through WS08's `markMemberEventEmail` (section 2.2), which puts "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." under its heading and makes the organizer's email its reply-to (`replyTo` in `functions/utils/email.ts`); WS04's reminders, WS07's round emails, WS08's announcements and WS15's scanner emails go through the same helper.
  - Entry lines are $0 until the organizer has a connected account (D24; none until Q3); an order on such an event charges only an LCA membership line, when the organizer requires one (D26), which is LCA's and gets LCA's receipt.
  - Under-18s show as first name and last initial on its pre-event lists, as everywhere (D15).
  - While the site setting is off (D25) the checkout shows the same short note as the event page (WS05); a closed event (WS08) takes no new entries.
- **Plain words in what the endpoints answer:** the registration endpoints' messages and Stripe line names lose their dashes ("Only 2 spots left, fewer than the 3 players you selected"; "2027 Paul Morphy Open · Leo Robichaux (U1400)"; today in `batch.ts`, `registrations.ts` and `registrations/[id].ts`), and the lookup's refusal in `functions/api/uscf/lookup.ts` says "US Chess ID" (WS01 follow-up from step 10). They follow `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON`: with it unset the endpoints answer today's words, since a changed word is a visible change (0.2).
- **Registration ids** take a random part (`reg-<tournament>-<random>`), so two players entering in the same millisecond never collide (WS01 follow-up "Ids that can collide").

**Family accounts: one player, one profile** (the model behind WS06, WS07, WS08, WS12 and WS13; `DESIGN_REPLAN_phase1.md` section 7 has the reasoning)
- **Identity.** Each person who plays has exactly one player profile, a `members` row whose id never changes: children keep `child-<uuid>`, adults keep their Supabase id. Registrations, payments, pairings, rating at entry, results, reminders and the card QR all point at that id, so nothing moves when a login is added or a guardian changes. A profile is identified by its US Chess ID when it has one (unique across non-guest profiles), then by its full name plus its household (TDs see "Robichaux family · Danielle Robichaux"), then by the per-profile QR on the digital card. Email is not part of a player's identity; it belongs to a login.
- **Logins.** A login is attached to a profile through `members.auth_user_id`. Adults need no change: when `auth_user_id` is empty, sign-in falls back to today's rule, where the member id equals the Supabase id. A child has no login until a guardian gives her one, offered only after the guardian ticks "Maya is 13 or older" and a consent checkbox, both saved with a timestamp (assumed, S7.2); she sets her own password from the invite and her existing row gets the login. A guardian can remove the login while she is under 18, and her history stays.
- **Guardians.** `member_guardians` links a profile to every adult who can act for it. One link is the owner, also kept in `members.guardian_id` so today's queries keep working; the others are guardians with their own logins. Links are never deleted; they end with a date and a reason (`left`, `removed`, `handed_over`, `turned_18`), so there is a record if households disagree. The owner can invite or remove guardians and hand ownership to another guardian (only the owner removes; co-guardians can only leave; assumed, S7.6). A guardian can leave. The last active guardian of a child with no login cannot leave. Co-guardians see each other as first name and last initial.
- **Duplicates, with no volunteer work.** A request to be added as a guardian never confirms that a named child exists: it answers "Request sent", goes to the owner and is never granted automatically. A US Chess ID already on LCA blocks a second profile and offers the request instead. History moves between two rows only when they are provably the same person (the names match, the US Chess IDs do not conflict, the row being absorbed has no children and no staff roles, and the two rows are not both entered in the same event); every move is written to `profile_moves` so a developer can reverse it; an empty self-made row is simply absorbed; anything else is refused with a plain message and never goes into a volunteer queue (assumed, S7.5).
- **Family plan.** On each guardian link the guardian ticks which children their plan covers (`family_covered`), up to 3, chosen at purchase and at renewal; an open spot can be filled at any time, and the ticks otherwise change at renewal (assumed, S7.8). With two households each guardian's plan can cover the child, and the later expiry wins. A fourth child is offered Scholastic $5 in the same checkout (WS12).
- **Notifications.** `recipientsFor(db, memberId)` decides who gets each email: the player's own login email if she has one, plus every active guardian with copies on; a guardian's address is read live from their own row; while a child has no login at least one guardian keeps copies on; mail is grouped per address, so a parent of three gets one email per send; receipts go only to the payer; if a teen's address bounces, her mail goes to her guardians and her card shows "Maya's email isn't receiving mail · Update". Texts follow the same rule when SMS exists (D8).
- **Display and data minimum.** On the pre-event entrants list a player shows as first name and last initial while any guardian link is active or the entry ticks a scholastic eligibility box; pairings, standings, results and winners show full names (D15). LCA stores no birthdate, grade or age, and no child email unless the child takes a login at 13 or older.

**Family flows** (`DESIGN_REPLAN_phase1.md` section 7.3 has the full copy)
- **Add a child** (My LCA > Family > "Add a family member", also inline in step 1 of the checkout): first name, last name, US Chess ID (optional) with "Look up by name" (`/api/uscf/lookup`, which shows the US Chess spelling: "US Chess has: ROBICHAUX, LEO · Use this spelling"); no email, grade or birthday; helper "Leo gets his own player profile. He doesn't need an email. You can give him his own login when he is 13 or older."; button "Add to the family"; the line under it shows the plan spot ("Your Family plan covers 3 children · 1 spot left", or that a 4th child is added with a Scholastic membership). `POST /api/me/children` inserts the `members` row as today plus one `member_guardians` row (kind `owner`, `family_covered` 1 if a spot is open). A name already in this family returns 409 as today ("You already have a Leo Robichaux in your family"). A US Chess ID already on a non-guest profile creates nothing and says "This US Chess ID is already on an LCA player profile. If this is your child, ask to be added as a guardian. The family that added it can approve you." with the button "Send request"; after sending, the parent sees only "Request sent", and a matching name with no ID reveals nothing. A US Chess ID matching a walk-in guest row becomes this child only when that guest entry's payer email is the parent's signed-in email. The child card appears with "No login · emails come to you".
- **Buy a Family membership** (WS12): the "Who's covered?" screen, the 4th child as Scholastic $5 in the same payment, no child email, and the success page listing who is covered.
- **Register my family from an event page:** the picker, then Byes, US Chess, LCA membership (only if someone needs one), Check your answers and Pay. One `registration_orders` row with `payer_member_id` set to the signed-in guardian and one `registrations` row per player (`player_kind` `dependent` for children) with `eligibility_json` saved per entry. One confirmation card per player with its own Withdraw link, and one grouped email per recipient.
- **A parent's view of a child:** no account switching. My LCA > Family shows one card per child and the filter chips "Everyone · Maya · Leo"; "Open Maya's page" goes to `/me/players/:memberId`, the same component Maya sees on her own login (entries, byes, pairings, results history, reminders, card QR) with the strip "Maya Robichaux · you manage this profile". On event days the Family day view shows each child's round, board, colour, opponent and result side by side, from `GET /api/me/event-mode` `players[]` read from `member_guardians` (WS02, WS13). On the event page the WS07 "My players" strip reads "Maya: Board 4, White · Leo: Board 12, Black", and `myPeople[]` covers everyone the viewer acts for. Reminder and round-alert choices are set per child on the child's page and owned by the parent.
- **A child later gets her own login:** "Give Maya her own login" asks for her email, a required "Maya is 13 or older" checkbox and a required "I'm Maya's parent or guardian and I agree she can have her own LCA login" checkbox (both saved with a timestamp; no age stored), then "Send Maya an invite" (single use, valid 14 days) or "Copy invite link". Maya gets "Danielle set up your LCA login" with "Set my password". On accept: a new login sets `auth_user_id` on her existing row; an existing login with no registrations or payments is absorbed; one with history passes the same-person check and offers "Move your 4 past events onto this profile" with a Confirm button (logged in `profile_moves`), or is refused with "This email already belongs to a different LCA player. Use another email" and nothing changes. `members.email` becomes her address and `claimed_at` is set. The card then reads "Maya has her own login · since Sat, Oct 10" with a "Send me copies" toggle (on by default) and "Remove Maya's login".
- **A teen with her own login:** her own My LCA with her entries, pairings, results, reminders and card; she can register herself and choose "Pay now" or "Ask my guardian to pay"; she cannot add children, remove guardians or see guardians' receipts, card details or siblings' profiles. Each guardian can turn off their own copies; she cannot turn guardians' copies off before she manages her own profile (assumed, S7.3).
- **A second guardian and two households:** Family > Guardians > "Invite a guardian" (their email, the children ticked, all by default); accepting adds a `member_guardians` row (kind `guardian`) per child, and they can register, pay with their own card, see the day view, get their own copies and tick their own Family plan to cover the child. The other route in is "Send request" from Add a child: the owner gets "A parent or guardian asks to be added for Leo. Approve / Decline", and on approval a duplicate profile the requester made passes the same-person check and offers "Move Leo's 2 entries onto this profile". The owner can remove a guardian (`removed`) or hand over ownership; a guardian can leave; the last active guardian of a child with no login is blocked with "Invite another guardian or give Leo his own login first."; if the owner closes their login, the longest-standing guardian becomes owner. The pairing engine's `keep_apart` 'family' means sharing any active guardian.
- **Turning 18:** LCA never stores a birthday, so a person makes the change. The owner's "Maya is 18: hand over her profile" takes effect at once (`handed_over`). Maya's own "I'm 18 or older: manage my profile myself" in Settings takes effect after 7 days; her guardians are emailed at once with a "This isn't right" link, and if any guardian cancels, a second request needs the owner's approval (assumed, S7.4). Either route needs her own login first, so the button reads "Give Maya her own login first" until she has one.
