On release every guardian link gets an `ended_at`, `guardian_id` becomes NULL, paid coverage runs to its expiry, and her Family spot opens at the next renewal ("Maya now manages her own profile · Adult $15"); guardians get one email, "Maya now manages her own LCA profile." From then on her full name shows on entrants lists and email goes only to her.
- **A TD looking up a child** and **a walk-in child becoming a profile** are in WS08's check-in.

**The "Who's playing?" picker, content** (`Event-Final-Family`; the replan's section 7.5 as amended by its sections 11 and 12)
- **Event page action area.** Signed out: button "Register my family" with the line "One checkout for you and your children. See how it works", opening the sheet "How family registration works" with the picker filled with the sample Robichaux family and the buttons "Get a Family membership · $25 a year" (the explainer line under it reads exactly "Everyone on the plan gets a LCA membership"), "Start: create your login" and the link "I already have a login: sign in". Signed in and meeting the Family rule above: "Register my family". Everyone else signed in: "Register", whose first step is the same picker.
- **Page** `/tournaments/:id/register`. Heading "Who's playing?"; subheading with the event, dates with weekdays and city ("2027 Paul Morphy Open and Morphy Scholastic · Sat, Jun 12 to Sun, Jun 13, 2027 · New Orleans"); intro "Tick everyone who is playing, choose a section for each, and check each name. Names appear on pairings and results exactly as shown here." On a member-created event the D27 line and notice above sit under the subheading.
- **One row per person the viewer can register,** everyone unticked at first. A row shows the name, a detail line ("Rating 1102 · US Chess ID ending 7731 · On your Family plan"), the public-name line ("Entrants list before the event: Maya R. · Pairings and results: Maya Robichaux"), Edit name, and once ticked the required Section select, whose options show the fee and the entered count only ("Morphy Scholastic K-8 · Sat only · $25 · 9 of 48 entered"; side events in the same select, so a child can be placed in the Scholastic from the picker). A hint under the select names the sections not shown and why ("Not shown: U1000 (1102 is over 1000). Show K-5, K-3, U1800 and Open"). When a scholastic section is chosen its eligibility checkbox appears ("Maya is in 8th grade or below (required for this section)") with the help "One yes or no. We never ask for a grade or a birthdate." With two schedules, a Schedule choice follows the section.
- **Row states.** Locked: "Already registered by Marc R. for K-8" (the checkbox is disabled). Edit name locked: "Checked in. Ask the director to fix the name." Not covered: "Not on your Family plan. An LCA membership (Scholastic $5) is added in step 3." (shown only when the event requires a membership; assumed, S11.2). 10th person: "One order holds up to 9 players. Register the rest in a second order."
- **Under the list:** the link "Add a family member" (first name, last name, US Chess ID optional; no email), with the plan line ("Your Family plan covers 3 children · 1 spot left"; when the plan is full, that a 4th child is added with a Scholastic membership, still allowing the add; disabled at 8 profiles with "Your family list is full (8)"). Note under it: "Only the Family membership is shared. Each player pays their own section's entry fee."
- **Name confirmation** (required): "These names are spelled the way they should appear on pairings and results." Error if left unticked: "Check the names, then tick the box to confirm them."
- **Running total** (sticky on phones, a side panel on desktop; updates live): one line per player ("Maya · Morphy Scholastic K-8 · $25"), the count and total ("2 players · $50"), and the footer "Entry fees only. Anyone without an LCA membership gets one added in step 3. No card fees are added at payment."
- **Continue:** "Continue with 2 players · $50", disabled until each ticked player has a section, any eligibility box their section needs and a schedule where there are two, and the names box is ticked; helper "Choose a section for Leo to continue." Next: byes, the US Chess check, LCA membership when someone needs one, then Check your answers.
- **Scrolling:** on desktop the picker and the family panel sit in `ScrollPanel` (WS01) with the total beside them, each control keeping at least 4px of room for its focus ring (WS01 follow-up "ScrollPanel on the long screens"); on phones the picker scrolls inside the sheet. The sticky total never covers a focused control (D16), and nothing scrolls sideways at 390 or 320 px.
- **Phones:** the options show no price or count under the selector (they are in the sheet that opens); the detail line under each name is gone and the one essential sits beside the name ("Maya Robichaux · 1102", "Leo Robichaux · unrated"); the public-name line lives in the Edit name sheet.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_registration_orders.sql  (verify registrations' current columns first: bye requests, waitlist, check-in (checked_in_at),
-- withdrawn_at and grade (the confirmed range, migration 0043) exist)
CREATE TABLE registration_orders (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),   -- TEXT: tournaments.id is TEXT (0.2)
  payer_member_id TEXT REFERENCES members(id),      -- null for guests
  payer_email TEXT NOT NULL,
  stripe_session_id TEXT UNIQUE,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending' CHECK (status IN ('pending','paid','expired','refunded','partially_refunded')),
  subtotal_cents INTEGER NOT NULL, total_cents INTEGER NOT NULL,   -- all-in pricing, no card-fee line (D2)
  hold_expires_at TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
ALTER TABLE registrations ADD COLUMN order_id INTEGER REFERENCES registration_orders(id);
ALTER TABLE registrations ADD COLUMN player_kind TEXT CHECK (player_kind IN ('member','dependent','guest'));
ALTER TABLE registrations ADD COLUMN guest_name TEXT;
ALTER TABLE registrations ADD COLUMN guest_email TEXT;
ALTER TABLE registrations ADD COLUMN eligibility_json TEXT;   -- the boxes ticked on this entry, e.g. {"grade_range": "0-8"}; asked per entry, never on the profile
UPDATE registrations SET eligibility_json = json_object('grade_range', grade) WHERE grade IS NOT NULL AND eligibility_json IS NULL;   -- entries made before WS06
ALTER TABLE registrations ADD COLUMN emergency_contact_json TEXT;   -- the scholastic emergency contact, copied from the owner's account on a child's scholastic entry (assumed, S7.9)
CREATE TABLE waitlist_offers (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  registration_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registrations(id),   -- TEXT: registrations.id is TEXT (0.2)
  offered_at TEXT NOT NULL, expires_at TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','claimed','expired'))
);
CREATE TABLE stripe_events (
  event_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                      -- Stripe's event id, inserted before any email is sent (D14)
  received_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
```

```sql
-- 00xx_player_profiles.sql  (the replan's 0052_player_profiles.sql, numbered in landing order; all additive, no members rebuild)
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN auth_user_id TEXT;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_members_auth_user ON members(auth_user_id) WHERE auth_user_id IS NOT NULL;   -- no backfill for adults
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN claimed_at TEXT;
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN scholastic_emergency_contact_json TEXT;   -- the owner's scholastic emergency contact (assumed, S7.9); verify none exists; shown only to an event's TD
CREATE TABLE member_guardians (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  guardian_member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'guardian' CHECK (kind IN ('owner','guardian')),
  send_copies INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  family_covered INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  added_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id),
  added_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  ended_at TEXT,
  ended_reason TEXT CHECK (ended_reason IN ('left','removed','handed_over','turned_18'))
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_member_guardians_active ON member_guardians(member_id, guardian_member_id) WHERE ended_at IS NULL;
CREATE INDEX idx_member_guardians_guardian ON member_guardians(guardian_member_id);
-- Backfill, written in one db.batch: one 'owner' row per members row whose guardian_id IS NOT NULL; family_covered = 1 where fewer
-- than 3 siblings (same guardian_id) have an earlier created_at (ties broken by rowid), which matches today's syncFamilyCoverage.
-- members.guardian_id stays and always equals the active owner.
CREATE TABLE profile_invites (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('claim','guardian','guardian_request','release')),
  member_ids TEXT NOT NULL, invited_email TEXT,
  invited_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  token_hash TEXT UNIQUE,
  confirmed_13_plus INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, consent_at TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','accepted','declined','cancelled','expired')),
  expires_at TEXT NOT NULL, used_at TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE TABLE profile_moves (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  from_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, to_member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  moved_json TEXT NOT NULL, moved_by TEXT NOT NULL,
  reason TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (reason IN ('claim','guardian_request')),
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
-- K runs this read-only preflight on production before the index (0.2):
--   SELECT uscf_id, COUNT(*) FROM members WHERE uscf_id IS NOT NULL AND role != 'guest' GROUP BY 1 HAVING COUNT(*) > 1;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_members_uscf_unique ON members(uscf_id) WHERE uscf_id IS NOT NULL AND role != 'guest';
```
No birthdate, grade or child-email columns. `members.email` stays NOT NULL and holds the owner's address for unclaimed children, but nothing reads it for sending. There is no `dependents` table: today's children rows are the player profiles, and `registrations.player_kind` keeps `dependent`. Member-created events add no column here; the marker is WS08's.

**API**
- `POST /api/registrations/orders` (new): creates an order plus held registrations. It checks the section cap and the overall limit by `entry_limit_mode`, holds seats per section and waitlists per section when limits are by section; it writes each entry's `section_id` and `schedule_id`; it adds the membership lines step 3 works out and refuses an order that lacks one a player needs (`needsMembershipAtCheckout`); it rejects an entry already made for the same `member_id` by another guardian and refuses more than 9 players with the plain second-order message; it refuses a closed event (WS08) and, on a member-created event, any entry line above $0 (D24). Extend `functions/api/registrations/batch.ts` (it already handles family batches and holds `MAX_ENTRIES = 9`) rather than duplicate it.
- `POST /api/registrations/orders/:id/checkout` (new): creates a Stripe Checkout Session with one line item per player, plus the membership lines from step 3, and `metadata.order_id`; every line is paid into LCA's account (`functions/utils/stripe.ts`; nothing uses Stripe Connect, Q3).
- `functions/api/stripe/webhook.ts`: handles `checkout.session.completed` for orders. It writes the Stripe event id into `stripe_events` in the same `db.batch` that marks every registration in the order paid and activates any membership in the order, so a duplicate delivery fails that batch and stops, and a batch that fails for any other reason records nothing and is run again on Stripe's retry (this replaces today's read-then-write guard, which can double-send under concurrent deliveries); only after the batch commits does it call `sendRegistrationConfirmations(orderId)` and `sendPaymentReceipt(orderId)` from that one place; a membership bought inside an order is a line on the order's receipt and sends no second receipt.
- `GET /api/registrations/manage/:token` and `POST .../withdraw` (new; signed token): guest self-service.
- `POST /api/registrations/:id/waitlist-claim/:token` (new).
- `POST /api/admin/registrations/:id/refund` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): refunds one line.
- `GET /api/uscf/lookup` (existing): reused inline; its refusal says "US Chess ID".
- Family (new): `GET /api/me/family`; `POST /api/me/children/:id/invite-login` `{email, is13Plus, consent}`; `DELETE /api/me/children/:id/login`; `POST /api/me/family/guardians/invite` `{email, memberIds[]}`; `POST /api/me/children/guardian-request` `{uscfId or fullName}`; `POST /api/profile-invites/:token/accept` and `/decline`; `PATCH /api/me/children/:id/guardians/me` `{send_copies}`; `DELETE /api/me/children/:id/guardians/:guardianId` (the owner removes, or a guardian leaves); `POST /api/me/children/:id/release` (owner); `POST /api/me/release` and `POST /api/profile-invites/:id/cancel-release`; `GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/checkin-search?q=` (WS08).
- `GET /api/me/event-mode` (WS02) reads `players[]` from `member_guardians`; `GET /api/tournaments/[id]` (WS05) returns `myPeople[]`, and `myRegistration` leaves it once `EVENT_PAGE_ON` is set.
- Each endpoint this workstream adds or changes takes its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test, moves to `parseBody`, and leaves `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` (0.2).

**Code changes** (the family model; server helper paths as WS01 step 16 left them)
- `functions/utils/auth.ts` `requireAuthedMember` and `optionalAuthedMember`: select by `auth_user_id = user.id`, else by `id = user.id AND auth_user_id IS NULL`.
- `functions/utils/admin/members.ts` `upsertMemberFromAuth`: resolve the same way and update the email on the resolved row; once `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` is set, refuse a signup US Chess ID that is on a managed profile with "Ask your parent or guardian to give you your own login from My LCA".
- The places that use `user.id` as a member id switch to `member.id` (the replan counts 17; verify: count them).
- `functions/utils/family.ts`: `canActFor` and `listChildren` read active `member_guardians`; `syncFamilyCoverage(db, guardianId)` covers that guardian's links where `family_covered = 1`, at most 3, keeping the later expiry; `MAX_CHILDREN` (8; today in `functions/api/me/children.ts`) counts active links.
- `functions/api/me/children/[id].ts`, once `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` is set: PATCH name is refused once the child is checked in to a running event; DELETE only by the sole active guardian, and only with no registrations or payments.
- `functions/api/registrations/batch.ts` keeps its shape, uses the new `canActFor`, rejects an entry already made for the same `member_id` by another guardian, and returns the plain over-9 message; with `functions/api/registrations.ts` it refuses an entry that needs a membership once `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` is set (above).
- The public event endpoint adds `myPeople[]` (WS05); the entrants rule and its `publicName` call belong to WS04's entrants endpoint, and the event answer's standings keep full names (D3).
- `functions/api/admin/tournaments/[id]/generate-pairings.ts` `familyOf`: any shared active guardian.
- `functions/api/admin/impersonate/[memberId].ts` allows a child only when `auth_user_id` is set.
- New `functions/utils/recipients.ts`, used by `functions/utils/registration/registrationEmails.ts`, the reminder senders and `workers/daily-emails`; new `functions/utils/receipts.ts`; WS05's `functions/utils/siteSettings.ts` (`readSiteSetting(db, key)`) for `membership_required_for_all`.
- `createCheckoutSession` (`functions/utils/stripe.ts`) and `sendEmail` (`functions/utils/email.ts`) return early on an empty key rather than calling Stripe or Resend with an empty bearer (WS01 follow-up "Empty keys do not stop the request").

**Frontend**
- Change:
  - `src/pages/TournamentDetailPage.tsx`: its registration parts (the in-page form and the family panel) move into the registration area, which WS05's thin page composes (WS01 follow-up "Pages to thin in WS05, WS06 and WS08").
  - `src/areas/households/components/FamilyRegistrationPanel.tsx` (its job passes to the picker) and `FamilyCard.tsx`, whose `USCHESS_RENEW_URL` import moves to `src/areas/membership/usChess.ts`, ending its deep import, unless WS12 does it first.
  - `src/areas/membership/components/UscfSearchInput.tsx`, `UsChessMembership.tsx`.
  - `src/pages/RegisterPage.tsx`, the sign-up page: it shows the managed-profile refusal above.
  - `src/lib/pricing.ts` (a re-export of `domain/registration/pricing.ts`), `src/lib/family.ts`.
- New: `src/pages/EventRegistrationPage.tsx` (`/tournaments/:id/register`); in `src/areas/registration/components/`: `RegistrationFlow.tsx` (the engine), `WhoStep.tsx` (the picker), `SectionStep.tsx`, `ByesStep.tsx`, `UsChessStep.tsx`, `MembershipStep.tsx` (required when the event requires it; in place of the planned `MembershipAddOn.tsx`), `ReviewStep.tsx`, `ConfirmStep.tsx`, `RegistrationSheet.tsx`, `FamilyExplainerSheet.tsx`, `EligibilityCheck.tsx` and `OrganizerNotice.tsx` (D27); `src/pages/PlayerPage.tsx` (`/me/players/:memberId`); in `src/areas/households/components/`: `AddFamilyMemberForm.tsx`, `InviteLoginSheet.tsx`, `GuardiansPanel.tsx` (WS13 renders it).
- From WS01: `ScrollPanel`, `FilterChip`, `StatusBadge`, the table primitives, the gold `Button`, and `formatDate` and `formatTime` (with `{ stored: 'utc' }` for a stored timestamp).

**Jobs & integrations**
- Stripe Checkout and refunds, all into LCA's own account. Stripe's own email receipts are turned off in the Stripe dashboard on the day the change that sets `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` deploys (Receipts, above).
- Resend for receipts, confirmations, manage links and waitlist offers: `sendRegistrationConfirmations` in `functions/utils/registration/registrationEmails.ts` stays as the player-confirmation sender, extended to take an order id and, on a member-created event, to add the D27 line and the organizer as reply-to; the new `sendPaymentReceipt(orderOrPaymentId)` in `functions/utils/receipts.ts` sends the receipt through the branded email layout; both are called from the webhook's order completion and from the free-order path. Membership activation and donations (WS12) and walk-in card payments (WS08) call `sendPaymentReceipt` too. Recipients come from `recipientsFor`.
- Phases of the five-minute run of `workers/daily-emails` (section 2.2, Scheduled runs) expire holds, waitlist offers and the 14-day profile invites; WS08's scheduled publish is a phase of the same run, and once WS08's `automation_runs` table exists (Phase 2) each phase records its runs there for the admin's automation health.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. A guardian registers herself and two children in different sections with one payment. The result is three registrations under one order, and replaying the webhook changes nothing.
2. Only eligible sections can be selected. Eligibility is asked as a checkbox on each entry, never as a grade or birthdate, and never stored on the profile.
3. The total on "Check your answers" equals the amount Stripe charges, and no card-fee line appears anywhere; the Reg-C board's "Card processing" line is gone (D2).
4. A guest completes registration in 8 fields or fewer, receives a manage link, and can withdraw.
5. A US Chess membership that expires before the event is flagged inline with a renewal link.
6. Waitlist offers expire after 24 hours and move to the next person automatically.
7. Holds release after 15 minutes. Under concurrent checkouts a section is never oversold (race test), with the section cap and the overall limit both enforced by `entry_limit_mode`.
8. A partial refund of one player in a three-player order succeeds, and the order shows `partially_refunded`.
9. With `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` set, every completed payment, including an order that carries a membership line, produces exactly one receipt to the payer and one confirmation per player, verified by the integration harness's email outbox under a replayed and a concurrent webhook delivery.
10. With `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` set, memberships and donations produce a receipt; with it unset, every payment sends what it sends today, the confirmation keeps its Paid total, and no LCA receipt goes out. A unit test holds the constant equal to `householdCheckout` (criterion 15).
11. A mixed free-and-paid order produces one confirmation per player, not two.
12. An order of 10 players is refused with the second-order message and 9 is accepted; a 9th profile is refused with "Your family list is full (8)"; a child already entered by another guardian is locked in the picker and refused by the server.
13. When the event requires a membership, an order with a player who lacks an active one cannot reach Pay without the membership step, and the membership is a line in the same Stripe session; when the event does not require one, the step is skipped.
14. Register my family renders for a signed-out visitor and for an active Family member (own, or held by another guardian covering a child the viewer guards), and for nobody else; the explainer sheet's one-form path creates the login and the first child and lands on the picker.
15. With `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` set, no online way in enters a player whom `needsMembershipAtCheckout` says needs a membership without a membership line in the same payment: the household checkout adds it, every Register control opens that checkout, and `registrations.ts` and `batch.ts` refuse with the plain message; with `membership_required_for_all` on, every event requires it. A unit test holds `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` equal to `householdCheckout`.
16. A section at its cap sends its next entry to that section's waitlist while the other sections keep taking entries, and the overall limit still holds.
17. With two schedules each registration carries the schedule its player chose in `registrations.schedule_id`, written by the order; with one schedule it carries the primary; no registration depends on the 0053 trigger.
18. The pre-event entrants list shows "Leo R." for a player with an active guardian link or a marked entry (an entry made before WS06 included, through the backfill), shortens "Leo Robichaux V" and "Leo Robichaux 3rd" to "Leo R.", and pairings, standings and results show the full name.
19. On a member-created event the checkout shows "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." and, before Pay or Register, the notice that the event is not run or rated by LCA and that each player's name and US Chess ID, and the email used for them, go to the organizer; every email about the event carries the line and has the organizer's email as reply-to; no entry line above $0 is charged; a closed event refuses new entries; while the site setting is off its entrants see the paused note.
20. The picker and the family panel scroll inside `ScrollPanel` on desktop and inside the sheet on phones; the sticky total never covers a focused control; nothing scrolls sideways at 390 or 320 px.
21. With `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` set, no message the registration endpoints answer, and no Stripe line name, holds an em or en dash or the old abbreviation for US Chess, while with it unset they answer today's words; with it unset, a section change, a sign-up with a managed profile's US Chess ID and `PATCH` and `DELETE /api/me/children/:id` answer as today; bye rounds are whole numbers; two players entering in the same millisecond get two registrations.

**Tests:**
- Extend `test/integration/family.test.ts` (`member_guardians`, invites, guardian requests, release) and `registration-rules.test.ts` (whole-number byes, the bye checks before capacity).
- New `orders.test.ts` (holds, the oversell race per section, caps to the waitlist, `schedule_id` written by the order, webhook idempotency through `stripe_events`, and a delivery whose batch fails leaving no `stripe_events` row so that Stripe's retry marks the order paid, partial refund).
- New `receipts.test.ts`: the email outbox under a replayed and a concurrent webhook delivery; memberships and donations; an order that carries a membership line (one receipt); the mixed free-and-paid order; and, with `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` unset, today's confirmation with its Paid total and no LCA receipt (criterion 10).
- New `waitlist.test.ts` and `guest.test.ts` (guest rows convert to managed profiles only for the payer's email).
- New `membership-at-checkout.test.ts`: acceptance criteria 13 and 15, with `membership_required_for_all` read from `site_settings`; new `test/unit/household-checkout-switch.test.ts`: the constant equals the switch; new `test/integration/household-checkout-unset.test.ts`: criterion 21's cases with the constant unset.
- Extend `test/unit/public-name-edges.test.ts` (the three suffixes, an entry marked by the backfill) and `test/integration/registration-plain-language.test.ts` (no dashes, US Chess wording and Stripe line names with `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` set, today's words with it unset).
- New `member-event-checkout.test.ts` (Phase 4): acceptance criterion 19, through the harness's email outbox for the line and the reply-to.
- Role-safety cases for the refund endpoint; `role-safety.test.ts` gains: a claimed child cannot act for siblings or read guardian receipts, and adult sign-in is unchanged.
- New `test/integration/profile-moves-keys.test.ts`: enumerates every foreign key to `members` and fails if profile moves skip one.
- New `test/integration/guardians-backfill.test.ts`: the `member_guardians` backfill and its `family_covered` rule on fixture households (ties broken by rowid, a fourth child left uncovered). Before the PR merges, K compares the rule with today's coverage through read-only queries on production (0.2), and `REDESIGN_STATUS.md` records the result.
- `test/e2e/register-family.spec.ts` drops `test.fixme` and runs through the `e2e-preview.yml` GitHub Actions job, started by hand against a branch preview with the preview database migrated and seeded and Stripe test keys set (WS01 follow-up "Remove test.fixme").
- Browser: `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` gains the signed-out explainer sheet and the guest path; the signed-in picker joins once the test servers can sign in (WS01 follow-up "The pages AC1 does not name").

**Depends on:** WS01 and WS05 (the page that hosts the flow, and the `site_settings` table); WS08 in Phase 4 for member-created events. WS08's Admin › Site control for `membership_required_for_all` comes in Phase 4; until then the row keeps its default, off.
**Risks & open decisions:**
- D2: with all-in pricing LCA absorbs Stripe's card fee. If that matters, nudge entry fees up rather than adding a fee line.
- Stripe Checkout supports per-line refunds only through the PaymentIntent and amount, so store the per-line amounts.
- Once WS05 drops the 0053 triggers, a registration writer that forgets `section_id` or `schedule_id` writes an entry with no section or schedule; `orders.test.ts` and WS05's audits guard it.
- The organizer of a member-created event receives entrants' emails (D27), which the notice says before anyone registers; otherwise the organizer sees what any event's director sees.

---

## WS07 · Live mode (pairings, standings, My board, result reports, alerts, TV, print)
**Why:**
- At the venue, players check pairings on phones in a crowd. The evidence calls for:
  - find-my-name
  - the player's own game pinned
  - an "updated N minutes ago" time
  - bigger text on demand
  - real tables with a frozen Player column
  - results as letter plus colour
- QR posters replaced piles of printouts in Chess67's Campbell story.
- Before WS01, `Crosstable` printed "3.5" and marked colour with a faint 10px "w/b". Under `newLook` both tables now write ½ through `formatScore` and mark colour with `ResultCell`; WS07 builds the live pages on them.
- K's note on page 7 (Fri, Oct 9, 2026) likes players reporting their own result, as long as a report "should not determine the match result, this should help steer the td"; asks for a hall TV that can show every section on one screen when the TD wants it; and asks that the US Chess rating report import cleanly, since "getting it right first would build trust".

**Canvas boards** (decided from K's note of Fri, Oct 9, 2026; they replace `Live-A` to `Live-E`):
- `Live-Final-Phones`: My board on phones: Find your board signed out or not playing, the pinned "Your game" card with the text-size menu, Report your result and what the TD sees, a parent's My players, and the round emails.
- `Live-Final-Pairings` and `Live-Final-Pairings-2`: the pairings page as a signed-in player, and in the TD view with the Reported results queue (WS08).
- `Live-Final-Standings`: standings and crosstable with the Download menu, and the TD's close-out panel (WS08).
- `Live-Final-TV`: the hall TV, cycling one section at a time or showing every section on one screen, and the TD's Hall TV settings (WS08).
- `Live-Final-Print`: the print and QR kit with the large pairings titles, one Download menu, the printed rating report checklist and optional board cards.
- `Looks-7-Broadcast-Tile` and `Looks-7-Broadcast-Page`: the live look and its type.

Where a board and this text differ, the text wins: the pairings board's note that the page checks every 60 seconds (D7's 20 seconds stands), the found-player card's link to a player page and rating history (there is none, D9), and the how-to words under the alphabetical sheet's order line (dropped; assumed, P2.1).

**User-facing scope**
- **Pairings** (`/tournaments/:id/pairings`):
  - round tabs and section chips with counts; By board or A to Z; each choice in the address (`round=`, `section=`, `order=`), so a shared link opens the same view
  - type-to-find highlights the matching rows (an edge in the gold-ink token on a light ground, brand gold only on a dark one, the typed letters marked), opens a collapsed section that holds a match and scrolls to the first; Enter moves to the next. No result text sits beside the box: the highlighted row is the answer
  - the text-size menu (below)
  - "Updated 1 min ago" with Refresh; the page checks every 20 seconds while it is visible (D7)
  - byes in words, with the standings' result codes: "H · requested bye, ½ point", "B · pairing bye, 1 point" and "U · not paired"
  - results show only once a TD has confirmed them (assumed, P2.4); until then the cell is blank, read to a screen reader as "No result yet", whether the game is still being played or has been reported
  - no em or en dash in the list or in the page's title (today's list writes one for a game with no result and before each bye, and the page and print titles hold one too)
  - the "Round 3 pairings are ready" banner and the links to the hall TV and the print kit stand
  - for the event's TDs and LCA admins, WS08's "TD view | Public view" switch, with the Reported results queue (assumed, P2.6)
- **Who sees what first:** a signed-in player paired in the current round gets "Your game" pinned at the top: board in big type, colour in words and by shape, opponent with rating, start time, minutes left, where the board is in the room when the section's `room_note` is set ("Boards 1 to 20, main hall"; assumed, P2.44), and "Updated N min ago". On phones its tab reads My board. A signed-in guardian gets My players. Everyone else, signed out, not entered, or not paired this round, gets Find your board: the first tab on phones and the find box on desktop, finding board, colour and opponent across every section, with "Playing today? Log in and your own board is pinned here."
- **Text size:** one 44px "Aa" menu, Small · Medium · Large, each shown in its own size, Medium by default, remembered on the device (local storage, read safely, with Medium when storage is blocked) and never sent to the server. It sits at the end of the first controls line on desktop and in the app bar on phones, on the pairings, standings and My board pages at every width. Large sets the pairings table to 21px with board numbers at 24px, and the standings table to 17px. No A / A+ / A++ button row renders anywhere. The hall TV and the print kit have no text-size menu.
- **Report your result** (D6, amended in 1.4): on the "Your game" card, for the signed-in White or Black player of that game and not a guardian (assumed, P2.3), once the round has started and until a TD result exists. A sheet with I won · Draw ½-½ · I lost, naming the opponent, "Your TD confirms every result" and one Send button. After sending, the card reads "Reported, awaiting TD" and "You reported: 1-0 · Waiting for the TD"; the opponent's card shows the report with "That's right" and "Report something else".
  - One report is enough: it reaches the TD's Reported results queue (WS08) at once, with no wait for the opponent. Either player can change their report until the TD confirms.
  - Only the two players and everyone `requireTournamentManager` admits for the event see a report: its directors, LCA admins and, for a club's event, the club's rep, with the region's representative from WS09 (assumed, P2.4). Nobody else sees any sign of it.
  - Nothing posts by itself: the TD taps once to confirm, also when both players agree (assumed, P2.5). When the TD confirms or enters the result, both cards read "Confirmed by TD".
- **Standings and crosstable** (`/tournaments/:id/standings`): one place-ordered table, as on `Live-Final-Standings`, drawn by one component (today's `Crosstable` and `StandingsTable` merge).
  - Columns: Place, Player (pinned with the place), Rating, Score (½), round cells, tiebreaks, Prize.
  - The key spells out the shorthand the first time: "Rd: round · Pts: points · W-D-L: wins, draws and losses", and each of those header cells has the full words as its accessible name. Tiebreaks carry US Chess's names (Modified median, Solkoff, Cumulative, Opponents' cumulative), with "How ties are broken" beside the table.
  - Round cells read "W ○ 17": result letter, tint, then a hollow ring for White or a filled disc for Black, then the opponent's number. Tapping a cell jumps to that opponent.
  - Result codes: W/L/D, H (half-point bye), B (full-point bye), U (unplayed), X/F (forfeit win/loss).
  - Phones get a column chooser (Rounds, Tiebreaks, Prizes; Place, Player, Pts and W-D-L show first) and a player card.
  - One Download button where today's links were, opening a menu (a sheet on phones) of Standings as PDF, Crosstable as CSV and Wall chart PDF for the section on screen. Everyone `requireTournamentManager` admits for the event also sees "US Chess rating report" under "Tournament directors only" (assumed, P2.9), which opens WS08's close-out panel before anything downloads. The item shows on every event, listed or not, since K wants rating reports available for every event (Mon, Oct 19, 2026).
  - Find a player highlights the row and opens the found-player card (place, prize, the round-by-round list, and the board's rating estimate, labelled as an estimate until US Chess rates the event), with no text beside the box (assumed, P2.44) and no player page or rating-history link (D9).
  - Standings count TD-confirmed results only (assumed, P2.4); during a round the line reads "after Round 3 · 8 of 13 results confirmed".
  - Once the event is finalized (WS10), this page is the event's results page: it reads the archived snapshot, says "Final results" and links "Read the recap" once a recap is published.
- **My board and My players:** a pinned card with board, colour, opponent and rating, start time and "updated" time. A follow star on any player feeds a "My players" strip. The children a guardian acts for (every active `member_guardians` link) are followed automatically.
- **Round-ready alerts (email only):**
  - When the TD publishes a round with Notify players on, registered players who have not switched off and their guardians get an email through Resend, and so, once `followerAlerts` is on, do the people who follow a player in the round or the event. The publish endpoint enqueues only when its request asks (`notify: true`), which only WS08's console sends, and only while `roundAlerts` is on; today's manage page, which publishes through the same endpoint, never asks, so a round published with every switch off emails nobody. The followers' lines follow `FOLLOWER_ALERTS_ON` (`functions/utils/live/alerts.ts`), a constant set in the same change as `followerAlerts`, with a unit test that the two agree. A registered player is opted in at registration and can switch off from the event page's round alerts card (WS05) or My LCA; a guardian gets one switch per registered child, sent to the guardian's address (assumed, S9.4). Recipients come from `recipientsFor` (WS06), grouped per address: a parent of two gets one email per round, subject "Round 3 pairings: Maya, Leo", each line starting with the child's name.
  - Web Push comes in Phase 5.
  - Text messages are out of scope for now (D8). Each text costs money and US business texting needs carrier registration, while email and web push are free. No alerts surface shows an SMS or phone-notification toggle, or a "Phone notifications come later" line; "Text me" stays hidden until SMS exists.
- **Hall TV** (`/tournaments/:id/tv`):
  - built for 1080p, in the live look
  - by default two big pairing columns per section, cycling sections every 15 seconds; or, when the TD picks it in the Hall TV settings (WS08), every section on one screen, two across, each with its own header, names at 30px, no cycling. The settings mark All on one screen "Suggested" at 30 players or fewer and unavailable above 16 boards, each with the reason in words (assumed, P2.7). The screen never changes layout by itself, and follows the TD's pick within 20 seconds.
  - in All on one screen, with "Show standings after results come in" on (the default), the screen alternates pairings and standings every 15 seconds once results are confirmed (assumed, P2.8)
  - the round start time and a countdown, and a QR code to the phone view
  - a visible Pause control (and Space on the laptop driving the screen), since the screen cycles and WS01's motion rule asks for one; the other keys `Live-Final-TV` draws (the arrow keys step, A switches between one section and every section, S shows standings now) are listed in the Hall TV settings
  - only TD-confirmed results reach the TV (assumed, P2.4)
- **Print and QR kit** (`/tournaments/:id/print`): extends `TournamentPrintPage` with:
  - a hall poster with a QR code and short link; the same code is on every sheet and on the hall TV
  - pairings, alphabetical and by board: each sheet's large title is the round and the section, "Pairings · Round 3 · Open", in place of today's "find your name" heading, with the order as a small line under it ("Alphabetical by player", "By board"), where the round start and the "Pts" key also stay; the how-to words on the alphabetical sheet are dropped (assumed, P2.1); the page header carries only the event name and the date
  - standings and the wall chart (crosstable) under the same title pattern, "Standings · after Round 2 · U1800" and "Wall chart · Final · U1800" (assumed, P2.44)
  - a one-page final report
  - for the people who manage the event, sheet 7: the printed US Chess rating report checklist, with the event and section details exactly as WS08's three files carry them
  - optional board cards: a folded card for each table with its board number and its own QR, which opens that board's Report your result for a signed-in player in that game; printed once per event and reused every round
  - every score on every sheet written with `formatScore` in every look, the score going into a round on the pairing sheets included: 10½, never 1½ (today's sheet, with `newLook` off, writes 10.5 as 1½; assumed, Q33)

  All Letter size (the wall chart also at 11 × 17), readable in black and white, with QR codes generated in the browser and White and Black marked by shape as well as by word. The kit's files sit under one Download menu, with "Print Round 3 set" as the one primary action (assumed, P2.44). Anyone can print the public sheets; the "Tournament directors only" bar, the checklist and the final report show only to those `requireTournamentManager` admits. The by-board sheet's foot line reads "A result reported on a phone counts only once the TD confirms it." The official logo (D1 gives it to the print kit) sits on the hall poster and the final report beside LCA's name, and every other sheet keeps a header of the event name and the date only, as the board draws (assumed, Q9). Each QR on an LCA event's sheets and hall TV carries the rook mark at its centre (D1) and still scans; on a member-created event's the centre is plain (assumed, Q12).
- **Printing a live page:** paper always takes the base light look, since the live look's colours apply on screens only (WS01 step 24). A browser print of the pairings or standings page prints its table on Letter and leaves the controls off: the "Aa" menu, Download, Refresh, the find box, the Night toggle and the report sheet. The print kit is the way to print sheets.
- **Results reporting (D6, amended in 1.4):** the event's TDs enter and confirm results through the WS08 console. LCA admins can do anything a TD can on any event (pair, enter or correct results, publish) as a backstop. Signed-in players report their own game as a steer for the TD, in the first build (assumed, P2.2): one report reaches the TD at once, with no opponent confirmation, and a report never sets a result.
- **Member-created events** (decided Tue, Oct 20, 2026; created through WS08): every function above works on them and is shared by link: pairings, standings, My board, reports, alerts, the hall TV and the kit.
  - Their live pages carry noindex, as WS05's event page does, and their event header reads "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." where an LCA event shows its LCA tag.
  - Each round email goes through WS08's `markMemberEventEmail` (section 2.2), which adds the same line and makes the organizer its reply-to.
  - Its pairings and standings pages carry "Report this event" at their foot, as the event page does (WS05, WS08).
  - Every printed sheet carries the line, and no LCA logo, name block or rook mark, its QR codes included (assumed, Q12).
  - They never feed `GET /api/live/now`, so they never show a "Live" tag on Tournaments or a live hero on the homepage.
- **Theme:** live surfaces use the live look (`data-theme="live"`), with a "Night" toggle remembered per device. WS07 binds the live type the Looks-7 tile sets: Barlow Condensed for headings, board numbers and the TV's big type, and Barlow for body text; figures, moves and clocks stay in Geist Mono (WS01). The live type loads on live pages only, never on `/`, whose live hero takes the colour tokens only (WS03).

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_live_mode.sql
-- tournament_games (migration 0019): id TEXT, tournament_id TEXT, round, board, section TEXT, white_member_id,
-- black_member_id, result CHECK IN ('1-0','0-1','1/2-1/2','1-0 F','0-1 F','0-0 F','bye','bye-half','pending')
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN live_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'idle' CHECK (live_state IN ('idle','live','finished'));
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN current_round INTEGER;
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN tv_layout TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'cycle' CHECK (tv_layout IN ('cycle','all'));
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN tv_show_standings INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1;
ALTER TABLE tournament_games ADD COLUMN section_id TEXT REFERENCES tournament_sections(id);
ALTER TABLE tournament_games ADD COLUMN result_confirmed_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);   -- the TD or admin who confirmed or entered it
ALTER TABLE tournament_games ADD COLUMN result_confirmed_at TEXT;
ALTER TABLE tournament_sections ADD COLUMN room_note TEXT;   -- 'Boards 1 to 20, main hall', set in setup (WS08); left off the card when empty (assumed, P2.44)
CREATE TABLE round_publications (
  tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id), round INTEGER NOT NULL,
  section_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournament_sections(id),
  published_at TEXT NOT NULL, published_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id), version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  PRIMARY KEY (tournament_id, round, section_id)
);
CREATE TABLE result_reports (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  game_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournament_games(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  round INTEGER NOT NULL,
  reporter_member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  reporter_side TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (reporter_side IN ('white','black')),
  result TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (result IN ('1-0','0-1','1/2-1/2')),
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','accepted','overridden','replaced')),
  reported_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  resolved_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id),   -- the TD or admin who confirmed or overrode
  resolved_at TEXT
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_result_reports_open ON result_reports(game_id, reporter_member_id) WHERE status = 'open';
CREATE INDEX idx_result_reports_queue ON result_reports(tournament_id, round, status);
CREATE TABLE follows (
  member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  target_kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (target_kind IN ('player','club','event')),
  target_key TEXT NOT NULL,          -- US Chess ID / registration id / club id / tournament id
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')), PRIMARY KEY (member_id, target_kind, target_key)
);
CREATE TABLE round_alert_optouts (   -- a row turns round emails off for one person on one entry
  registration_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registrations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),   -- the player, or a guardian for that child's entry
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  PRIMARY KEY (registration_id, member_id)
);
CREATE TABLE notifications (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, kind TEXT NOT NULL, channel TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'email',
  member_id TEXT REFERENCES members(id), email TEXT, dedupe_key TEXT UNIQUE, payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued' CHECK (status IN ('queued','sent','failed')),
  attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')), sent_at TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE rate_limits (                 -- the shared limit behind functions/utils/rateLimit.ts, first used by Report your result; WS09's forms reuse it
  bucket TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                 -- the route, a one-way scrambled form of the visitor's network address (or the member) and the hour; the address itself is never stored
  hits INTEGER NOT NULL, window_start TEXT NOT NULL
);
-- Phase 5: push_subscriptions (member_id TEXT, endpoint, p256dh, auth, created_at)
```
- **`tournament_games.section_id`** (a WS01 follow-up): the migration fills it for every existing game from the game's section name; migration 0053 already made an archived section row for each name a game uses that had none, for this link. Pairing generation, walk-ins and the results entry set it on every new game, and `/live`, the live pages and `round_publications` read it. `section` stays written for today's readers until each one moves, so a section rename no longer has to move games by name.
- **Results:** `tournament_games.result` stays the only result, and only the TD and admin paths write it, setting `result_confirmed_by` and `result_confirmed_at`. Changing a report marks the earlier row `replaced` and adds a new one, so every report is kept. Confirming or entering a result marks the game's open reports `accepted` where they match and `overridden` where they do not. A result entered with no report touches no report rows.
- **State:** publishing a round (WS08) sets `live_state = 'live'` and `current_round`; Finalize (WS10) sets `'finished'`.
- **Alerts:** a registered player is opted in at registration, so no `round_alert_optouts` row means the emails are on (S9.4).
- The text size and the Night choice are device preferences and are not stored on the server.

**API**
- `GET /api/tournaments/:id/live` (new, public): compact pairings, standings and progress plus a `version`. Returns an `ETag` and honours `If-None-Match` with 304. Edge cache 10 seconds, for an event anyone may open; an answer that depends on who asks (a hidden draft, or a closed member-created event, WS08) is sent `Cache-Control: private, no-store` and never put in the edge cache. It answers for an event exactly when `GET /api/tournaments/:id` does (a hidden draft only to those who may see it today, a member-created event to anyone with its link), and reads `tournament_games.result` only, so a player's report changes neither its data nor its `version`.
- Today's data: `GET /api/tournaments/:id` (`functions/api/tournaments/[id].ts`) returns the event with its `pairings`, `standings` (from `computeStandings`, shared with the manage endpoint) and `prizes`, and both `TournamentPairingsPage` and `TournamentPrintPage` read it; there is no separate pairings or standings endpoint. `/live` reuses `computeStandings`, and the live pages read `/live` rather than more endpoints being added.
- `GET /api/live/now` (new; contract in WS02): names every listed LCA event live now, and feeds the public "Live" tag on Tournaments (WS02), the Live tag on WS04's rows and previews, and the homepage live hero (WS03). It never names a hidden draft, a partner listing or a member-created event. The personal event strip reads `/api/me/event-mode`, not this endpoint.
- `GET /api/me/event-mode` (WS02) adds the viewer's own report for the current game (`myReport`: result, status, time, and the opponent's report if any). It stays private and no-store; no public endpoint reads `result_reports` (assumed, P2.4).
- `POST /api/tournaments/:id/games/:gameId/report` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): the caller must be the White or Black player of that game, the round published and started, and no TD-confirmed result yet; body `{ result }`; a second call replaces the caller's open report. A guardian acting for a child is refused (assumed, P2.3). Rate-limited through `functions/utils/rateLimit.ts`, which WS07 builds with its `rate_limits` table and WS09's public forms reuse, and audited.
- `GET`, `POST` and `DELETE /api/me/follows` (new).
- `GET` and `PUT /api/me/round-alerts` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): the viewer's round email switches, one per registered person they are or act for, which WS05's round alerts card and My LCA (WS13) show.
- The publish endpoint, the TD's queue and confirm, and the Hall TV settings are in WS08.
- Every new or changed endpoint takes its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test in `test/integration/contracts/`, and comes off `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` (the pending list only shrinks, 0.2).

**Transport (D7):** in plain terms, each open pairings page quietly asks the server "anything new?" every 20 seconds. Clients poll `/live` every 20 seconds while the page is visible and stop when it's hidden (Page Visibility API). If D1 load ever becomes a problem, upgrade to a Durable Object per event broadcasting over WebSocket.

**Frontend**
- Change:
  - `src/pages/TournamentPairingsPage.tsx`: the pairings page; its standings and crosstable move to the standings page.
  - `src/pages/TournamentPrintPage.tsx`: the kit, with the sheet titles, the Download menu, the checklist and the board cards.
  - `src/areas/live/components/Crosstable.tsx` and `StandingsTable.tsx` merge into one component, `ResultsTable.tsx`, carrying `ResultCell`, the row header, the two pinned columns and the key, on the table primitives (`src/components/ui/table.tsx`, with `TableRowHeader`). Whether the live look's cells also take the Looks-7 tile's win, loss and draw colours and its underline is decided against the tile and recorded in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`; the letter and the mark carry the result either way, and every colour pair meets 4.5:1 on its ground.
  - `src/areas/live/components/ResultsEntry.tsx`: its round tabs say which round is shown (`aria-pressed`, with every switch off, since it changes nothing seen, 0.2), and under `newLook` they take `FilterChip`'s selected style in place of a navy fill, which is 1.17:1 against the dark card.
  - `src/areas/events/components/PrizeWinners.tsx`.
- New (in `src/areas/live/components` unless a path is given):
  - `src/pages/TournamentStandingsPage.tsx`, `src/pages/TournamentTvPage.tsx` (the cycling and all-sections layouts from `tv_layout`)
  - `RoundTabs.tsx`, `SectionChips.tsx`, `FindYourBoard.tsx` (in place of `FindMyName.tsx`), `MyBoardCard.tsx` (with Report your result and its states), `MyPlayersStrip.tsx`, `LiveStatus.tsx`, `ColumnChooser.tsx`, `QrCode.tsx`, `TextSizeMenu.tsx` (in place of a toggle), `DownloadMenu.tsx`, `ReportResultSheet.tsx`, `ReportStatus.tsx` (the card's reported states for both players), `printSheetTitle.ts` (the sheet title builder)
  - `src/hooks/useLivePoll.ts`, with WS01's `useNow` for "Updated N min ago"
  - `functions/utils/rateLimit.ts`, the shared rate limit, with its `rate_limits` table
- From the design system (WS01), used rather than rebuilt:
  - `ThemeScope scope="live"` around each live page, with `ScopeStylesheetReady scope="live"` holding the page until `src/styles/themes/live.css` has arrived (bounded by the loader's hold, as `HeritageLayout` does for heritage), so the Barlow faces are in place before it paints.
  - `ResultCell`, `ColorMark` and `ResultLegend` for every result cell and key; `StatusBadge` with the `status` prop (`live`, and `lca` on an LCA event's header) for status in words; `FilterChip` for the section chips with their counts; `LcaMark` at each QR's centre and `LcaLogo` on the hall poster and the final report; `formatScore`, `formatDate`, `formatTime` and `formatTimeControl` from `src/lib/format.ts`.
- The pages call the API through `src/areas/live/api.ts`, never `fetch` in a page.
- **Switches** (0.2's table, all off by default): `newLive` (the rebuilt pairings page, the standings and crosstable page, My board and My players with follows, Find your board, the text-size and Download menus, the hall TV, the kit's new sheets, and the live look and type on them); `resultReports` (Report your result, the board cards and the TD's Reported results queue); `roundAlerts` (the round-ready emails to players and guardians, and WS05's round alerts card, which shows only while they are sent); `followerAlerts` (the round-ready emails to followers, with `FOLLOWER_ALERTS_ON`, after the pilot review); and the existing `eventStripLive`, `liveMarker` and `homeLive`, which can go on in WS07.

**Jobs & integrations**
- The notifications sender gets an outbox processor: a phase of the five-minute run of `workers/daily-emails` (section 2.2, Scheduled runs), keeping its per-phase isolation and mark-only-on-success pattern.
- At publish time, send immediately with `context.waitUntil` and let the cron retry failures.
- Resend handles delivery. A preview has no Resend key, so a send there fails safely and stays in the outbox as `failed`; checks on a preview read the outbox rows.
- The outbox is shared: WS10's digest, WS11's season email and WS14's "Email me when this changes" go through it with their own dedupe keys.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. For 100 players in 5 sections, the pairings page loads in under 1.5 seconds on simulated 4G and reflects a newly published round within 30 seconds, without a reload.
2. Typing 3 letters of a name highlights and scrolls to that row, and nothing is written beside the box. A signed-in player paired in the current round sees "Your game" pinned; a viewer who is signed out, not entered or not paired this round sees Find your board first on phones.
3. Standings show ½ glyphs, labelled tiebreaks with a legend, and colour markers that don't rely on colour alone. The key spells out Rd, Pts and W-D-L in words, and each of those header cells has the full words as its accessible name.
4. Publishing a round with `notify: true` enqueues exactly one email per recipient who has not switched off (dedupe key `round:{id}:{n}:{member}`), grouped per address through `recipientsFor`, and a publish without it, as today's manage page sends, enqueues none. Followers get a line only with `FOLLOWER_ALERTS_ON` set, and a unit test holds it equal to `followerAlerts`. Failed sends retry up to 3 times. A guardian who switches off one child's entry still gets the other child's line.
5. The TV page is readable from 3 m (body text 28 px or larger). It cycles through sections by default, or shows every section on one screen when the TD picks it (marked "Suggested" at 30 players or fewer and unavailable above 16 boards; assumed, P2.7), never changing layout by itself. It has a visible Pause control, and its QR code opens the phone view.
6. Every print sheet fits on Letter at 100% and its QR code scans. Each pairings sheet, alphabetical and by board, carries "Pairings · Round N · Section" as its large title, and its page header carries only the event name and the date.
7. The live look applies only inside live surfaces. On paper every live page and sheet takes the base light look, and a browser print of the pairings or standings page shows none of its controls.
8. `/live` answers 304 when nothing has changed, and hidden tabs send no requests.
9. A player's report changes no public result: after a report, `/live` answers with the same `version`, and the pairings, standings, hall TV, `event_results` and the rating report are unchanged until a TD confirms or enters the result (for the public pages, assumed, P2.4).
10. Only the signed-in White or Black player of a game can report it, after the round starts and before a TD result exists; anyone else signed in gets 403, and a signed-out request 401. The report is visible to those two players and to everyone `requireTournamentManager` admits for the event, and to nobody else (assumed, P2.4).
11. Text size is one "Aa" menu with three sizes on the pairings, standings and My board pages at 390 px and 1440 px, remembered on the device; no A / A+ / A++ button row renders.
12. The standings page shows one Download button; its menu lists the PDF, CSV and wall chart for everyone, and the US Chess rating report only for everyone `requireTournamentManager` admits for the event, on listed and member-created events alike (assumed, P2.9).
13. `GET /api/live/now` never names a hidden draft, a partner listing or a member-created event, even while one is playing a round.
14. A member-created event's pairings, standings, TV and print pages carry noindex and the line "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event.", and show no LCA tag; its pairings and standings pages carry Report this event.
15. A member-created event's round emails carry "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." and have the organizer as their reply-to.
16. A member-created event's printed sheets carry the "Organized by" line and no LCA logo, name block or rook mark, with a plain centre on every QR code; an LCA event's hall poster and final report carry the official logo (assumed, Q9 and Q12).
17. Every game has a `section_id` after the migration, naming the section row its section name matches. Renaming a section changes no game's `section_id`, and the live pages show the game under the new name.
18. Inside a live page, headings are set in Barlow Condensed and body text in Barlow; no live font file appears in the network log of `/`; switching a container to the live look causes no layout shift (WS01 AC8); and CLS on the pairings and standings pages stays under 0.1 across the font swap.
19. `/tournaments/:id/pairings`, `/tournaments/:id/standings`, `/tournaments/:id/tv` and `/tournaments/:id/print` are in the accessibility matrix (`PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`), with zero serious or critical axe violations in the light, dark and live looks at 390 px and 1440 px.
20. No em or en dash renders in the words or titles of the pairings, standings, TV or print pages.
21. The round tabs on the live pages and in the results entry tell a screen reader which round is shown, with every switch off too, and under `newLook` show it by more than a navy fill.

**Tests**
- Unit:
  - `test/unit/results-table.test.tsx`: the combined table (½, the result codes, colour markers, tiebreak labels, the Rd, Pts and W-D-L key and those header cells' names); `test/unit/standings-format.test.tsx` and `test/unit/result-cell.test.tsx` follow the merge.
  - `test/unit/text-size-menu.test.tsx`: the preference (read, write, and Medium when storage is blocked).
  - `test/unit/print-sheet-title.test.ts`: the sheet title builder.
  - `test/unit/live-words.test.tsx`: the pairings, standings, TV and print pages hold no em or en dash and write byes in words.
  - `test/unit/features.test.ts`: `newLive`, `resultReports` and `followerAlerts` start off (WS05's case holds `roundAlerts`); `test/unit/follower-alerts-switch.test.ts` (new): `FOLLOWER_ALERTS_ON` equals `followerAlerts`.
  - `test/unit/find-your-board.test.tsx` (new): criterion 2: three typed letters highlight and scroll to the row with nothing written beside the box; a paired signed-in player gets "Your game" pinned; a viewer signed out, not entered or not paired gets Find your board first on phones.
  - `test/unit/round-tabs.test.tsx` (new): criterion 21, with every switch off and under `newLook`.
  - `test/unit/print-kit.test.tsx` (new): the official logo on an LCA event's hall poster and final report and on no other sheet; a member-created event's sheets with the "Organized by" line, no LCA logo, name block or rook mark, and plain QR centres (criterion 16); a score of 10½ going into a round printed as 10½.
  - `test/unit/live-member-event.test.tsx` (new, Phase 4): a member-created event's live headers with the line and no LCA tag, and Report this event on its pairings and standings pages (criterion 14); WS04's `test/integration/link-previews.test.ts` gains the noindex of its pairings, standings, TV and print paths.
- Integration (`test/integration`):
  - `live-endpoint.test.ts`: `/live` ETag and 304; a report leaves the ETag and the public pairings unchanged; a hidden draft answers as the event endpoint does.
  - `live-now.test.ts`: a hidden draft, a partner listing and a member-created event in play never answer; two listed events in play both answer, the earlier round first.
  - `result-reports.test.ts`: each side can report; a third player and a guardian get 403; signed out gets 401; before the round starts and after a TD result it answers 409; a second report replaces the first and keeps it as `replaced`; the rate limit answers 429 in words; the Download menu's rating report item is absent for a player.
  - `round-alerts.test.ts`: publish with `notify: true`, outbox and sender with idempotency; a publish without it enqueues nothing; per-address grouping; a switched-off entry gets no line; followers get no line with `FOLLOWER_ALERTS_ON` unset and theirs with it set; a member-created event's emails carry the line and the organizer's reply-to.
  - `follows.test.ts`: follow and unfollow, and a guardian following each registered child automatically.
  - `games-section-id.test.ts`: the migration's fill, and a rename that changes no game's `section_id`.
  - Extend `pairing-flow.test.ts`; a contract test for each new endpoint in `test/integration/contracts/`.
- Browser:
  - `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` (criterion 19), with `newLive` on in the a11y-on build (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`) and `test/a11y/baseline.json` unchanged, and `test/a11y/chess-data.spec.ts` (the combined table in the base and live looks and in dark mode).
  - `test/a11y/hall-tv.spec.ts` (new): criterion 5: body text at 28 px or larger at 1080p, the default cycling and the TD's All on one screen with no change of layout by itself, the Pause control and Space, and the QR code's link to the phone view.
  - `test/a11y/live-fonts.spec.ts`: criterion 18.
  - `test/a11y/print-look.spec.ts`: the pairings and standings pages printed from the live look and from dark mode.
  - `test/e2e/publish-round.spec.ts` drops `test.fixme` once WS07 and WS08's Publish round ship. K starts the `e2e-preview.yml` GitHub Actions job by hand against a branch preview whose own database, `lca-db-preview`, K has migrated and seeded; it never runs against `lca-db`.
- By hand, recorded in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`: criterion 1, a lab run of the pairings page with 100 players in 5 sections on simulated 4G, and a round published while the page is open showing within 30 seconds.

**Depends on:** WS01, WS02 (`/api/me/event-mode` and the "Live" tag), WS05 (the event page and its round alerts card), WS06 (`recipientsFor` and `member_guardians`), WS08 (publish, and the confirm path for reports): WS07's migration lands first in Phase 2, as a PR of its own, then WS08's console, which writes its tables, then WS07's pages (section 4). Member-created events come from WS08's creation work.

**Risks & open decisions:**
- Resend volume and plan limits.
- D6 to D8 are settled, D6 as amended in 1.4; see section 5.
- TDs must publish and confirm every result on the site, so pilot at one LCA event first. A 96-player event can bring 48 confirmations a round, so the queue must be quick on a tablet.
- A switch is site-wide. For the pilot, `roundAlerts` goes on only while the pilot event is the one event publishing rounds on the site, and `followerAlerts` stays off, so the sends leave followers out until K turns it on after the pilot review (section 4); if that cannot be arranged, WS07 limits sends to a list of pilot events in `site_settings` and records how in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`.
- The live type adds font weight to live pages only; keep it lazy.
- Open for K: P2.1 to P2.9 and P2.44, and three defaults this brief adds: where the official logo sits in the print kit (Q9), a member-created event's pages and kit carrying no LCA logo, name block or mark (Q12), and today's print page's half scores of 10 and more (Q33).
