## WS08 · Director & admin tools (tournament creation, TD console, setup checklist, admin home & queues)
**Why:**
- At the venue, directors work on laptops and tablets. Their priorities are speed at check-in, pairing, results entry, publishing and printing.
- Today's `TournamentManagePage` has 5 tabs and about 11 cards in 2,380 lines, and its tables need 640 to 840px, so they scroll sideways on a tablet.
- Admin tabs were added one after another. K chose an admin-only `/admin` with a grouped sidebar, plus a separate `/workspace` for regional representatives, TDs and auditors (a club's rep keeps its page at `/my/club/:slug`, WS09); officers keep today's view-only Admin (`Account-P`).
- Today only an LCA admin, or a `club_rep` for its own club, can create a tournament (`functions/api/admin/tournaments.ts`), and every new event starts as a hidden draft. K wants the community's trusted directors, the president and the admins creating events now, the regional representatives later, and every member last, with members' events never listed (D20).
- K picked `Work-J` for "the way I can quickly see the health of my automations" (Mon, Oct 19, 2026).

**Canvas boards:**
- `Work-E` and `Work-E-2`: the TD console, one stage at a time (replaces `Work-A`), built from `Live-Final-Pairings-2` (the Reported results queue), `Live-Final-Standings` frame B (the close-out panel) and `Live-Final-TV` inset B1 (Hall TV settings).
- `Work-L` and `Work-L-2`: the setup checklist, one step at a time (replaces `Work-D`), redrawn without its jump list: a step too long to fit scrolls inside the middle section, so the steps rail never leaves view (K, Mon, Oct 19, 2026; D16). Until the redraw is on the canvas, `Work-L` read with that change is the reference; its insets K1 to K3 (a representative's club event, a new event, a live event's stop) are drawn on `Work-K`.
- `Work-J` and `Work-J-2`: the admin home (replaces `Work-C`): a dashboard with the queue's five most urgent rows, the site banner, the health of the automations and the latest audit lines, with Needs attention one click away (frame B, the full queue table `Work-I` draws).
- `Event-Final-Setup`: the TD setup panel with the Phase 1 options, the content of the setup steps.
- `Account-Final-Hub-2`: section G, the event rules (G1 the Fees step's notice for anyone not on D24's list, G2 the standing director's fee note, G3 Publish and the admin's after-the-fact row, G4 the rating report on every event); section H, the way in and back and the admin backstop strip; F3, the console at 1024 with the rail folded into the breadcrumb trail.
- `Live-Final-Print` sheet 7 (the printed checklist) and `News-Final` frame B (the Recap tab, WS10).
- My region (`Work-H` and `Work-H-2`) is WS09's: the representative's room, whose Add a club event opens this setup checklist.
- No board draws a member-created event's setup, the admin's page of member-created events, or where a plain member starts one; the text below is the reference.

**User-facing scope**
- **Parts, phases and switches** (0.2's table; each switch off by default, added to `src/lib/features.ts` with its comment and to `test/unit/features.test.ts`):
  - Tournament creation, now (Phase 1, ahead of the console; assumed, Q11): the creation roles and the entry-fee rule (D20 to D22, D24), on today's pages, behind `standingDirectors`.
  - The TD console, the setup checklist, QR check-in and the Rating report step (Phase 2), behind `tdConsole`.
  - The admin home, Needs attention and Admin › Site (Phase 4), behind `adminHome`, with `ADMIN_HOME_ON` (`functions/utils/admin/queue.ts`) for the server's half. While it is unset, what this brief sends "until Needs attention exists" (the D22 notice, a payout request, a representative's club event to approve, a club report at 14 days, a rep request while a seat is open) goes to the LCA admins by email, or to today's support list; once it is set, each becomes a queue row instead, so nothing goes quiet between the merge and the switch.
  - Member-created events (Phase 4), turned on and off by an LCA admin with the site setting `member_events_enabled` (D25), which is not a build switch; its control shows once `adminHome` is on.
  - A server change that a switch governs follows a constant set in the same change as its switch, with a unit test that the two agree: `CREATION_ROLES_ON` in `functions/utils/events/creation.ts` for `standingDirectors`, since it widens who may create and narrows who may set a fee above $0; `TD_CONSOLE_ON` in `functions/utils/events/console.ts` for `tdConsole`, since the rating report and walk-in endpoints today's manage page uses gain checks and new words; and `ADMIN_HOME_ON` for `adminHome`, since it moves the admins' notices into the queue (functions cannot read `src/lib/features.ts`; 0.2 lists every such constant).
- **Tournament creation (D20 to D24).** What changes from today, phase by phase:
  - **Today:** an LCA admin, or a `club_rep` for its own club, creates; a new event is a hidden draft (`is_visible = 0`) until someone who manages it publishes it; an admin or the organizing club's rep names its directors (`functions/api/admin/tournaments/[id]/directors.ts`); the `tournament_director` role opens no event by itself.
  - **Now** (`standingDirectors`):
    - **Standing directors** (D20) may always create their events here. The role is today's `tournament_director`, shown as "Standing director", which an LCA admin grants on the member's admin page as roles are granted today; K decides who holds it (assumed, Q4). Before `standingDirectors` goes on, K reads the list of members who hold the role today (one read-only query on production) and takes it from anyone who should not create.
    - **The president** (D20, D21): the member with an active assignment to the President seat (`board_members.slug = 'president'`, which migration 0025 derived from "President"; verify: K checks it on production with one read, 0.2) may always create, and may name a one-event director on any event the president drafted. The seat is the grant, and `members.role` is unchanged (the president keeps `lca_observer`).
    - **LCA admins** create as today and name one-event directors (D21).
    - **Club reps:** nothing changes until K answers Q2: a `club_rep` creates its own club's events as hidden drafts and names their directors, as today.
    - **A one-event director** is named on one event through `tournament_directors`: by an LCA admin on any event, by the president on an event the president drafted, later by a regional representative on a club event they drafted (D21), and by the organizing club's rep as today. They run that event (every setup step, the console, close-out and the rating report) and nothing else. What a one-event director may not do (the membership switch, naming directors, deleting the event) is in the table below (`Work-K` note 5). No other officer drafts or names (D21).
    - **The creator runs the event as its first director:** a standing director, the president, or later a member organizer, who creates an event is written as its first director in `tournament_directors`, which today's `canManageTournament` (`functions/utils/admin/permissions.ts`) already admits, so Events you direct (WS13) and the rating report's director line find them, and an LCA admin can remove them as any director. `canManageTournament` gains no branch for `tournaments.created_by`: every existing event records its creator (today an LCA admin or a club rep), and a creator who has lost the grant, such as a former admin or a club rep since removed, keeps nothing on the event. A director row stays until an LCA admin removes it, so a standing director who loses the role keeps the events they direct, as any director does.
    - **Listing (D22):** a standing director's event lists on Tournaments the moment it is published, and nothing ever holds it. The LCA admins are told after the fact, once per event, at its first publish. The first publish of an event whose creator then holds the standing director grant (and not an admin's or the president's) sets `tournaments.review_notified_at`, and the notice goes only while that column is empty, so unpublishing and publishing again, or a scheduled publish, sends no second one. It goes by email to every LCA admin while `ADMIN_HOME_ON` is unset, and once it is set as a `published_review` queue row in place of the email (an event with `review_notified_at` set and `reviewed_at` empty; "Published by Gerald Landry, standing director · Lafayette Winter Quads · Sat, Dec 5 · listed now", with "After the fact" in words, Look it over and Done; `Account-Final-Hub-2` G3). An event the president or an admin creates lists when they publish it, as an admin's does today.
    - **On today's pages** until the console lands (Q11): standing directors get New tournament on `/workspace`'s events list (today's `TournamentList` with `canCreate`), the president on Admin › Tournaments in the view-only Admin officers keep, and both lists show the events the person created; the new event opens in today's manage page, whose access check admits its directors, its creator among them. From Phase 2 the setup checklist takes over at `/workspace/tournaments/new`, and once `myLcaRail` is on (WS13, Phase 4) the way in is New tournament at the head of Events you direct.
  - **Later** (Phase 3, with WS09's My region; assumed, Q1): the regional representatives create events for their region's clubs (Add a club event opens this checklist with the club picked) and name one-event directors on the club events they drafted (D20, D21); `canManageTournament` admits the representative for the club's region (WS09), all with `CLUB_PAGES_ON`. A representative's club event stays a hidden draft until an LCA admin approves it, as `Work-H` and `Work-L`'s inset K1 draw ("Submit for LCA review"; WS09 stores the request and the approval, and the server lists nothing before it; assumed, Q15). Representatives are not on D24's list.
  - **Last** (Phase 4): member-created events, below.
  - **Every event, whoever made it, gets the Rating report stage** (D23).

- **Who may do what on an event.** This one table is the rule for the server's checks (`POST`, `PATCH` and `DELETE /api/admin/tournaments`, `requireCanAssign`, and `canManageTournament` behind `requireTournamentManager`), the setup steps' locks, `test/integration/directors.test.ts` and WS13's Events you direct. Each grant applies from its phase, and a one-event director runs every other part of the event they direct.

  | Action | LCA admin | President | Standing director | Club rep | Regional representative (Phase 3; Q1) | Member organizer (Phase 4) | One-event director |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | Create an event | Any | Always | Their own | Its own club's, as hidden drafts (today's rule; Q2) | For a club in their region | Their own member-created event, while the setting is on | No |
  | Run the event: the setup steps, entries and refunds, the console, close-out and the rating report (the rows below set fees, the membership switch, directors, publishing and deleting) | Any event | The events they direct, their own drafts among them | Their own events, as their first director | Its own club's events (today's rule) | Every club event in their region, a club rep's own included (K: the reps "manage their regions clubs and their tournaments"; WS09) | Their own event, as its first director | The event they direct |
  | Set an entry fee above $0 (D24) | Yes, but not on a member-created event | Yes | Yes, with G2's note | Yes | No: G1's notice | No: G1's notice, and $0 until a connected account exists (Q3) | No; a save that leaves the fees as they are passes |
  | Change the membership switch (D12) | On a club-run or member-created event; an LCA-run event always requires one | No: their events are LCA-run | No: their events are LCA-run (assumed, Q37) | Its own club's events | Club events they drafted | Their own event | No |
  | Name or remove directors (D21) | Any event | Events the president drafted | No | Its own club's events (today's rule; Q2) | Club events they drafted | No: an LCA admin adds one on request | No |
  | Publish | Any event, and Approve and publish for a representative's club event (Q15) | Events they manage, but a representative's club event only once approved | Their own events, listed at once (D22) | Its own club's events, but a representative's club event only once approved (Q15) | Submit for LCA review on the club events they drafted (Q15) | Their own event, by link and never listed | The event they direct, but a representative's club event only once approved |
  | Delete the event | Any event | A draft they created, unpublished and with no entries | The same | The same | The same | The same | No |

  Deleting a draft is new for creators: once `CREATION_ROLES_ON` is set, `DELETE /api/admin/tournaments/[id]` admits an event's creator while they still hold the grant that let them create it and the event is unpublished and has no entries, so nobody is stuck with a draft they no longer need.

- **Entry fees above $0 (D24).** For now only LCA admins, club reps, the president and standing directors may set an entry fee above $0. The rule is about the person saving, and about the event: a save by anyone else that sets or raises any fee above $0 is refused by the server with the same plain words the step shows, and a save that leaves the fees as they are passes, so a one-event director can finish an event the president priced. On a member-created event every fee stays $0 whoever saves, an LCA admin included, until the event has a connected account (D26; none until Q3), since its checkout charges no entry line above $0 (WS06). One helper decides it, `canChargeFees(person, event)` in `domain/events/creation.ts`, which the server and the Fees step both read. The server's refusal follows `CREATION_ROLES_ON`, since the rule narrows today's rights: with it unset, a fee save behaves as today, where anyone `requireTournamentManager` admits, an assigned director included, may set a fee.
  - Anyone else who types a price above $0 sees `Account-Final-Hub-2` G1's information panel, never an error (navy words on the info tint, an "i", no red), titled "Entry fees need a payout account first": "LCA takes entry fees through its own Stripe account. Before your event can charge a fee, LCA sets up an account for you inside LCA's Stripe account, called a connected account (Stripe Connect). It keeps your event's money apart from LCA's and pays it out to you. Until then, your event can run at $0." Under it come two buttons, Keep it at $0 and Ask LCA to set up my payout account (which sends the LCA admins a note: a support ticket of kind `payout_request`, in Needs attention from Phase 4), and the line "Who can charge fees for now: LCA admins, club reps, the president and standing directors." Nobody is left stuck: the event runs at $0.
  - A standing director's fee is accepted, with G2's note under it: "You can charge an entry fee. Fees are paid into LCA's Stripe account. To have them paid straight to you, LCA sets up a connected account for you inside its Stripe account (Stripe Connect), so your event's money stays apart from LCA's. Until then, your fees wait in LCA's account and are paid out to you after the event." K took that default on Mon, Oct 19, 2026, so the board's "Proposed" tag goes.
  - No connected account is built and no payment is routed to one until K answers Q3 (who pays Stripe's fee on a connected account, and who handles refunds and disputes); every online payment still goes into LCA's own account (`functions/utils/stripe.ts`).
- **Member-created events** (D25 to D27; Phase 4):
  - **The switch (D25):** Admin › Site holds "Member-created tournaments", a `site_settings` row (`member_events_enabled`, off) beside `membership_required_for_all`, which only an LCA admin changes; it needs no deploy, and it stays off until K has tested. Turning it off stops new creation only: events already made keep running, and their organizers and entrants see a short plain note with a direct way to reach the LCA admins (WS05 on the page, WS06 at checkout); a member who opens New tournament meanwhile sees "LCA has paused new member-run tournaments. Questions? Contact the LCA admins." (wording assumed, Q23).
  - **Who:** any active LCA member (D26) whose own profile passes `hasActiveMembershipOn` for today (`domain/membership/requirement.ts`; assumed, Q5), who has not had the ability removed, and who ticks the organizer box.
  - **Where they start** (no board draws it): while the setting is on, My LCA's You section shows an active member one line, "Run your own tournament · shared by link, never listed on LCA's pages", opening `/workspace/tournaments/new` (WS13 renders the line); once they have an event, it is in Events you direct with New tournament at its head (assumed, Q19).
  - **The organizer box:** the first step asks one tick, "I am 18 or older, and I am responsible for this event. LCA does not run it.", required to continue and saved with its time (`tournaments.organizer_confirmed_at`); no birthdate or age is asked (D26).
  - **Never listed, shared by link:** the event is marked `member_event = 1` at creation and nothing unmarks it (adopting one into the listed set is left for later). `listedWhere` (`functions/utils/events/listing.ts`, WS02) leaves it out, and every public query of `tournaments` goes through it, every branch of `GET /api/tournaments` included, so it is on no tournaments page, home block, news digest, results list, champions archive, sitemap or structured data (WS02, WS03, WS04, WS09, WS10); its pages carry noindex (WS04). Publishing it makes its page answer to anyone with the link.
  - **Every other function works:** registration, pairings, standings, event day, the scanner, the results page and the rating report.
  - **Three open events at most** per organizer, where an event is open from its creation until it is marked completed or an admin closes it; a fourth is refused with "You have 3 open events, the most at one time. Mark one as completed, or delete a draft you no longer need, to start another." The organizer may delete their own event while it is unpublished and has no entries (the table above).
  - **The organizer directs it:** the organizer is its first director and manages it as that director; naming other directors stays with D21's people, so an LCA admin can add one on request.
  - **Fees (D24):** $0 whoever saves, with G1's notice for the organizer, until the event has a connected account (none until Q3).
  - **Membership (D26):** the event starts with `requires_lca_membership` off, and its organizer may turn it on; `requiresLcaMembership` reads a member-created event's own column, as it reads a club-run event's, while an event with no club and no member marker stays LCA-run and always requires it. `MembershipRequirementTournament` (`domain/membership/requirement.ts`) gains `member_event`, and the PATCH's rule that forces the requirement on for an event with no club, with its 400 (`LCA_RUN_NEEDS_MEMBERSHIP`), applies only while `member_event = 0`, so a later save of any setup step keeps the organizer's choice. `membership_required_for_all` covers every event, member-created ones included (assumed, Q22).
  - **Entrants' details (D27):** the organizer's entrants list and its CSV show each entrant's full name, US Chess ID and email (a parent's or guardian's for a child), as the registration form told them (WS06); on every other event the console keeps Email guardian, which sends through LCA without showing the address.
  - **The rating report:** it carries only the affiliate ID and director IDs the organizer enters as their own. On a member-created event no ID is filled in from LCA's records, and the report settings refuse LCA's affiliate ID with "This is LCA's affiliate ID. An event LCA does not run is rated under your own affiliate's ID." (D23, D26). LCA's affiliate ID is a constant in `domain/events/affiliate.ts` (verify the number with K, or from the report settings of a past LCA event).
  - **Report this event:** every page of a member-created event carries it (WS05 places it on the event page, and WS07's live pages carry the same link); a report becomes a support ticket of kind `event_report` with the event's id, in Needs attention.
  - **Admin tools:** Close this event (`tournaments.closed_at` and `closed_by`; what a closed event shows is WS05's, assumed, Q20), and Remove this member's ability to create member events, or give it back (`member_event_blocks`). Both are LCA admin only and audited, and neither touches the rest of the member's account or their events already running.
  - **The admin's view:** Admin › Tournaments › Member-created events (`/admin/member-events`, LCA admins only; assumed, Q35): every member-created event with its organizer, dates with weekdays, status in words, entrants and reports, and the use of the service, events created and entrants by month and the organizers who ran them (K: "I want to be able to see it as a LCA admin to see how often our services are being used and by who"). Representatives never see member-created events, in Admin, My region or My LCA (WS09, WS13): in Admin only an LCA admin sees them, so a representative who also holds `lca_officer` or `lca_observer` does not (assumed, Q35).
- **TD event-day console** (`Work-E`, `/workspace/tournaments/:id/live`, `tdConsole`), tablet-first, for every event run on this site:
  - **The frame:** the breadcrumb trail "My LCA ▾ › Events you direct › <event> › Event day", where My LCA ▾ opens the folded rail (`Account-Final-Hub-2` F3), with Exit console back to Events you direct; until `myLcaRail` is on (WS13, Phase 4), the trail is "My LCA › <event> › Event day", My LCA opens `/dashboard`, and Exit console goes back to today's events list at `/workspace`. The word Workspace never shows as a title, a menu row or a breadcrumb trail. After a finishing action a Done line gives the way back ("Done · Round 4 published and 26 players told · Back to Today").
  - **Status band** (navy): a small label above the heading with the weekday date and the time, the event's name, and a sub-line with players, sections, rounds and the time control with its plain companion; the next round's start as a weekday and a time, with no countdown; Hall TV and Public page; "Players see: Round 3 in progress · 8 of 13 results confirmed"; the sync pill "Saved on this tablet · synced 9:31 PM · keeps working if the venue Wi-Fi drops", or "Offline · 3 changes waiting · retrying" on its tint with navy words.
  - **Stage rail:** Check-in · Round 1 to Round n · Hall TV · Close-out, each tab with its state in words ("Closed Sat 9:52 AM", "Done", "8 of 13 confirmed · Now", "Sun 9:30 AM"), the current one marked `aria-current` with the word Now beside its gold-ink bar. Tapping a tab shows that stage alone at full width; the rail wraps to two rows under 1100px and never scrolls sideways.
  - **Next step strip,** above every stage: three numbered steps, "Confirm round 3 results · 5 left", "Pair round 4" (unlocks when every result of the round is confirmed) and "Publish round 4 and notify" (unlocks after pairing), one big button at a time; it counts the reports waiting.
  - **Check-in stage:** one search field, "Name, US Chess ID or parent's name" (`GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/checkin-search?q=`), and Scan a card; counts in words (Here · Byes · Not here yet · Withdrew); chips as `FilterChip` (All · Needs a look · Not here · Byes · US Chess flags); 64px rows with one action each, showing the full name, section, rating, the US Chess ID ending, points, the household line ("Robichaux family · Danielle Robichaux", the owner, shown only here behind `requireTournamentManager`; assumed, S7.7), how the player arrived ("scanned the door QR", "tapped in by the TD") and no club (D17). "Check in all 3 from this family" when siblings are in the event and not checked in. QR check-in from the per-profile card (WS12) finds the exact row with no typing. Email guardian sends through LCA without showing the address. Add a player at the door asks for the US Chess ID first, so an existing profile is found and not created twice, and a walk-in's rating is a whole number ("Enter the rating as a whole number, or leave it blank.", WS01 follow-up). Mark everyone left as here. Name fixes after check-in are made here, because a parent's Edit name locks once the child is checked in to a running event. A walk-in child is added as a guest as today, and the payer gets "Save these details, create an account"; the TD membership desk (WS12) sells Family for card or cash and turns walk-in guest children of the paying adult into managed profiles on the spot (assumed, S7.12). The checked state's words read at least 4.5:1 in every look of the console (`--ok-fill`, `#047857`, 5.48:1). Today's manage page keeps its toggle until the console replaces it: it reads 5.48:1 under `newLook` already, and 3.67:1 with it off (WS01 follow-up), which stays until `tdConsole` is on, as WS09 leaves today's club pages' two contrast misses for `newClubs`.
  - **Walk-in card payments:** taken at the desk, these complete through the same webhook path and call `sendPaymentReceipt` (D14); the admin's payment row carries "Send this receipt again".
  - **Entrants list and CSV export:** the TD's entrants list with full names, and the CSV export for directors (moved here from the public table view; assumed, S3.2), with entrants' emails on a member-created event only (above).
  - **Round stage:** the Reported results queue above the board list for the round. Each row shows the board, the players, the result reported, by whom and when, and the other side in words ("Opponent has not reported", "Opponent agrees", "Reports disagree"), with a matching tint. Disagreements come first, then oldest first. "Confirm 1-0" takes one tap, also when both players agree (assumed, P2.5), or "Enter a different result" (the W/D/L/F entry). The TD's entry is final and overrides any report; every report stays on the record with names and times, under the TD's entry, with an optional note, and nothing is deleted. A Status column in words on every board (Confirmed by TD · Reported by White · Reported by Black · Both agree · Reports disagree · Not reported), with Edit on confirmed rows. Counts in words: "8 of 13 confirmed · 3 reported, waiting · 2 not reported". Results entered in the console or from a paper sheet skip the queue; a sheet scanned through WS15 is not an entry, and joins the queue as a "Scoresheet says" row (assumed, Q30). The results grid has keyboard entry (1 White wins, 2 draw, 3 Black wins, arrows between boards), undo, 48px result buttons, and a visible conflict when two devices edit the same board (the server wins and the difference is shown). The public pages show only confirmed results (assumed, P2.4). Pair next round runs the engine in `functions/utils/live/swiss/` and shows a preview before anything is published, and waits until every result of the round is confirmed.
  - **Publish round:** a Notify players toggle, shown, and on by default, only while `roundAlerts` is on, which sends `notify: true` with the publish (WS07); with it off, or with `roundAlerts` off, a publish emails nobody, as today's manage page's publish does.
  - **TD view on the public pages:** the event's TDs and LCA admins get WS07's "TD view | Public view" switch on the pairings and standings pages, with this console's queue and close-out panel (assumed, P2.6).
  - **Hall TV stage:** Show sections (One at a time, cycling every 15 seconds, the stored default · All on one screen, as WS07 describes, marked "Suggested" with the reason in words at 30 players or fewer; assumed, P2.7), "Show standings after results come in", on by default (assumed, P2.8), and the keys for the laptop driving the TV. All on one screen is greyed with the reason in words above 16 boards. The screen changes layout only when the TD picks.
  - **Close-out stage** (the rail's last, and the standings page's TD view): the rating report on every event (D23), due within 7 days of the last round and shown with its weekday date. First the checks before files are built, each with "Passed" in words or what to fix: every result confirmed by a TD with no reports waiting; every result entered, with forfeits marked and left out of rating; US Chess IDs on file and memberships current; bye limits respected; the affiliate ID, the Chief TD's US Chess ID, city, state and ZIP filled in (with the affiliate rule above). Then the three files by name, each with what it holds and a record count, one at a time for US Chess's three upload boxes (a .zip of all three as a backup copy); a read-back preview of the detail file; "Test this file before a real event", with its status in words, the steps for a test run (which stop before US Chess's final submit and fee payment, so no event is rated twice and a practice event is never submitted), a box that takes SwissSys's three files and compares them field by field, and "US Chess accepted the files" or "US Chess rejected a file" with US Chess's exact message; then "Mark as submitted to US Chess", "Send the one-page final report to the LCA board" and "Mark as completed", which calls WS10's Finalize (the snapshot and `finalized_at`) and opens the Recap tab (WS10) once standings are final; until WS10 lands it marks the event completed as today's manage page does. On screen each player's total is written with `formatScore` (½), while the three files and the CSV keep the plain number US Chess reads (today the report table writes `toFixed(1)`; WS01 follow-up). Built on `src/areas/events/components/UsChessUploadPanel.tsx`, `src/areas/events/uschessUpload.ts` and `functions/api/admin/tournaments/[id]/rating-report.ts`, once the US Chess 2C fix to the export lands (`DESIGN_REPLAN_phase2.md` section 3.8; Q8).
  - **Recap tab** after Mark as completed (WS10), for the event's TDs and LCA admins.
  - **Print/QR:** links to the WS07 kit, with the printed checklist (`Live-Final-Print` sheet 7).
  - **Announcements:** a banner on the event page, with an optional email to the event's entrants (through `recipientsFor`, and on a member-created event through `markMemberEventEmail`, section 2.2), and a log of what was sent.
  - **Offline tolerance:** a queue of pending writes with retry and the sync pill's "Offline · 3 changes waiting"; while offline, Publish reads "Publishes when the Wi-Fi is back".
  - **Admin backstop (D6):** LCA admins can open any event's console and do everything its TDs can, so an event never stalls if a TD can't; while they do, the strip "You're here as an LCA admin · <name> directs <event> · your changes are logged · Back to Admin" shows (`Account-Final-Hub-2` H).
- **Setup checklist** (`Work-L`, `/workspace/tournaments/new` and `/:id/setup`, `tdConsole`): one page that replaces `TournamentWizard` and the manage page's setup tabs, one step at a time.
  - **Layout:** the breadcrumb trail "My LCA › Events you direct › <event> › Setup" ("My LCA › <event> › Setup" until `myLcaRail` is on) and Back to My LCA; the band with the status in words ("Draft · LCA event"), the title, "Step 2 of 10 · Sections", "Saved 7:38 PM · leave any time and pick up where you stopped", Preview public page, Save draft and Publish (the gold `Button`, greyed with the reason in words until the required steps are done). Three columns: the steps rail (the ten steps, each with a status word, Done · Now · To do · 1 note · Off · Last step; any step can be opened; the current one with `aria-current="step"` and a gold-ink bar); the step in the middle, with a 56px foot bar "Back · Skip for now (marks To do) · Save and continue"; and the preview, sticky, showing the event card redrawn from the step and "What this step changes on the page". There is no jump list (K, Mon, Oct 19, 2026): a step too long to fit scrolls inside the middle section, which is a `ScrollPanel` (WS01), so the rail stays in view, and a sticky bar never covers a focused control (`scroll-margin`, `--bottom-chrome-h`; D16). At 1024 the rail becomes a top row of numbered chips with the current one named, and the preview drops under the step.
  - **Start from last year:** copies sections, fees, schedule, byes and prizes and moves every date forward a year onto the same weekday, with Undo; a new event starts with every step To do.
  - **Every option is editable after creation,** and a change that would strand paid players is stopped in words ("Dropping U1000 would strand 3 paid players. Move them first from Entries.", `Work-L` inset K3). A refused field is named beside the field ("A section's fee can't be below $0"), never the generic "Some fields are missing or not valid." (WS01 follow-up from the checkpoint B review). A save writes only the columns it changed (WS05's dirty-diff PATCH), so two people saving different steps at once lose nothing (WS01 follow-up "Concurrent edits lose fields").
  - **Steps,** in this order:
    1. **Basics:** dates with weekdays, where, type, format, the time control with its plain companion, the tagline and the championship title (a slug matching `state_champions.title`, WS10). For a member organizer, the organizer box and "This event is never listed on LCA's pages. Share it by its link." (D26).
    2. **Sections:** presets (Open, U1800, U1400, U1000, or K-12, K-8, K-5, K-3), the entry-limit mode (Overall, the default, or Limits by section) with per-section caps, "Where in the room" per section (`tournament_sections.room_note`, WS07; assumed, P2.44), and "Scholastic presets ask one yes/no question, never a grade or birthdate." A rename box renames a section and keeps its id and its entries (WS01 decision 7).
    3. **Fees:** the fee ladder, with Regular required and Early and Late optional, one price per section per tier, shown as a timeline with weekday dates and a preview of the price on any date: "One price per section per tier. There is no member price." (D11). A section's own Early price is a price, not a discount: it applies while `early_deadline` is set and has not passed, in `priceEntry` and in `tierFees` (`domain/events/sections.ts`) alike, and an Early price above Regular is refused ("An early price is lower than the regular price"); the answer says which tier prices are the section's own (`ownFees`), so `priceShownSection` drops its comparison with $0 and an own Early price of $0 is a free early entry, never read as a clamped discount (WS01 follow-ups, settled here before any row has `fee_early`). A section with no fee of its own keeps following the event's fee: `buildSaveSections` keeps `fee_regular` NULL when a save echoes the event fee back for such a section, before directors can change the event fee (WS01 follow-up "Bare-name section pinned to the event fee"). The entry-fee rule and its notices (D24, above). "LCA membership: Required to enter (default) · Not required" (`requires_lca_membership`, D12), which an LCA admin, the organizing club's rep, the region's representative on a club event they drafted (WS09) and a member-created event's organizer can change; everyone else sees it locked with who sets it, and on an LCA-run event, a standing director's among them (assumed, Q37), it is locked on ("Set by LCA admins for LCA events"). While `membership_required_for_all` is on it reads "Required on every event · set by LCA in Admin › Site" and is locked. The switch shows only while `membershipRequirementSwitch` is on, on today's manage page and in this checklist alike, and that switch goes on only once WS06's checkout enforces the requirement (WS01 follow-up "Enforcement at checkout").
    4. **Schedule:** the rounds with weekdays and times and the check-in window, checks for overlapping rounds and for finishing before the venue closes, and the tight-turnarounds note with its two choices (Keep as is · Add 30 min to each gap, `Work-K`). A **Schedules** option, off by default, adds a second schedule with its own label, rounds, time control and merge round; the step writes `tournament_schedules` rows, with the rounds in `tournament_schedule_rounds` (0052). A save that switches the primary schedule sends both labels, and a label that already ends in "schedule" is not given a second one in messages (WS01 follow-up "Schedules in WS06 and WS08").
    5. **Byes & rules,** using `SectionRulesEditor`: half-point byes per player, a last round with zero-point byes only, when requests close, and the lapsed US Chess membership choice (Warn · Warn + email · Block).
    6. **Venue & travel:** the venue fields always (name, address, parking, getting in, food; `venue_notes`), a "Travel details" switch (airport, drive times, between rounds) and "Add a hotel" (host hotel, LCA rate, block deadline, booking link), together `travel_json`; and "good to know".
    7. **Prizes,** using `PrizesEditor`, "based on N entries".
    8. **Side events,** off by default: child events of kind `side` or `meeting`, each with its own sections, fees and caps.
    9. **Directors:** the Chief TD and any one-event directors, each with their US Chess TD ID, and the naming rule in words, built for that event from the table above (on an event the president drafted, "Directors on this event are named by LCA admins and the president; a director can't add or remove directors"; on a club's event, "by LCA admins and the club's rep", with the region's representative from WS09's phase on a club event they drafted); the step is open to whoever that table lets name directors on the event.
    10. **Publish:** the display override (Automatic · Before · During · After, `display_state`), the share card (WS04) with the event's link and Copy, the steps summary, and Publish now or on a date and time (`tournaments.publish_at`); a representative's club event takes no date until an LCA admin approves it, since Approve and publish lists it (Q15). It reads "Lists on Tournaments as soon as you publish. LCA admins get a note to look it over afterwards." for a standing director (G3), "Submit for LCA review" for a representative's club event (WS09), and "Anyone with the link can open it. It is never listed on LCA's pages." for a member organizer. The two boxes `Work-K` draws under Publish, "Post it to LCA News" and "Email members who follow <region> events", are not built: no workstream builds follows by region, and News shows finished events through WS10's Recent tournaments (assumed, Q21).
- **Admin home** (`Work-J`, `/admin`, `adminHome`; LCA admins signed in with two-step sign-in, and officers in today's view-only Admin, `Account-P`):
  - **Frame:** the breadcrumb trail "My LCA › Admin" and Back to My LCA. The screens have one main landmark: today `src/pages/AdminPage.tsx` draws a `main` inside the site's own (line 173), and the inner one becomes a plain element (WS01 follow-up).
  - **The grouped sidebar** (`Work-I`, shared with `Work-J`): Home; Needs attention · count; Members (Members & export, Roles & access, Log in as a member); Tournaments (Tournaments, New tournament, Season schedule from WS11, and Member-created events from Phase 4, for LCA admins only, assumed, Q35); Clubs; Content (News from WS10, Site banner, Festivals from WS05); Board (Board seats & regions, Board inbox); Email (Group email & history); Site (Membership required for all, Member-created tournaments, Automations & health); Audit (Audit log). The current row carries `aria-current` and a gold-ink bar; at 1024 the sidebar becomes a select.
  - **The home:** the title with its weekday date; a shortcuts row (New tournament · Post a site banner · Email a group · Log in as a member · Export members); on the left, the Needs attention card with the five rows that rank first, each with its source in words, waiting time, owner and action, and Open the queue (12), then "Assigned to you"; on the right, the site banner card ("Live now", the day it ends, Take down now), the automations card and the latest audit lines with Open the audit log. Empty: "The queue is empty." with the last change; busy: still five rows and Open the queue (40). At 1024 the columns stack with the queue first.
  - **The health of the automations at a glance** (K's reason for `Work-J`): one row per scheduled job with its last run in words and its outcome beside its colour: "Gulf South event sync · Ran 5:00 AM · OK", "Daily emails · Ran 6:00 AM · 41 sent, 2 failed · Check", "Stripe payments · last webhook 8:14 AM · OK", "Facebook mirror · refreshed 7:00 AM · OK" (WS10), "Monthly club check emails · next run Mon, Nov 2 · Scheduled" (WS09). A job that missed its expected run says so ("Didn't run since Sat 6:00 AM"). A failed or missed run also lands in Needs attention until someone marks it seen, and the card heads "Automations · 1 needs a look" while any does.
  - **Needs attention** (`/admin/needs-attention`, `Work-J` frame B): one table over every source, with owner, waiting time in words (amber words from 7 days) and one action per row; count tiles that filter; chips All · Assigned to me · Waiting 2+ days · Open seats; "Email me this queue weekdays at 7:00 AM"; failures stay until someone resolves them, and nothing important shows only as a pop-up. Admins can assign and reassign. The sources:
    - open support tickets, other than those of a kind listed below
    - board inbox tickets, the Champions tab's "Missing years" card among them (assumed, P2.36)
    - approvals: a representative's club event sent for review and not yet approved (`approval_requested_at` set and `approved_at` empty, WS09), with the Review sheet (`Work-I` inset I1: the summary; Approve and publish, which sets `approved_at` and lists it; Ask for changes, which clears the request and emails the representative; Open the setup; assumed, Q15); champion submissions are not a source (assumed, P2.36)
    - failed emails: campaign recipients and the notifications outbox
    - club reports: a visitor's report or suggestion unanswered at 14 days, or one for a club whose region's seat is open (WS09)
    - rep requests: a request to be named a club's rep while the region's seat is open, with the admins as the backstop (D17, WS09)
    - partner-listing problem reports (`listing_report`, WS04)
    - a standing director's published event to look over (`published_review`: `review_notified_at` set and `reviewed_at` empty, D22)
    - "Report this event" on a member-created event (`event_report`, D26), for LCA admins only (assumed, Q35)
    - a request for a payout account (`payout_request`, D24)
    - a failed or missed automation run (`automation_failure`)
  - A `listing_report`, `event_report` or `payout_request` ticket counts once, under its own source.
  - **Admin › Site:** "Membership required for all LCA events" (`membership_required_for_all`, D12), with a line saying what it does, which replaces the constant `FORCE_LCA_MEMBERSHIP` in `domain/membership/requirement.ts` (removed with its test; WS01 follow-up "Enforcement at checkout"); "Member-created tournaments" (`member_events_enabled`, D25); both LCA admin only, audited, and live with no deploy. The site banner and the automations panel sit here too.
  - **Admin › Members › Roles & access:** the role list names `tournament_director` "Standing director", with one line on what it allows (D20, Q4), and offers Remove ability to create member events (D26).
  - **Categories are not statuses:** under `newLook`, where the clash is (blue and purple both take the partner tag's navy there), the audit log's entry kinds (`src/areas/admin/components/AuditLogPanel.tsx`) and the support ticket badges (`src/pages/AdminSupportPage.tsx`, `SupportPage.tsx`) show as worded tags in one neutral look, never a status colour; with `newLook` off they keep today's colours. Status words and colours stay on statuses (WS01 follow-up "Categories drawn in a tone's colour", which WS13 follows).
  - **The email preview** (`src/pages/AdminEmailPage.tsx`) draws `/email-logo.jpg`, the 280-pixel file the real email uses, in place of the 2.4 MB `/lca-logo.jpg` (WS01 follow-up). It is the same picture at the same size, so nothing anyone sees changes and it needs no switch; only the download shrinks. `public/lca-logo.jpg` stays in the deployed files as the logo's source (assumed, Q24).
  - The existing tools stay but move under the groups: group email, site banner, members table and export, board seats, champions, posts, the audit log and Log in as (with a reason saved to the audit log). The admin lists that use `ADMIN_SCROLL` today scroll in `ScrollPanel` (WS01 follow-up).
  - A direct link to an Admin screen opened in a fresh tab lands on that screen, not on `/dashboard` (verify with a real sign-in first; if the WS01 finding holds, `memberLoading` starts true when a session is found).
  - `GET /api/admin/summary` gives WS13's Admin row its counts.

**Data model**
```sql
-- Phase 1 (standingDirectors): no table. The standing director is today's tournament_director role (Q4), the president is a seat
-- (board_members, board_seat_assignments), one-event directors are tournament_directors rows, and the creator is created_by.
-- 00xx_ticket_kinds.sql (Phase 1, ahead of WS04, section 4): the ticket kinds, which WS04's listing_report also uses, and the mark
-- that the D22 notice has gone
ALTER TABLE support_tickets ADD COLUMN kind TEXT;       -- NULL for an ordinary ticket; 'listing_report', 'event_report' or 'payout_request'
ALTER TABLE support_tickets ADD COLUMN about_id TEXT;   -- the clearinghouse listing or the tournament the ticket is about
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN review_notified_at TEXT;   -- the D22 notice went at this event's first publish; it is sent only while NULL

-- 00xx_console.sql (Phase 2, tdConsole)
CREATE TABLE rating_report_tests (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  export_version TEXT NOT NULL,      -- changes whenever the file writer changes
  outcome TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('accepted','rejected')),
  message TEXT,                      -- US Chess's exact words on a rejection
  recorded_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  recorded_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN rating_submitted_at TEXT;
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN rating_submitted_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN cloned_from_id TEXT REFERENCES tournaments(id);   -- TEXT, not INTEGER (0.2)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN publish_at TEXT;                                   -- Publish on a date and time
CREATE TABLE automation_runs (                     -- created here, in Phase 2, before any later job records a run
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  job TEXT NOT NULL,                 -- 'clearinghouse_sync', or 'daily:<phase>' and 'five_minute:<phase>' for the two runs of workers/daily-emails (2.2)
  started_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  finished_at TEXT,
  outcome TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (outcome IN ('ok','partial','failed')),
  sent_count INTEGER, failed_count INTEGER,
  detail TEXT,                       -- one plain line for the admin; never a secret, a token or an email address
  seen_at TEXT, seen_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_automation_runs_job ON automation_runs(job, started_at);
-- registrations.checked_in_at exists (checked on main): check-in reuses it.

-- 00xx_admin_queue.sql (Phase 4, adminHome)
CREATE TABLE queue_assignments (
  item_kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (item_kind IN ('support','board_ticket','approval','email_failure','club_report','rep_request',
    'listing_report','published_review','event_report','payout_request','automation_failure')),   -- every source this brief names; a new one later needs a table rebuild
  item_id TEXT NOT NULL, assignee_member_id TEXT REFERENCES members(id),
  updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')), PRIMARY KEY (item_kind, item_id)
);
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN reviewed_at TEXT;                         -- Done on a standing director's published event (D22)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN reviewed_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
UPDATE tournaments SET reviewed_at = review_notified_at WHERE review_notified_at IS NOT NULL;   -- the events already emailed (Phases 1 to 3)
                                                                -- start reviewed, so the queue lists those published from then on; reviewed_by stays NULL
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN queue_digest INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;      -- "Email me this queue weekdays at 7:00 AM", per admin

-- 00xx_member_events.sql (Phase 4, with the member_events_enabled setting)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN member_event INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (member_event IN (0,1));   -- set at creation under D26; never listed; nothing unsets it
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN organizer_confirmed_at TEXT;   -- the organizer's one tick (18 or older, responsible, not run by LCA), with its time; no birthdate
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN closed_at TEXT;                -- an LCA admin closed it (D26)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN closed_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
CREATE INDEX idx_tournaments_member_event ON tournaments(member_event, created_by);
CREATE TABLE member_event_blocks (               -- a row means the member may not create a member event; deleting it gives the ability back
  member_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES members(id),
  blocked_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  reason TEXT,
  blocked_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
INSERT OR IGNORE INTO site_settings (key, value) VALUES ('member_events_enabled', '0');   -- off until K has tested (D25)
```
The Rating report step reads "Not yet tested with US Chess" until a row with `outcome = 'accepted'` exists for the current `export_version`, so a change to the file writer asks for a fresh test. Each migration is written with `npm run db:generate` and the Drizzle schema in `functions/db/schema.ts`, with `--custom` for the columns that carry a foreign key.

**API**
- **Creation.** `POST /api/admin/tournaments` (exists): who may create widens from an LCA admin and a `club_rep` for its own club to standing directors and the president while `CREATION_ROLES_ON` is set, then to a regional representative for a club in their region (WS09), then to any active member while `member_events_enabled` is on. One helper in `domain/events/creation.ts` (with its database half in `functions/utils/events/creation.ts`) works out the person's grant (admin, president, standing director, club rep, regional representative, member, or none) and answers `canCreate`, `canChargeFees` and `lcaAffiliateAllowed`. A new event is a hidden draft as today, with `created_by` set; a standing director, the president or a member organizer is written as its first director (a `tournament_directors` row), the one way a creator runs an event, since `canManageTournament` never reads `created_by`. A member's create sets `member_event = 1` and `requires_lca_membership = 0`, needs `organizerConfirmed: true` (400 without it), and is refused while the setting is off (403 with the paused note), while the member is blocked (403), without an active membership (403) and at three open events (409).
- `GET /api/me/tournament-creation` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): what the signed-in person may create (their grant, whether they may charge fees, and for a member their open member-created events out of three and whether the setting is on), read by the setup checklist, Events you direct and My LCA's line (WS13).
- `PATCH /api/admin/tournaments/[id]` (exists): the D24 fee check on any save that sets or raises a fee above $0 (once `CREATION_ROLES_ON` is set), and every fee held at $0 on a member-created event; who may change `requires_lca_membership`, as the table above says (today a `club_rep` or an admin), with the forced-on rule and its 400 only while `member_event = 0`; `publish_at`, refused on a representative's club event until an LCA admin approves it, as `isVisible = 1` is (Q15); the D22 notice at the first publish of a standing director's event, only while `review_notified_at` is empty; a member-created event stays unlisted whatever it publishes; `isVisible = 1` on a representative's club event refused, whoever saves it, until an LCA admin approves it (WS09; assumed, Q15); refusals name the field (`{ error, fields }`); the report settings refuse LCA's affiliate ID on a member-created event.
- `POST` and `DELETE /api/admin/tournaments/:id/directors` (exist): `requireCanAssign` follows the table above: it also admits the event's creator when that is the president (D21), and from WS09 the region's representative on a club event they drafted; a one-event director still gets 403. `DELETE /api/admin/tournaments/[id]` admits an LCA admin on any event and, once `CREATION_ROLES_ON` is set, the event's creator while they still hold the grant that let them create it and the event is unpublished and has no entries.
- `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/reviewed` (new; `requireAdmin`, audited): Done on a `published_review` row.
- `POST /api/me/payout-request` (new; `requireAuthedMember`, one open request per member): a support ticket of kind `payout_request`; the admins see it in today's support list while `ADMIN_HOME_ON` is unset, and in Needs attention once it is set.
- **Console.** `GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/checkin-search?q=` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): the one check-in search over name, US Chess ID and the owner's name, returning the household line only here. `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/checkin`: takes a registration id or a signed member-card token. Pairing and publishing extend `functions/api/admin/tournaments/[id]/generate-pairings.ts` and `rounds/[round].ts` (verify what each does today) and the helpers in `functions/utils/events/tournament-manage.ts`. `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/clone` (new). Once `TD_CONSOLE_ON` is set, the walk-in request's rating is `.int()` with its plain message (`walkInRequestSchema`); with it unset the endpoint takes the number it takes today, since today's manage page posts to it.
- **Reports, TV and the rating report** (from the phase 2 replan): `GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/reports?round=` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): the queue, disagreements first, then oldest first. `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/reports/:reportId/confirm` (new; `requireTournamentManager`, audited): writes `tournament_games.result`, `result_confirmed_by` and `result_confirmed_at` through the same path as the results grid, and marks the game's reports; the results grid's write marks open reports `accepted` or `overridden` and sets the two confirmation columns. `PATCH /api/admin/tournaments/:id/tv` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): `tv_layout` and `tv_show_standings`. `GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/rating-report` (exists): once `TD_CONSOLE_ON` is set, adds the checks and answers 409 with the failing checks while a result is unconfirmed or a report waits, and with it unset answers as today, since today's manage page reads it; the three files are still built in the browser from its answer by `buildUploadFiles` in `src/areas/events/uschessUpload.ts`, the writer the 2C fix and its SwissSys comparison test cover; it is for everyone `requireTournamentManager` admits for the event (assumed, P2.9; today it uses `requireTournamentView`, which also lets the view-only roles read it: verify whether they keep that); on a member-created event its suggested director IDs are empty; its refusal "Some games still have pending results" loses its dash ("... results. Enter all results before submitting.") once `TD_CONSOLE_ON` is set, and keeps today's words until then (0.2). `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/rating-report/tests` and `POST .../rating-report/submitted` (new; `requireTournamentManager`, audited). Mark as completed calls WS10's `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/finalize` once WS10 lands, and until then sets the event's status to completed as today.
- **Admin.** `GET /api/admin/queue` (new; `requireAdminView`): every source in one list, each ticket of a kind counted once under its source; `event_report` rows, and any row about a member-created event, go to LCA admins only (assumed, Q35). `POST /api/admin/queue/assign` (new; `requireAdmin`). `PATCH /api/admin/queue/digest` (new; `requireAdmin`): the admin's own 7:00 AM email. `GET` and `PATCH /api/admin/site-settings` (new; `requireAdmin`, audited): `membership_required_for_all` (D12) and `member_events_enabled` (D25), through `functions/utils/siteSettings.ts`. `GET /api/admin/automations` (new; `requireAdminView`) and `POST /api/admin/automations/:id/seen` (new; `requireAdmin`). `GET /api/admin/summary` (new; `requireAdmin`): the counts for WS13's Admin row, none without two-step sign-in.
- **Member-created events.** `GET /api/admin/member-events` (new; `requireAdmin`): every member-created event and the monthly use; a regional representative, an officer and the president get 403 (assumed, Q35), and the branch of `GET /api/tournaments` for the view-only roles leaves out member-created events other than the person's own (organized, directed or entered), so a representative who also holds `lca_officer` or `lca_observer` sees none there. `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/close` (new; `requireAdmin`, audited). `PUT` and `DELETE /api/admin/members/:id/event-creation-block` (new; `requireAdmin`, audited). `POST /api/tournaments/:id/report` (new; public, rate-limited and spam-checked with WS07's `functions/utils/rateLimit.ts` and WS09's `functions/utils/spamCheck.ts`): "Report this event", for member-created events only (404 otherwise), as a support ticket of kind `event_report`.
- Every write goes through `functions/utils/admin/audit.ts`, and every endpoint this workstream adds or changes takes its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test and leaves `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` (0.2).

**Frontend**
- Change:
  - `src/pages/TournamentManagePage.tsx` (2,380 lines at the end of WS01) gives way to two thin pages, `src/pages/EventSetupPage.tsx` (`/workspace/tournaments/new` and `/:id/setup`) and `src/pages/EventConsolePage.tsx` (`/:id/live`), composed from `src/areas/workspace/components`; once `tdConsole` is on, `/admin/tournaments/:id` goes to the event's setup. These routes do not take today's `/workspace` guard (`WORKSPACE_ROLES` or a director assignment, `src/App.tsx`), which would turn away the president and a member organizer: `/workspace/tournaments/new` admits whoever `GET /api/me/tournament-creation` grants, and `/:id/setup`, `/:id/live` and `/:id/recap` admit the event's managers (`canManageTournament`). The manage page's bare ISO date tooltip goes with it (WS01 follow-ups "Pages to thin in WS05, WS06 and WS08" and from step 10).

  - `src/areas/admin/components/TournamentList.tsx` (`canCreate` and the creator's own events), `src/areas/admin/roles.ts` ("Standing director"), `src/pages/AdminEmailPage.tsx`, `src/areas/admin/components/AuditLogPanel.tsx`, `src/pages/AdminSupportPage.tsx` and `src/pages/SupportPage.tsx`.
- New, in `src/areas/workspace/components/` (a new area, which the boundary rules pick up from the folder): for the console, `ConsoleStatusBand.tsx`, `StageRail.tsx` (in place of the planned `RoundRail.tsx`), `NextStepStrip.tsx` (in place of `NextStepCard.tsx`), `CheckInStage.tsx`, `QrCheckIn.tsx`, `ResultsGrid.tsx`, `ReportedResultsQueue.tsx`, `PublishRoundDialog.tsx`, `HallTvSettings.tsx`, `CloseOutStage.tsx` with `RatingReportStep.tsx` (built on `UsChessUploadPanel.tsx`), `OfflineQueue.tsx` and `AdminBackstopStrip.tsx`; for setup, `SetupChecklist.tsx`, `SetupStepsRail.tsx`, `SetupPreview.tsx`, one file per step (`BasicsStep.tsx` to `PublishStep.tsx`), `FeeNotice.tsx` (G1 and G2) and `OrganizerBox.tsx`. In `src/areas/admin/components/`: `AdminSidebar.tsx`, `AdminHome.tsx`, `NeedsAttentionQueue.tsx`, `ReviewSheet.tsx`, `AutomationHealth.tsx`, `SiteSettingsPanel.tsx` and `MemberEventsPanel.tsx`.
- From WS01: `StatusBadge` and `StatusDot` (status in words), `FilterChip` (the check-in and queue chips), `ScrollPanel` (the setup step, the admin lists), the table primitives with a sticky first column and the `bounded` and `label` props (the results grid, the check-in list, the queue), `ResultCell`, the gold `Button` (Publish), and `formatDate` and `formatTime` with `{ stored: 'utc' }` for stored times (audit lines, run times, waiting times) and `formatScore`.
- The stage rail, the steps rail and the sidebar mark the current item with `aria-current` and take `FilterChip`'s selected look rather than a navy fill that dark mode hides (WS01 follow-up "Selected navy controls in dark mode").

**Jobs & integrations**
- QR scanning through the camera, using `BarcodeDetector` with a JS fallback library, pinned exactly and at least two weeks old.
- `sendPaymentReceipt` for walk-in card payments (WS06, D14).
- The email to every LCA admin at the first publish of a standing director's event while `ADMIN_HOME_ON` is unset; once it is set, a `published_review` queue row instead.
- Scheduled publish: a phase of the five-minute run of `workers/daily-emails` (section 2.2) publishes events whose `publish_at` has passed and that need no LCA approval or have it (`needs_lca_approval = 0`, or `approved_at` set; Q15), and sends the D22 note where it applies, only while `review_notified_at` is empty.
- The queue email, weekdays at 7:00 AM Louisiana time, to each admin who ticked it (a timed phase of the five-minute run, section 2.2).
- Every scheduled job records each run in `automation_runs` through `recordRun` in `functions/utils/admin/automations.ts`, which also lists each job's expected interval: `workers/clearinghouse-sync`, and each phase of both runs of `workers/daily-emails` (section 2.2), from today's daily phases and the campaign sweep to WS06's expiry, this scheduled publish, WS07's outbox sender, the queue email, WS09's monthly club check, WS10's Facebook sync and digest and WS15's sweep. Stripe's last webhook is read from `stripe_events` (WS06). The daily run deletes runs older than 90 days.
- Stripe: no Stripe Connect until K answers Q3.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. The console works at 1024×768 without horizontal scrolling, its stage rail wraps to two rows under 1100px, and every tap target is at least 44 px (check-in rows 64 px).
2. Checking in any of 100 players takes at most 2 taps from search. QR check-in marks the correct registration and rejects forged or expired tokens.
3. Publishing a round makes the pairings public; with Notify players on (`roundAlerts` on) it enqueues alerts exactly once, and with it off, or from today's manage page, it enqueues none.
4. Results entry supports keyboard and undo, and shows a conflict when two devices edit the same board.
5. Cloning produces an event that matches last year's sections and fees, with dates on the same weekdays.
6. The admin queue shows items from every source listed under Needs attention with owner and waiting time; a `listing_report`, `event_report` or `payout_request` ticket appears once, under its own source; assigning an item updates it immediately and writes an audit entry.
7. An LCA admin who isn't a TD of the event can pair, enter results and publish rounds on it (D6), and sees the backstop strip while doing so.
8. A player's report never writes `tournament_games.result`. Confirm writes the reported result once; Enter a different result writes the TD's result and marks every open report for that game `accepted` or `overridden`; a second report that disagrees flags the board "Reports disagree" and moves it to the top of the queue.
9. The rating report's three files match US Chess's published file layout for the header, section and detail files (field names, types and widths, each section's round count (S_TOT_RNDS) and last pairing number (S_LST_PAIR) matching its detail records, and opponent 0 on every bye, forfeit and unplayed round), checked by an automated test on a fixture event and compared field by field with a SwissSys reference set for the same event; the files import into US Chess's rating report system without hand edits. Until a TD records an accepted test upload for the current file writer, the step reads "Not yet tested with US Chess".
10. The rating report step builds no file while any result is unconfirmed or any report waits, and names what to fix.
11. Only those `requireTournamentManager` admits for the event can change the hall TV setting, and the TV follows it within 20 seconds.
12. With `CREATION_ROLES_ON` set, a standing director and the president can create an event, and an LCA admin and a club rep for its own club can as before, while a plain member (with the member setting off), an officer who is not the president, a one-event director and an auditor get 403; with it unset, the standing director and the president get 403 as today. A standing director's, the president's and a member organizer's new event has its creator as its first director in `tournament_directors`; an event's creator who holds no director row and has lost the grant that let them create it, such as a former club rep or a former LCA admin, gets 403 on its setup, its entrants and refunds, its console and its rating report. A unit test holds `CREATION_ROLES_ON` equal to `standingDirectors`.
13. A standing director's event appears in the public lists the moment it is published, with nothing holding it, and the LCA admins get exactly one notice for it (an email while `ADMIN_HOME_ON` is unset; one `published_review` row once it is set), however often it is unpublished and published again and whether it is published by hand or on its date; Done clears the row and writes an audit entry; the Phase 4 migration starts every event already emailed as reviewed.
14. The president can name a one-event director on an event the president created; that director can save every setup step and use the console, but gets 403 on the membership switch, on naming or removing directors and on deleting the event; an officer who is not the president gets 403 on naming; every cell of the table "Who may do what on an event" holds, grant by grant, and an event's creator can delete it while they still hold the grant that let them create it and it is unpublished and has no entries, and gets 403 once it is published or has an entry, or once they have lost that grant.
15. A save by anyone not on D24's list that sets or raises a fee above $0 is refused with the plain words, while a save that leaves the fees as they were passes; on a member-created event an LCA admin's save of a fee above $0 is refused too; the Fees step shows G1's panel with Keep it at $0 and Ask LCA to set up my payout account, which opens exactly one `payout_request` ticket; a standing director's fee is accepted with G2's note; no checkout sends money anywhere but LCA's account. With `CREATION_ROLES_ON` unset, fee saves behave as today: an assigned director's save of a fee above $0 passes.
16. With `member_events_enabled` off, a member who may not otherwise create gets 403 with the paused note and no event is made. With it on, an active member who ticks the organizer box creates an event with `member_event = 1`, `requires_lca_membership = 0` and the tick's time saved; without the tick, without an active membership, while blocked, or with three open events, the create is refused with the matching plain message; a later save of any setup step keeps `requires_lca_membership` at 0 until the organizer turns it on. Turning the setting off again leaves every existing event working.
17. A member-created event appears in no public list (a test walks every public query of `tournaments` through `listedWhere`, every branch of `GET /api/tournaments` included, where only its organizer, its directors and entrants, and LCA admins get it) and opens by its link for anyone once published.
18. On a member-created event the rating report suggests no director ID and refuses LCA's affiliate ID with the plain message; on a listed event, and on an event whose creator is a standing director, the president, an LCA admin or a regional representative, LCA's affiliate ID is accepted.
19. "Report this event" creates one `event_report` row in Needs attention; Close this event and Remove the ability to create are LCA admin only and each writes an audit entry; `/admin/member-events` lists every member-created event with its organizer and the monthly counts, and a regional representative, an officer (a representative who holds `lca_officer` included), the president and a plain member get 403 from its endpoint and see no `event_report` row in the queue (assumed, Q35).
20. The setup checklist shows one step at a time with the steps rail always in view: a step taller than the screen scrolls inside the middle `ScrollPanel`, there is no jump list, and Shift+Tab onto a control under the sticky header or above the foot bar leaves it fully visible with its whole focus ring, including at the panel's sides (WS01 follow-up "ScrollPanel on the long screens").
21. A section's own Early price applies until `early_deadline` passes, in `priceEntry` and `tierFees` alike; an own Early price of $0 is charged as $0 until then; an Early price above Regular is refused; a section with no fee of its own still follows the event fee after its sections are saved again.
22. Admin › Site's "Membership required for all LCA events" changes whether every event requires a membership with no deploy, only an LCA admin can change it, each change writes an audit entry, and `FORCE_LCA_MEMBERSHIP` no longer exists.
23. The automations card shows one row per job with its last run in words and its outcome beside its colour; a failed run and a job that missed its expected run show in words and land in Needs attention until marked seen.
24. In the console the checked check-in toggle's words read at least 4.5:1 in every look; under `newLook` today's manage page's toggle already does, and with it off today's page is unchanged.
25. Under `newLook` the audit log's kinds and the support ticket badges show as worded tags in one neutral look, and no two kinds share a status colour; with it off they keep today's colours.
26. The rating report screen writes each player's total with ½ (`formatScore`), while the three files and the CSV keep the plain number US Chess reads.
27. The email preview loads the 280-pixel `/email-logo.jpg`, never the 2.4 MB `/lca-logo.jpg`.
28. `/admin` and `/admin/:section` have one main landmark each; a console stage, a setup step and the admin home pass axe in every look at 1280 and 390 px through fixture pages until the test servers can sign in, and then `/workspace`, `/admin`, `/admin/:section`, `/admin/clubs/:id` and the setup and console routes join `PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` (WS01 follow-up "The pages AC1 does not name").
29. Every kind of email about a member-created event (the registration confirmation, the waitlist offer, the manage link, each reminder kind, the round email, the TD's announcement and, once WS15 lands, the scanner's ready email and linked-game notice) carries "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." under its heading and has the organizer's email as its reply-to, through `markMemberEventEmail`; the same kinds about an LCA event carry neither.
30. With `TD_CONSOLE_ON` unset, `GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/rating-report` answers as today, with today's words; with it set it answers the checks and the refusal's new words. A unit test holds `TD_CONSOLE_ON` equal to `tdConsole`.

**Tests:**
- Integration:
  - `tournament-create.test.ts` (extend): every grant in criterion 12, with `CREATION_ROLES_ON` set and unset, the hidden draft, the creator as first director, the creator's delete of a draft (criterion 14), and the member-created cases of criterion 16; role-safety cases: a former club rep and a former LCA admin who created an event get 403 on its setup save, its entrants list, a refund, its console and its rating report, and on deleting its draft.
  - `directors.test.ts` (extend): criterion 14, cell by cell for the table's naming and membership rows.
  - `entry-fees.test.ts` (new): criterion 15, with role-safety cases, an LCA admin's refused fee on a member-created event, and, with `CREATION_ROLES_ON` unset, an assigned director's fee save passing as today.
  - `published-review.test.ts` (new): criterion 13: the email through the harness's outbox with `ADMIN_HOME_ON` unset and the queue row with it set, no second notice after unpublishing and publishing again or after a scheduled publish, a scheduled publish that skips a representative's club event not yet approved, and the Phase 4 backfill.
  - `member-events.test.ts` (new): criteria 16 to 19, including the walk of every public query through `listedWhere`; a save after creation keeping `requires_lca_membership` at 0; the organizer deleting an unpublished event with no entries; and an officer who is also a representative, and the president, getting 403 from `/admin/member-events` and seeing no `event_report` row in the queue and no member-created event but their own in `GET /api/tournaments`.
  - `queue.test.ts` (new): aggregation over every source, assignment, and the digest choice.
  - `console.test.ts` (new): pair and publish, check-in by token and by search, clone, the whole-number walk-in rating with `TD_CONSOLE_ON` set, and today's answer with it unset, two devices saving the same board, the server's copy winning with the difference in its answer (criterion 4), and an LCA admin who is not one of the event's TDs pairing, entering a result and publishing a round (criterion 7).
  - `member-event-emails.test.ts` (new, Phase 4): criterion 29, walking every kind of event email through the harness's email outbox for a member-created event and for an LCA event (WS15's two kinds join when it lands).
  - `hall-tv.test.ts` (new): criterion 11: the setting saved by everyone `requireTournamentManager` admits, 403 for a player and for a TD of another event, and the live answer carrying the layout so the TV follows on its next 20-second check.
  - `result-reports-queue.test.ts` (new): criterion 8 (confirm, override, the disagreement order; a player and a TD of another event get 403).
  - `rating-upload.test.ts` (extend): the checks, 409 while reports wait, the test and submitted records, the affiliate rule of criterion 18, role safety (a player and a TD of another event get 403), and today's answer and words with `TD_CONSOLE_ON` unset (criterion 30).
  - `site-settings.test.ts` (new): both settings, LCA admin only, audited, and WS06's checkout reading `membership_required_for_all`.
  - `automations.test.ts` (new): a run recorded per job, a missed run, the queue row and Mark seen.
  - `sections-writer.test.ts` (extend): the bare-name section keeps following the event fee.
  - Extend `role-safety.test.ts`.
- Unit:
  - `test/unit/creation-rules.test.ts` (new): the grants, `canChargeFees` (a member-created event's fees held at $0 whoever saves), `lcaAffiliateAllowed`, the open-events count and every cell of the table "Who may do what on an event".
  - `test/unit/creation-switch.test.ts` (new): `CREATION_ROLES_ON` equals `standingDirectors`; `test/unit/td-console-switch.test.ts` (new): `TD_CONSOLE_ON` equals `tdConsole`; `test/unit/admin-home-switch.test.ts` (new): `ADMIN_HOME_ON` equals `adminHome`.
  - `test/unit/admin-backstop-strip.test.tsx` (new): the strip shows for an LCA admin in the console of an event they do not direct, with the director's name and Back to Admin, and never for one of the event's TDs (criterion 7).
  - `test/unit/results-grid.test.tsx` (new): keyboard entry (1, 2 and 3, and the arrows between boards) and undo (criterion 4).
  - `test/unit/category-tags.test.tsx` (new): criterion 25; `test/unit/rating-report-step.test.tsx` (new): criterion 26; `test/unit/admin-email-preview.test.tsx` (new): criterion 27; `test/unit/admin-landmark.test.tsx` (new): `AdminPage` draws no `main` of its own, so `/admin` and `/admin/:section` hold one (criterion 28).
  - `test/unit/app-routes.test.tsx` (extended): `/workspace/tournaments/new` for the president and a member organizer, and `/:id/setup`, `/:id/live` and `/:id/recap` for the event's managers only.
  - `test/unit/uschess-upload.test.ts` (extend): the dBase writer with fixtures covering a full-point bye, a half-point bye, a forfeit, a withdrawal, an unrated player, two sections and a name with accents, and a golden-file comparison with a SwissSys reference set.
  - `test/unit/pricing.test.ts` and `test/unit/sections-domain.test.ts` (extend): criterion 21.
