- Browser: `test/a11y/console.spec.ts` (new): fixture pages for a console stage, a setup step and the admin home at 1024×768 and 390 px, with no sideways scroll, the Shift+Tab check and axe in every look, and the checked check-in toggle's words measured in every look (criterion 24).
- `test/e2e/publish-round.spec.ts` drops `test.fixme` with WS07, once this workstream's publish action ships (WS01 follow-up "Remove test.fixme").

**Depends on:** WS01; WS05 (the event page and the `site_settings` table), WS06 (orders, receipts, the signed tokens and `stripe_events`), WS07's migration (the live tables and columns the console and setup write, which lands first as a PR of its own, section 4) and WS07's pages (the TD view and player reports), which land after the console (section 4). QR check-in also needs WS12. The creation roles need only WS01 and today's pages (Phase 1; assumed, Q11). The Rating report step builds on the US Chess 2C fix (`docs/redesign/uschess-fix-brief.md`; Q8). Member-created events need WS02's `listedWhere`, WS04's head and WS05, WS06, WS07 and WS10's parts in Phase 4, and WS07's rate-limit helper and WS09's spam check for Report this event. The automations card reads the runs that WS06, WS07, WS09 and WS10's jobs record.
**Risks & open decisions:**
- Offline editing on two tablets needs conflict rules; keep it simple, with server-wins and a visible diff.
- With only 2 or 3 admins, the owner column may go unused; it's still worth it for accountability.
- `members.role` holds one role, so a club rep cannot also hold the standing director role (the president and LCA admins create whatever their role).
- Every member who holds `tournament_director` today becomes able to create once `standingDirectors` is on, so K reads that list first (above).
- A standing director's fees sit in LCA's account until connected accounts exist (Q3), and LCA passes them on by hand after each event, outside the site.
- A member-created event is open to anyone with its link; noindex keeps it out of search engines but is no lock. The three-event limit, Report this event, Close and Remove are the only brakes, so K tests with the setting on before members are told.
- Whether a club rep also creates the club's events is still Q2, and whether officers' view-only Admin and the president see member-created events is Q35.

---

## WS09 · Clubs as living listings
**Why:**
- With the map beside the list, 95% of testers used it. On list-only sites, 65% never found the map (Baymard).
- 7 of the 25 clubs show no schedule today.
- For most Louisiana clubs, their LCA page is their only web presence. K, Tue, Oct 13, 2026: "we very rarely have up to date information from our clubs", so the pages show what each club says in its own words and promise nothing week by week, and "SEO is a must here for their club names".
- parkrun answers a newcomer's two questions first: when, and what happens on a first visit.
- NC's directory puts "when they meet" first, with a "report outdated info" link on every listing; here every card and club page carries Suggest an update, which reaches whoever keeps the listing.

**Canvas boards** (picked by K on Thu, Oct 15 and Mon, Oct 19, 2026; they replace every earlier directory and club page option, `Clubs-A` to `Clubs-G`, `Clubs-I`, `Clubs-J`, `Clubs-Phones`, `Club-A` to `Club-F`, `Club-H` and `Club-I`, and `Work-B` and `Work-G`):
- `Clubs-H` (the directory: one list, always whole, beside the map) and `Clubs-H-2` (phones, the two forms and seven states), as drawn (K: "For 8, I like H and H-2.").
- `Club-G` (the club's own one-page site) and `Club-G-2` (the editor with its previews, the thin club, a phone and the states), as drawn.
- `Club-Final-Bare` and `Club-Final-Bare-2`: Club-G for a club whose rep has filled in nothing yet (the basics an LCA admin enters, a phone, Ask to be its named rep, the page as it fills), approved as drawn on Mon, Oct 19, 2026.
- `Work-H` (My region, a card per club) and `Work-H-2` (each club's own page in My region), as drawn (K: "I prefer H, and H's clubs own page screen"). Where `Account-N` (My LCA's Your region, WS13) differs, Work-H wins.
- `Account-Final-Hub-2`: the "Your club" row and inset G1 (the Fees step's notice when a representative types a fee above $0).
- Where the picked boards disagree, this text says which wins. Work-H-2's Tonight tab, and the "Checked" month and "Report outdated info" on its card preview, give way to Club-G and Clubs-H, which make no weekly claims (`Account-Final-Hub-2` already reads it so). Clubs-H-2's line that the region's representative "is copied" on every Suggest an update gives way to Club-G-2's routing (below). Clubs-H-2's "What changed?" choices are the one set for both pages.

**User-facing scope**
- **What the site says about a club.** A club's meeting is the club's own statement in its own words ("1st, 3rd and 5th Tuesday evenings at 5:30 PM at First Baptist Church."), shown as words and never turned into dates. No page under `/clubs` and no club page computes a next meeting, a Tonight or Meets today state, a count of clubs meeting, a Meets soonest order or a "Checked" month, and there is no calendar, timetable or week grid anywhere. A club with no statement reads "Schedule not listed". Events the club runs on louisianachess.org are the one exception, shown with full dates and weekdays, because they live here.
  - So WS09 builds no structured schedule: v1.3's `club_schedules`, `club_schedule_exceptions`, occurrence expansion, the Tonight switch, `GET /api/clubs/this-week` and the meeting `.ics` feed are not built.
  - The home page's this-week strip (WS03, `homeWeek`) gets no data from WS09 and stays off while P2.14 is open. If K keeps the strip, it needs a schedule model of its own, planned then.
- **Directory (`/clubs`, `Clubs-H`).**
  - The title strip reads "Chess clubs in Louisiana", with "25 clubs · 7 regions · Find the nearest, or look around the whole state." Under it, a 460px drawer beside a 980px map, 780px tall and sticky.
  - The drawer lists all 25 clubs A to Z from the first paint. A card has at most three lines: the club's name and town (and its distance once a place is known), then the meeting statement clamped to two lines, or "Schedule not listed · Know when they meet? Suggest an update" with the club's own description in grey. The whole card is one link to the club page, with no button, chip, colour, logo or initials on it. The caption over the list reads "All 25 clubs, A to Z. Meeting times are as each club states them; confirm before a first visit."
  - The map shows Louisiana at state zoom with every pin: solid where a statement is listed, hollow where none is, marked when selected by a larger pin with a ring in the gold-ink token (never brand gold on the light map, which reads 2.28:1, as WS04's selected marks say), and a count circle where several clubs share a town (a click opens it one zoom step). Pins carry no numbers. Hovering or focusing a card lifts its pin; clicking a pin scrolls the drawer to its card and selects it. One caption line in words explains the pins, and "All of Louisiana" zooms out and keeps the order. No popover, legend panel, region overlay or Search this area.
  - One field, "City, ZIP or club name": a place reorders the list, and a name narrows it ("1 club matches kni · Show all 25"). Near me reads the device's location once, on tap; the browser turns it into the nearest ZIP centre, and only that ZIP goes in the address (`?near=70808`). A typed city or ZIP is sent only to look up a point. The place is never stored or logged. A denied location says "We couldn't get your location. Type a city or ZIP."
  - Two orders: A to Z (the default) and Nearest, once a place is known, with "about N mi" in road miles (assumed, P2.12) and a thin "Farther than 75 miles" divider. Neither order ever removes a club. The ✕ on the location pill returns to A to Z at state zoom.
  - Filters, in a panel under the Filters button: Region (the seven, with counts), "Plays on weekends" and "Scholastic friendly" ("Only clubs that say so themselves"), each read from what the club sets on its own page. A filter acts on the list and the pins together; the caption then reads "5 clubs match · Clear filters", and no match reads "No clubs match these filters. Clear filters." The filters and the place live in the address; there is no Copy link control (assumed, P2.13).
  - States, as `Clubs-H-2` draws them: no club in the typed town ("No club in Natchitoches yet. The nearest are listed first. Start a club?"), a place outside Louisiana ("That's outside Louisiana. Picayune, MS is our one club over the line."), the thin card, and the keyboard order ("Skip to the list" before the map; the pins are buttons in list order).
  - Below the fold: "Who keeps these listings", one sentence and one quiet row of the seven regions, each with its representative's name and Message (assumed, P2.41), or "Seat open · LCA admins keep these · Volunteer" (assumed, P2.16). Message reaches the region's seat through the board contact (`POST /api/contact`, `functions/api/contact.ts`, with the region as its target, routed to the seat through `createTicket`), and Volunteer sends interest in the open seat to the board inbox, tagged with the seat, as WS14's About page does. WS09 adds the region target, with the endpoint's contract and the rate limit it lacks today (through `functions/utils/rateLimit.ts`), and WS14 reuses it; no address is shown. Then "Not here?": Missing a club? (Suggest a club), No club near you? (Start a club) and Something out of date? (how Suggest an update works).
  - Phones (`Clubs-H-2`): the map fills the screen, with the field and Near me over it and a bottom sheet ("25 clubs · A to Z", the first card peeking). A pin raises the sheet to its card; the full sheet keeps the order, and a "Map" pill drops it.
- **Club page (`/clubs/:slug`, `Club-G`).** One template for every club, read top to bottom like a small club website.
  - **Address:** a slug made from the club's name at the first save and kept on rename (`louisianachess.org/clubs/baton-rouge-chess-club`). Today's `/clubs/:id` addresses and any old slug answer 301 to it. Only an LCA admin changes a slug.
  - **Cover:** photo 1 at 1440 × 340, with its caption as alt text, the logo tile and the h1. With no photo, a 260px cover in the club's colour (navy until the club sets one) with a white initials tile, and the meeting statement under the h1, so when and where is on the first screen. Under the h1, the one line the site writes: "Chess club in {city}, Louisiana · {region}". Buttons: Follow this club (gold fill, navy text), Contact the club and Share.
  - **The author line** says once who keeps the page: "Kept by the club's rep, {first name and last initial}"; with no rep, "This club hasn't named a rep yet · Kept by {name}, the {region} representative" and "Is this your club? Ask to be its named rep"; while the seat is open, "Kept by LCA admins until the {region} seat is filled". Suggest an update sits in the author line and beside Contact, never on each block. A signed-in keeper sees "This is your club's page · Edit", or "You can edit this page as the {region} representative · Edit".
  - **Sections,** in a sticky nav that lists only the sections with something behind them:
    - About: the club's words, with Cost, Who is welcome and Find us online beside them when filled. A single sentence is set large, across the full width of the section.
    - When and where: the statement as a quote, with "As told by the club, updated {month}", or "As told to LCA, {month}" while it is what an LCA admin entered; a map with up to two pins, geocoded once on Save; and a row per place with the club's note for first-timers and Directions ↗.
    - Events: the club's events on louisianachess.org, each with its `DateBlock`, full date with weekday, status in words, Register and "N of M registered", and Add to calendar (the event's own `.ics`, WS04). A member-created event (D26) never shows. A partner event the club organizes links out with "Organizer's site ↗" (verify: nothing links a clearinghouse row to a club today; until something does, the section holds the club's own events only).
    - Club news, newest first, each "Also on LCA News › From the clubs" (WS10). Photos, up to six, captions as alt text. Contact (below).
    - Then "Chess clubs near {town}": the three nearest, each with "about N mi" and its statement "as the club says", and All {region} clubs · Clubs near you. A site foot line closes the page: "{Club} · {city}, Louisiana · a club listed by the Louisiana Chess Association · {address} · Copy link".
  - **Contact (D29).** The form takes a name, an email and a message, with a spam check. It sends to the club's own email when the club has given one ("Your message goes to {contact name}, the club's contact, by email. Their address stays private."), and otherwise to the region's representative ("No club address on file, so your message goes to {name}, the {region} representative."), whose copy opens by saying the club has no connected email yet. While the seat is open it goes to the LCA admins (assumed, P2.16). When the message goes to the club's own email, the visitor's email is its reply-to, so the club answers directly. The copy to the representative, or to the LCA admins while the seat is open, says that replying from their own mail shows the visitor their address, and offers Reply through LCA instead: a link to a short form on the site, open to that representative or an LCA admin once signed in, whose answer LCA sends from its own address, signed with the representative's first name and region and no address; the visitor's email travels only inside the link's signed token (WS06), valid 30 days, and is stored nowhere (assumed, Q36). The message is relayed, never stored: no table holds it, the visitor's name or their email, and it is not a support ticket. The editor asks each club for an email of its own, since that takes the load off its representative.
  - **Never on a club page:** a contact email, an officer's email, an officers list, a roster, a member count, "LCA members · N list this as their club" or a leaderboard (D17).
  - **The bare state** (`Club-Final-Bare`; K, Thu, Oct 15, 2026: "make sure it looks good regardless"). Most clubs start with only the basics: one sentence about the club; how the club meets, in words; where (a place and its pin); a contact name; and a contact email that stays private. Every optional block (the club's own words with Cost, Who is welcome and Find us online, Events, Club news, Photos, the logo and colour) is absent with its nav link, never shown empty: no brackets, no "not listed", no empty card, no Suggest an update on each block. The page still reads finished, and each block appears the first time someone adds to it. K enters the basics for every club (K: "I can input the basics of what is necessary if you see fit"), one short form per club on the admin club page; they read "As told to LCA, {month}" until the club's rep rewrites them.
  - **Search and sharing:**
    - The title is "{Club} · Chess club in {city}, Louisiana · Louisiana Chess Association", with a canonical link to the slug address. The meta description is About's opening, then the statement, at most 155 characters. The share card is photo 1's share crop, else the logo, else the initials card on navy.
    - JSON-LD `SportsClub`: name, sport, description, url, address, geo from the pin and `memberOf` LCA; an image only with a photo, `sameAs` only with a link, and an `Event` per club event.
    - `functions/clubs/[slug].ts` writes the h1, the city line, the statement, About, the places, the club's events with their dates and Contact into the shell's body before any script runs, only for sections with content.
    - Every club page is in `sitemap.xml`, with `told_updated_at` as its last change, and so is every listed event's page, taken through `listedWhere` (WS02), so a hidden draft or a member-created event is never in it (`functions/sitemap.xml.ts`, which WS09 creates; it joins `test/unit/listing-rule.test.ts`).
  - **Phones:** the cover at 180px (200 with no photo), the section nav as a pill row stuck under the app bar, and a club bar, "Directions · Message the club", docked above the tab bar in WS02's `BottomDock`.
- **Who keeps a club page (D17).** The club's named rep (today's `club_rep` role with `members.club_id`), the representative whose regional seat covers the club's region (`seat_regions`; Appendix C, border clubs as they stand, assumed, P2.15), and LCA admins. An open seat's clubs fall to LCA admins until it is filled (assumed, P2.16). Only an LCA admin creates or deletes a club, or changes its region or slug.
  - **One editor for every keeper** (`Club-G-2`, and `Club-Final-Bare-2` frame B for the basics):
    - The five basics first, then the rest in site order: name and city with the address card; cover and photos with captions and the crop; the club in its own words; how the club meets, with up to two places and their notes and pins; cost; who is welcome, with two ticks, "The club plays on weekends" and "Scholastic friendly"; the contact name shown, and the email "never shown; the Contact form relays to it"; links; logo and colour.
    - It collects nothing else: no grade, no birthdate, no roster, no schedule table.
    - A sticky preview column shows the page, its search result ("Google shows about the first 60 characters, so your name and city come first") and its share card.
    - Save and publish puts the change live at once. When someone else saved it, the region's representative gets an email naming what changed.
    - The club's rep opens it at `/my/club/:slug` ("Your club's page"), the representative at `/workspace/region/clubs/:id`, and an LCA admin at `/admin/clubs/:id`. Today's `/manage/club` goes to `/my/club/:slug`.
  - **Ask to be its named rep** (`Club-Final-Bare-2` frame D): a short form behind Log in, "How are you connected to the club?" (I run it · I help run it · I'd keep the page for the club · Something else) with an optional note. It reaches the region's representative and the LCA admins. The representative for the club's region approves it, with LCA admins as the backstop (K took this default on Mon, Oct 19, 2026), and an admin decides while the seat is open. Approving names the member the club's rep and emails them; there is no self-serve claim. Once named, the rep's My LCA gains Your club (WS13).
  - **Suggest an update,** one form from any card or club page: "What changed?" (Meeting day or time · Venue or address · Cost or who it's for · Contact · The club has closed or moved · Something else), one text box, an optional email "in case there's a question", and the spam check. It reaches the club's rep when the club has one (as `Club-G-2` draws it), otherwise the region's representative, or LCA admins while the seat is open; the representative also sees it on the club's card in My region. With no answer in 14 days it also reaches the LCA admins, and the region's representative when the club's rep had it. Sent: "Sent. Whoever keeps this club's listing checks it before anything changes."
  - **Suggest a club and Start a club,** one form with a switch (`Clubs-H-2`): the club's name, a city or ZIP, where and when in the person's own words, and their name and email (private). It is routed by city or ZIP (`zip_regions`) to the region's representative, or to LCA admins while the seat is open; they check with the person, and an LCA admin adds the club.
- **My region (`/workspace/region`, `Work-H`).** WS09 builds My region and each club's page in it, and WS13's rail entry Your region opens it; WS08 keeps the TD console, the setup checklist and Admin.
  - A "This month" bar: "Next monthly check lands Mon, Nov 2 · 5 of 9 checked in the last 90 days · 4 not checked in 90 days · 2 reports · 1 event in review", with "Confirm all still accurate" at its right.
  - A card per club: the club's colour band with its logo or initials; name, city and region; the statement in two lines, or "Schedule not listed · Suggest an update"; a stamp row in words ("Checked Sep 2026", or "Not checked since Jun 2026" on the amber tint) and the open items ("1 report · 9 days", "1 suggestion · 1 day", "Event in LCA review"); Still accurate (44px) and Open. Cards sort needs a check first, then open items, then A to Z. A card with an open report reads "Answer it, then confirm", and its Still accurate waits.
  - Under the cards, `Account-N`'s to-do rows: each report and suggestion with its age and Reply, Apply this or Not an issue; each rep request with Name as rep and Decline; each club event in LCA review; the monthly check line. A seat to which an LCA admin has added an open seat's region (`seat_regions`) shows those clubs too.
  - **Each club's own page** (`/workspace/region/clubs/:id`, `Work-H-2`): the breadcrumb trail "My LCA › My region › {club}"; a header with "{town} · {region} · Checked {month} · kept by {name}", View public page ↗ and Still accurate; an open report's banner with Fix the listing, Reply to visitor (an email to the visitor when they left one; the reply is not kept) and Not an issue, and "Unanswered at 14 days this also reaches the LCA admins". Tabs: **Listing** (the editor at full width, with a live preview of the directory card and the search result, and a page checklist: statement, place pinned, contact email, a photo, logo, colour), **News** (posts to the club page, From the clubs and the digest, WS10; until WS10 lands, today's title, date and excerpt) and **Events**. There is no Tonight tab.
  - **A club's events.** Add a club event opens WS08's setup checklist with the club picked. The representative names its tournament director (D21; from WS09, assumed, Q1) and may change its membership switch, as WS08's table "Who may do what on an event" says, and runs every club event in the region, a club rep's own included (that table's Run the event row). The event is marked for review when the representative creates it (`needs_lca_approval`), and waits for an LCA admin's approval before it lists, as `Work-H` draws it (assumed, Q15): Submit for LCA review sets `approval_requested_at`; until an LCA admin's Approve and publish sets `approved_at`, `approved_by` and `is_visible = 1` together, a save that sets `isVisible = 1` or a `publish_at` date is refused in words, whoever makes it, the director the representative named included, and the scheduled publish passes the event by (WS08); Ask for changes clears the request and emails the representative. Representatives are not on D24's list, so a fee above $0 shows the Fees step's notice (`Account-Final-Hub-2` G1: "Entry fees need a payout account first", with Keep it at $0 and Ask LCA to set up my payout account). Whether a club's rep creates the club's events is Q2; until K answers, today's rule stands (a `club_rep` creates its own club's events as hidden drafts).
  - **The monthly check.** On the first Monday of each month at 8:00 AM Louisiana time (a timed phase of the five-minute run, section 2.2), each representative gets one email listing every club the seat keeps, each with Still accurate (one tap, no login, valid 30 days) and Update (the club's page, after sign-in). Still accurate, or any save by a keeper, sets the club's last check. The check lives in My region and My LCA only; no public page shows it.
  - While WS08's `ADMIN_HOME_ON` is unset, what reaches the LCA admins (a report unanswered at 14 days, a rep request while the seat is open, a club event to approve) arrives by email and shows on the admin club page; once it is set, it lands in the queue.
- **Players are not associated with clubs** (D17; the rest of P2.18, assumed): the admin page's "club members" designation and the club roster, the club page's officers list with the admin officer tools, the group email's "by club" audience and the "keep families and clubmates apart" pairing option go once `newClubs` is on, their server parts with `CLUB_PAGES_ON`; until then today's screens keep them, since each is a visible change. An event already set to keep clubmates apart keeps families apart. `members.club_id` is read only for a `club_rep`'s own club; the column and `club_officers` stay in the tables, unread. Nothing is removed until every reader is checked (the list is under Frontend).
- **Switch** (0.2's table, off by default): `newClubs` turns on the directory, the club pages, the relayed Contact, the forms, the editor at `/my/club/:slug` and My region. The server's half follows `CLUB_PAGES_ON` in `functions/utils/clubs/pages.ts`, set in the same change that turns `newClubs` on: the 301s from today's addresses, the server-written body and JSON-LD, the sitemap, the P2.18 removals, and the representatives' grants (creating a club's event, naming its directors, its membership switch, and `canManageTournament` for the club's region). A unit test fails if the two differ, and with the constant unset a representative gets 403 on all four, as today. With the switch off, today's club pages work as they do now.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_club_pages.sql. clubs.id is TEXT (0001), so every club_id below is TEXT.
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN slug TEXT;                          -- from the name at the first save; kept on rename
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_clubs_slug ON clubs(slug);
CREATE TABLE club_slug_history (                                 -- today's ids and every old slug, each answering 301
  old_slug TEXT PRIMARY KEY, club_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clubs(id)
);
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN about_text TEXT;                    -- the one sentence, or the club's own paragraphs
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN how_we_meet_text TEXT;              -- the meeting statement in the club's words; empty = "Schedule not listed"
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN cost_text TEXT;
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN welcome_text TEXT;
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN weekend_play INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;         -- set by the club on its page
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN scholastic_friendly INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;  -- set by the club on its page
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN contact_name_public TEXT;           -- "Ray A."; clubs.contact_email (0001) stays private
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN facebook_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN website_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN other_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN told_updated_at TEXT;               -- the last save of the club's words: "updated <month>"
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN told_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);     -- an admin: "As told to LCA"; the club's rep: "As told by the club"
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN last_confirmed_at TEXT;             -- the monthly check, shown in My region and My LCA only
ALTER TABLE clubs ADD COLUMN confirmed_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
CREATE TABLE club_places (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, club_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clubs(id),
  name TEXT NOT NULL, street TEXT, note TEXT,                    -- note: "Meeting room past the front desk"
  latitude REAL, longitude REAL,                                 -- geocoded once on Save; the pin can be dragged
  sort INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0                                -- at most two places; place 1 is the directory's pin
);
CREATE TABLE club_photos (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, club_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clubs(id),
  r2_key TEXT NOT NULL, caption TEXT NOT NULL,                   -- the caption is the alt text
  share_crop TEXT, sort INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,              -- photo 1 is the cover and the share image; at most six
  added_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id), created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE TABLE club_reports (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  club_id TEXT REFERENCES clubs(id),                             -- null for Suggest a club and Start a club
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('suggestion','new_club','start_club')),
  field TEXT,                                                    -- the "What changed?" choice
  message TEXT NOT NULL,
  proposed_json TEXT,                                            -- a new club's name, city or ZIP and meeting words
  region TEXT,                                                   -- from zip_regions, for a new club
  reporter_email TEXT,                                           -- optional on a suggestion
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','applied','answered','dismissed')),
  resolved_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id), resolved_at TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  CHECK ((kind = 'suggestion') = (club_id IS NOT NULL))
);
CREATE TABLE club_rep_requests (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, club_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clubs(id),
  member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  connection TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (connection IN ('runs_it','helps_run_it','keeps_page','other')),
  note TEXT,
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open' CHECK (status IN ('open','approved','declined')),
  decided_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id), decided_at TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN needs_lca_approval INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;   -- set when a representative creates a club event (assumed, Q15)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN approval_requested_at TEXT;   -- Submit for LCA review
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN approved_at TEXT;             -- an LCA admin's Approve and publish
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN approved_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
CREATE TABLE zip_regions (                                       -- moved from WS14
  zip TEXT PRIMARY KEY, region TEXT NOT NULL, latitude REAL NOT NULL, longitude REAL NOT NULL
);
CREATE TABLE club_distances (                                    -- assumed, P2.12
  club_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clubs(id), zip TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES zip_regions(zip),
  road_miles REAL NOT NULL, minutes INTEGER, computed_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  PRIMARY KEY (club_id, zip)
);
-- rate_limits is WS07's (00xx_live_mode.sql, Phase 2); WS09 adds no table for it
```
- The migration fills each club's slug from its name and writes today's id into `club_slug_history`. It fills `how_we_meet_text` with the meeting line `Clubs-H` shows for that club (`src/areas/clubs/clubMapData.ts`, migration 0013), and `about_text` from today's `description` for the clubs with no meeting line. It fills place 1 from `clubs.location`, `latitude` and `longitude` (0051), and leaves `told_by` empty, so every club reads "As told to LCA, {month}" until K or the club's rep saves it. verify: the meeting lines in `clubMapData.ts` against the `clubs` rows, and Heart of Worship's point, which the data puts near Bunkie rather than Opelousas.
- `clubs.region` exists (0012). Production gets Appendix C's assignments from `00xx_club_regions.sql`, a numbered migration WS09 ships ahead of `00xx_club_pages.sql`, which sets a club's region only where it is blank, so a correction K made on the admin club page stands; a builder runs `club_regions.sql` against the local database only (`--local`).
- `zip_regions` holds the centres of Louisiana's ZIP codes (and Picayune's) from the Census ZIP code tabulation areas, which are public data, each with its region from Appendix C's map. The points also go to the browser, so Near me can find the nearest ZIP without sending the device's position.
- Road miles are worked out once for each club and ZIP and cached in `club_distances`; a club's column is redone when its place 1 moves. verify: the map provider's price for about 12,500 pairs before the first run; if it is not small, P2.12's other answer (straight-line miles, with no provider call) is K's to choose.
- Not added: `clubs.schedule_status` (an empty statement says it), `club_schedules`, `club_schedule_exceptions`, a Tonight column, `clubs.kind`, `clubs.rep_member_id` (the club's rep is today's `club_rep` designation) and a club follows table (Follow uses WS07's `follows`, with `target_kind = 'club'`).
- `club_news` gains its posting columns in WS10's migration; WS09 adds none.
- Write the schema in `functions/db/schema.ts` and generate the migration (0.2); add a column that references another table with `npm run db:generate -- <name> --custom` and write the clause by hand (a WS01 follow-up on drizzle-kit).

**API**
- `GET /api/clubs` (extend `functions/api/clubs.ts`): each club's slug, name, town, region, statement (or none), the grey description for a club with none, place 1's pin and the two ticks, and each region's keeper (the representative's name, or "LCA admins"). With `near=<zip>`, each club's road miles from that ZIP, sent `no-store`, and `near` reaches no log line. Edge cache 5 minutes without `near`.
- `GET /api/clubs/:slug/page` (new): the page's sections, the author line, the three nearest clubs, the club's listed events (never a member-created one) and its published news. It never carries `contact_email`, an officer or a roster.
- `GET /api/clubs/:id` (today's `functions/api/clubs/[id].ts`, for today's club page): every officer's email has been out of the response since the Phase 0 public-answer trim (section 4). The same trim takes `contact_email` out of the response and off today's club page behind `clubEmailHidden`, whose server half is `CLUB_EMAIL_HIDDEN` (assumed, Q14). Once K turns that switch on, no public answer carries a club's address, before `newClubs` as after it.
- `POST /api/clubs/:slug/contact` (new; public, rate-limited, spam-checked): the relay. It writes nothing to the database except the rate-limit count.
- `GET` and `POST /api/clubs/reply/:token` (new; WS06's signed token, and a sign-in as the representative the message reached or an LCA admin; rate-limited): Reply through LCA. It writes nothing to the database except the rate-limit count (assumed, Q36).
- `POST /api/clubs/:slug/reports` (new; public, rate-limited, spam-checked): Suggest an update.
- `POST /api/clubs/reports` (new; public, rate-limited, spam-checked): Suggest a club and Start a club, with no club, routed by `zip_regions`.
- `POST /api/clubs/:slug/rep-requests` (new; `requireAuthedMember`, rate-limited).
- The keepers' endpoints, all behind `requireClubRep`, whose check (`canManageClub` in `functions/utils/admin/permissions.ts`) already admits an LCA admin, the club's `club_rep` and the representative for the club's region:
  - `PATCH /api/admin/clubs/:id` (exists): the new fields; region and slug stay with LCA admins. Each save writes `told_updated_at`, `told_by` and the last check, and emails the region's representative what changed when someone else saved it.
  - `POST` and `DELETE /api/admin/clubs/:id/photos` (new): uploads to the `CLUB_LOGOS` bucket under `photos/` (verify the bucket's limits), at most six, each with a caption. WS09's first PR binds `CLUB_LOGOS` under `[env.preview]` in `wrangler.toml` to `lca-club-logos-preview` (unless WS04 has bound it already for its stored link-preview pictures), a bucket K creates, as WS15 binds `SCANS`, so a logo or photo saved on a preview never reaches the live bucket (0.1 rule 8).
  - `PUT /api/admin/clubs/:id/places` (new): up to two places, geocoded once on Save.
  - `POST /api/admin/clubs/:id/confirm` (new): Still accurate.
  - `POST /api/admin/clubs/:id/reports/:reportId` (new): apply, answer (with an optional reply emailed to the reporter and not kept) or dismiss.
  - `POST /api/admin/clubs/:id/rep-requests/:requestId` (new): approve or decline, for an LCA admin or the representative for the club's region only (a club's rep cannot name another). Approving sets `club_rep` and `members.club_id` on the member and emails them. verify: `members.role` holds one role, so a request from a member who holds another (a standing director, an officer) goes to the LCA admins, who are told why in words.
  - Club news keeps `functions/api/admin/clubs/[id]/news.ts` (WS10 extends it).
- `GET /api/workspace/region` (new): the clubs a member keeps through their seats (`getRegionalClubIds`, which covers every region in the seat's `seat_regions`, including an open seat's region that an LCA admin has added to it), or every club for an LCA admin, with each club's check, open items and events, the This month figures, the to-do rows and the rep requests.
- `GET /api/clubs/confirm/:token` (new; WS06's signed tokens; changes nothing) and `POST /api/clubs/confirm/:token` (new): the monthly email's Still accurate for one club opens a page naming the club with one Still accurate button, and the POST sets its last check and `confirmed_by`.
- **The representative's grants** (assumed, Q1), in place of v1.3's club rep workspace, each with `CLUB_PAGES_ON` (above) and as WS08's table "Who may do what on an event" sets them:
  - `canManageTournament` admits the representative for an event's club (`isRegionalRepFor` on `tournaments.club_id`), beside today's `club_rep` branch, which stays (Q2): the representative runs every club event in the region, a club rep's own included, as WS08's table's Run the event row says, while the membership switch, directors and publishing stay with the club events they drafted.
  - `POST /api/admin/tournaments` (`functions/api/admin/tournaments.ts`) admits the representative for a club in their region; the event starts as a hidden draft with `needs_lca_approval = 1`, which an LCA admin approves (above).
  - `requireCanAssign` in `functions/api/admin/tournaments/[id]/directors.ts` (who names and removes an event's directors) and the `requiresLcaMembership` check in `functions/api/admin/tournaments/[id].ts` admit the representative on a club event they drafted. An assigned TD still may not change the membership switch.
  - WS07's report visibility follows `canManageTournament`.
- `/workspace/region` and its club pages admit any member who holds an active regional seat (`getRegionalClubIds` is not empty), whatever their role. verify: today `/workspace` admits only `WORKSPACE_ROLES` or a director assignment (`src/App.tsx`, `RoleProtectedRoute.tsx`). `/my/club/:slug` admits whoever `canManageClub` admits.
- Every new or changed endpoint gets its contract and contract test and leaves the pending list (0.2).

Words on a club page are the look's ink, and the initials tile is navy on white. So the new pages use neither `clubTextColor` nor `clubTextOnDark`, which settles the WS01 follow-ups on a club's colour as words and on dark grounds.

- Not built: v1.3's `ThisWeekGrid.tsx`, `TonightPanel.tsx`, `TonightSwitch.tsx`, `ScheduleEditor.tsx`, `FreshnessBadge.tsx`, `CompactProfile.tsx`, and `src/lib/clubSchedule.ts` with its server mirror.
