**Jobs & integrations** (in `workers/daily-emails`, each with its own isolated phase):
- The monthly email to each representative, on the first Monday of the month at 8:00 AM Louisiana time, a timed phase of the five-minute run (section 2.2).
- The 14-day clock on unanswered reports: to the region's representative and the LCA admins (by email while WS08's `ADMIN_HOME_ON` is unset, then into the queue).
- Road miles for a club whose place 1 moved since the last run.
- Follower emails when a club posts news or adds an event, through WS07's outbox. Nothing weekly, and no follower count anywhere.
- The contact relay, the save notices, the reply to a reporter and the rep's naming email go through Resend (`functions/utils/email.ts`) at once, not through a job.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. No page under `/clubs`, no club page and no My region page shows a computed next meeting, a Tonight or Meets today state, a count of clubs meeting, a calendar, timetable or week grid; public pages show no "Checked" month. A club's meeting shows as its own words, or as "Schedule not listed · Know when they meet? Suggest an update" when it has none.
2. `/clubs` lists all 25 clubs A to Z on first load beside a map with every pin. Near me and a typed place reorder the list nearest first, with "about N mi" and the "Farther than 75 miles" divider, and never remove a club. A typed club name narrows the list to its matches, with "N clubs match <text> · Show all 25" ("1 club matches kni"). Filters (region, plays on weekends, scholastic friendly) narrow the list and the pins together. The filters and the place live in the address.
3. Hovering or focusing a card lifts its pin, and selecting a pin scrolls to its card and selects it. Pins carry no numbers. Each card is one link to its club page with no button or second link inside it (a WS01 follow-up).
4. The device's position never leaves the browser: Near me sends only the nearest ZIP. A typed city or ZIP is used only to find a point, and `near`, the city and the ZIP appear in no log line and no table. A denied location reads "We couldn't get your location. Type a city or ZIP."
5. Every club page lives at `/clubs/:slug`, made from the name at the first save and kept on rename; today's `/clubs/:id` addresses and every old slug answer 301 to it.
6. A club page renders only the sections with something in them, each with its nav link. With only the five basics it shows the cover with initials, the author line, About, When and where and Contact, and no bracket, "not listed", empty card or Suggest an update on a block (`Club-Final-Bare`). A block appears the first time it has content.
7. The page's title, canonical link, meta description (About's opening, then the statement, at most 155 characters), share card (photo 1, else the logo, else the initials card on navy), JSON-LD `SportsClub` and server-written body (the h1, the city line, the statement, About, the places, Contact, and the club's events with their dates) are in the HTML before any script runs, and `sitemap.xml` lists every club page with its `told_updated_at`, and every listed event, never a hidden draft or a member-created event.
8. No public page, API response or page HTML carries a club's contact email, an officer's email, a roster, a member count, a leaderboard or an officers list (D17, D29). The Contact form sends to the club's own email when it has one, otherwise to the region's representative with the line that the club has no connected email yet, and to the LCA admins while the seat is open; the visitor's email is the reply-to only on the copy to the club's own address, and the representative's or the admins' copy offers Reply through LCA, which shows no volunteer's address (assumed, Q36); no table holds the message, the visitor's name or their email.
9. Only an LCA admin, the club's named rep and the representative for the club's region can change a club page, its photos or its news; a representative of another region, another club's rep, a one-event director and a member get 403. Only an LCA admin changes a club's region or slug, or creates or deletes a club.
10. Suggest an update reaches the club's rep when it has one, otherwise the region's representative (LCA admins while the seat is open), and shows on the club's card in My region; with no answer in 14 days it also reaches the LCA admins, and the region's representative when the club's rep had it. Suggest a club and Start a club reach the representative of the region their city or ZIP falls in, or the LCA admins while that seat is open.
11. Ask to be its named rep needs a sign-in and reaches the region's representative and the LCA admins. Only the representative for the club's region or an LCA admin can approve it; approving names the member the club's rep (`club_rep` with `members.club_id`) and emails them, and the club's page then names its rep and no longer shows the invitation.
12. A member who holds an active regional seat opens `/workspace/region` whatever their role, and sees the region's clubs (with the clubs of open regions their seat keeps) and no others. Cards sort needs a check first, then open items, then A to Z. Still accurate sets `last_confirmed_at` and `confirmed_by` for that club only, and waits with "Answer it, then confirm" while a report is open.
13. The monthly email reaches each representative once on the first Monday of the month, listing the clubs the seat keeps; its Still accurate works with no login for 30 days and refuses an expired or forged link.
14. With `CLUB_PAGES_ON` set, a representative creates an event for a club in their region, names its director and changes its membership switch, runs every club event in the region (a club rep's own included: a setup save, a refund, the console), and a representative of another region gets 403 on all of them; with it unset, every representative gets 403, as today. The event lists only after an LCA admin's Approve and publish: until then a save that sets `isVisible = 1` or a `publish_at` date is refused, the representative's and the named director's alike, and the scheduled publish lists nothing. A fee above $0 is refused with D24's plain notice.
15. `/clubs` and `/clubs/:slug` scroll sideways at neither 320 nor 390 pixels (a WS01 follow-up) and have zero serious or critical violations in the light and dark looks at 390 and 1440 pixels. A club's own colour is never words, and a club with no colour of its own shows navy.
16. A member-created event (D26) never shows on a club page, while the club's own events show with full dates and weekdays, Register and "N of M registered".

**Tests**
- Unit: `test/unit/club-slug.test.ts` (made once, kept on rename, unique), `test/unit/club-sections.test.ts` (which sections render for the bare club, a full club and each block filled in turn), `test/unit/club-order.test.ts` (A to Z, nearest with the divider, a place that removes no club, a typed name that narrows the list to its matches with "1 club matches kni · Show all 25", filters on list and pins together), `test/unit/near-zip.test.ts` (the nearest ZIP centre in the browser), `test/unit/region-card-sort.test.ts`, `test/unit/club-pages-switch.test.ts` (`CLUB_PAGES_ON` equals `newClubs`), `test/unit/club-pages-no-dates.test.tsx` (new: criterion 1, the directory, a club page and My region drawn with no computed date, Tonight, count, calendar or public "Checked" month), `test/unit/wrangler-config.test.ts` (extended: `CLUB_LOGOS` bound to `lca-club-logos-preview` under `[env.preview]`), and `test/unit/features.test.ts` (`newClubs` starts off).
- Integration (`test/integration`):
  - `clubs-directory.test.ts`: the list's fields, `near=` with its miles, `no-store` and no log line, the region keepers.
  - `club-page.test.ts`: the slug, the 301s from today's ids and old slugs, the page's sections, and no `contact_email`, officer email or roster in any response; no member-created event among the club's events, which show with their dates, weekdays, Register and counts (criterion 16).
  - `club-page-route.test.ts`: `functions/clubs/[slug].ts` writes the title, canonical link, description, JSON-LD and body for a full club and a bare club, and only the old link preview with `CLUB_PAGES_ON` off (the page route tests a WS01 follow-up asked for).
  - `club-contact-relay.test.ts`: the club's own email, the representative with the no-connected-email line, the LCA admins for an open seat, the reply-to only on the club's copy, Reply through LCA (the signed link, the sign-in, no address in the answer, nothing stored), the spam check and the rate limit.
  - `club-reports.test.ts`: routing for a club with a rep, without one and with the seat open; the 14-day step; Suggest a club routed by `zip_regions`.
  - `club-rep-requests.test.ts`: sign-in required; the region's representative and an admin approve; another region's representative, the club's own rep and a member get 403.
  - `workspace-region.test.ts`: admission whatever the role, region scoping with open seats, Still accurate, the confirm link (valid, expired, forged).
  - `club-events.test.ts`: the representative's create, name-a-director and membership-switch grants, another region's 403, all of them refused with `CLUB_PAGES_ON` unset; the representative running a club rep's own event in the region (a setup save, a refund, the console) and another region's representative refused; Publish and a `publish_at` date refused before approval, for the representative and the director they named, and a stored date publishing nothing before approval; Approve and publish; Ask for changes; and D24's refusal of a fee above $0.
  - Extend `club-permissions.test.ts` (criterion 9) and `role-safety.test.ts`; a contract test for each new or changed endpoint in `test/integration/contracts/`.
- Worker: `test/integration/club-check-emails.test.ts` (new): `workers/daily-emails`' monthly email (one per representative, the first Monday, 8:00 AM Central in summer and winter) and the 14-day step, by email while `ADMIN_HOME_ON` is unset and as a queue row once it is set.
- Browser: the a11y-on build turns `newClubs` on (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`), `/clubs/:slug` joins `PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` and `/clubs` takes its new heading; `test/a11y/scopes-edges.spec.ts` reads today's contact link only with the switch off; `test/a11y/clubs-reflow.spec.ts` checks criterion 15's widths; `test/a11y/clubs.spec.ts` (new) checks criterion 3 (a card's hover and focus lift its pin, a pin selects its card, no numbers on pins, one link per card) and the selected pin's ring at 3:1; `test/a11y/baseline.json` stays unchanged. `/my/club/:slug` and `/workspace/region` join once the browser-test servers can sign in (WS01 left this open).

**Depends on:** WS01, WS02 and Appendix C's region assignment; WS06's signed tokens (the monthly email's link); WS07's `follows`, outbox and rate-limit helper (Follow, follower emails and the forms' limits); WS08's setup checklist for a club's events. WS10 adds the club news columns, and WS14 reuses the region target WS09 adds to `POST /api/contact`.

**Risks & open decisions:**
- Volunteer engagement: most pages start bare, so the bare state has to read as finished (K's own words), and the monthly email has to do the remembering for the representatives.
- K enters 25 clubs' basics; the migration's seed from today's data shortens that to a check.
- The map provider's cost for road miles (P2.12) and for looking up a typed city.
- Until WS09's first PR binds `CLUB_LOGOS` to `lca-club-logos-preview` on previews, a logo or photo uploaded on a preview would land in the live bucket, so none is uploaded on a preview (0.1 rule 8); a preview's club pages show no logo until one is uploaded there.
- `members.role` holds one role, so a standing director or an officer cannot also be named a club's rep until roles can be combined.
- Open for K: P2.12 to P2.16, P2.18 and P2.41; Q1 and Q2; whether a representative's club event waits for an LCA admin's approval (Q15); whether today's club page should stop showing the club's contact email before `newClubs` goes on (Q14); and how a representative answers a relayed message without showing their address (Q36).

---

## WS10 · Results archive, champions, news & recaps
**Why:**
- Recaps with named winners turn events into community stories, and champions and history give people a reason to share the site.
- For chronological feeds, rows scan faster than cards (NN/g), and every item needs a date.
- The site already computes standings. It just doesn't keep them as history.
- K's note on page 11 (Fri, Oct 9, 2026): "the bulk of posting will likely be done from the facebook", so News is built to work with the LCA Facebook page; and tournaments already have results pages, so News carries "just highlights of recent ones".

**Canvas boards** (decided Fri, Oct 9, 2026):
- `News-Final`: frame A, `/news`; frame B, the tournament's Recap tab in Workspace.
- `News-Final-2`: frame C, Promote to story (drawn as Workspace › News, built as `/admin` › Content › News; assumed, P2.33); frame D, the phones.
- `About-Final-2` frame 4: the Champions tab, which WS10 builds first as `/about/champions`.
- `Live-Final-Standings`: the results view, which is the event's standings page in its final state (WS07).
- They replace `News-A` to `News-D` and `About-D`. Where `News-Final` differs from this text, the text wins: the recap card's US Chess rating report link (the report stays with the people who manage the event), its note that a correction made in Pairings & results flows through (a finalized result changes only through the audited correction below), and its assumption that only the region's representative posts a club's news (the club's own rep does too, since K's page 9 pick keeps the club rep; section 7.2 of the phase 2 replan).

**User-facing scope**
- **Finalize → archive:** when a TD finalizes an event (WS08), the site snapshots final standings per section into `event_results`: place, player, pre-event rating (and post-event once US Chess publishes it), score, tiebreaks and prize. Snapshots are immutable; an LCA admin's correction is audited. Finalize waits until every game has a TD result and no player's report is waiting (WS07), and names the boards that hold it up, so the snapshot holds TD-confirmed results only.
- **The event's results page** is its standings and crosstable page (`/tournaments/:id/standings`, WS07). Once the event is finalized it reads the snapshot, says "Final results" and shows "Read the recap" when a recap is published. Recent tournaments' Full results, the results index and the champions' links open it. From WS15 (Phase 5), games players link from the scanner show there too (section 7.6 of the phase 2 replan).
- **Results index** (`/results`): past LCA events, newest first, filterable by year and type. Each event links to its results page and its recap.
- **No player pages (D9):** LCA is community-focused, not player-focused. Results live on event pages, the results index, recaps and the champions page, with names shown in full (D3) and not linked to profiles.
- **Member-created events never appear here** (decided Tue, Oct 20, 2026; section 7.9 of the phase 2 replan). They are not in the results index, the Tournaments page's Results tab (WS04), Recent tournaments or Latest on News, the email digest, the RSS feed, the champions page or the homepage's results and recap blocks (WS03); `GET /api/results`, `GET /api/champions`, `GET /api/news`, the digest builder and the feed leave them out. A member-created event still finalizes into the snapshot, and its results page works for anyone with its link, with noindex as WS05's event page has. It has no Recap tab, since a recap exists to put an event on News, in the digest and on Facebook (assumed, Q13).
- **Champions** (`/about/champions`, `About-Final-2` frame 4):
  - WS10 builds it in Phase 3, with `/champions` and `/state-champions` redirecting to it; WS14 wraps it as the About page's Champions tab in Phase 4. The event page's "Past champions ↗" and the Scholastic page's "Show all years" open it.
  - `state_champions` (migration 0049) links each title to the event it was won at, when that event is in the archive, and never to a member-created event.
  - Reigning champions show one card per title and feed the homepage band (WS03).
  - The honour roll is grouped by title, with "Show all N years"; co-champions share a row; a missing year says so in words, and a "Missing years" card writes to the whole board through `POST /api/contact` (assumed, P2.36). WS10 adds the whole-board target for it unless WS11 has already; the endpoint's rate limit and contract come with WS09's region target (section 2.2).
  - Names are plain text, and the year or the event links to its archived results (assumed, P2.37).
  - `tournaments.championship_title` (WS05) is a slug matching `state_champions.title`; the honour roll accepts `?title=` and the event page's "Past champions ↗" sub nav link opens it there. Its relation to the existing `is_state_championship` flag is not settled: verify, and keep both until the flag can be derived from a non-empty title.
  - In the heritage look (`themeHeritage`), inside `HeritageLayout`: the honour board's heads take `.small-caps`, its cards `card` and its table frame `table`, and a display figure on the page, if one is drawn, takes `figure`. The page carries the official logo, as D1 gives it the history pages (recorded in WS01 step 28). If a champion's name needs a letter outside the heritage faces' latin files, WS10 adds their latin-ext files (and Source Serif 4's vietnamese file) with their `unicode-range`, so only a page that shows such a name fetches them.
  - The About-D timeline is not built (assumed, P2.40), nor its submissions form (assumed, P2.36) or its player pages (D9).
- **Recap tab** (`/workspace/tournaments/:id/recap`, `News-Final` frame B), for the event's TDs and LCA admins: greyed with "Opens when standings are final" until the event is finalized; then pre-filled with a suggested headline, which the director can change, and the section winners from `event_results` (read-only). The director adds up to 400 characters and up to four photos (one marked for the card), from a phone or from the LCA Facebook page's recent posts, stored in the `CLUB_LOGOS` bucket under `news/` either way. Publish recap needs the photo consent box ticked when there are photos, and the recap shows "Ask us to take a photo down", which opens the contact form. It works on a phone at the venue, with Save draft and Publish recap fixed above the tab bar (D16). The standings snippet, the US Chess report link and the key-game slot leave the recap: standings, the crosstable and the key game stay on the results page, and the US Chess rating report stays in the standings Download menu and the console's Rating report step (assumed, P2.9).
- **Publish recap** fills the event's card in Recent tournaments, adds a Results row to Latest and "Read the recap" to the results page, makes the share card, puts the recap in the next Monday digest and offers Facebook's share window, each exactly once.
- **News page** (`/news`, `News-Final`):
  - **Latest**, at the top: one dated list grouped by month, each row with its weekday date, category and source mark; category chips with counts (Results · Announcements · Clubs · Scholastic · Board) and a From switch (All · Facebook · LCA), both in the address; one pin with an end date ("Pinned until Sun, Oct 11"), which drops off by itself; "Load 10 more"; Browse by year (`/news/2026`, `/news/2025`, older years pointing to the old site). No featured story (assumed, P2.39). Club posts are not in Latest (assumed, P2.31).
  - **Facebook first:** posts from the LCA Facebook page sit in Latest with their own text and photo, marked "From our Facebook page ↗" (`DESIGN_REPLAN_phase2.md` section 3.5; assumed, P2.42). An LCA admin can promote one to a story or hide it (assumed, P2.32). No Facebook plugin or script loads on the site.
  - **Recent tournaments:** three cards, newest finished LCA event first: headline, one line of section winners, one photo (or the event name on navy before a recap is published), and Full results, which opens the event's results page. No standings or crosstables on `/news`.
  - **From the clubs:** its own section under Recent tournaments, with each club's colour dot beside its name, listing news posted from club pages by whoever keeps the page: the club's own rep, the region's representative or an LCA admin (section 7.2 of the phase 2 replan, in place of P2.29's default of the representative alone). It ends with "Post your club's news". Each post also shows on its club page and in the weekly email.
  - **The rail:** Follow on Facebook and the email digest sign-up.
  - **Share cards:** every story and recap gets WS04's share-preview card when published, and Share to Facebook opens Facebook's share window; the site does not post to Facebook by itself (assumed, P2.30).
  - Every Facebook link the page carries today stays: Follow on Facebook, and a link back on every mirrored and every promoted post.
  - Every date on the new pages carries its weekday ("Thu, Oct 1, 2026"), through `formatDate`. Today's `/news`, post page and admin list keep `formatPostDate`, which leaves the weekday off, until `newNews` is on, since the weekday is a visible change; it goes with today's pages.
- **Digest and RSS:** an opt-in weekly email, Mondays at 8:00 AM, Louisiana time (assumed, P2.44): new stories and recaps, upcoming LCA events and club news, with one-click unsubscribe. `/news/rss.xml` carries the site's stories and recaps, promoted posts among them.
- **Content › News** in `/admin` (assumed, P2.33; WS08 files it in the grouped sidebar): every post with its source (Facebook post, Story, Hidden), Promote to story and Hide on mirrored posts, and New story. The pinned post reads "Pinned until" and its date in words beside the pin icon. Club news is posted from each club page and only listed here; recaps are edited from their tournament's Recap tab.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_results_archive.sql
CREATE TABLE event_results (
  tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  section_id TEXT REFERENCES tournament_sections(id),
  section TEXT NOT NULL,                     -- the section's name when the event was finalized
  place INTEGER NOT NULL,
  registration_id TEXT REFERENCES registrations(id), player_name TEXT NOT NULL, uscf_id TEXT,
  rating_pre INTEGER, rating_post INTEGER, score_halves INTEGER NOT NULL, tiebreaks_json TEXT,
  prize_cents INTEGER, prize_label TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  PRIMARY KEY (tournament_id, section, place, player_name)
);
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN finalized_at TEXT;
-- state_champions.tournament_id exists since 0049 (TEXT); no ALTER needed
-- tournaments.championship_title (added in WS05's migration) is the slug joined to state_champions.title for the ?title= roll

-- lca_posts (0037): id TEXT, slug, title, summary, body_html, image_url, link_url, link_label,
-- status 'draft'|'published', pinned, published_at, created_by, updated_by, created_at, updated_at
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'news' CHECK (kind IN ('news','recap'));
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN category TEXT CHECK (category IN ('results','announcements','clubs','scholastic','board'));
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'lca' CHECK (source IN ('lca','facebook'));
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN facebook_post_id TEXT;
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN facebook_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN promoted_at TEXT;      -- a mirrored post given a headline and its own page
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN promoted_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN hidden_at TEXT;        -- taken off the site; Facebook is untouched
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN hidden_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN tournament_id TEXT REFERENCES tournaments(id);
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN photos_json TEXT;      -- a recap's photos, up to four: {url, caption, on_card}
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN photo_consent INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE lca_posts ADD COLUMN pinned_until TEXT;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_lca_posts_facebook ON lca_posts(facebook_post_id) WHERE facebook_post_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_lca_posts_recap ON lca_posts(tournament_id) WHERE kind = 'recap';
-- lca_posts.featured is not added: the featured story is dropped (assumed, P2.39)

-- club_news (0001 and 0003): id TEXT, club_id TEXT, title, news_date, excerpt, created_at
ALTER TABLE club_news ADD COLUMN body_html TEXT;
ALTER TABLE club_news ADD COLUMN image_url TEXT;
ALTER TABLE club_news ADD COLUMN posted_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);
ALTER TABLE club_news ADD COLUMN status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'published' CHECK (status IN ('draft','published'));
ALTER TABLE club_news ADD COLUMN updated_at TEXT;

CREATE TABLE digest_subscriptions (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, member_id TEXT REFERENCES members(id), email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  frequency TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'weekly', created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
```
- A mirrored post is one `lca_posts` row with `source = 'facebook'`, `status = 'published'`, a `slug` of `fb-` plus the Facebook id, its first sentence as `title`, its text in `body_html` and its first photo, copied to the `CLUB_LOGOS` bucket under `news/`, in `image_url`. It has no page of its own until promoted and links out to `facebook_url` (every public reader enforces this; see the API below); promoting it sets the headline, a new slug, the category and `promoted_at`.
- Only one post is pinned at a time: `pinned` (0037) stays, with `pinned_until` beside it. A recap is a row with `kind = 'recap'` and its `tournament_id`, one per event. `category` drives the chips; the Clubs chip lists LCA stories about clubs, and club posts stay in From the clubs.
- Dates: `published_at` holds a date; a mirrored post's Facebook time and every `datetime('now')` column hold UTC and are shown with `formatDate`'s `{ stored: 'utc' }` (WS01 step 3).

**API**
- `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/finalize` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): writes the snapshot, sets `finalized_at` and opens the Recap tab. It answers 409, naming the boards, while a game has no TD result or a report waits; a second call changes nothing.
- `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/results/correct` (new; `requireAdmin`, audited in `admin_audit_log`): an LCA admin's correction to one snapshot row, with a reason; the recap's winners follow it.
- `GET /api/results` (new; public, cached) and `GET /api/results/:tournamentId` (new; public): the finalized LCA events by year and type, and one event's snapshot, which its results page reads. The list never holds a hidden draft or a member-created event; one event's snapshot answers whenever its event page does.
- `GET /api/champions` (exists, `functions/api/champions.ts`; extend with links to archived event results, and `?title=` for one title's roll, which the event page's Past champions link uses). It never links a member-created event.
- `GET /api/news` (extend): `source=all|facebook|lca`, `category=`, `year=` and a cursor for Load 10 more; returns Latest (hidden rows and club news left out; assumed, P2.31), Recent tournaments (the three newest finalized LCA events, with the published recap or the suggested headline and winners) and From the clubs (the latest published club news). Today `GET /api/news` (`functions/api/news.ts`) returns the latest 50 club news rows with each club's name and colour, and `GET /api/posts` (`functions/api/posts.ts`) lists published LCA posts.
- Every public reader of `lca_posts` (`functions/api/posts.ts`, `functions/api/posts/[slug].ts`, `functions/news/[slug].ts`, and, once they exist, WS02's `GET /api/search` (its news group), `/api/home`'s news (WS03) and `/news/rss.xml`) leaves out rows with `hidden_at` set, and serves a page only for `source = 'lca'` or `promoted_at IS NOT NULL`; `/news/fb-<id>` answers 404. Search links an unpromoted mirrored post to its `facebook_url`, never to `/news/fb-<id>`.
- `GET /api/admin/news` (new; `requireAdminView`): every post with its source and state. `POST /api/admin/news/:id/promote` `{ title, category, image }`, `POST /api/admin/news/:id/hide` and `/unhide` (new; `requireAdmin`, audited; LCA admins only, assumed, P2.32).
- `GET` and `PUT /api/admin/tournaments/:id/recap` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): the draft, pre-filled from `event_results`; 409 until `finalized_at` is set; 404 for a member-created event. `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/recap/publish`: refuses photos without consent, writes the post once, queues the digest entry and returns the share link.
- Club news keeps `functions/api/admin/clubs/[id]/news.ts` behind `requireClubRep`, whose check (`canManageClub`) admits an LCA admin, the club's own rep and the representative for the club's region, extended to the new columns.
- `GET /news/rss.xml` (new function, `functions/news/rss.xml.ts`).
- `POST /api/digest/subscribe` (new; public, rate-limited) and `POST /api/digest/unsubscribe/:token` (new; WS06's signed token, `functions/utils/tokens.ts`), sent by the email's one-click `List-Unsubscribe-Post` header and by the one button on the page its link opens; opening the link changes nothing.
- Every new or changed endpoint takes its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test, and comes off `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` (the pending list only shrinks, 0.2).

**Frontend**
- Change:
  - `src/pages/NewsPage.tsx` (Latest, Recent tournaments, From the clubs, the rail), `NewsPostPage.tsx` ("First posted on our Facebook page ↗" on promoted posts), `ChampionsPage.tsx` (served at `/about/champions`; its title toggles become `FilterChip`s, keeping `aria-pressed` and showing the chosen title by more than today's navy fill, which is hard to see in dark mode; `.small-caps` on the honour board heads; `LcaLogo`).
  - `src/areas/admin/components/PostsPanel.tsx` (Content › News: Promote to story, Hide, and the pin's end date in words) and `ChampionsPanel.tsx` (its event picker never offers a member-created event).
  - `src/lib/posts.ts`: the new pages date posts with `formatDate` from `@domain/format`, with the year option, and `{ stored: 'utc' }` for a stored timestamp; `formatPostDate` goes in the PR that removes today's News pages after `newNews` is on, and `test/unit/news-pinned.test.tsx` and `news-pinned-off.test.tsx` follow.
  - `src/areas/governance/components/RichTextEditor.tsx` moves to `src/components/RichTextEditor.tsx` with `scripts/move-files.ts`, since news posts and the governance pages share it; WS14 fixes its link extension.
  - `src/components/FacebookFeed.tsx`: the full variant on `/news` retires with the mirror; WS03 moves the compact rail on `/` to the mirror; `facebook_feed_cache` and `GET /api/facebook-posts` retire once nothing reads them.
  - `src/lib/themeScopes.ts` and `src/App.tsx`: `/about/champions` joins `HERITAGE_ROUTES` and the `HeritageLayout` route once `newNews` is on; `/champions` then redirects and, like `/state-champions` today, resolves to base. Until then `/champions` stays the heritage page it is.
  - `src/areas/events/api.ts`, where `getNews`, `getPosts` and `getChampions` live today: the news, results, recap and digest calls.
- New (in `src/areas/events/components`, beside `PrizeWinners`, unless a path is given):
  - `src/pages/ResultsPage.tsx` (the index)
  - `LatestList.tsx`, `CategoryChips.tsx` and `SourceFilter.tsx` (both on `FilterChip`, with counts), `RecentTournaments.tsx`, `FromTheClubs.tsx`, `PromoteSheet.tsx`, `DigestSignup.tsx`
  - `RecapTab.tsx` with `RecapEditor.tsx` and `WinnerCards.tsx`, the form inside `ScrollPanel` with its two buttons fixed above the tab bar on phones (D16)
- Pictures in Latest, Recent tournaments and recaps hold their box from the start (width and height, or an aspect ratio) and load lazily below the fold, so nothing moves when they arrive. `ResponsiveImage` serves the build's own generated pictures, so uploaded and mirrored photos are plain images with their size set.
- A club's colour dot is decoration beside the club's name, which always shows; it follows WS09's rule for a club's colour: never words, and navy where a club has no colour of its own or still has the database's default gold.
- From the design system (WS01): `FilterChip`, `ScrollPanel`, `LcaLogo`, `HeritageLayout` with its hooks, `ThickThinRule` and `Seal` on `/about/champions`, and `formatDate`.
- Not built: `FeaturedStory.tsx` (assumed, P2.39), `ResultsEventPage.tsx` (the event's standings page is its results page), the About-D timeline (assumed, P2.40) and champion submissions (assumed, P2.36).
- **Switches** (0.2's table, all off by default): `newNews` (the News page with Latest, the Facebook mirror, Recent tournaments, From the clubs and the digest sign-up; the Recap tab; the results index; Content › News; and `/about/champions`, with `/champions` and `/state-champions` redirecting to it); the existing `homeRecap` can go on in WS10, and `themeHeritage` covers `/about/champions`. The Facebook sync, which writes rows today's readers would list, follows `NEWS_MIRROR_ON` (Jobs, below).

**Jobs & integrations**
- **Facebook sync** (new): a phase of the five-minute run of `workers/daily-emails` that acts in the runs at :00 and :30, every 30 minutes (section 2.2), and only while `NEWS_MIRROR_ON` (`functions/utils/news/mirror.ts`) is set, a constant set in the same change that turns `newNews` on, with a unit test that the two agree. Until then no mirrored row exists, so today's News page and `GET /api/posts`, which list every published row and link each to a page of its own, never list a Facebook post. It pulls the LCA page's recent posts through the Graph API with the page settings `functions/api/facebook-posts.ts` uses today (verify: today that function calls the Graph API on each request and falls back to the single-row `facebook_feed_cache`). It upserts by `facebook_post_id` and updates edited text. It treats a post as removed, and takes it off the site, only when a fetch whose `since`/`until` range covers that post's `created_time` no longer returns it; posts older than the fetch window are left alone. A post an admin hid stays hidden. On first sync each post's first photo is copied to the `CLUB_LOGOS` bucket under `news/`, since Graph API photo URLs expire, and `image_url` holds the copy (recap photos picked from Facebook are copied the same way); WS09, which lands first in Phase 3, has bound that bucket's preview copy, so a preview never writes to the live bucket (0.1 rule 8). The worker gets `FACEBOOK_PAGE_TOKEN` and `FACEBOOK_PAGE_ID` as its own secrets. It keeps the worker's per-phase isolation, and no new cron or worker is added for it. Each run records its outcome through WS08's `recordRun`, which the admin home's automation health shows (WS08, `Work-J`); the digest's runs are recorded the same way. The site reads only the table.
- **Digest:** a timed phase of the five-minute run of `workers/daily-emails` (section 2.2) that sends in the first run at or after 8:00 AM on a Monday in America/Chicago (`functions/utils/time.ts`), once a week by its own last run, so the hour follows Louisiana's clock in summer and in winter. Each send goes through WS07's outbox with the dedupe key `digest:<ISO week>:<subscription id>`, so a subscriber gets one email a week.
- Optionally, a nightly job that backfills `rating_post` from US Chess once official ratings appear, rate-limited.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. Finalizing creates an immutable snapshot and a recap draft in the Recap tab with the winners filled in; running it twice does nothing; while a game has no TD result or a report waits it answers 409 and names the boards.
2. No player profile pages exist (D9); names in results and recaps are plain text, in full (D3).
3. Champions show the reigning holders and a full honour roll with "Show all N years". Each title links to its archived event results when they exist, and names stay plain text (assumed, P2.37).
4. News shows the one pin until its end date, filters by category and by source with both in the address, dates every row with its weekday, and loads 10 more at a time.
5. The RSS feed validates, and unsubscribing from the digest takes one click.
6. A recap can't be published with photos until the consent box is ticked.
7. A post on the LCA Facebook page shows in Latest after the next sync, marked "From our Facebook page ↗", with no Facebook script on the page; Hide removes it from the site, leaves Facebook alone, and survives later syncs.
8. Promote to story gives a mirrored post a headline and its own page that keeps "First posted on our Facebook page ↗"; only LCA admins can promote or hide (assumed, P2.32).
9. Recent tournaments shows at most three finished LCA events, newest first, each with Full results to the event's results page; no standings table or crosstable renders on `/news`.
10. The Recap tab is disabled with "Opens when standings are final" until the event is finalized. Publishing fills the card, adds the Results row to Latest and "Read the recap" to the results page, makes the share card and adds the recap to the next digest, exactly once.
11. A published club post shows on its club page, in From the clubs and in the digest, and not in Latest (assumed, P2.31). The club's own rep, the region's representative and an LCA admin can post one; anyone else gets 403.
12. The digest goes out on Mondays at 8:00 AM, Louisiana time (assumed, P2.44), with new stories and recaps, upcoming LCA events and club news, once per subscriber per week.
13. An unpromoted mirrored post has no page (`/news/fb-<id>` answers 404), search never links to one, and a hidden post is absent from `/api/posts`, `/api/home`, `/api/search`, `/news/<slug>` and the RSS feed.
14. A finalized member-created event is absent from `GET /api/results`, `/results`, Recent tournaments, Latest, the digest, the RSS feed and `GET /api/champions`, and its recap endpoints answer 404; its results page opens from its link and carries noindex.
15. `/about/champions` draws inside `HeritageLayout` with `themeHeritage` on, and `/champions` and `/state-champions` redirect to it, keeping `?title=`. Its title filter is `FilterChip`s that say which is on (`aria-pressed`), its honour board heads take `.small-caps`, and it shows the official logo (D1).
16. With `newNews` on, no date on `/news`, `/news/:slug` or the admin News list lacks its weekday; with it off today's pages keep today's dates; `formatPostDate` goes with today's pages.
17. `/news` carries Follow on Facebook, and a link to Facebook on every mirrored and every promoted post.
18. `/news` and `/news/:slug` are in the accessibility matrix (`PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`), and the matrix reads `/about/champions` where WS01's AC1 names `/champions`, with zero serious or critical violations in the light and dark looks (and the heritage look for Champions) at 390 px and 1440 px.
19. No picture on `/news` moves the page when it arrives: CLS stays under 0.1 with the pictures held back.
20. A champion's name with a letter outside the latin files is drawn in the heritage face, and the latin-ext file is fetched only on a page that shows such a name.
21. With `NEWS_MIRROR_ON` unset the Facebook sync writes no row, so today's News page lists no Facebook post; a unit test holds `NEWS_MIRROR_ON` equal to `newNews`.

**Tests**
- Integration (`test/integration`):
  - `results-archive.test.ts`: finalize and its snapshot (idempotent; 409 while a game has no TD result or a report waits), the admin correction (audited, admins only, with a role-safety case), and `GET /api/results`.
  - `results-member-events.test.ts`: criterion 14.
  - `facebook-sync.test.ts`: insert, edit, a post leaving the page, a post older than the fetch window staying visible, a hidden post staying hidden, a second run changing nothing, and nothing written with `NEWS_MIRROR_ON` unset (criterion 21).
  - `news-readers.test.ts`: an unpromoted mirrored post answers 404 at `/news/fb-<id>`, and a hidden post is absent from every public reader.
  - `news-api.test.ts` (new): `GET /api/news`'s Recent tournaments, at most three finished LCA events, newest first, each with its results page's link (criterion 9).
  - `news-promote.test.ts`: promote and hide, admins only, with a role-safety case.
  - `recap.test.ts`: the draft and publish (409 before finalize, 404 for a member-created event, photos refused without consent, publishing twice changing nothing).
  - `club-news.test.ts`: the club's own rep, the region's representative and an admin can post; the rep of another club and the representative of another region get 403.
  - `digest.test.ts`: subscribe, unsubscribe by token, the Monday 8:00 AM Central run in summer and in winter, and one email a week.
  - `rss.test.ts`; extend `champions.test.ts` (`?title=`, links to archived results, no member-created event); a contract test for each new endpoint in `test/integration/contracts/`.
- Unit: `test/unit/recap-draft.test.ts` (the headline suggestion and the winners), `test/unit/digest-builder.test.ts`, `test/unit/news-dates.test.tsx` (weekday dates with `newNews` on, criterion 16, and `{ stored: 'utc' }` for a Facebook post's time), `test/unit/news-latest.test.tsx` (new: criterion 4, the one pin until its end date and gone after it, the category and source chips in the address, a weekday on every row, and Load 10 more), `test/unit/news-page.test.tsx` (new: no standings table or crosstable on `/news`, criterion 9, and Follow on Facebook with a Facebook link on every mirrored and promoted post, criterion 17), `test/unit/news-mirror-switch.test.ts` (new: `NEWS_MIRROR_ON` equals `newNews`), `test/unit/themeScopes.test.ts` and `test/unit/heritage-app-routes.test.tsx` (`/about/champions` heritage, `/champions` base once it redirects, and `/champions` and `/state-champions` redirecting to `/about/champions` with `?title=` kept once `newNews` is on, criterion 15), `test/unit/heritage-hooks.test.tsx` (`.small-caps` on the honour board heads), `test/unit/features.test.ts` (`newNews` starts off).
- Browser: `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` (criterion 18), with `newNews` on in the a11y-on build (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`) and `test/a11y/baseline.json` unchanged, `test/a11y/heritage.spec.ts` (`/about/champions`, and a fixture champion whose name needs a letter outside the latin files, drawn in the heritage face with the latin-ext file fetched on that page only, criterion 20), and `test/a11y/news-shift.spec.ts` (new): the picture-shift check on `/news` (criterion 19).

**Depends on:** WS01, WS07 (the outbox, and the standings page that becomes each event's results page), WS08 (Finalize, and the Recap tab's place in the console), WS09 (the club pages whose news From the clubs shows); WS04's share previews and WS06's signed tokens are reused; member-created events come from WS08's creation work.

**Risks & open decisions:**
- Digitising historical champion records is volunteer work.
- Photos of minors need the consent routine.
- The Facebook page token expires: the sync must say so in the admin home's automation health rather than fail quietly, and Facebook may change what the Graph API returns for shared posts.
- Open for K: P2.30 to P2.33, P2.36, P2.37, P2.39, P2.40, P2.42 and P2.44, and Q13: a member-created event has no recap.

---

## WS11 · Scholastic page, season schedule & series
**Why:** Scholastic families are LCA's biggest group of newcomers. Most of them aren't chess players, and most are on phones. Peers walk families through a first tournament:
- grade-band sections
- a safeguarding link
- a first-year discount: LCA has one entry fee per event (D11), so it offers none, and the first two apply

(New South Wales, Virginia, Ireland, England.) No Gulf South peer does this. Jargon needs explaining inline, and the site should say plainly how little it collects about children. K's note on page 10 (Fri, Oct 9, 2026) keeps Schol-A's sections for parents, a minimal block of the nearest clubs ("these will just have to be clubs since we have no real youth programs associated"), the scholastic honor roll, and a space for the yearly scholastic schedule once one is published.

**Canvas boards:** `Schol-Final` (the page at 1440px) and `Schol-Final-2` (phones), decided Fri, Oct 9, 2026, with `Looks-6-Scholastic-Tile` and `Looks-6-Scholastic-Page` for the look. They replace `Schol-A`, `Schol-C` and `Schol-Phones`; `Schol-B` stays parked for Phase 5. As drawn, both still head Schol-A's guide "Before your first event", leave Schol-A's questions out and say "Kids get no login". Until they are redrawn, this text wins: the guide is "Your child's first tournament", the questions sit under "Before your first event", and the privacy card says "Kids under 13 get no login" (assumed, P2.27).

**User-facing scope**
- **One page for parents** (`/scholastic`): no hero, no doors and no audience switch (assumed, P2.43). The heading "Scholastic chess", the line "For parents of K-12 players", and seven jump links in page order: Your child's first tournament · Before your first event · Upcoming events · This season's schedule · Clubs near you · Honor roll · Ask a person. Coach and school tools come later (assumed, P2.43).
- **Your child's first tournament**, first (Schol-A's guide; assumed, P2.43): the day from check-in to awards (times marked Sample, "~" for a round that starts when the one before ends; opened from an event, `/scholastic?event=<id>`, the event's own times); what to bring (ticks saved on the device only, with Print); chess words in plain English (G/30+5 with "30 minutes each, plus 5 seconds per move", section, bye, rated, touch-move, notation); sections by grade band, with eligibility as one yes or no box; costs (Scholastic $5 and Family $25 a year, and each event's one entry fee; LCA-run events need an LCA membership, added at checkout; no member price, no sibling or lunch discount, D5 and D11); safety, with Report a concern; and "What LCA keeps about your child": first and last name, an optional US Chess ID, the eligibility confirmations, and the scholastic emergency contact whenever WS06 collects it (S7.9; assumed, P2.28), nothing else. There is no separate `/scholastic/first-tournament` page (it is not a route today).
- **Before your first event:** Schol-A's questions parents ask, as Schol-A drew them, kept as K picked them.
- **Upcoming scholastic events:** LCA and partner, from `GET /api/events` (WS04), which never lists a member-created event. LCA rows show status in words, places left and one fee; partner rows say "Registers on the organizer's site ↗" with no Register and no count. The New Orleans Youth Chess Grand Prix is one line above the list; its Sat, Nov 14, 2026 and Sat, Dec 5, 2026 meets list as LCA events, and its Sat, Mar 20, 2027 and Sat, May 8, 2027 meets as partner events (assumed, P2.25). Nearby-state events sit behind "Show nearby-state events". The iCal link stays.
- **This season's scholastic schedule** (new): the placeholder "The 2026 to 2027 scholastic schedule is being set." with one email field, "Email me when it's out", and the promise "we store your email and nothing else" (assumed, P2.44), until an admin publishes the season; then dated rows with weekdays, each linking to its event page once the event exists, while rows for events not yet created show the date, name and city, with no link. One line points to "Season at a glance" on the Tournaments page for open events (assumed, P2.22). The admin's season screen never offers a member-created event to link.
- **Find a club near you** (replaces the youth program finder): the three clubs nearest the visitor's place, nearest first, each with when it meets, in the club's own words (`how_we_meet_text`, WS09), and how far (`club_distances`); "Near you: Baton Rouge 70808 · Change" above them; until a place is given, the block shows the city or ZIP field and "Use my location". The place is read once; a typed city or ZIP is sent only to look up a point, and is never stored or logged. Clubs only, no program-type filters, and "LCA lists clubs; it doesn't vet or certify them". "Kids welcome" shows on a card only when the club lists it, through the "Scholastic friendly" tick on its page (`clubs.scholastic_friendly`, WS09).
- **Honor roll:** the latest State Scholastic champions by grade band, names in plain text, and "Show all years" to `/about/champions?title=` for the scholastic titles once `newNews` is on (WS10), and to today's `/champions` until then.
- **Still unsure? Ask a person:** a short form that writes to the board's Scholastic Director seat (`scholastic-director`), or to the whole board while the seat has no holder (assumed, P2.24). Report a concern routes the same way. No personal email appears on the page.
- **Phones:** the same page and order; the timeline as a list; what to bring, chess words, safety and privacy as accordions with 52px rows; sections and costs open; four event rows, then "Show 3 more"; one club at a time with "1 of 3", Previous and Next (44px) and a swipe; the honor roll as a chip-filtered list, one chip per grade band.
- **Team and coach entries** move out of Phase 3 to wait with Schol-B in Phase 5 (assumed, P2.26); the coach role is not added now. In Phase 5:
  1. A coach role registers a team: players plus sections.
  2. Each parent gets a confirmation link and confirms, then pays or marks it "school pays" (invoice).
  3. Players whose parents haven't confirmed are not registered.
- **Pathway and series** (Schol-B, Phase 5):
  - A staircase: club night → first meet → NOLA Youth Chess Grand Prix → State Scholastic → Nationals.
  - Its rungs list the real upcoming events.
  - Series standings are computed from `event_results` (WS10), never from a member-created event. Partner-run meets count only when organizers run them in LCA's tools or upload final standings.
- **Theme:** the Bright Scholastic look covers `/scholastic` only, never registration, checkout, the event page, pairings or standings, which show a grade-band chip with its words ("K-5"). Navy carries all text and buttons; a band's hue only ever marks its grade band, beside the band's words; status tags use the base status tokens. The look stays light on a dark device. WS11 adds its Baloo 2 and Nunito faces, loaded only inside the scope, and K previews the look before `themeScholastic` goes on.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_season_schedule.sql
CREATE TABLE season_schedule (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
  season TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- '2026-27'
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('scholastic','open')),
  event_date TEXT NOT NULL,                        -- first day, YYYY-MM-DD; shown with its weekday
  end_date TEXT,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  city TEXT,
  tournament_id TEXT REFERENCES tournaments(id),   -- set once the event exists; the row then links to it
  published_at TEXT,                               -- a season shows only once published
  created_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id),
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
CREATE INDEX idx_season_schedule ON season_schedule(kind, season, event_date);
CREATE TABLE season_notify (
  email TEXT NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('scholastic','open')),
  season TEXT NOT NULL,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  PRIMARY KEY (email, kind, season)
);
-- clubs.kind ('youth_program') is not added: LCA has no youth programs, only clubs.

-- Phase 5 (assumed, P2.26): 00xx_scholastic_teams.sql
CREATE TABLE team_entries (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, tournament_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  coach_member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id), school_name TEXT NOT NULL,
  payment_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'parents' CHECK (payment_mode IN ('parents','school_invoice')),
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'open', created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
ALTER TABLE registrations ADD COLUMN team_entry_id INTEGER REFERENCES team_entries(id);
CREATE TABLE series (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, name TEXT NOT NULL, season TEXT NOT NULL, scoring_json TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE series_events (series_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES series(id), tournament_id TEXT REFERENCES tournaments(id),
  clearinghouse_slug TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (series_id, tournament_id, clearinghouse_slug));
```
Publishing a season sends one email to each address in `season_notify` for that kind and season, through the WS07 outbox, then deletes those rows. Nothing else is kept. In Phase 5, add a `coach` role to `src/areas/admin/roles.ts` and to the server-side role checks, after verifying how roles are stored (`members.role` and its CHECK constraint).

**API**
- `GET /api/scholastic` (new): the upcoming scholastic events, the published scholastic season (or none), the three nearest clubs for `near=` (a ZIP, a city or a rounded point, sent only to look up a point and never stored or logged; distances from WS09's helper), and the latest scholastic champions.
- `GET /api/season?kind=scholastic|open` (new; public, cached) for this page and WS04's strip.
- `POST /api/season/notify` (new; public, rate-limited) `{ email, kind }`.
- WS11 builds the admin writes in Phase 3: `GET` and `PUT /api/admin/season` and `POST /api/admin/season/publish` (new; `requireAdmin`, audited), with `src/areas/admin/components/SeasonSchedulePanel.tsx` in today's `/admin` page. WS08 files it under Tournaments › Season schedule when the grouped sidebar lands in Phase 4.
- Ask a person and Report a concern use `POST /api/contact` (`functions/api/contact.ts`), which already opens a ticket routed to a seat by its slug through `createTicket` (`functions/utils/admin/tickets.ts` since WS01 step 16; tickets carry `seat_id`). WS11 adds a whole-board target, unless WS10 has added it for its Missing years card; the region target, the rate limit the endpoint lacks today and its contract (it is on the pending list today) come earlier in Phase 3 with WS09, and WS14 reuses the region target. No new board endpoint is built. verify: how an unknown or unheld seat falls back today (as read, an unknown slug becomes a general inquiry with no seat, and a seat with no holder still gets the ticket, with only the staff notice to `CONTACT_EMAIL` sent).
- A club page's contact form (WS09) is a relay to the club, never a ticket; WS11 does not change it.
- Phase 5 (assumed, P2.26): `POST /api/teams` (coach), `POST /api/teams/:id/confirm/:token` (parent, signed token) and `GET /api/series/:id/standings`.
- Every new or changed endpoint takes its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test, and comes off `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` (the pending list only shrinks, 0.2).

**Frontend**
- Change: `src/pages/ScholasticPage.tsx` becomes the whole page:
  - its `fetch('/api/clearinghouse?upcoming=true')` moves into `src/areas/events/api.ts`, and the page comes off `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY` in `test/unit/area-boundaries.test.ts`
  - below 1024 px its grid takes one column (`grid-cols-1`), so nothing scrolls sideways at 320 px (WCAG 1.4.10; today the page is 334 pixels wide there)
  - its status pills move from `StatusBadge`'s `tone` path to the `status` prop
- `src/lib/themeScopes.ts`: `routeScope` gives `/scholastic` the scholastic look once `themeScholastic` is on (the map's comment already leaves this to the Scholastic work). `src/lib/scopeStylesheets.ts` gains `src/styles/themes/scholastic.css` with the Baloo 2 and Nunito faces (`@fontsource-variable/baloo-2` and `@fontsource-variable/nunito`; verify each name on npm, and pin each exactly at a version at least two weeks old).
- New (in `src/areas/events/components`, beside the scholastic rule in `src/areas/events/scholastic.ts`, unless a path is given): `DayTimeline.tsx`, `WhatToBring.tsx`, `Glossary.tsx`, `SeasonSchedule.tsx` (shared with WS04's strip), `NearestClubs.tsx` (in place of `ProgramFinder.tsx`), `HonorRollByBand.tsx`, `AskAPerson.tsx`; `src/areas/admin/components/SeasonSchedulePanel.tsx` (the season list and Publish season, for LCA admins).
- From the design system (WS01): `ThemeScope scope="scholastic"` as the page's box, with `ScopeStylesheetReady scope="scholastic"` holding the page until the faces' stylesheet arrives, as `HeritageLayout` does for heritage; the scholastic tokens (`--coral`, `--mint`, `--sky`, `--grape` and their `-tile`s) for the grade-band chips, always beside the band's words; `StatusBadge` with the `status` prop for event status in words; `DateBlock` for event and season dates; `FilterChip` for the honor roll's grade-band chips on phones.
- Not built in Phase 3: `FirstTournamentPage.tsx` (folded into `ScholasticPage.tsx`), `AudienceSwitch.tsx` (assumed, P2.43) and `ProgramFinder.tsx`; `TeamEntryForm.tsx` and `SeriesStandings.tsx` wait for Phase 5 (assumed, P2.26).
- **Switches** (0.2's table, all off by default): `newScholastic` (the one page for parents); `seasonSchedule` (the season block here, WS04's Season at a glance and the admin season screen); the existing `themeScholastic` (the Bright Scholastic look and its faces on `/scholastic`, shown only with `newLook` on).

**Jobs & integrations:** publishing a season sends one email to each address on its list through WS07's outbox (dedupe key `season:<kind>:<season>:<email>`), then deletes the list. In Phase 5, Resend handles parent confirmations and reminders for team entries.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. At 390 px the guide shows with no sideways scroll and body text of 16 px or more, and glossary terms open inline without leaving the page.
2. The "What LCA keeps about your child" list matches the fields WS06's registration form and family pages actually collect about a child, the scholastic emergency contact included whenever WS06 collects it (assumed, P2.28); a test compares the two. The privacy card reads "Kids under 13 get no login" (assumed, P2.27).
3. Find a club near you shows the three clubs nearest the visitor's place, nearest first, each with when it meets; changing the place re-ranks them; there are no filters; on phones one club shows at a time with "1 of 3"; and the place is never stored or logged.
4. The scholastic look applies only under `/scholastic`, never on registration, checkout, the event page, pairings or standings.
5. Moves to Phase 5 with team entries (assumed, P2.26): team entry registers no player until that player's parent has confirmed through the signed link.
6. With no published scholastic season, the schedule block shows the placeholder and one email field. Publishing the season shows dated rows with weekdays, each linking to its event page when one exists, and sends exactly one email to each address on the list, after which the list is empty.
7. Ask a person and Report a concern create a ticket, through `POST /api/contact`, for the Scholastic Director seat, or for the whole board while the seat has no holder (assumed, P2.24); no personal email appears in the HTML.
8. No member price, sibling discount or lunch discount renders anywhere under `/scholastic` (D5, D11).
9. `/scholastic` does not scroll sideways at 320 px, and its event status shows through `StatusBadge`'s `status` prop, in words.
10. No member-created event appears among the upcoming events or in the season rows.
11. The scholastic font files never appear in the network log of `/`; CLS on `/scholastic` stays under 0.1 across the font swap; and `/scholastic` has zero serious or critical axe violations in the scholastic look, on a light and on a dark device, at 390 px and 1440 px.
12. `ScholasticPage.tsx` calls no `fetch`.

**Tests**
- Integration (`test/integration`): `season-schedule.test.ts` (publishing a season and its one email, admins only with a role-safety case, the list emptying after the send, and `GET /api/season?kind=open` for WS04's strip), `contact-routing.test.ts` (Ask a person and Report a concern with the seat held and with the seat open, and the rate limit), `scholastic-endpoint.test.ts` (the nearest-clubs order with fixture clubs, no member-created event, and `near=` kept out of every log line and table), and a contract test for each new or changed endpoint in `test/integration/contracts/`.
- Unit: `test/unit/scholastic-fields.test.ts` (the field-list guard for criterion 2), `test/unit/scholastic-page.test.tsx` (no member price words, tags through the `status` prop, the "under 13" line), `test/unit/glossary.test.tsx` (new: each chess word opens its plain meaning in place, with no new page, criterion 1), `test/unit/nearest-clubs.test.tsx` (new: the three nearest first, a new place re-ranking them, no filters, and on a phone one club at a time with "1 of 3", Previous and Next, criterion 3), `test/unit/themeScopes.test.ts` (`/scholastic` in the scholastic look only with the switch on), `test/unit/area-boundaries.test.ts` (the page off `PAGE_FETCH_TODAY`), `test/unit/features.test.ts` (`newScholastic` and `seasonSchedule` start off).
- Browser: `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` reads `/scholastic` in the scholastic look, beside the token fixture page, with `newScholastic` and `themeScholastic` on in the a11y-on build (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`) and `test/a11y/baseline.json` unchanged; `test/a11y/scholastic-fonts.spec.ts` (criterion 11's network log and CLS, the 320 px width, and criterion 1's guide at 390 px); and WS04's `test/a11y/tournaments.spec.ts` gains Season at a glance with a published open season and with none, the Scholastic page's line linking to it (WS04's criterion 15).
- Phase 5: `test/integration/team-entries.test.ts` (team entry and parent confirmation through the signed link, and role-safety for the coach role) and `test/unit/series-scoring.test.ts`.

**Depends on:** WS01, WS04 (`GET /api/events`; Season at a glance shares `SeasonSchedule.tsx`), WS06 (the registration form the field-list guard compares with), WS07 (the outbox), WS09 (the place lookup and distance helper, and `POST /api/contact`'s rate limit and contract). The series also needs WS10.

**Risks & open decisions:** partner organizers have to cooperate for series points. A coach role (Phase 5) means more volunteer support. The season list needs an admin to enter it each year. Open for K: P2.22, P2.24 to P2.28, P2.43 and P2.44.
