## WS12 · Membership, giving & digital card
**Why:**
- Membership tiers depend on who you are, not on upgrading: Family ($25) equals Adult plus two Scholastic, so it only saves money with three children.
- Every tier carries the same benefits, so saying what a membership is for before its price leaves the tier cards nothing to compare but who they cover (`Member-E`).
- K, Tue, Oct 13, 2026: "we are service focused. people mainly donate by participation. but it should be there as an option." So the dues are told as the work they pay for, and giving is one quiet line and one plain page.
- Only 4% of nonprofit sites say where donations go (NN/g); here the treasurer's report is the one place with figures, linked from both pages.
- 19% of people abandon a payment because they don't trust the form (Baymard).
- The old system emailed PDF cards. A card with a QR code also speeds up check-in.

**Canvas boards** (K, Thu, Oct 15, 2026: "For 13, I like E and E-2 best, but I prefer the "What you dues fund" section from F (the What your dues do). Take that from it."):
- `Member-E` (benefits first) and `Member-E-2` (phones, and the states: signed in and active, signed in and expired, the Am I a member? answers), with `Member-F`'s "What your dues do" band, and its two thin states from `Member-F-2` (a tile with no photo, no report published), in place of Member-E's "What your dues fund" ledger. Until the redraw is on the canvas, build from Member-E with Member-F's band.
- `Give-A`, the plain Give page, as drawn. K did not name it, so it stands as the default (assumed, Q6).
- The card: `Member-D` (the digital and printable card), shown in My LCA as `Account-D` draws it (WS13), with Show my card up top on phones (`Account-Final-Hub-2` F1). Open `Member-D` on the canvas: it has no copy in `docs/redesign/canvas/boards`.
- They replace `Member-A`'s page, `Member-B` and `Member-C`. Member-A's two questions stay, as the household optimizer behind "Not sure? Answer two questions".

**User-facing scope**
- **`/membership` (`Member-E`), in this order:**
  - **The hero** (navy, one column): "MEMBERSHIP", the h1 "Join 300+ Louisiana chess players", the line under it, "From $5 a year. Every membership gets the same four things; the price depends only on who it covers." and three paths: See memberships · Renew · Am I a member?
  - **What you get,** four tiles: Entry to the tournaments LCA runs ("An LCA membership is required to enter them ... Entry fees are the same for everyone.", D11, D12); A member profile (your events, results and membership in one place, with the card and its QR for check-in at the director's table); Voting rights (at the annual meeting, Labor Day weekend); Online registration (yourself and your children in one order, and renewing in one tap from My LCA).
  - **Pick by who it covers,** the four tier cards: Adult $15 (18 and up), Scholastic $5 (K-12), Family $25 (you and up to 3 children, tagged "Best value with 3 children", with the honest math: "with 1 child, Adult + Scholastic ($20) costs less; with 2 it's a tie; with 3 Family saves $5"), Senior $10 (65 and up). Each has Join and a Renew link. Prices come from `domain/membership/tiers.ts`, which the checkout reads too. The footnote says prices are per year and are what you pay (D2). "Not sure? Answer two questions →" opens the household optimizer: who's joining (just me, me and children, children only) and whether anyone is 65 or older; it prices every valid combination (Adult or Senior plus Scholastic × n, or Family), recommends the cheapest and shows the math ("Family only saves with three children").
  - **What your dues do** (`Member-F`'s band): the small label above the heading, "WHERE THE MONEY GOES", the h2, and the intro "LCA is a volunteer board and 300+ members. Dues and entry fees pay for nearly everything below, which is why most people support LCA simply by joining and playing." Four story tiles, each with a photo slot (the section's icon on a cream panel until LCA supplies a photo): The tournaments LCA runs · The scholastic program · The State Championship · This website. Then one link row, "The figures are in the treasurer's report, {fiscal year} →", to the newest treasurer's report on the Minutes page (a web page with a PDF copy). No amounts, no bars, no placeholders. With no report published, the row reads "The treasurer publishes a report after each fiscal year; the latest is on the Minutes page." with no link.
  - **Membership that takes care of itself,** four panels: auto-renew, if you want it (off by default; an email a week before each charge); reminders 30 and 7 days before the end, with a one-tap renew link that needs no password; a check when you register (WS06's membership step, D12); and give one as a gift (below).
  - **Questions,** six, the first two open: "Is this the same as US Chess membership?"; "When does my membership end?" ("One year from the day you join. Renewing early adds a year on top of the time you have left, so it never loses you time."); "Do I need to be a member to play in LCA events?"; "Who counts as a child on the Family membership?" ("We never ask for a grade, a birthdate or a child's email."); "Are there any fees on top of the price?" (no, D2); and "Can I get a refund?", which shows only once the board has written the rule (assumed, Q17).
  - **The Give line,** near the end: "Want to give beyond your dues?" with "Most people support LCA by joining and playing. If you'd like to give more, you can, once or monthly." and a small Give button to `/give`. No fund, goal, bar or amount anywhere on the page.
  - Gone from v1.3 and from `Member-B`: every member-price phrase (D11), the home-club member counts (D17) and the Nationals fund framing.
  - **Signed in** (`Member-E-2`). Active: the header shows Donate · My LCA ▾; a line under the hero's first sentence reads "You're a member through {weekday date} · {tier} · My LCA"; the member profile tile gains "Open my card"; and the tier's button reads "Renew · $25 · through {new weekday date}". Expired: Renew takes the Join slot in the header, the hero's first button is "Renew · $25", and the first tile adds "Your membership ended {weekday date}; renew to enter LCA events." Phones as `Member-E-2` draws them.
- **Join and Renew** are separate paths. Renew shows the current status and end date, and extends from the current end date, not from today. Auto-renew is optional (a yearly Stripe subscription), with an email a week before each charge and an off switch in My LCA. Signed out, Renew asks for a sign-in or sends a renewal link by email.
- **"Am I a member?"** (`Member-E-2`): a US Chess ID or an email. It answers only the first name, last initial, tier, status in words and the month it ends ("Danielle R. · Family · Active · ends February 2027"). An expired membership adds "Renew by email link", which sends a signed renewal link to the address on file without showing it. Not found: "No membership found for that. You may have joined with a different email. Write to LouisianaChess@gmail.com." After 10 lookups from one connection in an hour: "Too many lookups from this connection. Try again in an hour."
- **Family coverage is chosen, not by date:** at purchase and renewal the guardian ticks up to 3 children per plan (`member_guardians.family_covered`) on a "Who's covered?" screen: the parent's own line fixed, each saved child with "Covered by this plan" (the first 3 ticked, a 4th tick disabled with "Family covers you and 3 children"), and an inline "Add a family member". An open spot can be filled at any time; the ticks otherwise change at renewal (assumed, S7.8). A 4th child is offered Scholastic $5 as a line in the same payment ("Not covered · Add Scholastic for $5") and is never silently left out. In two-household families each guardian's plan can cover the child, and the later expiry wins. Family's one-line description everywhere is "Everyone on the plan gets a LCA membership".
- **No child emails at purchase:** "Children don't need an email. Everything comes to you. Each child can get their own login at 13 or older from My LCA."
- **Success page:** "Covered through Sun, Oct 10, 2027: Danielle, Maya, Leo. One open spot.", each name as it will print on entry lists with "Fix a spelling", and the buttons "Register my family for an event" and "Go to My LCA".
- **Renewal reminders,** 30 and 7 days before the end: they list the covered children, flag anyone who now manages their own profile ("Maya now manages her own profile · Adult $15"), and carry the one-tap renew link.
- **Inside the checkout:** the household optimizer runs in WS06's membership step when the event requires a membership and someone in the order lacks one (D12). WS06 builds its pricing rule in Phase 1 (section 4); `HouseholdOptimizer.tsx` puts the two questions in front of the same rule.
- **TD membership desk:** sells Family for card or cash at the venue; walk-in guest children of the paying adult become managed profiles on the spot (assumed, S7.12).
- **Gift memberships** (`Member-E`'s fourth panel, new in 1.4): the buyer picks Scholastic $5 or Adult $15, types the person's email (for a child, a parent's or guardian's), and pays through Stripe Checkout, with the one receipt (D14). The person gets an email with a signed link and claims the membership onto their own profile, or onto a child's profile they manage. Nothing activates until it is claimed, and the buyer sees it in My LCA as "Sent to {email} · not claimed yet · Send again". No name, grade or birthdate is asked.
- **Give** (`/give`, `Give-A`; assumed, Q6), in place of v1.3's Donate page with its funds:
  - The small label "GIVE" above the heading, the h1 "Give to Louisiana chess", and one sentence: "Most people support LCA by joining and playing: dues and entry fees pay for nearly everything a volunteer board and 300+ members do. If you'd like to give beyond that, this is the place, and thank you."
  - One form: Once or Monthly; $10 · $25 · $50 · $100 · Other ("$1 or more", with a numeric keypad); "Put it toward (optional)": General (the default) · Scholastic program · Equipment, with "No goals, no targets; the treasurer's report says what each year's gifts paid for."; and one gold button, "Continue to Stripe · $25" (or "Continue to Stripe · $25 a month"). Under it: Stripe's checkout asks for the email and card, "LCA never sees or stores your card number", and "Your receipt comes by email from LCA, right away." There are no name or email fields on the page; a signed-in member's email goes to Stripe so the receipt reaches them.
  - "Where the money goes": the same treasurer's report link as the membership page. "Most help isn't money": Join LCA · Enter a tournament · Bring someone to club night · Volunteer at a scholastic event (write to LouisianaChess@gmail.com). The "Prefer a check?" line shows only once the board supplies the mailing address (assumed, Q17).
  - Nothing about tax deductibility: verify LCA's tax status before any such claim, and make none unless the board confirms it.
  - States: an Other amount under $1, or blank, keeps the button off with "$1 or more"; a cancelled or failed Stripe session returns to `/give` with the choices kept and "Nothing was charged. Try again when you're ready."; a member who already gives monthly sees "You give $15 a month to the Scholastic program · Change or cancel" in place of a second ask. Cancel ends the monthly gift at once, from that line, from My LCA's Payments settings or from the signed link in each monthly receipt; Change is a new gift after it.
  - The thank-you page (`/give/thanks`, today's `DonationSuccessPage` rewritten): "Thank you" and "Your $25 gift to the Scholastic program is in. Your receipt is on its way from LCA to {email}.", with See upcoming tournaments and Back to Membership. No name wall, no share buttons, no second ask.
  - `/donate` goes to `/give`, and `/donate/success` to `/give/thanks`; the header's and footer's Donate (WS02) open `/give`.
  - If K answers Q6 the other way, v1.3's purpose funds with goals and progress (`donation_funds`) come back in place of "Put it toward".
- **Digital card** (`Member-D`; in My LCA's You as `Account-D` draws it, WS13):
  - The name, the tier, the LCA member ID, "Valid through {weekday date}" and a QR on a white plate, with "Show this at the desk. The QR checks you in to an LCA event." One card and one signed QR per player profile (`member_id`), so a parent sees one per child (the Card button on each child's card, `Account-I`), and WS08's check-in uses it to find the exact row.
  - The official logo goes on the card (D1), on a cream plate, since it is never recoloured and sits on white or cream; the rook mark goes at the QR's centre (D1, QR centres) only if the QR still reads with it, and otherwise the centre stays plain (a WS01 follow-up). On screen the card is drawn as `Account-D` draws it, navy with "MEMBER" in gold; the printable card takes the Heritage look that 1.1 lists for the membership card (assumed, Q16).
  - Show my card: on phones the first control under the page title in My LCA, opening the card full screen with the QR at 240px; on desktop it moves to the card (WS13).
  - A printable card (print CSS or PDF). The QR is a signed token (`member_id`, `exp`) that WS08 verifies at check-in. Apple and Google Wallet passes come in Phase 5 (D4).
- **Switch** (0.2's table, off by default): `newMembership` turns on `/membership` as above, `/give` with its thank-you page and redirects, gift memberships, and the card with its QR.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_membership_giving.sql  (verify how memberships are stored today: members.* columns vs payments rows; payments.type includes 'donation' since an earlier fix; payments.id is TEXT)
ALTER TABLE payments ADD COLUMN give_toward TEXT CHECK (give_toward IN ('general','scholastic','equipment'));
                                                           -- Give-A's optional choice: a memo the treasurer honours, not a fund with its own accounting
ALTER TABLE payments ADD COLUMN recurring INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;
ALTER TABLE payments ADD COLUMN stripe_subscription_id TEXT;      -- a monthly gift
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN auto_renew INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;              -- verify placement
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN stripe_subscription_id TEXT;
CREATE TABLE membership_gifts (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, payment_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES payments(id),
  tier TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (tier IN ('scholastic','adult')),
  recipient_email TEXT NOT NULL,                            -- the one thing asked of the buyer
  claimed_member_id TEXT REFERENCES members(id), claimed_at TEXT,
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now'))
);
-- member_guardians.family_covered (WS06's migration) replaces the by-date rule in syncFamilyCoverage; the webhook writes it from the ticks
-- donation_funds and payments.fund_id are not built while Give-A stands (assumed, Q6)
```

**API**
- `GET /api/membership/options` (new): tiers and prices from `domain/membership/tiers.ts`.
- `POST /api/membership/checkout` (exists, in `functions/api/membership/checkout.ts`): extend for households, renewals and gifts.
- `POST /api/membership/confirm` (exists).
- `POST /api/membership/lookup` (new; public, rate-limited): the US Chess ID or email comes in the body, never in the address, so it reaches no log line; it answers as above.
- `POST /api/membership/renewal-link` (new; public, rate-limited): emails a signed renewal link (WS06's tokens) to the address on file; the answer is the same whether or not a membership was found.
- `GET /api/membership/renew/:token` (new; signed): opens the renewal for that profile, with no password.
- `POST /api/membership/gifts/:token/claim` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): claims a gift onto the caller's own profile or a child's profile they manage (a guardian link, WS06); a second claim is refused.
- `GET /api/governance/documents?category=treasurer` (exists): both pages read its newest row for the report link.
- `POST /api/donations/checkout` (exists): add `giveToward` and Monthly (Stripe's subscription mode). The success and cancel addresses stay today's (`/donate/success?paymentId=` and `/`, `functions/api/donations/checkout.ts` lines 48 and 49) unless the request names the new ones (`returnTo: 'give'`), which only `GivePage` sends, behind `newMembership`: then `/give/thanks` is the success address and `/give`, with the choices, the cancel address. So today's Donate page never returns to a page that does not exist yet.
- `PATCH /api/me/auto-renew` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): on or off; off cancels the subscription.
- `POST /api/me/monthly-gift/cancel` (new; `requireAuthedMember`) and `POST /api/donations/cancel/:token` (new; signed): end a monthly gift. The link in each monthly receipt opens `/give/cancel/:token`, a page that reads the gift through `GET /api/donations/cancel/:token` (new; signed; changes nothing) and offers one Cancel this gift button, which sends the POST.
- `GET /api/me/card` (new; `requireAuthedMember`) and `GET /api/me/card/:memberId` (new; for a guardian of that profile, WS06): card data plus a signed QR token.
- The webhook (`functions/api/stripe/webhook.ts`) records gifts with their choice, monthly gifts and subscription renewals; activates memberships through `functions/utils/membership/membershipActivation.ts` (writing `family_covered` on the ticked links and running `syncFamilyCoverage`); records a gift membership as unclaimed; and, once WS06's `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` is set, calls `sendPaymentReceipt` (WS06, `functions/utils/receipts.ts`) for every standalone membership, gift membership and donation payment (a membership inside a registration order is on that order's receipt, WS06), after the `stripe_events` row is written in the same batch as the payment's own changes (WS06). Reuse `functions/utils/membership/membershipExpiry.ts`.
- v1.3's `GET /api/donations/funds` and the admin fund endpoints are not built while Give-A stands (assumed, Q6).
- Every new or changed endpoint gets its contract and contract test and leaves the pending list (0.2).

**Frontend**
- Change: `src/pages/MembershipPage.tsx`, `MembershipSuccessPage.tsx`, `DonationSuccessPage.tsx` (becomes the `/give/thanks` page), `src/components/DonateButton.tsx` (opens `/give`) and `src/App.tsx` (the routes and the redirects).
- New: `src/pages/GivePage.tsx` and `src/pages/GiveCancelPage.tsx` (`/give/cancel/:token`); and in `src/areas/membership/components/`: `WhatYouGet.tsx`, `TierCards.tsx`, `HouseholdOptimizer.tsx` (with the two questions), `WhatYourDuesDo.tsx` (`ResponsiveImage` for the photos), `TakesCareOfItself.tsx`, `MembershipQuestions.tsx`, `GiveLine.tsx`, `MemberLookup.tsx` (status in words through `StatusBadge`), `RenewPanel.tsx`, `GiftMembership.tsx`, `GiveForm.tsx` (the amounts as one radio group; the gold `Button`), `MemberCard.tsx` (`LcaLogo` on the card, `LcaMark` at the QR's centre) and `PrintableCard.tsx` (inside `ThemeScope scope="heritage"`, under the default for the card's look).
- `USCHESS_RENEW_URL` moves to `src/areas/membership/usChess.ts` (re-exported from `src/areas/membership/index.ts`), which clears the `react-refresh` warning in `src/areas/households/components/FamilyCard.tsx` (a WS01 follow-up), unless WS06 has moved it already.
- Stored times (a receipt's date, `claimed_at`) go through `formatDate` with `{ stored: 'utc' }` (0.2).

**Jobs & integrations**
- Stripe subscriptions for auto-renew and monthly gifts; Stripe Checkout in payment mode for a single gift.
- Resend, through `workers/daily-emails` and the expiry logic it already has: the reminders 30 and 7 days before the end (listing the covered children, with the one-tap link), the email a week before an auto-renew charge, and the gift email. Receipts go through `sendPaymentReceipt` (D14), never a second receipt from Stripe.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. The optimizer always recommends the cheapest valid combination. Unit tests cover 0 to 5 children, seniors, ties and children-only households.
2. Renewal extends from the current end date, and the one receipt (`sendPaymentReceipt`, D14) shows the new date with its weekday.
3. The lookup answers only the first name, last initial, tier, status and end month, takes its ID or email in the body, and refuses with the plain line after 10 lookups per connection per hour. Renew by email link never shows the address and answers the same whether or not a membership was found.
4. A gift's "Put it toward" choice is on its payment row and in its Stripe metadata. A monthly gift makes a subscription, which its giver can end from My LCA or from the signed link in its receipt, and nobody else can. No page shows a fund goal, a progress bar or a total.
5. A valid QR token checks a member in; forged or expired tokens are rejected.
6. Donate is one click from every page on tablet, and on desktop wherever the header row has room for it; when the desktop row is tight it is the About menu's last item, one click after opening About. On phones it is the first of the three buttons after the section list in the Menu sheet (two taps). Every Donate opens `/give`, and `/donate` and `/donate/success` redirect.
7. With `HOUSEHOLD_CHECKOUT_ON` set, a membership, gift membership or donation payment produces exactly one receipt to the payer (WS06 AC10), and "Send me this receipt again" in My LCA re-sends it.
8. The "Who's covered?" screen never ticks more than 3 children, offers the 4th as Scholastic $5 in the same payment, asks for no child email, and the success page lists who is covered with the expiry's weekday.
9. `/membership` shows, in order: the hero, What you get, the four tier cards, What your dues do (no amount, no bar, and one link to the newest treasurer's report, or the no-report line), Membership that takes care of itself, Questions and the Give line. No member-price wording renders (D11), and "300+" is on the page.
10. A gift membership is claimed once through its signed link, onto the claimer's own profile or a child's profile they manage. A forged link, a second claim and a claim onto anyone else's profile are refused, and until it is claimed it activates nothing.
11. Auto-renew is off until a member turns it on; its email goes a week before each charge; turning it off in My LCA cancels the subscription.
12. `/give` asks for no name, email or address, refuses an Other amount under $1, keeps the choices after a cancelled checkout, and says nothing about taxes.
13. The card carries the official logo, and its QR holds the signed token that WS08's check-in verifies (a unit test reads the token back from the QR's data).
14. `/membership` (already in the accessibility matrix), `/give`, `/give/thanks` and `/membership/success` have zero serious or critical violations in the light and dark looks at 390 and 1440 pixels, and the last three join `PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`.

**Tests**
- Unit: `test/unit/household-optimizer.test.ts` (criterion 1), `test/unit/who-is-covered.test.tsx` (new: criterion 8, never more than 3 children ticked, the 4th offered as Scholastic $5 in the same payment, no child email asked, and the success page's list of who is covered with the expiry's weekday), `test/unit/app-routes.test.tsx` (extended: with `newMembership` on, `/donate` and `/donate/success` redirect to `/give` and `/give/thanks`, and every Donate opens `/give`, criterion 6), `test/unit/membership-page.test.tsx` (new: criterion 9, the sections in order, no amount or bar, the treasurer's report link or the no-report line, no member-price words and "300+"), `test/unit/membership-tiers.test.ts` (the page and the checkout read one price list), `test/unit/give-form.test.tsx` (the amounts, Other, Monthly and the button's words), `test/unit/member-card.test.tsx` (the logo, the weekday, and the token read back from the QR) and `test/unit/features.test.ts` (`newMembership` starts off).
- Integration (`test/integration`): `membership-checkout.test.ts` (households, renewals, gifts and the checkout metadata), `stripe-webhook-membership.test.ts` (a membership, a gift membership, a donation with `give_toward`, a subscription renewal, one receipt each), `membership-lookup.test.ts` (the answer's fields, the body-only input, the rate limit), `membership-renewal-link.test.ts`, `membership-gifts.test.ts` (criterion 10), `member-card.test.ts` (the token; a guardian's card for a child; another member's card is 403), `auto-renew.test.ts` and `monthly-gift-cancel.test.ts` (from My LCA, from the receipt's link, a forged link, another member's gift), `donations-checkout.test.ts` (new: today's success and cancel addresses unless the request asks for `/give`'s, and `giveToward` and Monthly on its metadata); a contract test for each new or changed endpoint in `test/integration/contracts/`.
- Browser: `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` (criterion 14), with `newMembership` on in the a11y-on build and `test/a11y/baseline.json` unchanged.

**Depends on:** WS01 and WS02; WS06 (the household checkout, `member_guardians` with `family_covered`, `sendPaymentReceipt` and `stripe_events`, and the signed tokens). WS13 shows the card; WS08 uses the card QR.

**Risks & open decisions:**
- D4 (wallet passes).
- Q6: Give-A as drawn, with no goals or bars. If K answers the other way, the funds come back, and with them the treasurer's upkeep of each fund's progress.
- The refund rule and the mailing address for checks are the board's to write, and the card's look is K's to confirm (Q17 and Q16).
- Tax-status wording.
- The What your dues do tiles need photos from LCA; the icon panels stand in until then.
- Gift memberships are new in 1.4: `Member-E`, which K picked (with Member-F's dues section), draws them in its fourth panel.
- The rook mark at the QR's centre must not stop the QR reading; test it on two phones before the switch goes on.

## WS13 · My LCA dashboard
**Why:**
- Dashboards should show status and the next action by default (NN/g, Baymard).
- One login can be member, parent, club rep, director, representative and board member at once, yet most of the 300+ members hold only You, so the plain page has to be good on its own.
- KingRegistration flags a US Chess membership that expires before an entered event.
- K, Mon, Oct 19, 2026, on Today: "we should not have this section as the default if that is the case, as in not open it first if its empty", and "use their attention wisely". On the card, which is shown to a TD from a phone: "having the show card button up top and apparent for mobile, but not over emphasizing it".
- Today's `DashboardPage` doesn't bring these together, and volunteers reach their work through a Workspace page and the admin panel.

**Canvas boards** (K, Mon, Oct 19, 2026, page 14, and the hub K saw that evening):
- The frame: `Account-Final-Hub` and `Account-Final-Hub-2` (K: "I like A for general or no other role, but I prefer B when they have more than one role"): one page for a person who holds only You, or You and Family (Int-A), and a left rail for a person who holds a volunteer section (Int-B), each entry opening its full room as its own page. `Account-Int-C` is not used.
- The default page: `Account-D` with `Account-D-2` (one column, the card a section), redrawn on phones with an empty Today folded to its one line and Show my card up top. Until the redraw is on the canvas, `Account-D` read with that change is the reference.
- Today: `Account-H` (an urgent band, then by role), redrawn with its empty state. Until then, `Account-H` frame 1 and the empty-day rule below.
- Family: `Account-I` with `Account-I-2`. Events you direct: `Account-L` with `Account-L-2`. Your region: `Account-N` with `Account-N-2`, where `Work-H` (WS09) wins wherever the two differ (K: "if this conflicts with the design for this from 14, know that I prefer this ones diagrams"). Board: `Account-P` with `Account-P-2`. LCA admin: `Account-R` with `Account-R-2`. The small roles (the auditor, the one-event director, the president and the club rep): `Account-S` and `Account-S-2`, read with the hub, which opens the club rep's Your club on `Club-G-2`'s editor and leaves the president's group email in Admin.
- They replace `Account-A` to `Account-C` and the other page 14 options. Each section board says "Drawn inside Int-A"; under the decided frame, the hub's rail applies.

**User-facing scope**
- **One page, or the rail** (`Account-Final-Hub`):
  - A person who holds only You, or You and Family, gets one page at `/dashboard`: Today, You, Family (when held), Receipts and Settings, with a sticky "On this page" list at the left and nothing that hints at volunteer work. Chips ("Member · Family plan", "Parent · Maya, Leo") and "Role looks wrong?" (the contact form with the subject "My roles") show only with two sections.
  - A person who holds a volunteer section gets a 240px left rail with one entry per section, in a fixed order: Today · You · Family · Events you direct · Your region · Your club · Board · Admin · Member lookup. Each entry opens its full room as its own page. The current entry has `aria-current`, bold type and a 3px gold-ink bar; counts are words beside the entry, never colour alone. Family alone never brings the rail.
  - Until `myLcaRail` is on (Phase 4), everyone gets the one page with their own sections (Today, You, Family when held, Receipts and Settings), volunteers included; Today then carries only You's and Family's items, and volunteers keep today's Workspace page and the header menu's role rows for their work (WS02).
  - The rail comes and goes with the grant. A one-event director has Events you direct while named on an event, until its rating report is marked submitted; a standing director and the president always have it; a seat holder has Board, and a regional seat also Your region, while the term runs; a member who organizes a member-created event (D26) has it while that event is open; a club's named rep has Your club; an LCA admin has Admin; an auditor has Member lookup.
  - On an event's days the live event is pinned under Today ("The Baton Rouge Classic · Live · Round 3", status in words beside its tint) until 48 hours after its last round, and opens the console.
  - The rail folds: on tablets into a row of tabs under the header; on phones into a sheet behind the page title ("My LCA ▾ · Today"); in the TD console at 1024 into the "My LCA ▾" breadcrumb trail, with Exit console back to Events you direct.
  - Addresses: `/dashboard` (Today, or You when Today is empty), `/dashboard/you`, `/dashboard/family` and `/dashboard/directing`; on the one page, `/dashboard/you` and `/dashboard/family` scroll to `#you` and `#family`. The rooms keep their decided paths: `/workspace/tournaments/new`, `/workspace/tournaments/:id/setup` and `/live` (WS08), and `/recap` (WS10's Recap tab); `/workspace/region` and `/workspace/region/clubs/:id` (WS09); `/my/club/:slug` (WS09); `/board/inbox`; `/admin` (WS08); and `/workspace/lookup`. Under the rail no room needs "Back to My LCA", since the rail is the way back. `/workspace` goes to `/dashboard`, and the word Workspace is never a title, a menu row or a breadcrumb trail.
  - The header's My LCA ▾ menu (WS02) is short for everyone: the personal to-dos, My LCA, the event line while event mode is on, Family, Membership card, the Admin row a person holds ("Admin (view only)" for officers, "Admin · 12 need attention" for LCA admins), and Log out. The header count is personal only (`src/lib/todo.ts`, WS02); volunteer items never count there.
- **Today** (`Account-H`):
  - An item is something the viewer can finish or decide today, with a deadline within 14 days or a person waiting now, and exactly one action button in words. A status, a count of things that are fine, and information with no action are never items; they belong in the sections or the event strip.
  - What each role adds:
    - You: an unpaid entry before its deadline; a US Chess membership ending before an entered event, from 7 days before; an LCA membership within 30 days of its end, or ended, never pending; an open waitlist offer with its claim time; a bye request answered; a reminder you set that fires today; and on an event day, "Playing today · Round 1 at 10:00 AM · My board".
    - Family: the same for each child, each line starting with the child's first name ("Leo needs a US Chess ID before Sat, Nov 14"); a guardian request to approve; a child's invite not accepted after 7 days.
    - Director, per event: results needed before the next round can be paired, and the reports waiting, with disagreements named; a bye request; entrants whose US Chess membership ends before the event, as one item with the count, from 7 days before; a setup step left before registration opens or before day 1; "Round times missing: players see time to be announced"; the rating report due within 7 days of the last round; "Mark as completed" after the last result.
    - Representative: the region's clubs not checked in 90 days, as one item with the count and the names, or the monthly check when it lands; a Suggest an update with no answer, on the 14-day clock; a club event you created that waits for LCA's approval; a rep request to decide (WS09).
    - Club rep: "Is the {club} listing still accurate?" once the club is 90 days unchecked, with Still accurate; a Suggest an update for the club with no answer.
    - Board seat: the messages waiting in each seat's inbox, one item per seat with the count and the oldest date; the next board meeting within 7 days.
    - Officer role: support tickets assigned to you, answered in Admin; for the secretary, minutes not posted 14 days after a meeting.
    - LCA admin: one line for the queue ("12 items need attention in Admin · oldest 3 days"), each item assigned to this admin by name, and a failed automation; never the whole queue.
    - The president adds nothing beyond the seat, and the auditor is never an item.
  - Ranking, in five tiers: 1, due today or already past, from any role; 2, money and eligibility with a date, in this order: an unpaid entry (including a teen's "Ask my guardian to pay"), US Chess ending before an entered event, an LCA membership within 30 days, an open waitlist offer, then the nearer date; 3, a person waiting on you, longest wait first; 4, dated upkeep; 5, counts and summaries. Inside a tier, the sooner deadline comes first, then the role order You · Family · Director · Representative · Club rep · Board · Admin, so a personal item never sinks under a volunteer item of the same urgency.
  - Layout: the heading "Today" with its weekday date, or "Today across your roles" with "9 things · 1 is yours" when two or more roles add items. An urgent band holds every tier 1 item and anything due within 48 hours, from any role, in deadline order, each row keeping its role word; a band with nothing in it is not drawn. Below it, one group per role in the role order, each headed by the role's name, its count and a link to its entry. Three rows show open per group and seven in all; the rest sit behind "Show 4 more", grouped by role. Nothing is dropped, since each section lists all of its own items. When one role holds every item there are no groups and no role words. On an event day the live event's items form one group at the top.
  - **The empty day.** "Nothing needs you today." with no box and no picture, then in grey the next dated thing ("Next: The Baton Rouge Classic, Sat, Oct 24 · you're entered and paid"; for a new member, "Nothing entered yet · Find a tournament"), and for a role holder one line per role with its next dated thing. My LCA then opens on You, with the card in view; Today keeps its row with no count, and the header shows no count. With anything in it, My LCA opens on Today.
  - An item leaves the list the moment the thing is done. Nothing is dismissed by hand except the board meeting line, which hides until the next meeting is set (kept on the device, inside try/catch). The same providers feed Today, the menu's to-do rows and each section's flags, so one thing is never worded two ways.
- **You** (`Account-D`, `/dashboard/you`):
  - The head: the name; "Adult member · valid through Mon, Oct 26, 2026 · US Chess ID 30188264 · Regular rating 1912"; Show my card (outline) and Find a tournament. On a phone, Show my card is never a large card that takes over the page: on the one page it is the first control under the page title, full width and 48px tall; under the rail it is a 44px Card button beside the page title (`Account-Final-Hub-2` F1 and F2). Either opens the card full screen with the QR at 240px. On desktop it moves to the card.
  - Membership: the line in words ("Adult · valid through Mon, Oct 26, 2026 · Renew or turn on auto-renew"), and the card (WS12) beside the US Chess block: the ID, the ratings, the membership's end (with "Ends before your event on Sat, Jan 16" in words when it does) and "Checked against US Chess every night · last check 2:10 AM today".
  - Entries: one date-ordered timeline of `DateBlock` rows: entries with the four-step tracker (Registered · Paid · Pairings posted · Results) and Pay · Change section · Byes · Withdraw; saved events with their reminder and Change · Stop; dashed deadline rows (an early price ending, the membership ending); partner events with "Organizer's site ↗" and "I entered there". Filter chips (`FilterChip`): All · Entered · Saved · Deadlines. An entry in a member-created event carries "Organized by {name}. Not an LCA event." (D27). Under the timeline, once WS07's follows and WS09's club pages exist, "Clubs you follow", each with the club's own words and never a computed date.
  - Receipts and history: past payments with "Send me this receipt again" (WS06, D14), and the events played (date, event, score with ½, place, rating change) from WS10's archive once it exists, in the table primitives.
  - Settings, six cards and one line: Profile; US Chess ID; Privacy ("Show me on Who's coming lists"); Payments; Family (Add a family member); Sign-in and security (`/account/security`); and "How we email you" (four ticks, with "Email only for now. Texts come later.", D8). A member's own messages to LCA stay on `/support`, linked from Settings.
  - Empty, for a new member: "You're a member through Sun, Mar 14, 2027. Nothing entered yet." with Find a tournament and Add a family member; the card still shows.
  - A claimed teen's page (`Account-D`'s inset): her own You with "Danielle R. gets copies of your emails", an unpaid entry reading "Ask my guardian to pay" with Pay now as a link, no Family unless she guards someone, and in Settings "I'm 18 or older: manage my profile myself" (takes effect after 7 days, and her guardians are told at once).
- **Family** (`Account-I`, `/dashboard/family`):
  - "Children don't need an email or a login. You register and pay for them and get their emails. From 13 you can give one their own login, and they keep all their history."
  - One card per child: the facts (US Chess ID, rating, and the US Chess end date, on the amber tint with its words when it is missing or ends first), what's coming up with the four-step tracker and its status sentence, Family plan coverage, the last result, the login line ("No login · emails come to you", "Invite sent Tue, Oct 13 · Resend", "Has her own login"), the Card button (WS12), "Open Maya's page" (`/me/players/:memberId`, the view Maya would see, with the strip "you manage this profile"), "Give Maya her own login" (the 13-or-older and consent ticks), "Remove Maya's login" and "Maya is 18: hand over her profile".
  - Chips Everyone · Maya · Leo, with `?child=` in the address. **Register all** opens WS06's household flow prefilled with every child; Add a family member asks for a first name, a last name and an optional US Chess ID (no grade, birthdate or child email; the eighth-grade tick is on each entry, WS06).
  - **The guardians panel:** Invite a guardian, Approve or Decline a request, Remove (owner only), Leave, and "Send me copies" per child, disabled while the child has no login ("Someone has to get Leo's emails until he has his own login."). Co-guardians show as first name and last initial. The Family plan cell lists who is covered, with "Change who's covered at renewal". Moving a profile between households, or merging two, goes through Website help.
  - **On event day** the family day view sits at the top of the section: each child's round, board, colour, result and "Updated 11:04 AM", from `GET /api/me/event-mode` `players[]`. Round alerts arrive as one grouped email per guardian per round, each line starting with the child's name; no "Text me" (D8).
  - Empty: "No one else on your account yet. Children don't need an email or a login. You register and pay for them and get their emails." with Add a family member.
- **Events you direct** (`Account-L`, `/dashboard/directing`): "You're named on these events.", with one line under it built from WS08's table "Who may do what on an event" for the person's events (for example "LCA admins and the president name the directors of the president's events", or "LCA admins and the club's rep" on a club's event, with regional representatives only from WS09's phase). One card per event with the five-stage track (Set up · Registration · Event day · Close out · Rating report), status in words, and the current stage's panel: during registration, the counts by section and the flags in words, with one-tap decisions (Approve or Decline a bye, Email both a reminder); on the day, in the live tokens, the round, "8 of 13 results confirmed", the reports waiting and any disagreement, with Open event day; at the end, the rating report's checks. Setup, Event day, Recap and Rating report show only when they apply and open WS08's rooms. "Past events you directed" sits at the foot.
  - **A standing director** (today's `tournament_director` role; how one is named is Q4): the entry is always there, with New tournament at its head (D20), and one lookup line, "Look up a member: name or US Chess ID", answering the name, status in words and the end date, never an email, phone or address. Their event lists on Tournaments the moment it is published, and the LCA admins are told after the fact (D22); its card says so in words. Fees above $0, and how the money reaches them, follow D24 in WS08's Fees step.
  - **A one-event director** gets the cards for the events they are named on and those events' rooms, never the lookup; the membership switch, naming directors and deleting the event follow WS08's table "Who may do what on an event".
  - **The president:** New tournament always (D20), the drafts they made with the one-event director they named (D21), and Board.
  - **A member's own event,** once an LCA admin has turned member-created events on (D25): it shows here for its organizer with "Organized by you. Not an LCA event." (D27) and how many of their three open events are in use (D26). While the setting is on, You shows an active member the one line WS08 sets out, "Run your own tournament · shared by link, never listed on LCA's pages", opening `/workspace/tournaments/new` (assumed, Q19). It never shows on anyone else's My LCA except in its entrants' Entries.
- **Your region** (`/workspace/region`): WS09's My region (`Work-H`), opened from the rail, with `Account-N`'s to-do rows under the cards. WS13 adds the rail entry with its count ("Your region · 4") and its Today items.
- **Your club** (`/my/club/:slug`): WS09's editor (`Club-G-2`) for the club's named rep, opened from the rail; Today carries the club's 90-day check and its unanswered suggestions. A rep not yet linked to a club sees "Your account is a club rep account but isn't linked to a club yet. Ask an LCA admin to assign your club." Whether a club rep creates the club's events is Q2; until K answers, today's rule stands, and the club's events stay reachable as today.
- **Board** (`Account-P`; a desk above `/board/inbox`):
  - The seat and its term, then a desk of one-line rows, each a count in words and one way in: each seat's inbox ("Treasurer inbox · 3 waiting · oldest Fri, Oct 2 · waiting 3 days", Open inbox); the next board meeting with Add to calendar and the agenda when posted (WS14's meetings); the document this officer keeps ("Treasurer's report, September · Draft · Edit"; minutes for the secretary); "Support tickets · 4 open · 1 assigned to you · oldest 3 days" with Answer in Admin; and Look up a member (Admin's members table, read-only).
  - Then the row **Admin (view only).** Officers keep today's view-only Admin (K: "it helps stuff run smoother if we dont have to wait for one person, especially in volunteer work, and there is no sensitive info to hide anyway"), opened with a "View only" badge. Deletes, roles, memberships, refunds and group email stay with LCA admins.
  - The president's desk adds "Email the members · last sent Thu, Oct 1 · 41 sent, 2 failed · Write in Admin" (today's `/admin/email`). No votes. There is no `/workspace/tickets` and no `/workspace/email`.
- **Admin** (`Account-R`): one row, "12 need attention in Admin · oldest 4 days · 2 assigned to you · 1 automation failed", with Open Admin (`/admin`: WS08's `Work-J` home, in Admin's own area and sidebar; Back to My LCA returns to the rail). Empty: "Nothing needs attention in Admin", with the last change. Without two-step sign-in: "Admin · confirm it's you to see the count", with Confirm it's you (`/account/security`). No other person's data renders on My LCA, not even a count by source. The admin's view of member-created events (every such event, its organizer, and how often the service is used and by whom, D26) is a page in Admin, built by WS08 and reached through this row; a representative's My LCA never shows member-created events.
- **Member lookup** (`Account-S`, the auditor): one field, "Name or US Chess ID". A result reads the name and "Active through Sun, Mar 14, 2027" or "Expired Wed, Sep 30, 2026", nothing else, and "Open the full lookup" goes to `/workspace/lookup` (today's lookup tab, moved). Read-only: no write, no export, and never an email, phone or address. It is never an item in Today.
- **Categories are not statuses** (a WS01 follow-up): a ticket's or an audit entry's kind is shown as words in a neutral tag, never in a status's tone; `StatusBadge` carries statuses only, always with their words.
- **Nightly US Chess check:** for entrants of upcoming events, refresh membership expiry through `functions/utils/uscf.ts`, throttled, cached and capped at N lookups per run, writing `uscf_checked_at`. "Check again now" runs one check for the viewer's own profile, or a child they manage, at most once an hour per profile.
- **Switches** (0.2's table, off by default):
  - `newDashboard` (Phase 1): the one page (Today, You, Family, Receipts, Settings), which everyone gets until `myLcaRail` is on, the empty-day rule, and Show my card, which lights up when WS12's card exists. The Family section's WS06 controls (Register all, the guardians panel, the login invites) show only once `householdCheckout` is on too.
  - `myLcaRail` (Phase 4): the rail and the volunteer entries, the Board desk, Member lookup at `/workspace/lookup`, and `/workspace` going to `/dashboard`. Each entry also waits for its room: Events you direct's rooms for `tdConsole`, Your region and Your club for `newClubs`, and Admin's new home for `adminHome` (until then the row opens today's `/admin`). Until `myLcaRail` is on, volunteers keep today's Workspace page and the menu's role rows.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_dashboard.sql  (verify existing columns on members before adding)
-- members.uscf_expiration already exists (migration 0042) and carries the US Chess expiry for every profile, children included;
-- it replaces the uscf_expires_on column planned in 1.2.
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN uscf_checked_at TEXT;   -- verify: absent as of Thu, Oct 22, 2026
```
No other table: Today is worked out from the existing rows on each load, and the one hidden line is kept on the device.

**API**
- `GET /api/me/dashboard` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): the entries the person holds, with their counts; Today (the band and the groups, or the empty day's lines); and what the one page needs. It is grant-aware and returns only the sections the person's grants allow.
- `GET /api/me/directing` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): the events the person directs, organizes or drafted, each with its stage and the current stage's figures.
- `GET /api/board/desk` (new; the seats and officer roles the person holds, through `requireSeatAccess` and the observer roles): the desk's rows.
- `GET /api/admin/summary` (WS08's, built with the admin home; `requireAdmin`, which asks for two-step sign-in): read for the Admin row's counts, and no counts without two-step sign-in.
- `GET /api/workspace/lookup` (new; `requireMemberDirectory`, which admits today's directory roles and LCA admins signed in with two-step sign-in): name, status and end date only.
- `POST /api/me/uscf-check` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): "Check again now", for the caller's own profile or a child they manage, at most once an hour per profile.
- Reused: `GET /api/me`, `/api/me/children` and `/api/me/family` (WS06), `GET /api/me/event-mode` (WS02), `GET /api/me/tournament-creation` (WS08, for Events you direct and the line of Q19), `GET /api/workspace/region` (WS09) and `GET /api/me/card` (WS12).
- The Today providers live in `functions/utils/today/`, one per role, and are ranked by `domain/today/rank.ts`, which is pure; the menu's to-dos (`src/lib/todo.ts`, WS02) and each section's flags read the same items.
- Every new or changed endpoint gets its contract and contract test and leaves the pending list (0.2).

**Frontend**
- Rewrite: `src/pages/DashboardPage.tsx`. Today's profile, membership, registrations and support ticket blocks give way to You, Family and Settings; the ticket list stays on `/support`.
- New, in `src/areas/account/` (with its `api.ts` and `index.ts`):
  - The frame: `MyLcaFrame.tsx` (one page or the rail, by the grants), `MyLcaRail.tsx` (its tablet tabs and phone sheet) and `OnThisPage.tsx`.
  - Today: `TodayList.tsx` with `UrgentBand.tsx`, `TodayGroup.tsx` and `EmptyToday.tsx` (status tags through `StatusBadge`; `useNow` for today and the 48-hour band).
  - You: `YouPage.tsx` with `MembershipSection.tsx`, `ShowMyCardButton.tsx`, `EntriesTimeline.tsx` (`DateBlock`, `FilterChip`), `ReceiptsHistory.tsx` (the table primitives, scores through `formatScore`) and `SettingsCards.tsx`.
  - Family: `FamilySection.tsx` with `ChildCard.tsx` and `FamilyDayView.tsx`; it renders WS06's `GuardiansPanel.tsx` and links to `src/pages/PlayerPage.tsx`.
  - The volunteer sections: `DirectingPage.tsx` with `StageTrack.tsx`, `BoardDesk.tsx`, `AdminRow.tsx`, and `src/pages/MemberLookupPage.tsx` (`/workspace/lookup`).
- `src/App.tsx`: the addresses above, and `/workspace` going to `/dashboard` under `myLcaRail`.
- Stored times (a receipt's date, a ticket's age, `uscf_checked_at`) go through `formatDate` and `formatTime` with `{ stored: 'utc' }` (0.2).

**Jobs & integrations:** the nightly US Chess expiry refresh in `workers/daily-emails`, with its own isolated phase. My LCA sends no digest email of its own.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. A person who holds only You, or You and Family, gets one page at `/dashboard` with the On this page list and no rail. With `myLcaRail` on, a person who holds any volunteer section gets the rail, with one entry per section in the fixed order, each opening its own page. Family alone never brings the rail, and a one-event director's entry (and, with nothing else held, the rail) leaves once the event's rating report is marked submitted. With `myLcaRail` off, a volunteer gets the same one page as everyone, with only You's and Family's items in Today.
2. With nothing in Today, My LCA opens on You with the card in view, and Today is the one line "Nothing needs you today." with the next dated thing (and a line per role for a role holder); its row carries no count and the header shows none. With one item or more, My LCA opens on Today.
3. Today's order follows the five tiers, then the sooner deadline, then the role order, with tier 2's personal items in the order unpaid entry, US Chess ending before an entered event, LCA membership within 30 days, waitlist offer. The band holds every tier 1 item and everything due within 48 hours, and is not drawn when empty. At most seven rows show open, and three per group, with a "Show N more" button. A unit test covers it.
4. On a 390px phone, Show my card is the first control under the page title on the one page (full width, 48px tall), and a 44px Card button beside the page title under the rail, on every My LCA page; either opens the card full screen with a 240px QR. On desktop it moves focus to the card.
5. The header count counts only WS02's personal to-dos (unpaid entries for self or children, a membership ending within 30 days or ended, US Chess ending before an entered event); no volunteer item ever reaches it, and Today may hold more, such as a reminder or a waitlist offer.
6. A US Chess expiry earlier than an entered event's date shows at least 7 days before the event, with a renewal link.
7. "Register all" from the family section opens the household flow prefilled with every child the viewer guards.
8. The dashboard never shows data the person's grants don't allow; role-safety tests cover each grant alone and in pairs. The Admin row shows no count without two-step sign-in, and no other person's data with it. A representative's My LCA shows no member-created event.
9. An officer's Board desk opens Admin with the View only badge, where answering a ticket works and deleting, changing a role or a membership, a refund and group email (except the president's) are refused; `/workspace/tickets` and `/workspace/email` do not exist.
10. With `myLcaRail` on, `/workspace` goes to `/dashboard`, and no title, menu row or breadcrumb trail under My LCA reads "Workspace".
11. On an event's days the live event is pinned under Today with its round, and it leaves 48 hours after the last round.
12. The member lookup answers the name, status in words and the end date only, never an email, phone or address; a plain member and a one-event director get 403.
13. `/dashboard` in both forms has zero serious or critical violations in the light and dark looks at 390 and 1440 pixels: through a fixture page until the browser-test servers can sign in (WS01 left this open), then in `PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`.

**Tests**
- Unit: `test/unit/today-rank.test.ts` (criterion 3, with every role's items, the band and the caps), `test/unit/my-lca-frame.test.tsx` (one page or the rail for each set of grants with `myLcaRail` on, the fixed order, Family alone; with `myLcaRail` off, the one page for a volunteer too, with only You's and Family's items in Today, criterion 1), `test/unit/empty-today.test.tsx` (criterion 2), `test/unit/todo-count.test.ts` (criterion 5, with WS02's `src/lib/todo.ts`), `test/unit/family-section.test.tsx` (new: Register all opens the household flow with every child the viewer guards, criterion 7), `test/unit/app-routes.test.tsx` (extended: `/workspace` goes to `/dashboard` with `myLcaRail` on, and no title, menu row or breadcrumb trail under My LCA reads "Workspace", criterion 10) and `test/unit/features.test.ts` (`newDashboard` and `myLcaRail` start off).
- Integration (`test/integration`): `me-dashboard.test.ts`, per grant and combination (a member; a parent; a teen with her own login; a one-event director before and after the report is marked submitted; a standing director; the president; a representative; a club rep; an officer; an admin with and without two-step sign-in; an auditor), and the live event pinned under Today on its days and gone 48 hours after its last round (criterion 11); `me-directing.test.ts`; `board-desk.test.ts`; `member-lookup.test.ts` (its fields, with role-safety cases); `uscf-check.test.ts` (once an hour per profile, a guardian for a child, 403 for anyone else); extend `role-safety.test.ts`; a contract test for each new or changed endpoint in `test/integration/contracts/`.
- Browser: `test/a11y/my-lca.spec.ts` on a fixture page (`/__fixtures/my-lca`, beside today's scholastic fixture) drawing the one page and the rail with sample data (criterion 13), with Show my card at 390px as the first control under the title on the one page (full width, 48px) and the 44px Card button under the rail, each opening the card full screen with a 240px QR (criterion 4), and `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` once sign-in works on the test servers.

**Depends on:** WS02 (the header's menu and count) and WS06 (the family model and `/api/me/family`) for the one page; WS07 for the event-day views and, with WS09's club pages, for Clubs you follow. The rail's entries light up as their rooms land: WS08 (the console, setup, recap and Admin's home), WS09 (My region and Your club), WS12 (the card) and WS14 (the meetings on the Board desk).

**Risks & open decisions:**
- US Chess lookup rate limits.
- The Today providers run on every My LCA load: each is one query bounded by the 14-day window, and the answer is kept for the visit.
- Q2 (a club rep's events), Q4 (how a standing director is named) and Q5 (what counts as an active member, for creating) shape what Events you direct and Your club show.
- The redraws of `Account-D` on phones and `Account-H`'s empty state are still to be drawn.

## WS14 · Governance & board
**Why:**
- A volunteer board earns trust by being visibly current: next meeting, minutes, bylaws as web pages, "last updated" stamps, a yearly financial summary.
- PDFs read badly, especially on phones (NN/g).
- Councilmatic and Keep a Changelog show how to make change history readable.
- K's note on page 12 (Fri, Oct 9, 2026) picks About-B's layout without "Who represents me?" ("its unrealistic to how things work"), About-C's bylaws and minutes reader, and the champions on a tab.

**Canvas boards** (decided Fri, Oct 9, 2026): `About-Final` (frame 1, Board & regions; frame 2, the Bylaws reader) and `About-Final-2` (frame 3, Minutes; frame 4, Champions; frame 5, phones). They replace `About-A` to `About-D`. About-Final's New Orleans Metro row ("... and 3 youth programs") reads as that region's eight clubs; LCA has no youth programs.

**User-facing scope**
- **One About page with five tabs:** About · Board & regions · Bylaws & rules (assumed, P2.38) · Minutes · Champions, at `/about`, `/about/board`, `/about/bylaws`, `/about/minutes` and `/about/champions`.
  - Each tab has its own address and "Last updated" under its title. The tabs are links, and the one on screen says so (`aria-current="page"`) at every width.
  - Today's `/governance` (to Board & regions, where it goes today), `/governance/board`, `/governance/bylaws`, `/governance/rules` (to Bylaws & rules, opened at the tournament rules) and `/governance/minutes` redirect to the matching tab. `/meeting` stays.
  - `/about/champions` is WS10's page, and it, its Champions tab and the redirects from `/champions` and `/state-champions` exist only once `newNews` is on (WS10); WS14 wraps it as the Champions tab. With `newAbout` on and `newNews` off, the tab row holds the other four tabs, and State champions in the About menu still opens today's `/champions` (WS02).
  - WS02's About menu rows point at the tabs.
- **About:** About-A's hub as WS14 already scopes it (assumed, P2.34), with no history timeline (assumed, P2.40): who LCA is, with real figures (the member figure stays the hard-coded "300+ members"); the records list, each with its status in words and "Last updated"; the yearly financial summary, entered by the treasurer (`[Amount]` placeholders never ship to production); elections; and contact the board.
- **Board & regions:** About-B's officer cards (role, term, one-line duties, and Message addressed to the role, not the person); the numbered map with seat status in words beside the colour; the seven region rows with the representative, the region's clubs and its next events from live data (partner events say "Registers on the organizer's site ↗", and a member-created event never shows); shared seats showing both people; and open seats as invitations, sending interest to the board inbox tagged with the seat. The top right holds the next board meeting card (weekday, date, 7:00 PM, online, the agenda, Add to calendar, the last meeting's minutes; assumed, P2.44). "Not sure who to ask?" writes to the whole board. There is no "Who represents me?" lookup or routing: nobody types a club, city or ZIP on the About page.
- **Messages** route through the board inbox (`POST /api/contact`, read in `functions/api/board/tickets.ts`), so no personal email address is ever exposed.
- **Bylaws & rules:** About-C's reader.
  - The web page is the official text. A sticky contents rail on the left marks the article on screen with a bar as well as a fill, and links to the change log, the tournament rules and the PDF.
  - The change log per vote, on the right, lists Added, Changed and Removed with the vote's weekday and date and its motions.
  - Optional "In plain words" boxes, written by the Secretary and labelled as a summary (assumed, P2.35).
  - Redlines in place show Removed and Added with the motion, the vote and a link to the minutes; view any version and compare two.
  - A generated PDF beside Print, and "Email me when this changes".
  - On phones the contents rail becomes a pinned bar that opens a sheet holding the change log and the PDF.
  - The tournament rules (today `/governance/rules`) read in the same reader.
- **Minutes:** About-C's archive. One search over minutes, bylaws and the tournament rules, with the matching sentence in each row and the word marked, and a line saying how many matched in each kind; status in words ("Draft", with the meeting it goes to for approval, or "Approved", with the meeting that approved it); year chips with counts and a meeting-type filter (Board, Annual, Special); "Last updated" on every row. The rail keeps the next meeting, the annual meeting and the email sign-up.
- **Champions:** WS10's `/about/champions` page as a tab, once `newNews` is on.
- **Annual meeting** (`/meeting`): stays its own page in the heritage look. With `newAbout` on (with it off the page stays as it is), its date and time come from `formatDate` and `formatTime` (today it formats with its own `'America/Chicago'` formatter), and its RSVP is the State Championship's business meeting, the side event of kind `meeting` on WS05's event page, linked from here (assumed, S9.6).
- **The heritage look** (`themeHeritage`), inside `HeritageLayout`: the reader's text column takes the `reader` hook, headings take `display` through `PageHero`, cards `card`, tables `table`, labels `.small-caps`, and a display figure ("1915", "300+"), if one is drawn, `figure`. Every form and editor on these pages sits in `ThemeScope scope="base"`, WS01's one permitted nesting. If an officer's or a representative's name needs a letter outside the latin files, the latin-ext files WS10 adds for Champions load for it too.
- **The official logo:** the About tab carries it, as WS01 placed it on `/about` (D1's About page), and the Champions tab carries WS10's. Board & regions, Bylaws & rules, Minutes and `/meeting` carry none until K says D1's history pages include them (assumed, Q9).
- **Plain words:** with `newAbout` on, the pages say "US Chess" (today's `/about` uses the old initials seven times and holds two em dashes and an en dash, and the Board and Annual meeting pages hold em dashes too), and no governance page's words hold an em or en dash; today's pages keep their words until then, since a changed word is a visible change.
- **One main landmark:** the tabbed page draws no `main` of its own (today `GovLayout` draws one inside the site's own on `/about` and the governance pages whenever `themeHeritage` is off).
- **Controls that say their state:** on Minutes and on Bylaws & rules, each document's edit and delete buttons, for those who manage documents, get names and move out of the card's own button, and a card that opens says so (`aria-expanded`). The board inbox (`/board/inbox`) says which filter and status are chosen (`aria-pressed`, with every switch off, since it changes nothing seen, 0.2), and under `newLook` shows it by more than a navy fill.
- **Loading:** each tab holds its height while its lists load, so the footer does not jump.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_governance.sql  (governance_documents, migration 0011, stays for uploaded files and categories; its id is TEXT)
CREATE TABLE governance_doc_versions (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, doc_key TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (doc_key IN ('bylaws','rules')),
  version TEXT NOT NULL, effective_on TEXT NOT NULL, motion_ref TEXT,
  body_json TEXT NOT NULL,            -- articles/sections tree with stable section ids
  plain_summary_json TEXT, created_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id), created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  UNIQUE (doc_key, version)
);
CREATE TABLE governance_changes (
  version_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES governance_doc_versions(id), section_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  change_kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (change_kind IN ('added','amended','removed')), summary TEXT   -- 'amended' shows as "Changed"
);
CREATE TABLE meetings (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('board','annual','special')),
  starts_at TEXT NOT NULL, location TEXT,
  agenda_document_id TEXT REFERENCES governance_documents(id),
  minutes_document_id TEXT REFERENCES governance_documents(id),
  minutes_status TEXT CHECK (minutes_status IN ('draft','approved')),
  minutes_approved_meeting_id INTEGER REFERENCES meetings(id),
  motions_json TEXT, status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'scheduled'
);
CREATE TABLE governance_watch (
  email TEXT NOT NULL,
  doc_key TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (doc_key IN ('bylaws','rules')),
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  PRIMARY KEY (email, doc_key)
);
```
- `zip_regions` leaves WS14, since it served only the lookup that is not built; it moves to WS09.
- `meetings.starts_at` holds Louisiana wall-clock time, which `formatDate` and `formatTime` read with their default; the `created_at` columns hold UTC and pass `{ stored: 'utc' }` (WS01 step 3).
- One email goes out per new version to each address watching that document, with a signed unsubscribe link (`functions/utils/tokens.ts`, WS06).

**API**
- `GET /api/governance/bylaws?version=` and `GET /api/governance/bylaws/diff?from=&to=` (new; the tournament rules through the same endpoints with `doc=rules`).
- `GET /api/governance/search?q=&year=&type=` (new; in place of v1.3's `GET /api/governance/minutes?q=&year=`): one search over minutes, bylaws and rules, returning the matching sentence and counts per kind. Use FTS5 if D1 supports it here (verify); otherwise LIKE over extracted text.
- `GET /api/governance/meetings/next` and `.ics` (new).
- `POST /api/governance/watch` (new; public, rate-limited) and `POST /api/governance/watch/unsubscribe/:token` (new; WS06's signed token), sent by the email's one-click `List-Unsubscribe-Post` header and by the one button on the page its link opens; opening the link changes nothing.
- WS14 reuses `POST /api/contact` (`functions/api/contact.ts`) for the region rows' Message, the officer cards' Message, open-seat interest and "Not sure who to ask?": the region target WS09 adds, routed to the region's seat through `createTicket`, and the whole-board target added in Phase 3 (WS10 or WS11). WS14 sends a region's message to the whole board while its seat is open. v1.3's `GET /api/board/represent` and `POST /api/board/contact` are not built.
- Writes go through `requireGovernanceEditor`. Reuse `functions/api/governance/documents*.ts`, `governance/board*.ts`, `board/seats.ts` and `board/my-seats.ts`.
- Every new or changed endpoint takes its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test, and comes off `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` (the pending list only shrinks, 0.2).

**Frontend**
- Change:
  - `src/pages/AboutPage.tsx` becomes the tabbed page under `GovLayout.tsx`; `BoardPage.tsx`, `BylawsPage.tsx`, `MinutesPage.tsx` and `ChampionsPage.tsx` render as its tabs; `AnnualMeetingPage.tsx` takes the shared date formats, the RSVP link and the plain words.
  - `src/areas/governance/components/GovLayout.tsx` (never a `main`) and `GovernanceDocuments.tsx` (named edit and delete buttons outside the card's own button, and `aria-expanded`).
  - `src/components/RichTextEditor.tsx` (moved there by WS10): its link is configured through `StarterKit` (with `openOnClick: false`) and the separate `Link` extension goes, which ends the editor library's "Duplicate extension names found: ['link']" warning.
  - `src/pages/BoardInboxPage.tsx`: its filters and statuses.
  - `src/lib/themeScopes.ts` and `src/App.tsx`: `HERITAGE_ROUTES` holds the `/about` tabs and `/meeting` once `newAbout` is on, and the old addresses until then (the map reads the switch, so an address that redirects never resolves to heritage); the tab routes sit inside the `HeritageLayout` route.
- New (in `src/areas/governance/components`): `AboutTabs.tsx` (a row of links, not an ARIA tab list, since each tab is its own page), `ContentsRail.tsx` (a sheet on phones), `ChangeLog.tsx`, `PlainWords.tsx`, `GovernanceSearch.tsx` (in place of `MinutesSearch.tsx`), `NextMeetingCard.tsx`, `VersionTimeline.tsx`, `Redline.tsx`, `SeatCard.tsx`, `RecordsList.tsx`, `WatchSignup.tsx`.
- From the design system (WS01): `HeritageLayout` with its hooks; `ThemeScope scope="base"` around every form and editor; `ThickThinRule` and `Seal`; `LcaLogo` on the About tab; `FilterChip` for the year chips with counts and the meeting-type filter; `DateBlock` on the next meeting card. `StatusBadge` is for event status, so minutes show Draft and Approved as words.
- Not built: `WhoRepresentsMe.tsx`.
- **Switches** (0.2's table, all off by default): `newAbout` (the tabbed About page with its reader, search and watch list, and the redirects from `/governance/*`); the existing `themeHeritage` draws it in the heritage look.

**Jobs & integrations:** "Email me when this changes" sends one email per new version to each watching address through WS07's outbox; nothing else.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. Bylaws render as accessible web text with a table of contents. A version picker works, and the diff highlights additions and removals with a text cue as well as colour.
2. The next-meeting card provides an `.ics`, and past meetings link to their minutes.
3. One search finds keyword matches across minutes, bylaws and rules, filters by year and meeting type, and shows the matching sentence and each row's status in words.
4. Each region row's Message reaches its seat through the board inbox, or the whole board while the seat is open; "Not sure who to ask?" reaches the whole board; no lookup by club, city or ZIP exists on the About page; no personal email appears in the HTML.
5. Every tab shows "Last updated" under its title.
6. Each tab has its own address, back and forward move between tabs, and the old `/governance/*` routes redirect to their tab. The Champions tab shows only with `newNews` on, and the `/champions` and `/state-champions` redirects are WS10's, checked under `newNews` (WS10 criterion 15).
7. On a phone the bylaws contents open as a sheet with the change log and the PDF in it.
8. Every `/about` tab and `/meeting` has exactly one `main` landmark, with `themeHeritage` off and on.
9. With `newAbout` on, no visible text under `/about` or on `/meeting` uses the old initials for US Chess or holds an em or en dash.
10. On Minutes and on Bylaws & rules, each document's edit and delete buttons have accessible names and sit outside the card's own button, and a card that opens exposes `aria-expanded`; the tab on screen carries `aria-current="page"` at every width.
11. With `themeHeritage` on, every tab and `/meeting` draw inside `HeritageLayout`, the reader's text column carries the `reader` hook, every editor and form keeps the base look, and CLS on `/about` and `/about/bylaws` stays under 0.1 across the font swap, and on a move from another page of the site while the documents' lists load.
12. Drawing `RichTextEditor` logs no duplicate extension warning.
13. `/about/board`, `/about/bylaws`, `/about/minutes` and `/meeting` are in the accessibility matrix (`PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`), `/about/minutes` in place of `/governance/minutes`, with zero serious or critical violations in the light, dark and heritage looks at 390 px and 1440 px. `/board/inbox` joins once the browser tests can sign in, and its chosen filter is announced to a screen reader.
14. "Email me when this changes" sends one email per new version to each watching address, and its link leads to a one-click unsubscribe.
15. The About and Champions tabs show the official logo, and Board & regions, Bylaws & rules, Minutes and `/meeting` show none (assumed, Q9).

**Tests**
- Integration (`test/integration`): `governance-versions.test.ts` (versions and diff, and editor-only writes with a role-safety case), `governance-search.test.ts` (three kinds, year and meeting type), `governance-watch.test.ts` (one email per version, unsubscribe by token), `contact-routing.test.ts` extended (a region with its seat held and open, an officer's Message, "Not sure who to ask?"), `meetings.test.ts` (the next meeting and its `.ics`), and a contract test for each new endpoint in `test/integration/contracts/`.
- Unit: `test/unit/about-tabs.test.tsx` (addresses, the `/governance/*` redirects, the Champions tab only with `newNews` on and four tabs with it off, `aria-current`, one `main`, "Last updated" under each tab's title, criterion 5, and the official logo on the About and Champions tabs and on no other tab or `/meeting`, criterion 15), `test/unit/governance-documents.test.tsx` (new: criterion 10's named edit and delete buttons outside the card's own button, and `aria-expanded`), `test/unit/governance-words.test.tsx` (no old initials for US Chess and no em or en dash in the pages' words), `test/unit/governanceEditors.test.tsx`, `governanceEditors-board.test.tsx` and `governanceEditors-bylaws.test.tsx` (the base boundary, and no duplicate extension warning), `test/unit/themeScopes.test.ts` and `test/unit/heritage-app-routes.test.tsx` (the tabs in heritage, and the old addresses base once they redirect), `test/unit/heritage-hooks.test.tsx` (the `reader` hook), `test/unit/features.test.ts` (`newAbout` starts off).
- Browser: `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` (criterion 13), with `newAbout` on in the a11y-on build (`test/a11y/global-setup.ts`) and `test/a11y/baseline.json` unchanged, and `test/a11y/heritage.spec.ts` (criterion 11's CLS on `/about` and `/about/bylaws`, and on a phone the bylaws contents opening as a sheet with the change log and the PDF, criterion 7).

**Depends on:** WS01, WS02 (the About menu rows), WS09 (the region target on `POST /api/contact`), WS10 (`/about/champions`), WS11 (the whole-board target, if WS10 has not added it); regions from Appendix C; WS06's signed tokens and WS07's outbox for the watch list.

**Risks & open decisions:** it builds trust only if the secretary and treasurer keep it current. The structured bylaws need a one-time conversion of today's document. Open for K: P2.34, P2.35, P2.38, P2.40 and P2.44, and Q9: which pages beyond About and Champions carry the official logo.
