## WS15 · Scanner 2.0
**Why:**
- The scanner is live (photo, moves, lichess), but nothing is saved, there is no board while correcting, and the photo can't be seen next to the moves.
- Chess67 now sells a scanner that shows the photo and the moves side by side. K asked for save and share PGN.
- On Mon, Oct 19, 2026 K picked one board for each of the scanner's eight parts, and asked that a scanned game can be linked to any tournament game its player has played or is playing, "not just if there is a live one", so the results pages can show the games themselves.
- K set the limits the same day and on Tue, Oct 20, 2026 (D28): nobody can browse another person's games, since "the link it to the tournament thing is good enough for that public aspect"; there is no live feed of the read, because "we should save api cost where it is smart to do so"; and the scanner is for active LCA members only ("Make this how the scanner works too, only active LCA memberships.").

**Canvas boards** (page 16, Scoresheet scanner: one pick per part, K, Mon, Oct 19, 2026; `DESIGN_REPLAN_phase2.md` section 7.6 and `docs/redesign/rounds/2026-10-19/README.md`):
1. Getting in and capture: `Scan-E` (camera first, then check the photo), with the person signed in first and its states drawn as `Scan-D` draws them (K preferred their look).
2. The reading job: `Scan-G` (hand-off), as drawn.
3. Review: `Scan-H` (photo, moves and board in three panes), with piece images in place of letters on the board.
4. Fixing a misread: `Scan-K` (fix in place), as drawn.
5. Save, share and export: `Scan-L` (the finish card), with `Scan-M`'s immediate private save and no Public option. Scan-M's screens are the states L needs; where the two differ, L wins (K: "if there is conflict, prefer L"). `Scan-L` draws "Keep the scoresheet photo with this game" ticked; here it starts unticked, to fit the 30-day rule K confirmed, until K says which he wants (Q31).
6. My games: `Scan-O` (event cards), with games outside tournaments in a group of their own, shown as well as the cards.
7. Into the tournament: `Scan-Q` and `Scan-Q-2` (from the crosstable, at the table), with scanning optional and several ways to verify a game.
8. The printed notation kit: `Scan-S` (a plain LCA sheet, the kit split by audience), with piece images in place of letters.

The redraws of `Scan-E`, `Scan-H`, `Scan-L`, `Scan-O`, `Scan-Q` and `Scan-S` are still to be drawn; until each is on the canvas, the board it grows from, read with K's change as written here, is the reference. These picks replace `Scan-A`, `Scan-B` and `Scan-C`. The other options (`Scan-F`, `Scan-I`, `Scan-J`, `Scan-N`, `Scan-P`, `Scan-P-2` and `Scan-R`) are not built, except Scan-N's dated list as Scan-O's List view and Scan-J's stepper inside Scan-K.

**User-facing scope** (all of it behind `scanner2`; with the switch off, `/scanner` is today's page and `POST /api/scan` answers as it does today)
- **Who may scan (changed, D28).** Active LCA members only, from `scanner2` on (assumed, Q39). Today `POST /api/scan` admits any signed-in account through `requireAuthedMember` (`functions/api/scan/index.ts` line 59), which makes a profile on a first sign-in, and the page says "The scanner is free with an LCA account". WS15's scanning endpoints admit a person whose own profile holds a membership active on today's Louisiana date (`hasActiveMembershipOn` in `domain/membership/requirement.ts`, the test the checkout uses); a profile covered by a Family membership counts, so a covered teen with her own login may scan (assumed, Q5).
  - Signed out: today's explainer, with Log in to scan and Create an account, and the rules before login in words: "Members only · log in with your LCA account", "Up to 20 scans a day · each page of a sheet is one scan" and the photo line (Privacy, below). No camera opens until the person is signed in (K: "make sure they are logged into their account first").
  - Signed in without an active membership: one card, "The scanner is for LCA members", with Join LCA, or Renew once a membership has expired (WS02's rule); no camera and no upload. Their own My games stays open to them (assumed, Q25).
  - The day's limit stays 20 pages per member, counted in `scan_usage`, and resets at midnight Louisiana time, as the picked boards say ("17 of 20 scans left today · resets at midnight"). Today it resets at midnight UTC, which is 7:00 PM in Louisiana in summer.
- **1 · Getting in and capture (`Scan-E`, in `Scan-D`'s states).**
  - On a phone, a member who taps Scoresheet scanner, Scan a sheet in My games, a round's Scan it or a crosstable cell's Add your game gets the phone's own camera (today's `capture="environment"` input; WS15 draws no camera of its own). Where the browser will not open the camera from that tap, the page shows one gold Take a photo button first.
  - Back on the page the photo shows with four corner handles and "Is the whole grid in the frame?", with Retake and Use this photo. A corner moves by dragging, by the arrow keys, or by tapping the corner and then the spot (WCAG 2.5.7). The photo is straightened from the corners in the browser, and a blurry or dark photo gets "Looks blurry. Retake?" before anything is sent.
  - Up to 3 pages (the front, the back, a continuation sheet), each one scan, joined in order before the moves are checked (`mergePages.ts`) and shrunk in the browser to a 1568px long edge (`downscaleImage`). "Nothing leaves your phone until you tap Scan this sheet."
  - On a laptop, a drop zone with Choose a photo, and the same corner check. On a phone the bottom tab bar hides while the check is open (WS02's `hideTabBar` on scanner capture).
  - Opened from a crosstable cell, a round's slot or a director's board row, a line names the game ("Louisiana State Championship 2026 · Open · Round 4 · Board 6 · Marcus Landry vs Priya Shah · from the pairings"), with "Not this game?".
  - The states, each in words: Loading; Empty ("Add a photo of your scoresheet"); Page limit ("3 of 3 pages. Scan the rest as a second game."); Blurry or dark; Camera refused or absent ("Camera not available here, choose a photo instead."); Not a photo (today's words); Daily limit reached ("You've used all 20 scans for today. Reviewing and saving games already read still works.", with Open My games); Offline ("Reading needs a connection. The photo stays on this phone until you are back online."); Not a member (above); and, for anyone `requireTournamentManager` admits for an event with published pairings, "Directors: scan a whole round" (part 7). Whose game it is gets asked at the finish (part 5), not here.
- **2 · The reading job (`Scan-G`).**
  - Scan this sheet uploads the pages and answers at once: "Sheet sent · 3:40 PM · Reading in the background. We'll email you when it is ready to check, and it will be in My games.", with Scan the next sheet (gold) and Open My games. While the page stays open it shows one quiet card: the stages in words, each ticked as it finishes (reading the handwriting, checking every move is legal, matching the names to the pairings), "This usually takes 10 to 30 seconds." and "You can leave this screen. The draft waits in My games."
  - There is no live feed of the read: no streaming move list, reading line or progress bar. The page asks for the job's state about every 3 seconds while it is visible and never while it is hidden, as 2.4 asks of pages that poll.
  - The moment the upload lands, My games holds a "Reading · started 3:40 PM" row. When the reading ends the row reads "Ready to check"; one email, "Your game is ready to check", goes to each address `recipientsFor` names for the game's owner (for a child's game, the guardians, with the child's first name); and an item lands in My LCA's Today (WS13). Both open the review on any device. A reading that fails sends no email.
  - Legality is worked out in the browser on whichever device opens the draft (`decoder.ts` in a Web Worker through `decodeInBackground.ts`), from the saved reading, so opening a draft again never pays for a second reading.
  - States in words: In line ("Your scan is next · the reader is busy"); Unreadable ("We couldn't read this sheet", with the sheet's notes, "Nothing counted" and Try another photo); Busy and Down (today's 503 and 502 words, nothing counted, Try again keeps the photo); Session expired (log in again); Descriptive notation (algebraic only, from `detectDescriptive`); Read but no legal game, and Partly decoded ("Ready to check · stops at move 24"); Offline mid-job ("Connection lost. The reading continues and the draft waits in My games."); Daily limit (refused before the upload; a running job finishes).
- **3 · Review (`Scan-H`, with piece images).**
  - On a laptop, three panes. Your photo: the move under review outlined on the sheet itself, "Show what we read", and zoom. Moves: the scoresheet's White and Black columns, every move a button labelled in words beside its colour (Fixed automatically, Check this, You checked; today's legend maps onto them, and the colours are the `--mark-warn` and `--mark-ok` marks), `FilterChip`s (All moves · Needs a look · Fixed automatically · You checked), the game line with the weekday date and "names and colours matched from the pairings" when the game is tied to its tournament game, and Edit game details. Board: the position at the selected move in piece images, with First · Back · Forward · Last and "Move 13 · Black".
  - On a phone, three tabs, Photo · Moves · Board, sharing one selected move, with the board held at the top while a flag is open.
  - Selecting a move in any pane moves the other two. The cell is found from where the reading says it sits on the photo (an optional place for each cell); a game read without places reviews with the outline off.
  - Game checks in words, each with a tick or a warning: every move is legal in order; no position repeated three times; the written result against the final position ("17. Rd8# is checkmate, which matches the result written on the sheet, 1-0"; "The sheet says 1-0, and the last move isn't checkmate. Resignations happen.", with Keep 1-0 as written). A draw reads ½-½.
  - States: All clear; Flags open ("Check · 1 move left"); Loading (the board's space kept); Game cut short ("We could follow the game to move 24. Keep the first 24 moves, or fix move 25."); Result mismatch; No header read (Edit game details opens with White, Black, Event, Round, Board, the date with its weekday and Result; a club night fills in "Club night"); No photo ("Photo not kept"; the moves and the board still work); Offline (checking and fixing work, and sending them waits); a junior's game ("Priya's game · only you and your family can see it"); Failure ("Couldn't load this game", with Try again).
- **4 · Fixing a misread (`Scan-K`).**
  - Any cell, flagged or not, opens the fix panel beside the board (on phones a bottom sheet that scrolls inside itself, in `ScrollPanel`, D16): the crop of that cell from the photo with what was written ("Smudged on the sheet. Which move is it?"), the board before the move, the candidates ranked by whether the later moves still work, each with its reason ("Qxd7 · Legal here, but then 15. Bxd7+ would capture nothing"), "Something else? Type the move" (`looseSan` and `legalMovesAt`, as today's move editor does), Confirm, Skip for now, and "Cut the game here · keeps moves 1 to 12".
  - The flags are a filter over the list, never a queue. With many flags, "Walk through them one by one" turns the panel into a stepper ("Check 1 of 9 · Next"), as `Scan-J` does.
  - Confirm keeps everything before the move and works the rest out again in the browser (`forcedSans`); Undo last change puts back the game as it was before.
  - Each fix is sent to the draft as it is made, so the same game opened on another device shows it; offline, fixes wait in this browser (read and written inside try/catch) and go when the connection returns.
  - States: Empty ("Nothing to check. Tap any move to change it"); Updating ("Updating the rest of the game…", with `role="status"`); Failure, with Undo; No candidate fits; Many flags; Skipped ("Check · 1 skipped", sharing closed).
- **5 · Saved at once, then share and export (`Scan-L`, with `Scan-M`'s save).**
  - The game is a private draft in My games from the moment the upload lands (part 2), so there is no Save button and nothing is lost. When no Check this is left, the finish card opens: "Game saved · In My games · only you", the final board and the game line.
  - The finish card, three columns on a laptop and stacked on a phone:
    - **Your game.** Whose game it is ("Your game · Marcus Landry"; a guardian gets the chips You · Priya, with "This looks like Priya's game" when the names on the sheet match a child's pairing). Who can see it, two choices in words: "Only me · Just you, on any device you log in on" (the default) and "Anyone with the link · Not listed anywhere; whoever has the link can open it". There is no Public choice. "Keep the scoresheet photo with this game", unticked (`Scan-L` draws it ticked; assumed, Q31), with "Otherwise it is deleted on Tue, Oct 6, 30 days after the scan". "Keep this game in My games", ticked: unticked, Done removes the game and its photo, after any export (K: saved "in their My games section by default, with the check mark available to uncheck it").
    - **Link it from the crosstable:** part 7.
    - **Share & export.** The preview card (final position, players, result); the share link, live only with Anyone with the link, with Copy link; Open in lichess; Download PGN; Copy PGN; Email; the phone's share sheet with the PGN file; today's "Copy & open chess.com", which `Scan-L` keeps; then Scan the next sheet. The PGN carries Event, Site, Date, Round, Board, White, Black, WhiteElo, BlackElo and Result.
  - Sharing, exporting and linking stay closed while any Check this is left ("Resolve 1 flagged move first"); the draft is in My games either way.
  - Who can see it can change at any time. Set back to Only me, the link stops working at once ("Set it back to Only me at any time and the link stops working"); turned on again, it is a new link.
  - A shared game opens at `/g/:slug`, read only: the board in piece images, the moves, Play through, Open in lichess and Download PGN, and never the photo. The page carries `noindex`, is in no sitemap and has no structured data. Its share preview (`GET /og/game/:slug.png`, WS04's renderer) is the final position with the players and the result.
  - States, as Scan-L draws them: Disabled; Saving a change; Failure ("Couldn't save. Your game is still here; try again", with Download PGN still live); Offline; Link copied; Photo not kept.
- **6 · My games (`Scan-O`).**
  - `/dashboard/games`, a page of My LCA reached from the scanner ("My games · 3 saved"), from a "My games · 3" line in My LCA's You section and "Priya's games" on a child's Family card (WS13), and from the ready email and the Today item. The header's My LCA ▾ menu does not change (WS02).
  - It is the only list of a person's games, and only the person and their guardians see it: "The games you've scanned. Only you can see this list" (there are no player pages, D9).
  - The head: Scan a sheet with the day's count; Print the notation kit; search by opponent, event or opening (named from the moves with an open list of named openings); `FilterChip`s with counts (All · Needs a check · Checked · Shared · On the crosstable); and Cards · List.
  - Cards: one per tournament on LCA's site, newest first, with the name and section, the dates with their weekdays, "3 of 5 rounds scanned" and links to the event's results page. Each round of the player's section has a slot: the game's final position in piece images, "Round 4 · Board 6 · vs Priya Shah · won", who can see it and the game's state in words beside its colour (`StatusBadge` with its words), and More. A round with no game is a dashed slot, "Round 3 · no sheet scanned", with Scan it.
  - Games outside tournaments (K: "we should make sure their games not connected to tournaments are displayed well too") have a group of their own below the cards: club nights, casual games and events not on LCA's site, gathered by the event or club named on the sheet or in Edit game details, newest first. Each game has the same final-position board, opponent, result in words, weekday date and state as a slot, and the same More menu.
  - List: Scan-N's dated rows, grouped by month, newest first.
  - More: Review, Share, Download PGN, Who can see it, Link it from the crosstable (or Take it off the crosstable), and Delete ("Delete this game? The moves and the photo are removed at once. A game on a crosstable is taken off it first.").
  - A guardian gets the chips Everyone · Priya, each child's games under the child's first name, family only, and "Sharing Priya's games: not allowed yet · Allow".
  - States: Empty ("No games yet. Scan a scoresheet after your next game"); Loading; Failure; Drafts ("Reading" rows with the stage in words, "Ready to check", "Draft · 1 to check"); "Photo not kept; the moves we read are kept"; Offline ("Showing your games as of 7:00 PM"); Limit (Scan a sheet and every Scan it closed at 0, with the words); a junior's games ("Only you and your parents").
- **7 · Into the tournament (`Scan-Q` and `Scan-Q-2`).**
  - **Scanning is optional.** A result never needs a scoresheet: the director confirms a round's results whether or not any sheet was scanned. A game can be checked several ways, each shown in words: the players' own reports (WS07), the sheet the director photographs at the table, the sheet a player adds from their own cell, and the director's own entry. The sheet's written result is set beside the crosstable's, and a difference is said in words.
  - **Tying a game to its tournament game (private).** A game opened from a director's board row, a player's own cell or a round's slot knows its game. Otherwise the reading suggests one, matching the names, round and board on the sheet against the pairings of the person and the child profiles they guard. The person can also pick any tournament they are in or have been in, newest first, and any of their games in it, live or past, with the game being played now, or else the latest, first. Only a game the person, or a child profile they guard, played can be picked. A tied game sits in its tournament's card in My games and takes its names and colours from the pairing.
  - **Linking it from the crosstable (the one public place).** "Link this game from both players' Round 4 results (W12 and L3)", unticked, with "Anyone who opens the results page can play it through." It is encouraged, never forced (D28). Once it is linked:
    - both players' cells on the event's results page read "Game" in words (a third line in `ResultCell`) and open the game in a sheet with Play through, Open in lichess and Download PGN, never the photo;
    - the game is listed under the crosstable in "Games from this event · 14 of 30 boards in Round 4", by round and board;
    - the opponent gets a My LCA line, "Marcus Landry linked your Round 4 game. Hide it from your row", and one email to each address `recipientsFor` names for them (for a junior, the guardians), after the link is made (assumed, Q27); hiding it takes it off that row only;
    - the owner can take it off at any time, and the event's directors and LCA admins can take any game off their event's crosstable, which the audit log records.
  - A tournament game has at most one linked game; a second is refused with "This game is already on the crosstable", and that copy stays private.
  - **From the crosstable, at the table.** Once the round is published, a signed-in player's own cell reads "Add your game" (a guardian sees "Add Priya's game" in the child's row) and opens the scanner with the event, round, board and both names filled in from the pairing. Saving from there offers "Save and add to the crosstable" (gold) and "Save without adding it (Only me)". The sheet's result is set beside the crosstable's ("The sheet says 1-0. The crosstable says 1-0. They match."); on a difference, "Tell the TD" puts the sheet in the director's queue as "Scoresheet says 1-0". A player never changes a result.
  - **The director, one sheet at a time (`Scan-Q-2`).** In the TD console's results grid (WS08) each board row gains a Sheet column with a camera button, "Scan sheet". The sheet is filed under that board with no matching, read like any scan, and a private copy lands in each player's My games ("Scanned by the director · check 2 moves"; the flags are left for the players). The Status column and the Reported results queue show "Scoresheet says 1-0" in words beside the players' reports, a sheet that differs first ("Scoresheet differs from your entry"), with Confirm 1-0 · Enter a different result · Open the sheet, and counts in words ("26 of 30 confirmed · 3 scoresheets waiting"; assumed, Q30). A scanned result never writes `tournament_games.result`: Confirm writes it through the results grid (WS08). "Directors: scan a whole round" on the scanner page opens the same camera after "Which board?".
  - **The event's allowance.** A director's sheets draw on the event's own allowance, shown in words ("Event allowance: 164 of 300 sheets used · players' own scans don't count here"), never on the director's 20 a day. By default it is two sheets for each game in the event's published pairings, the same for member-created events, and an LCA admin can raise it for one event (assumed, Q26).
  - **The key game.** Once the event is finalized, its directors or an LCA admin can make one linked game the key game ("Make this the key game", in TD view). The results page shows it as a Key game block with the board, Play through, Open in lichess and Download PGN. The recap carries no game (WS10).
  - States in words, as Scan-Q draws them: No match ("This event isn't on LCA's site, so there is no cell to add it from. It is still saved."); Match uncertain ("Is this Round 4, Board 6, Landry vs Shah?", Yes · Pick the board); Pairings not published; Waiting for a guardian's OK; Linked; Hidden by the opponent ("On the crosstable · hidden from Priya Shah's row"); Result mismatch; Not final (the key game waits); the director's Empty, Reading, Couldn't match ("Which board?") and Flags left ("left for the players"); Daily limit, and the allowance used up ("Event allowance used: 300 of 300 sheets. Ask LCA to raise it."); Offline; Failure.
- **8 · The printed notation kit (`Scan-S`, with piece images).**
  - **The LCA scoresheet:** a plain sheet with no corner marks and no QR. At the top, the official logo (`LcaLogo`, D1) with "Official scoresheet · moves 1 to 50", and where a QR would be, "Scan it at louisianachess.org/scanner" and "Log in · members only · up to 20 scans a day". Then Event, Section, Round, Board, White, Black, the date with its weekday and the time control; one boxed cell per move, 50 a side, with moves 51 to 100 on the back; result boxes 1-0 · ½-½ · 0-1; and signatures. Black ink on US Letter, nothing that relies on colour, and no grade or birthdate. Today's grid finder reads it as it reads any US Chess style sheet, and the typed names match the round's pairings, so the server needs nothing new.
  - **Printed from the pairings:** the print page's What to print (`src/pages/TournamentPrintPage.tsx`) gains Scoresheets, by round and section (a sheet for each player of each board, with the header typed) and Blank scoresheets, and the print kit's Download menu lists them (WS07). Before the pairings are published it reads "Print after Round 4 is published", with the blank sheets still there.
  - **Blank, for club nights:** the header as lines to write on, with "Write the event, round and board so the scanner can file the game".
  - **For parents and coaches:** the kids' notation cheat sheet (Scan-C's words, each piece's image beside its letter, every diagram in piece images) and the two cut-out cards (the TD-table card, "Scan your scoresheet", with its three steps; and the photo-tips card) are PDFs on `/scanner/kit`, a short page linking to them and to the director's print kit, with the blank scoresheet as a PDF. `Scan-S` puts the two for parents and coaches on the Scholastic page, which has no resources block (assumed, P2.43), so WS15 adds one line there itself, behind `scanner2` and on the new page only (`newScholastic`), in the guide's chess words beside notation: "Learning to write the moves? Notation cheat sheet for kids (PDF) · How to scan a scoresheet (PDF)", linking the two PDFs.
  - The cards' fine print: "Log in to scan · members only · up to 20 scans a day · Photos are kept 30 days, then deleted, unless you keep one with its game".
  - For a member-created event, the scoresheets printed from its pairings carry "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." in place of LCA's logo and name (D27; assumed, Q12).
- **Piece images.** Every board WS15 draws (the review, the fix panel, My games, the finish card, the shared game, the crosstable's game sheet, the Key game block, the share preview image and the kit's diagrams) uses one open-licensed set of flat pieces, cburnett, as lichess draws it, kept in the repo as 12 SVG files with its licence. Never chess.com's own art, and never letters in discs. verify: the set's licence and the credit it asks for, recorded beside the files and on `/scanner/kit`.
- **Privacy: what is kept, for how long, and who can see it (changed, D28).** Today nothing is kept, and three places say so: the page ("Photos are read once and never stored", `src/pages/ScannerPage.tsx` line 136), the endpoint's header comment (`functions/api/scan/index.ts` line 6) and migration 0035's note (`migrations/0035_scan_usage.sql` line 11). With `scanner2` on the scanner keeps the following, and every place that describes the scanner says so.

| What | How long | Who can see it |
|---|---|---|
| The photo of each page | 30 days after the scan, then deleted; as long as the game when its owner ticks "Keep the scoresheet photo with this game" (assumed, Q31); deleted at once with the game | The game's owner (the profile's own login) and that profile's guardians; for a director's sheet, also everyone `requireTournamentManager` admits for that event. Never on a shared link, a crosstable, a share preview or any other public page |
| The reading as written, cell by cell | As long as the game | The owner and the owner's guardians |
| The game: moves, details and fixes | Until the owner deletes it | Only me: the owner and the owner's guardians. Anyone with the link: whoever holds the link, until it is turned off. On the crosstable: anyone who opens the event's results page |
| A sheet that could not be read | Until it is dismissed, or 30 days | The person who scanned it |
| The day's count (`scan_usage`) | 30 days | Nobody: it holds counts only |

- **Nobody browses another person's games (D28, D9).** Nobody else can open a private game, LCA admins and the opponent included: nobody can open a person's profile and see their games, and there is no joined list of games. A link its owner shares opens that one game for whoever holds it and is listed nowhere. The only public places a game appears are its two crosstable cells, its event's "Games from this event" and, once chosen, the Key game block.
- **Juniors.** A junior's game (a profile with an active guardian link, D15's test) gets no share link and no crosstable link until a guardian allows sharing for that child in Family, once for each child and changeable at any time (assumed, Q29): "Sharing a junior's game needs a guardian's OK". A teen's own login gets "Ask a parent to publish", which puts "Priya asked to share a game · Allow sharing · Keep private" in the guardians' Today. Withdrawing the permission stops the child's share links and takes the child's games off every crosstable.
- **Names.** Names on a shared or linked game, and on a scoresheet printed from the pairings, show as results and pairings show them: full names, juniors included (D3; assumed, Q28).
- **The words, with `scanner2` on.** The page and the How it works rail: "Nothing leaves your phone until you tap Scan this sheet. Your game is saved where only you can see it, and the photo is kept for 30 days beside it, then deleted." The finish card gives the photo's deletion date with its weekday. The ready email: "The photo is kept until Tue, Oct 6 unless you keep it with the game." The printed cards: the fine print in part 8. Migration 0035 is not edited; the new migration's header says the rule changed.
- **Member-created events (D26, D27).** The scanner works on them as on any event. Their linked games, "Games from this event" and the Key game sit on the event's own pages, which are never listed and carry noindex; every scanner email about one of their games goes through WS08's `markMemberEventEmail` (section 2.2), which adds "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." and the organizer's reply-to; and their directors' sheets draw on the same allowance.
- **Unchanged contract.** The reading step transcribes exactly what is written, crossed-out moves and all, and never corrects a move; the TypeScript chess.js decoder alone applies legality, in the browser (`scanner/SCANNER_SPEC.md` sections 2.2 and 2.3); `npm run scanner:check` keeps passing. The reading may add one optional field, where each cell sits on the photo, which carries no chess knowledge (verify: SCANNER_SPEC.md section 3.1 takes the field without touching the verbatim rule). The reading service is a paid call on LCA's own key, so the draft keeps the reading, and opening a game again reads nothing twice.

**Data model**
```sql
-- 00xx_scanned_games.sql
-- From this migration on, a scoresheet photo is kept: in the SCANS bucket for 30 days after the scan,
-- then deleted, unless its owner keeps it with the game. Migration 0035's note that the images are
-- never stored describes the scanner before WS15; 0035 has run on production and is not edited.
CREATE TABLE scan_jobs (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                                   -- crypto.randomUUID()
  started_by TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),
  kind TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'player' CHECK (kind IN ('player','director')),
  game_id TEXT REFERENCES tournament_games(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- known when opened from a board row, a player's cell or a round's slot
  tournament_id TEXT REFERENCES tournaments(id),         -- a director's sheet draws on this event's allowance
  pages INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (pages BETWEEN 1 AND 3),  -- each page is one scan
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'reading' CHECK (status IN ('reading','read','unreadable','failed')),
  reading_json TEXT,                                     -- each page's reading as written (a RawScan per page), never corrected
  notes_json TEXT,                                       -- the reader's notes on a sheet it could not read
  sheet_result TEXT CHECK (sheet_result IN ('1-0','0-1','1/2-1/2')),  -- the result written on the sheet, when it reads as one
  sent_to_td_at TEXT,                                    -- a director's sheet when it is read; a player's sheet on Tell the TD
  td_resolved_at TEXT,
  td_resolved_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id),
  photos_deleted_at TEXT,                                -- the copies under scans/<id>/ are gone
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  finished_at TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX idx_scan_jobs_status ON scan_jobs(status, created_at);
CREATE INDEX idx_scan_jobs_event ON scan_jobs(tournament_id, kind);

CREATE TABLE scanned_games (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,                                   -- crypto.randomUUID()
  job_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES scan_jobs(id),
  member_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES members(id),        -- whose game: the scanner's own profile, a child profile they guard, or each player of a director's sheet
  suggested_member_id TEXT REFERENCES members(id),       -- "This looks like Priya's game", from the names on the sheet
  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'reading' CHECK (status IN ('reading','unreadable','to_check','checked')),
  flags_open INTEGER,                                    -- moves still marked Check this, once a browser has worked the game out
  fixes_json TEXT,                                       -- the owner's fixes in order, replayed by the decoder on any device
  pgn TEXT,                                              -- written once no flag is left; it must replay as legal moves
  final_fen TEXT,
  move_count INTEGER,
  white_name TEXT, black_name TEXT, white_rating INTEGER, black_rating INTEGER,
  event_name TEXT, section TEXT, round INTEGER, board INTEGER,
  played_on TEXT,                                        -- 'YYYY-MM-DD'
  result TEXT CHECK (result IN ('1-0','0-1','1/2-1/2','*')),       -- the game record's result; never written to tournament_games
  game_id TEXT REFERENCES tournament_games(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,  -- the tournament game this is (private until linked)
  linked_at TEXT,                                        -- linked from the event's crosstable: the one public place
  linked_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id),
  hidden_by_opponent_at TEXT,                            -- off the opponent's own row only
  hidden_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id),
  share_slug TEXT UNIQUE,                                -- Anyone with the link; null is Only me; a new slug each time it is turned on
  keep_photo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,                 -- 1: the photo is copied under kept/<id>/ and kept with the game
  created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (datetime('now')),
  updated_at TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX idx_scanned_games_member ON scanned_games(member_id, created_at);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX idx_scanned_games_linked ON scanned_games(game_id) WHERE linked_at IS NOT NULL;

ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN scan_allowance INTEGER;  -- null: two sheets for each game in the published pairings (assumed, Q26)
ALTER TABLE tournaments ADD COLUMN key_game_id TEXT REFERENCES scanned_games(id) ON DELETE SET NULL;
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN game_sharing_allowed_by TEXT REFERENCES members(id);  -- a guardian's OK to share a junior's games
ALTER TABLE members ADD COLUMN game_sharing_allowed_at TEXT;
```
- There is no `visibility` column: Only me is a game with no `share_slug` and no `linked_at`; Anyone with the link sets `share_slug`; the crosstable sets `linked_at` on a tied game. A share slug is 16 random characters from `crypto.getRandomValues`, with no names in it, so a link cannot be guessed.
- Every id is TEXT, and every column that names a tournament, a game or a member is TEXT (0.2). The migration takes the next free number when it lands.
- A game whose tournament game goes (a round paired again) keeps its row and loses its tie and its link, and its owner picks again; the readers treat a game with no `game_id` as unlinked.
- `scan_usage` keeps its shape. From WS15 its `day` is the Louisiana date (`LCA_TIME_ZONE`, `domain/format/centralTime.ts`), so the count resets at midnight Louisiana time; the rows `POST /api/scan` writes keep the UTC date until that endpoint goes.
- The photos live in a new R2 bucket bound as `SCANS`: `scans/<jobId>/<page>` for 30 days after the scan, and `kept/<gameId>/<page>` while kept. `wrangler.toml` binds `lca-scans` in production and `lca-scans-preview` under `[env.preview]`, so a preview never stores a member's photo in production; `test/unit/wrangler-config.test.ts` keeps the two lists in step. K creates both buckets.

**API** (every new endpoint ships with its contract in `domain/contracts/scanner.ts` and a contract test in `test/integration/contracts/`, and has a role-safety case)
- `POST /api/scan/jobs` (new; `requireActiveMember`, a new helper in `functions/utils/auth.ts` that runs `requireAuthedMember` and then `hasActiveMembershipOn` for today's Louisiana date, and otherwise answers 403 `{ error, code: 'membership_required' }` in plain words): a form with 1 to 3 photos (JPEG, PNG or WebP, each within `MAX_SCAN_BYTES`) and, when known, `gameId`.
  - With `kind: 'director'` it also needs `requireTournamentManager` for that game's event and draws on the event's allowance (409 in words when it is used up). Otherwise it claims one of the day's 20 for each page (429 at the limit, "You've used all 20 scans for today. The limit resets at midnight.").
  - It writes the photos under `scans/<jobId>/` in `SCANS`, creates the job and its draft (one for each player of a director's sheet), answers 202 `{ jobId, gameIds, scansLeftToday }`, and reads the pages side by side in `waitUntil`. After the reading it matches the sheet to the pairings (part 7) and stores the suggestion. A page the reader could not read, or a failure on the reading service's side, hands its scan back, as `releaseScan` does today. Without the reading service's key it answers 503, as today.
- `GET /api/scan/jobs/:id` (new; the person who started it): `{ status, stage, notes, gameIds }`, `Cache-Control: no-store`. A job still reading 10 minutes after it started is marked failed here, and its scans handed back.
- `GET /api/me/games` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): the caller's games and those of the child profiles they guard, grouped as Scan-O draws them (one entry for each tournament, with its section's round count, then the games outside tournaments), with the day's count; `no-store`.
- `GET /api/me/tournament-games` (new; `requireAuthedMember`): every tournament game the caller or a guarded child has played or is playing, member-created events included, newest event first, with the game being played now, or else the latest, first. Each has its event, weekday date, section, round, board, colour, opponent, result, and whether a game is already linked to it.
- `GET`, `PATCH` and `DELETE /api/games/:id` (new; the profile's own login and its guardians; anyone else, LCA admins and the opponent included, gets 404):
  - GET: the game with its reading, fixes and details.
  - PATCH: the fixes; the details; whose game (only to the caller's own profile or a child they guard); keep the photo; keep in My games; the share link (on makes a new slug, off clears it); the tied game (403 unless the caller or a guarded child played it); and the crosstable link (403 for a junior's game without a guardian's OK, 409 for a second linked game). It writes the PGN once no flag is left, refused with 400 unless the PGN replays as legal moves with chess.js; the server never decodes.
  - DELETE: takes a linked game off the crosstable first, then removes the row and its kept photo, and the job's photos too, unless the other player's copy of a director's sheet still uses them.
- `GET /api/games/:id/photos/:page` (new; the owner and the owner's guardians): the photo while it is kept, `Cache-Control: private, no-store`; 410 "Photo not kept" after.
- `POST /api/games/:id/tell-td` (new; the owner of a tied game): sends the sheet to the event's queue as "Scoresheet says".
- `POST` and `DELETE /api/games/:id/hide` (new; a linked game's opponent, or the opponent's guardians): takes the game off their own row, or puts it back.
- `POST /api/games/:id/ask-to-share` (new; a junior's own login): the item in the guardians' Today.
- `PUT /api/me/children/:memberId/game-sharing` (new; a guardian of that profile, through `member_guardians`): `{ allowed }`, recorded with who and when. Turned off, the child's share links stop and the child's games come off every crosstable.
- `GET /api/games/shared/:slug` (new; public): the shared game, read only, never the photo; 404 once the link is off; `no-store`. Served with the page route `/g/:slug` (WS04's way of adding share tags, with `noindex`) and `GET /og/game/:slug.png` (WS04's renderer).
- `GET /api/tournaments/:id/scanned-games` (new; public, as the event's results page is): the event's linked games with round, board, section, names, result, move count and PGN, which rows show each one, and the key game; `Cache-Control: public, max-age=60` for an event anyone may open, and `private, no-store` for one that answers only some viewers (a closed member-created event).
- `PUT` and `DELETE /api/admin/tournaments/:id/key-game` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): 409 until the event is finalized; the game must be linked from that event.
- `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/scanned-games/:scannedGameId/unlink` (new; `requireTournamentManager`, audited): takes a game off the event's crosstable. (`/api/admin/tournaments/:id/games` is today's results path, so the scanner's routes use `scanned-games` beside it.)
- `GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/sheets/:jobId/photos/:page` and `POST /api/admin/tournaments/:id/sheets/:jobId/resolve` (new; `requireTournamentManager`): Open the sheet, and keep the director's result for a sheet that differs.
- WS08's queue, `GET /api/admin/tournaments/:id/reports?round=`, adds the sheets sent to the director, disagreements first. WS08's results grid write stays the only writer of `tournament_games.result`, and also marks the game's sheets resolved.
- `PATCH /api/admin/tournaments/:id` (exists): `scan_allowance`, for LCA admins only.
- `POST /api/scan` stays as it is while `scanner2` is off, and goes with today's page once K has checked `scanner2` on the live site; its line then leaves `test/unit/contracts-pending.ts` and `PENDING_COUNT` drops by one.
- The form on `POST /api/scan/jobs` is checked by a schema of its fields. The two photo routes answer with an image, so they follow the rule `REDESIGN_STATUS.md` sets for endpoints that do not answer with JSON (a contract for their error answers only, or the coverage test's excluded list with the reason), and `functions/g/[slug].ts` joins the excluded page routes as a page that serves the site's HTML.

**Frontend**
- Thin `src/pages/ScannerPage.tsx` (1,048 lines; a WS01 follow-up): it keeps only the choice between today's scanner and the new one, and calls no `fetch`. While `scanner2` is off, today's scanner is drawn unchanged from `src/areas/scanner/legacy/TodayScanner.tsx`, which goes with `POST /api/scan`.
- A new area, `src/areas/scanner/` (`index.ts`, `api.ts` and `components/`), the eleventh, after WS13's `account` and WS08's `workspace`. The scanner's call moves there from `src/areas/live/api.ts`, and the barrel `src/lib/api.ts` and `scripts/moves/api-split.json` follow.
- New in `src/areas/scanner/components/`: `CaptureStep.tsx` (the phone's camera input and the laptop drop zone), `CornerCheck.tsx`, `PageStrip.tsx` (today's, moved), `CaptureStates.tsx`, `JobCard.tsx`, `ReviewPanes.tsx`, `PhotoPane.tsx`, `MoveList.tsx`, `BoardAtMove.tsx` (piece images, and a text alternative naming the position's move), `GameChecks.tsx`, `FixPanel.tsx`, `FinishCard.tsx`, `TournamentGamePicker.tsx`, `ShareExport.tsx`, `EventCard.tsx`, `OtherGames.tsx`, `GameActions.tsx` (the More menu and the Delete dialog), `GameViewer.tsx` (read only, for `/g/:slug`, the crosstable's game sheet and the Key game block), `SheetColumn.tsx` (the console's camera for each board), and for the kit `ScoresheetSheet.tsx`, `CheatSheet.tsx` and `HowToScanCards.tsx`.
- New pages: `src/pages/MyGamesPage.tsx` (`/dashboard/games`), `src/pages/SharedGamePage.tsx` (`/g/:slug`), `src/pages/ScannerKitPage.tsx` (`/scanner/kit`) and `src/pages/ScannerKitPrintPage.tsx` (`/scanner/kit/:sheet`, the print pages the kit's PDFs are made from).
- From WS01: the gold `Button` for each screen's one main action; `StatusBadge` with its words for a game's state (a category, not one of the eight event statuses); `FilterChip`; `ScrollPanel`; the third line of `ResultCell`; the table primitives, with `TableRowHeader` for the move numbers; `LcaLogo` on the scoresheet; `formatDate`, `formatTime` and `formatScore`; and the `--mark-warn` and `--mark-ok` marks, always with their words. Every control takes the shared focus ring, and the photo button's own gold ring (`ScannerPage.tsx` line 869, 2.28:1 on a light ground; `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`, WS01 step 25) goes.
- Changed elsewhere, behind `scanner2`: `src/App.tsx` (the new routes); `src/lib/features.ts` (`scanner2`, off, with a plain comment); WS07's `ResultsTable.tsx` (the cells' third line, Games from this event, the game sheet and the Key game block); WS08's `ResultsGrid.tsx` and `ReportedResultsQueue.tsx` (the Sheet column and the "Scoresheet says" rows); `src/pages/TournamentPrintPage.tsx` (Scoresheets); WS13's You section and Family cards; and `src/pages/ScholasticPage.tsx` (the one line linking the kit's two PDFs, on the new page only).
- Assets: `src/assets/pieces/cburnett/` (12 SVG files and the licence) and `public/kit/` (the cheat sheet, the cards and the blank scoresheet as PDFs, made from the print pages by `scripts/kit-pdfs.ts`, run by hand with the pinned Playwright, as `scripts/images.mjs` is).
- The accessibility test build turns `scanner2` on in the a11y-on build, and a `scanner` fixture on `/__fixtures/:name` draws the signed-in screens with sample data until the browser-test servers can sign in.

**Jobs & integrations**
- Reading jobs run in the request with `waitUntil`, with their state in D1 (simplest), or on a Queue if limits require it (verify: how long `waitUntil` runs after the answer, against three pages read side by side).
- A job still reading 10 minutes after it started is marked failed, and its scans handed back, by the next read of it and by the 5-minute run of `workers/daily-emails`.
- A scanner retention phase in the daily run of `workers/daily-emails`, isolated like the others, deletes the photos under `scans/<jobId>/` 30 days after the scan, removes sheets that could not be read after 30 days, and clears `scan_usage` rows older than 30 days. Kept photos live under `kept/<gameId>/` and go with their game. The worker gets the `SCANS` binding.
- A lifecycle rule on both buckets deletes anything under `scans/` 31 days after upload, as a backstop to the phase (verify: R2's lifecycle rules by prefix). K sets the rules when creating the buckets.
- Emails go through WS07's notifications outbox and `recipientsFor` (WS06): the ready email and the linked-game notice to the opponent. Today items go through WS13's providers.
- The reading service's key is unchanged; a preview has one only if K sets it as a Preview secret.

**Acceptance criteria**
1. `POST /api/scan/jobs` answers 401 signed out, 403 with `code: 'membership_required'` for a signed-in account whose own profile has no membership active on today's Louisiana date (pending, expired or none), and 202 for an active member, a profile covered by a Family membership included.
2. Signed out, `/scanner` shows Log in to scan with "Members only", the day's limit and the new photo line, and no camera or upload control; signed in without an active membership, it shows the members-only card with Join LCA, or Renew once a membership has expired, and no camera or upload control.
3. In iOS Safari and Android Chrome at 390px, a member who opens Scoresheet scanner reaches the phone's own camera with at most one more tap; upload works in the browsers `npm run test:a11y` runs and, by hand, in iOS Safari and Android Chrome; every photo passes the corner check before anything is sent, and each corner moves by dragging, by the arrow keys, and by tapping the corner and then the spot.
4. Each capture state shows its words: Loading, Empty, Page limit, Blurry or dark, Camera refused or absent, Not a photo, Daily limit reached, Offline and Not a member.
5. Scan this sheet answers 202 before the reading ends, the page then offers Scan the next sheet, and My games shows the draft's "Reading" row at once; no move streams in, and the page asks for the job's state no more than about every 3 seconds, and never while the tab is hidden.
6. When the reading ends, the draft reads "Ready to check", each address `recipientsFor` names for the game's owner gets exactly one "Your game is ready to check" email, and an item appears in My LCA's Today; a reading that fails sends no email and leaves the day's count where it was before the scan.
7. The draft exists from the moment the upload lands, and `GET /api/games/:id` answers 404 to everyone but the profile's own login and its guardians, an LCA admin and the opponent included.
8. Selecting a move in any of the three panes, or in any tab on a phone, moves the other two to it; the board draws the position in piece images with no letters and a text alternative naming the move; the cell is outlined on the photo when the reading gave its place, and the review works without it.
9. Every move's state shows in words beside its colour (Fixed automatically, Check this, You checked), and the three game checks show in words, with a draw as ½-½.
10. Any cell, flagged or not, opens the fix panel; Confirm works every later move out again in the browser; Undo last change puts back the game as it was; a fix made on one device shows when the same draft opens on another.
11. Sharing, export and the crosstable link stay closed while any Check this is left, and the draft stays in My games either way.
12. The finish card offers exactly two choices, Only me (the default) and Anyone with the link, and no Public; a shared link opens the game read only, never the photo; set back to Only me, the old link answers 404 at once, and turning it on again gives a different link; `/g/:slug` carries `noindex`, is absent from the sitemap and has no structured data.
13. No endpoint or page shows a person's games to anyone but that person and their guardians, except a game its owner shares, which opens only at its own link, and a game linked from a crosstable, which shows only on its event's results page: in its two cells, in Games from this event and, once chosen, as the key game.
14. The tournament picker lists every tournament the caller, or a child they guard, is in or has been in, newest first, with the current or latest game first, and any of their games can be tied or linked, live or past; tying or linking a game that neither the caller nor a guarded child played answers 403; a second linked game for one tournament game answers 409; the crosstable tick starts unticked.
15. Once a game is linked, both players' cells read "Game" and Games from this event lists it; each address `recipientsFor` names for the opponent gets exactly one email, and the opponent gets a My LCA line; Hide takes it off that row only; the owner, the event's directors and LCA admins can take it off, and a director's or an admin's removal is in the audit log.
16. A game whose profile has an active guardian link gets 403 with "Sharing a junior's game needs a guardian's OK" for a share link or a crosstable link until a guardian allows sharing for that child; Ask a parent to publish puts an item in each guardian's Today; withdrawing the permission makes the child's share links answer 404 and takes the child's games off every crosstable.
17. A sheet scanned from a board row is filed under that board, counts against the event's allowance and not the director's 20, puts a private copy in each player's My games, and shows "Scoresheet says" in words in the Status column and the queue; neither a scan nor Tell the TD ever changes `tournament_games.result`, which changes only through Confirm or Enter a different result.
18. With no sheet scanned, a round's results can all be confirmed and the next round paired, exactly as without WS15.
19. My games shows a card for each tournament, with a slot for each round of the player's section (dashed, with Scan it, where there is no game); the games outside tournaments in their own group below, with the same board, opponent, result in words, weekday date and state; a List view; and for a guardian the Everyone and per-child chips.
20. A photo is gone from `SCANS` 30 days after its scan unless its owner kept it with the game; Delete removes a game's photo at once, unless the other player's copy of a director's sheet still holds it within its 30 days; a game without its photo reads "Photo not kept; the moves we read are kept" and still opens in review.
21. With `scanner2` on, no page, email or printed card says photos are never stored; the page, the How it works rail, the finish card and the ready email say the photo is kept 30 days and who can see it, and the printed cards say it is kept 30 days.
22. The 21st page scanned by one member in one Louisiana day answers 429 with "You've used all 20 scans for today. The limit resets at midnight."; the count starts again at midnight Louisiana time; a failed reading hands its scan back.
23. `npm run scanner:check` passes; the reading never corrects a move, and only the chess.js decoder, in the browser, applies legality; a PGN that does not replay as legal moves is refused with 400.
24. The print page's Scoresheets prints, for a round and section, a Letter sheet for each player of each board with the header typed (the date with its weekday, the time control), result boxes 1-0 · ½-½ · 0-1, black ink and no marks or QR; on a member-created event each sheet carries "Organized by <name>. Not an LCA event." and no LCA logo; every board diagram in the kit draws piece images.
25. With `scanner2` off, `/scanner` draws today's page, `POST /api/scan` answers as today, and none of the parts above appears.
26. `/scanner` is in AC1's matrix (`PAGES` in `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts`) with `scanner2` on in the a11y-on build, and the scanner fixture's review, fix panel, finish card and My games pass axe in the light look and dark mode at 1280 and 390 pixels.
27. Every new endpoint has its contract in `domain/contracts` and a contract test, `test/unit/contract-coverage.test.ts` passes, and `PENDING_COUNT` never rises; `src/pages/ScannerPage.tsx` holds only the choice between today's scanner and the new one, and calls no `fetch`.
28. The finish card's Keep this game in My games starts ticked; unticked, Done removes the game and its photo after any export, and `GET /api/me/games` no longer lists it.

**Tests:**
- Integration (`test/integration`, the `invoke()` harness, the reading service stubbed):
  - `scan-jobs.test.ts`: the gate (401, 403, 202, and a profile covered by a Family membership); the 21st page in a Louisiana day; the scan handed back on unreadable, busy and down; the director's allowance (409 when used up) and 403 for a director of another event; a job still reading after 10 minutes marked failed, with its scans handed back; the ready email, once to each address `recipientsFor` names, through the outbox, and the Today item, and a failed reading sending no email and handing its scan back (criterion 6).
  - `games.test.ts`: reads by the owner, a guardian and a stranger (404 for an LCA admin and the opponent); PATCH for fixes, read back by a second device's GET (criterion 10), details, whose game and keep the photo; keep in My games unticked, which removes the game and its photo, and `GET /api/me/games` no longer lists it (criterion 28); the share link on, off and on again (a new slug); a PGN that does not replay (400); DELETE with the photo, and with the other player's copy of a director's sheet.
  - `games-linking.test.ts`: the picker (past and current events, the current game first); 403 for another person's game; 409 for a second linked game; the opponent's notice and Hide; removal by a director and by an admin (audited); the key game (409 before finalize, refused for a game not linked from that event).
  - `games-juniors.test.ts`: no share link or crosstable link without a guardian's OK; Ask a parent to publish; withdrawing the permission.
  - `scanner-queue.test.ts`: "Scoresheet says" rows from director sheets and Tell the TD, disagreements first; no scan writes `tournament_games.result`; Confirm writes it once; a round confirmed with no sheet scanned.
  - `scanner-retention.test.ts`: photos gone at 30 days, kept photos staying, unreadable sheets removed, `scan_usage` rows past 30 days cleared, and the 5-minute run's sweep of jobs still reading.
  - `contracts/scanner.test.ts`: one contract test for each new route.
  - Extend `role-safety.test.ts` with every scanner route.
- Unit (`test/unit`):
  - `scan-day.test.ts`: the Louisiana day either side of midnight, in summer and in winter.
  - `active-member.test.ts`: `requireActiveMember` on active, ending today, expired, pending and Family-covered profiles.
  - `share-slug.test.ts`: 16 random characters, no names, and a different slug each time.
  - `board-at-move.test.tsx`: piece images, no letters, and the text alternative.
  - `corner-check.test.tsx`: dragging, the arrow keys, and tapping the corner and then the spot.
  - `fix-panel.test.tsx`: any cell opens the panel; Confirm works every later move out again (`forcedSans`); Undo last change puts the game back (criterion 10).
  - `finish-card.test.tsx`: share, export and the crosstable link closed while a Check this is left (criterion 11); exactly two choices of who can see it, Only me first; Keep this game in My games ticked at first (criterion 28).
  - `my-games.test.tsx`: a card per tournament with a slot for each round (dashed, with Scan it, where there is no game), the games outside tournaments in their own group, the List view, and a guardian's chips (criterion 19).
  - `scoresheet-sheet.test.tsx`: the typed header with the weekday date and the time control, the result boxes, no marks or QR, a member-created event's line with no LCA logo, and piece images in every diagram (criterion 24).
  - `scanner-states.test.tsx`: each capture, reading and review state's words.
  - `scanner-words.test.ts`: the new photo line where it belongs, and no "never stored" in any file the new scanner draws.
  - `scanner-switch.test.tsx`: with `scanner2` off, `/scanner` is today's page, and the Scholastic page carries no kit line.
- Browser: `test/a11y/scopes.spec.ts` (adds `/scanner`) and `test/a11y/scanner.spec.ts` (the fixture's screens, light and dark, at 1280 and 390 pixels).
- Existing: `npm run scanner:check` (the sandbox checks).
- By hand, recorded in `REDESIGN_STATUS.md`: capture on a real iPhone in Safari and a real Android phone in Chrome; a printed scoresheet read back; the kit's PDFs printed at 100% on Letter.

**Depends on:** WS01 (the components and tokens named above); WS02 (`hideTabBar` on capture, and the Scoresheet scanner links); WS04 (the share-preview renderer and the share tags); WS06 (`member_guardians`, `recipientsFor` and profiles with their own login from 13); WS07 (the results page and its crosstable, players' reports, the print kit and the notifications outbox); WS08 (the results grid, the Reported results queue and the admin event page); WS10 (finalize, which the key game waits for); WS11 (the Scholastic page that takes the kit's one line, Phase 3); WS13 (My LCA's You, Family and Today).
**Risks & open decisions:**
- Some phones open the camera only inside the tap itself (iOS Safari in particular); the fallback is the one Take a photo button (verify on both phones).
- `waitUntil` may stop before three pages are read; read them side by side, and move to a Queue if it does (verify).
- Scoresheet photos, children's included, are now stored: they stay private, 30 days by default, never public, with a preview bucket of their own and the lifecycle rule as a backstop (verify R2's rules by prefix).
- The API cost stays bounded by the day's 20, the event's allowance and no second reading of a saved draft.
- The piece set's licence, and the licence of the list of named openings the search uses (verify both before they ship).
- The count's day moves from UTC to Louisiana time; today's endpoint keeps writing UTC dates until it goes.
- Straightening the photo from its corners is new browser code; when it fails on a photo, the photo goes as taken.
- Open for K, each with its default (section 5): Q5, Q12, Q25 to Q31 and Q39.
